### [1] [DeSpatial: The devil of Agentic Spatial Reasoning Lies in 3D Itself](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20990)
*Yifei Liu,Xiaolong Zhou,Ziyang Gong,Yifan Zhou,Yuanyuan Gao,Yuning Gong,Yuchen Yang,Hongjie Zhang,Xue Yang,Xiao Sun,Zhihang Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DeSpatial 框架，将空间理解显式解耦为"感知"与"推理"两部分，通过共享的 3D 感知工具把独立的重建/分割输出统一为度量、重力对齐的 3D 场景，在 VSI-Bench、MindCube-Tiny、ViewSpatial 及 VSI-SUPER 上取得领先性能，表明空间理解的瓶颈在于精确的 3D 感知本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间智能体虽可调用重建与分割工具，但预测不一致与物体身份碎片化使输出难以形成可靠的 3D 场景信息。一个 oracle 实验揭示该感知瓶颈：仅把工具预测的感知替换为真值 3D 边界框（推理工具不变），智能体即在五个以物体为中心的 VSI-Bench 任务上接近满分，说明问题根源在 3D 感知而非推理。

Method: DeSpatial 引入一个共享 3D 感知工具，将独立感知输出调和为统一、度量、重力对齐的 3D 场景，包含两个核心模块：(a) 联合分割–深度协同细化模块，合并同一物体的掩码并消除单帧内由分割与深度不一致引起的投影伪影；(b) 跨视角实例融合模块，合并体素重叠的实例但禁止同帧实例合并，并通过检查 3D 结构与源掩码外观来融合因多视角观测不足产生的空间不连通碎片。获得完整统一 3D 场景后，智能体再选择推理或计算工具求解空间问题。

Result: 在 VSI-Bench、MindCube-Tiny、ViewSpatial 上取得领先性能，并在 VSI-SUPER 上给出长视频结果。

Conclusion: 空间理解的瓶颈在于精确的 3D 感知本身；以统一 3D 场景表示弥合感知与推理的鸿沟，是提升空间智能体可靠性的关键。

Abstract: Spatial agents have access to reconstruction and segmentation tools, yet inconsistent predictions and fragmented object identities can prevent their outputs from becoming reliable 3D scene information. A simple oracle experiment exposes this perception bottleneck: replacing tool-predicted perception with ground-truth 3D bounding boxes raises an agent to nearly full score across five object-centric VSI-Bench tasks, while retaining the same reasoning tools. Motivated by this finding, we believe the devil of spatial reasoning lies in 3D itself, and introduce DeSpatial(Devil of Spatial), which explicitly decomposes spatial understanding into perception and reasoning. DeSpatial has a shared 3D perception tool to reconcile independent perception outputs into a unified, metric, gravity-aligned 3D scene. This perception tool has two core components: a) a joint segmentation--depth co-refinement module that consolidates masks of the same object and removes projection artifacts caused by inconsistent segmentation and depth within a single frame; b) a cross-view instance fusion module that consolidate voxel-overlapping instances but not allowed to merge same-frame instances. This fusion module additionally fuses spatially disjoint fragments caused by insufficient multi-view observations, by checking their 3D structure and source mask appearance. After receiving the complete and unified 3D scene information, the agent chooses reasoning or computational tools to solve the spatial problem. DeSpatial achieves leading performance on VSI-Bench, MindCube-Tiny, and ViewSpatial, with additional long-video results on VSI-SUPER, revealing the bottleneck of spatial understanding lies in a precise 3D perception itself.

</details>


### [2] [DefoliationCAM: An Edge-AI System for Real-Time Insect Defoliation Severity Assessment in Grapevines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20879)
*Fuchen Li,Wei-Zhen Liang,Pin-Chu Lai,Xin Qiao,Jingqiu Chen,Haw Yen,Zonglin Yanga,Chi-En Chiang,Rudolph Avalos,Abbigail L. Rath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出太阳能供电边缘AI系统DefoliationCAM（D-CAM），通过YOLOX-Nano定位葡萄藤主蔓、MobileNet通道注意力分类器评估冠层落叶程度，经LoRa传输至网页仪表盘，实现自动化、客观的落叶严重度监测与阈值分级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虫害引起的落叶威胁葡萄健康与产量，但严重度评估目前依赖主观人工巡田，效率低且不一致，缺乏高频、客观的监测手段。

Method: 两阶段流水线：YOLOX-Nano在4K图像中定位葡萄藤主蔓，MobileNet分类器加轻量通道注意力对320×320图块分五类（背景/无至可忽略/轻度/中度/重度）；29,674个田间图块标签经合并中重度以缓解类别不平衡（约40:1降至8.8:1），并采用合成增强；图块预测聚合为冠层级非线性全局落叶严重度（NGDS）评分，用二次判别分析确定分级边界；部署于Jetson Orin Nano（TensorRT FP16），LoRa通信。

Result: 合成增强使macro-F1从0.768升至0.838；分类器以1.08M参数优于ResNet-50（23.52M）。对86幅留出冠层图像验证，QDA给出9%和29%两条边界，区分可忽略、需监测的轻度与需现场巡查及考虑施药的处理级损伤；29%边界与葡萄日本甲虫25%–30%防治阈值吻合。单张4K图像处理约1.5s，完整运行周期57–60s；两台田间单元五周部署验证了自主太阳能运行、LoRa通信与远程监测可行性。

Conclusion: D-CAM实现了低成本、低功耗的边缘端冠层落叶自动评估，支持高频客观的定向巡田与及时植保决策，具备田间长期自主运行能力。

Abstract: Insect-induced defoliation threatens grapevine health and yield, yet severity assessment relies on subjective manual scouting. We developed DefoliationCAM (D-CAM), a solar-powered edge AI system that estimates canopy defoliation and transmits results via LoRa to a web dashboard. The pipeline uses YOLOX-Nano to localize grapevine cordons in 4K images and a MobileNet-based classifier with lightweight channel attention to classify 320 x 320 pixel patches. The 29,674 field-collected patches were labeled as background or one of four damage levels: no to negligible, mild, moderate, and severe. Merging moderate and severe classes reduced the class imbalance ratio from approximately 40:1 to 8.8:1. Synthetic augmentation increased macro-F1 from 0.768 to 0.838. The classifier outperformed ResNet-50 in macro-F1 with fewer parameters (1.08 M versus 23.52 M). Patch predictions were aggregated into a canopy-level Non-linear Global Defoliation Severity (NGDS) score and validated against an entomologist's ratings of 86 held-out canopy images. Quadratic discriminant analysis identified boundaries of 9% and 29% separating negligible damage, mild damage requiring monitoring, and action-level damage warranting on-site scouting and insecticide treatment consideration. The 29% boundary aligned with the 25%-30% action threshold range for Japanese beetle management in grapevine. Using TensorRT FP16 on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano, the pipeline processed each 4K image in approximately 1.5 s, with a complete operating cycle of 57-60 s. A five-week deployment of two field units demonstrated the feasibility of autonomous solar-powered operation, LoRa communication, and remote defoliation monitoring. D-CAM supports frequent, objective canopy damage assessment for targeted scouting and timely pest-management decisions.

</details>


### [3] [Temporal Periodic Image Registration with Implicit Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21845)
*Mathias Lowes,Kristine Aavild Sørensen,Maxime Sermesant,Rasmus R. Paulsen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Temporal-IDIR，将隐式神经表示（INR）扩展到周期性时序图像序列的形变配准，用单一时序INR同时建模所有帧的形变，并引入时序一致性损失实现自正则化，在DIR-LAB上取得1.03 mm的TRE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: INR已在形变图像配准中表现有效，但现有工作主要针对静态图像对；周期性时序图像序列（如呼吸/心动周期）中的多帧配准尚缺乏统一的INR建模方式，且帧间形变的时间一致性未被显式利用。

Method: 将INR扩展至时序配准：用单一INR以帧索引为条件同时表示整段序列中所有帧到参考帧的形变场；引入时序一致性损失，约束由源帧直接到目标帧的变换与经由中间帧复合得到的变换之间的差异，从而借助自身形变预测实现自正则化；同一INR还可对未给出的时间点插值，实现帧间平滑形变估计。

Result: 在DIR-LAB数据集上，以标注关键点与形变后关键点之间的目标配准误差（TRE）为指标，取得1.03 mm的TRE，优于其他基于INR的配准方法；框架支持时间帧之间的平滑插值。

Conclusion: 时序一致性自正则化的INR可以统一、有效地建模周期性时序图像的形变，在提升配准精度的同时天然支持时间连续插值，为时序形变配准提供了新范式。

Abstract: Implicit Neural Representations (INRs) have recently gained popularity for their ability to model functions using simple and lightweight networks. These methods have also proven effective in deformable image registration. In this work, we extend INRs for deformable image registration to the domain of temporal image registration, focusing on periodic temporal image sequences. Our approach, Temporal-IDIR, optimizes a single INR to model deformations across all frames in a temporal image sequence simultaneously, allowing for self-regularization through its own deformation predictions. To achieve this, we introduce a temporal consistency loss that penalizes discrepancies between direct source-to-target transformations and those traversing intermediate frames. We evaluate our framework on the DIR-LAB dataset, using the target registration error (TRE) between annotated and moved landmarks as the metric. Here, we achieve a TRE of 1.03 mm, outperforming other INR-based registration methods. Additionally, our framework supports smooth interpolation between time frames by estimating deformations between the given input frames. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/MMLowes/Temporal_INR.

</details>


### [4] [Learning to Think and Explore in 3D World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21783)
*Yijiang Li,Bingyang Wang,Nuno Vasconcelos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ACT3D：保持 VLM 原生单帧视觉输入，将 3D 场景仅作为可交互环境，通过"轨迹监督微调 + 仅以最终答案正确性为奖励的强化学习"让模型自主学习主动探索策略，在六个 3D 基准上平均 53.2%，较 Qwen 基线提升 22.0%，超过 Gemini 3.6 Flash。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 视觉语言任务通常向模型输入多视角图像、视频或显式 3D 表示，偏离了 VLM 原生的单图编码方式，也缺乏对人类式"边看边走边想"的主动探索能力建模。作者希望验证：能否不改变视觉编码接口，仅把 3D 表示为可交互环境，让模型像人一样逐帧观察并通过动作获取证据。

Method: 1) 环境设定：每次 rollout 从第一人称初始视角与一个视觉问题出发，模型通过离散动作（如左移）与渲染观测帧迭代交互，可进行推理、导航、调整朝向，最后给出答案。2) 两阶段训练：先做轨迹监督微调（trajectory-supervised finetuning）冷启动，再施加强化学习；RL 奖励主要由最终答案正确性驱动，对访问哪些视角、采取何种探索动作不作任何直接监督，探索策略完全从"交互是否提升表现"中习得。

Result: 六个 3D 基准平均准确率 53.2%，较基座 Qwen 提升 22.0%，超过 Gemini 3.6 Flash；优于现有开源 3D 方法且输入帧数减少 50–70%；在未见域上较监督初始化平均再提升 5.9%。分析显示探索具有问题条件自适应性：初始视角即可回答的问题提升仅 0.6%，需大量探索的问题提升达 16.3%；模型能寻找被遮挡目标、避免无效探索、为空间比较获取互补视角。

Conclusion: 基于结果的强化学习可使 VLM 不仅对视觉证据进行推理，还能学会"何时"与"如何"获取视觉证据，从而以原生单帧接口实现高效、可泛化的主动 3D 探索。

Abstract: We study how vision-language models (VLMs) can learn to think and actively explore in 3D space. Instead of providing multi-view images, videos, or 3D representations to the model, we preserve the native visual encoding of VLMs, i.e., a single image, one at a time, just like humans, and treat the 3D representation only as an environment with which the model can interact through actions (e.g., move left) and observations (rendered images). Each rollout begins from an initial first-person view and a visual question. The model can then iteratively reason, navigate, and reorient its viewpoint before committing to a final answer. Following an initial trajectory-supervised finetuning, we apply reinforcement learning (RL) with rewards driven primarily by final-answer correctness. Crucially, RL provides no direct supervision over which viewpoints the model should visit or which exploration actions it should take. Instead, the model must learn an effective exploration policy solely from the extent to which its interactions improve performance. Across six 3D benchmarks, our model ACT3D achieves an average accuracy of 53.2% that improves its base Qwen model by 22.0% and surpasses proprietary Gemini 3.6 Flash. ACT3D also outperforms existing open-source 3D methods while using 50-70% fewer input frames, and generalizes to unseen domains with an average improvement of 5.9% over its supervised initialization. Further analysis reveals adaptive, question-conditioned exploration. Performance gains over the base model increase from 0.6% on questions answerable from the initial view to 16.3% on those requiring extensive exploration. ACT3D searches for occluded targets, avoids unnecessary exploration, and acquires complementary viewpoints for spatial comparisons. These findings demonstrate that outcome-based RL enables VLMs not only to reason over visual evidence, but also to learn when and how to acquire it.

</details>


### [5] [DINO-FIL: Multi-Layer Transformer Fusion for Generative Inpainting Forgery Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21780)
*Simon Freitter,David Fischinger,Silvia Poletti,Marcel Hasenbalg,Martin Boyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DINO-FIL：基于DINOv3 ViT-H+/16多层特征与轻量融合解码器的Transformer框架，用于生成式图像伪造的像素级定位，跨数据集泛化性强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单层特征方法对编辑引入的细微、空间局部化伪影敏感度不足，且生成式图像伪造定位需要同时捕捉语义结构与纹理不一致，故需提升跨域泛化能力。

Method: 以预训练DINOv3 ViT-H+/16编码器提取多深度中间Transformer特征，经轻量融合解码器聚合多层表示，实现像素级伪造定位。跨数据集评估：B-Free训练，Beyond the Brush、CocoGlide、TGIF、SAGI-D测试。

Result: 平均性能优于现有基线，在五个评估数据集中的四个上领先；在真实（原始）图像上保持高真阴性率。

Conclusion: 多层特征聚合可有效增强对细微伪造痕迹的敏感性与跨数据集泛化能力，验证了强预训练ViT编码器在生成式图像取证中的潜力。

Abstract: We propose DINO-FIL, a transformer-based framework for pixel-level localization of generative image forgeries. The method leverages multi-layer representations from a pretrained DINOv3 ViT-H+/16 encoder combined with a lightweight fusion decoder to capture both semantic structure and fine-grained texture inconsistencies introduced by image editing. By aggregating intermediate transformer features across depths, the model improves sensitivity to subtle, spatially localized artifacts that are often missed by standard single-layer approaches. We evaluate DINO-FIL in a cross-dataset setting, training on B-Free and testing on Beyond the Brush, CocoGlide, TGIF, and SAGI-D. The model achieves strong generalization, outperforming existing baselines on average and on four of five evaluated datasets, while maintaining a high true negative rate on pristine images.

</details>


### [6] [FuseAdapt: Parameter-Efficient Multimodal Fusion for Semantic Segmentation with Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21779)
*Xin Zhang,Robby T. Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FuseAdapt：在冻结视觉基础模型(VFM)的适配空间内做多模态融合，Q/V低秩适配各模态私有、仅跨模态路由FFN低秩残差；并引入仅训练期使用的预测式模态建模(PMM)以增强跨模态冗余；在MCubeS/DELIVER/MUSES上以极小可训练预算显著提升全模态与缺失模态分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态语义分割依赖互补传感器，但测试时模态退化或缺失会使性能大幅下降。现有方法依赖专用融合模块或多分支跨模态编码器，模态缺失时融合不可靠；同时未充分利用VFM先验作为共享骨干。

Method: 参数量高效框架：冻结VFM作为共享骨干，为每个模态分配轻量适配分支；将Q/V低秩适配保持为模态私有，仅将FFN低秩残差激活跨模态路由，实现"适配空间融合"。预训练VFM保持固定，模态特定校正与显式跨模态交互均由适配路径学习。另提出Predictive Modality Modeling (PMM)：训练时遮蔽目标模态、由可见模态预测其嵌入，并以全模态输入下的教师目标为监督，为跨模态冗余提供直接表示级目标；PMM仅用于训练，无推理开销。

Result: 在MCubeS、DELIVER、MUSES三个基准上，FuseAdapt在完整与不完整模态设置下均稳定提升，可训练预算小；相比此前SOTA，全模态最高+3.01 mIoU，缺失模态最高+12.56 mIoU。

Conclusion: VFM先验可作为多模态分割的有效共享骨干，在适配空间内进行融合可在低参数预算下兼顾模态完整与缺失场景的鲁棒性；表示级跨模态预测目标(仅训练期)是应对模态缺失的有效手段。

Abstract: Multi-modal semantic segmentation benefits from complementary sensors, but this benefit is fragile when some modalities are degraded or missing at test time. Many existing methods rely on dedicated fusion modules or multi-branch cross-modal encoders, and the fusion often becomes unreliable when modalities are absent, leading to substantial performance drops. We propose \textbf{FuseAdapt}, a parameter-efficient framework that performs fusion within the adaptation space of a frozen vision foundation model (VFM), exploring whether VFM priors can serve as an effective shared backbone for multi-modal segmentation. Instead of attaching a standalone fusion network, FuseAdapt assigns each modality a lightweight adaptation branch and performs \emph{adaptation-space fusion} by keeping Q/V low-rank adaptation private to each modality and routing only FFN low-rank residual activations across modalities inside the frozen backbone. The pretrained VFM remains fixed while modality-specific corrections and explicit cross-modal interaction are learned through the adaptation paths. To handle missing modalities, we further introduce \textbf{Predictive Modality Modeling (PMM)}, a direct representation-level objective for cross-modal redundancy. During training, PMM masks a target modality and predicts its embedding from the visible ones, supervised by a teacher target computed under full-modality input. PMM is used only during training and adds no inference-time overhead. Across three multi-modal segmentation benchmarks (MCubeS, DELIVER, MUSES), FuseAdapt consistently improves both modality-complete and modality-incomplete segmentation with a small trainable budget, yielding up to +3.01 mIoU under full modalities and +12.56 mIoU under missing modalities over prior state-of-the-art. Project page: https://devinxzhang.github.io/FuseAdapt-Page/.

</details>


### [7] [Spurious Attention Heads in Vision Transformers: Mechanistic Identification of Annotation-Driven Bias in Medical Chest X-Ray Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21638)
*Joshua Muhumuza,Cornelius Kalenzi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在ViT-Base 16中发现单个注意力头L8H10充当"标注阅读器"，在胸片上关注角落文字而非肺部病变（62%），且在NIH ChestX-ray14、DeiT与ViT-Large中可复现；全量微调不能抑制该捷径，而输出级评估对此完全不可见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像中的ViT被指利用虚假相关（spurious correlations），但其内部机制尚未被识别，也难以修正，因此需要机制层面的审计手段来定位并验证这些捷径。

Method: 采用机制可解释性（mechanistic interpretability）方法定位ViT-Base 16中的具体机制：识别出注意力头L8H10专门关注胸片角落文字标注；通过inpainting进行因果验证，排除亮度与边缘密度等因素；并在NIH ChestX-ray14、DeiT-Base、ViT-Large 16上复现；进一步进行单头与多头消融及全量微调实验。

Result: L8H10在200张COVID-19影像的62%中关注角落文字；去除标注内容使受影响影像的肺部注意力提升1.52倍（p<0.0001）；复现率为NIH 79%、DeiT-Base 88%、ViT-Large 80%；全量微调后98.4%的虚假影像仍保持虚假，虚假率略升；单头消融p=0.340，三头消融p=0.532，即该经因果验证的"读标注回路"在输出级评估中完全不可见。

Conclusion: 虚假的"标注关注头"是ViT族架构跨两数据集、三架构的一致属性；全量微调强化而非消除该捷径；机制审计能够发现行为测试无法察觉的因果回路，强调了对医学ViT进行机制层面审计的必要性。

Abstract: Vision Transformers deployed in medical imaging exploit spurious correlations, yet the internal mechanisms remain unidentified and impossible to fix. We apply mechanistic interpretability to locate one such mechanism in ViT-Base 16: a single attention head (L8H10) that functions as a dedicated annotation-reader on chest X-rays, attending to corner text rather than lung pathology in 62% of 200 COVID-19 images. Causal validation via inpainting confirms annotation content rather than luminance or edge density drives the behaviour, increasing lung attention 1.52 times upon removal in affected images ($p{<}0.0001$). The finding replicates across NIH ChestX-ray14 (79%), DeiT-Base (88%), and ViT-Large 16 (80%), establishing spurious annotation-attending heads as a consistent property of ViT-family architectures across two datasets and three architectures. Full fine-tuning does not suppress the behaviour and may entrench it: 98.4% of spurious images remain spurious and the spurious rate rises marginally, as the shortcut is reinforced rather than removed. Critically, single-head ablation produces $p{=}0.340$ and multi-head ablation of all three identified carriers produces $p{=}0.532$, meaning a causally verified annotation-reading circuit is completely invisible to output-level evaluation. Mechanistic auditing finds what behavioural testing cannot.

</details>


### [8] [A Decision-Aware Evaluation Protocol for Uncertainty in Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21616)
*Saket Maganti,Shreyank N Gowda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种"决策感知"的医学分割不确定性评估协议，将不确定性估计与延期复核策略分离，在固定复核预算下评估二者的组合效果，并在视网膜血管、皮肤病变和息肉分割任务上实例化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学分割不确定性评估多用校准误差或像素级错误检测分数，但这些指标无法反映不确定性是否真正改善下游复核决策（哪些像素/区域/图像可接受、哪些需复核）。

Method: 提出决策感知评估协议：分离"不确定性估计"与"延期策略"，在固定复核预算下评估其组合；报告固定预算误差降低、风险-覆盖率行为、区域级复核代理指标、结构性失败模式、概率派生基线及对复核假设的敏感性；在三种任务、多种骨干网络与不确定性估计器上实例化（含MC Dropout、TTA、深度集成、学习式延期等）。

Result: 在匹配推理预算与标准多层MC Dropout下，TTA在匹配的MC–TTA比较中给出更强的错误检测信号（含架构与dropout位置检验）；但估计器排名并非普适：概率派生分数仍有竞争力，深度集成可优于TTA，学习式延期主要在基础估计器较弱时有效，排名会随复核单元、不确定性目标、纠正假设与代价模型变化；结构与第二观察者分析显示边界及细结构失败部分源于标注歧义。

Conclusion: 应将不确定性作为"估计器—策略—复核"的整体配置来评估，而非孤立的分数图。

Abstract: Medical segmentation uncertainty is often evaluated with calibration error or pixel-level error-detection scores, but these metrics do not show whether uncertainty improves downstream review decisions. We propose a decision-aware evaluation protocol that separates uncertainty estimation from deferral policy and evaluates their combination under fixed review budgets: which pixels, regions, or images should be accepted, and which should be reviewed. The protocol reports fixed-budget error reduction, risk-coverage behaviour, region-level review proxies, structural failure modes, probability-derived baselines, and sensitivity to review assumptions. We instantiate it across retinal vessel, skin lesion, and polyp segmentation with multiple backbones and uncertainty estimators. Under matched inference budgets and canonical multi-layer MC Dropout, test-time augmentation (TTA) gives a stronger error-detection signal across matched MC--TTA comparisons, including architecture and dropout-placement checks. However, estimator rankings are not universal. For example, probability-derived scores remain competitive, deep ensembles can outperform TTA, learned deferral helps mainly when the base estimator is weak, and rankings can change with review unit, uncertainty target, correction assumptions, and cost model. Structural and second-observer analyses further show that boundary and thin-structure failures are partly tied to annotation ambiguity. These findings support evaluating uncertainty as an estimator-policy-review configuration, not as a standalone score map. Code: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Uncertainity-in-Medical-Image-Segmentation/README.md

</details>


### [9] [SurgStreamer: proactive, request-driven monitoring of surgical video streams with structured state and a reusable reasoning layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21895)
*Bowen Xiang,Jon S. Heiselman,Michael I. Miga*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SurgStreamer，将手术视频事件监控拆分为"术式相关的感知"与"术式无关的推理"，在12例腹腔镜胆囊切除术密封测试集上宏事件F1达0.598，优于端到端流式视觉语言模型（≤0.540）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术团队需要能按请求监听视频事件（如"Calot三角解剖时夹钳出现就告诉我"）并回答已发生事件的助手。该任务需要精确语义与因果性的告警决策，而现有方法缺乏形式化定义与对告警时机的严格处理。

Method: 形式化"请求驱动的事件检测"任务，要求精确语义与因果告警决策。系统分离为两部分：(1) 术式相关感知：将1-FPS视频流映射为工具、动作、阶段的结构化状态；(2) 术式无关推理：在状态与事件记忆上评估形式化监控条件，可用精确规则或小型控制器（通过指针而非名称引用实体）。评估使用金标准条件。

Result: 12例腹腔镜胆囊切除术密封测试集全流程宏事件F1=0.598；仅给75秒窗口时达0.677。OneStreamer-4B各配置（含用其状态或改造视觉编码器微调）最高0.540（Holm校正 p≤0.012）。三个外部中心仅重训感知，留出术式性能由0.239升至0.470。新术式（垂体手术）上，在胆囊切除数据训练的控制器于oracle状态下与域内控制器相当（0.947 vs 0.938）；在预测状态下未达非劣效边界（-0.024 [-0.040, -0.008]），除非重新校准告警阈值。

Conclusion: 结果支持将感知与推理解耦的设计，并指出分布偏移下的感知是主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Surgical teams could benefit from assistants that watch the video for requested events, such as "tell me when the clip applier appears during Calot triangle dissection", and answer questions about what has already happened. We formalise this task as request-driven event detection with exact semantics and causal alert decisions, and present SurgStreamer. It separates procedure-specific perception, which maps the 1-FPS stream to a structured state over tools, actions and phases, from procedure-agnostic reasoning, which evaluates a formal monitoring condition over this state and an event memory, either with exact rules or with a small controller that addresses entities through pointers rather than names. We evaluate this monitoring core with gold conditions. On a pre-registered, sealed test set of 12 laparoscopic cholecystectomies, SurgStreamer reached a macro event-F1 of 0.598 over full procedures. On identical 75-s windows with only the window available to every method, it scored 0.677. The tested configurations of the streaming vision-language model OneStreamer-4B, including ones fine-tuned on SurgStreamer's state or on video with an adapted vision encoder, scored at most 0.540 (Holm-adjusted p <= 0.012). At three external centres, re-training only perception raised performance on held-out procedures from 0.239 to 0.470. On a new procedure, pituitary surgery, controller weights trained on cholecystectomy matched an in-domain controller on oracle state (0.947 vs 0.938). On predicted state they missed the pre-registered non-inferiority margin (-0.024 [-0.040, -0.008]) unless the alert thresholds were re-calibrated. The results support separating perception from reasoning and point to perception under distribution shift as the main limitation.

</details>


### [10] [Hear, Localize, and Reason: Spatially Aware Scene Understanding for Audio-Visual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21588)
*Kim Sung-Bin,Lee Jung-Mok,Jinwoo Jung,Oh Hyun-Bin,Hyeonggon Ryu,David Harwath,Tae-Hyun Oh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HLR-AVSceneQA基准与HLR-LLM模型，按"听见—定位—推理"层级评估并提升音视频大模型的空间音视频场景理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频大模型多将音频视为单声道语义内容，难以推理声源位置及其与视觉场景的关系；近期空间音视频研究仅覆盖空间对应或方向距离等单一能力，缺乏系统性的层级化评估与统一建模。

Method: 构建HLR-AVSceneQA基准，按hear-localize-reason层级考察三方面能力：识别所听内容、在以自我为中心（egocentric）和以他人为中心（allocentric）视角下定位声源、并利用定位声源推理其与视觉场景的关系。同时提出HLR-LLM，在强音视频基础模型的单声道语义音频通路之外增加双耳空间音频分支，并采用含末阶段思维链监督的课程学习进行多步空间推理训练。

Result: 实验表明现有AV-LLM在空间定位（spatial grounding）与关系推理上仍受限；HLR-LLM显著提升空间音视频场景理解，且不损害语义音视频感知性能。

Conclusion: 双耳空间音频分支与层级化课程训练可在保持语义理解的前提下有效增强空间音视频场景理解，HLR-AVSceneQA为系统评估此类能力提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Audio-visual large language models (AV-LLMs) have made strong progress in multimodal understanding, yet most treat audio as monaural semantic content, limiting their ability to reason about where sounds originate and how they relate to the visual scene. Recent spatial audio-visual studies address parts of this problem, but often focus on specific abilities, such as spatial correspondence or direction and distance reasoning. In this work, we introduce HLR-AVSceneQA, a comprehensive benchmark with a hear-localize-reason hierarchy that evaluates whether models can recognize what is heard, localize sound sources in egocentric and allocentric views, and use these localized sources to reason about how they relate to the visual scene. We further introduce HLR-LLM, which augments a strong audio-visual foundation model with a binaural spatial audio branch alongside its monaural semantic audio pathway. We train HLR-LLM with a curriculum that incorporates final-stage chain-of-thought supervision for multi-step spatial reasoning. Experiments show that existing AV-LLMs remain limited in spatial grounding and relational reasoning, while HLR-LLM substantially improves spatial audio-visual scene understanding without compromising semantic audio-visual perception.

</details>


### [11] [PET Quantification-Preserving Registration for Longitudinal Whole-Body PSMA PET/CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21451)
*Fryderyk Kögl,Anna Reithmeir,Daniel M. Lang,Emily Chan,Rickmer Braren,Julia A. Schnabel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将LapIRN粗到细微分同胚配准架构适配于Learn2Reg 2026 PSMAReg挑战的纵向全身PSMA PET/CT配准，并引入PET生物标志物保持损失（MTV/TLG）与骨骼刚性正则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向全身PSMA PET/CT配准需同时保证多模态形变配准精度与PET定量生物标志物（MTV、TLG）的一致性，而常规配准损失未显式约束这些临床指标。

Method: 在LapIRN粗到细微分同胚网络上，串联仿射预配准、多模态可形变配准与测试时实例优化；新增基于Jacobian约束的MTV与TLG保持损失，以及针对骨骼结构的逐结构刚性正则。

Result: 提交配置取得81.0% DSC与6.17 mm HD95，MTV与TLG误差均为3.3%（基于自设评估协议）。

Conclusion: 显式生物标志物保持损失与结构刚性正则可在保持配准精度的同时有效控制PET定量指标偏差；但结果基于自设协议，需在官方挑战评测下验证。

Abstract: We adapt the diffeomorphic coarse-to-fine LapIRN architecture for longitudinal whole-body PSMA PET/CT registration in the Learn2Reg 2026 PSMAReg challenge. The method combines affine pre-registration, multi-modal deformable registration, and test-time instance optimization. We further introduce PET biomarker preservation losses for metabolic tumor volume (MTV) and total lesion glycolysis (TLG), including Jacobian-based constraints, and a per-structure rigidity regularization for skeletal structures. The submitted configuration achieved 81.0% DSC and 6.17 mm HD95, with MTV and TLG errors of 3.3% and 3.3%, respectively, on our evaluation protocol. Code is available at https://github.com/koegl/psmareg.

</details>


### [12] [Purify Before You Align: Improving Representation Alignment for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20761)
*Yingheng Wang,Yaoqiang Li,Yaqin Wu,Junwen Bai,Jiatao Gu,Christopher De Sa,Volodymyr Kuleshov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出局部外观提纯（LAP），通过移除对齐目标中局部可预测的内容并对残差进行对齐，提升了扩散模型的生成质量，并使 MAE 等编码器成为更有效的对齐目标，同时提出常数读出分数以预测提纯收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准表示对齐只追求匹配预训练编码器的表示，却不区分其中哪些信息对生成更有用；作者希望明确"对齐目标强调什么信息"而非"包含多少图像信息"才是有效对齐的关键。

Method: LAP 仅修改对齐目标：从预训练表示中减去局部可预测（局部外观）部分，并让扩散模型的中间特征对齐该残差；不改变扩散训练或推理流程。作者给出标准对齐的变分解释，以及在固定局部预测器下逐 token 信息界之和的理论分析；并提出一个精确的常数读出测试，度量在不使用扩散模型隐状态时目标可被匹配的程度。

Result: 在固定编码器内的受控干预表明有效对齐取决于目标强调的信息类型而非信息量。LAP 在 MAE、AIMv2、CLIP、DINOv2、DINOv3 五种表示上均提升生成质量，MAE 增益最大：ImageNet-256 上 SiT-B/2 的 FID 由 53.1 降至 42.3；收益在更强对齐方案与分词器下依然存在。常数读出分数在扩散训练前计算，可预测提纯收益（13 个编码器上 Spearman ρ=0.94）。

Conclusion: 对齐目标的"信息构成"比"信息含量"更重要；通过对齐局部外观残差，LAP 是一种即插即用、无需改动训练与推理的改进手段，且其收益可在训练前用常数读出分数可靠预测。

Abstract: Representation alignment improves and accelerates diffusion training by encouraging intermediate features to match a pretrained visual encoder. However, standard alignment matches pretrained representations without distinguishing what information may be more useful for generation. We introduce local appearance purification (LAP), which improves generation quality and makes encoders such as MAE more effective alignment targets by removing locally predictable content and aligning to the residual. LAP modifies only the alignment target, and requires no changes to diffusion training or inference. Through controlled interventions within a fixed encoder, we show that effective alignment depends on what information the target emphasizes, not simply how much image information it contains. Our theory gives a variational interpretation of standard alignment and, for fixed local predictors, a sum of token-wise information bounds conditioned on local appearance. To understand why purification benefits some encoders more than others, we derive an exact constant-readout test that measures how well the target can be matched without using the diffusion model's hidden state. LAP improves generation quality across five pretrained representations (MAE, AIMv2, CLIP, DINOv2, DINOv3), with the largest gain for MAE: on ImageNet-256 with SiT-B/2, it reduces FID from $53.1$ to $42.3$. Its gains persist with stronger alignment recipes and tokenizers. Computed before diffusion training, the constant-readout score predicts the benefit of purification (Spearman $ρ=0.94$ across 13 encoders).

</details>


### [13] [Orbia: Do Generated Videos Form a Consistent 3D World?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20693)
*Dongyue Lu,Guian Fang,Hur Lim,Yonggang Wu,Yanrong Wang,Hongshan Chen,Shengju Qian,Yue Liao,Wei Chow,Lingdong Kong,Tianxin Huang,Yu Yang,Hongji Yang,Junchao Huang,Ao Liang,Yihao Wang,Zihan Wang,Rong Li,Hanlin Chen,Xin Wang,Wei Tsang Ooi,Mike Zheng Shou,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Orbia——首个系统、全面地评估交互式世界模型三维一致性的基准。它通过受控探索与回访，从输入保持、生成内容持久性、三维自一致性三个维度评测，构建了 720 个室内外样本、6 种探索-回访协议，并适配 27 个文本/动作/相机条件模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成器被用作可探索与模拟的交互世界，但画面真实美观并不意味着描绘了一致的三维世界。现有基准仅评估部分几何性质，或用视觉语言模型判断记忆与一致性，缺乏系统的三维评测。

Method: 构建 Orbia 基准，通过受控探索与回访将评测分为三维度：输入保持（用配准的 RGB-D 参考与标注锚点测外观、几何、空间稳定性）、生成内容持久性（将输入中缺失区域的重访与首次生成观测对比）、三维自一致性（跨视角深度与特征对应、区域尺度一致、留出高斯渲染）。并加入分钟级长时探索与回访，数据来自公开数据集、自采场景与仿真，形成 720 个样本、覆盖室内外与 6 种协议；通过原生接口适配器评测 27 个模型。

Result: 形成覆盖室内外环境、6 种探索-回访协议、共 720 个实例的基准，并对 27 个文本/动作/相机条件模型完成评测，可定位各类三维不一致问题。

Conclusion: Orbia 为诊断三维不一致、发展更具持久性的视频世界模型提供了系统化基准基础。

Abstract: Video generators serve as interactive worlds for exploration and simulation, yet realistic and visually appealing frames do not necessarily depict a consistent 3D world. Existing benchmarks assess selected geometric properties or use vision-language models to judge memory and consistency, leaving systematic 3D evaluation underexplored. We introduce Orbia, the first benchmark dedicated to a systematic and comprehensive evaluation of 3D consistency in interactive world models. Through controlled exploration and revisit, we organize the evaluation into three dimensions: input preservation, generated-content persistence, and 3D self-consistency. For input preservation, registered RGB-D references and annotated anchors support appearance, geometry, and spatial stability measurements. For generated-content persistence, we compare revisits to regions absent from the input image with their first generated observations to test whether models preserve what they create. For 3D self-consistency, cross-view depth and feature correspondence, regional scale agreement, and held-out Gaussian rendering test whether generated views describe the same 3D scene. Minute-long exploration and revisits further test long-term consistency. Our construction and annotation pipeline brings together public datasets, self-captured scenes, and simulation, yielding 720 instances across indoor and outdoor environments and six exploration-and-revisit protocols. Evaluation spans 27 text-, action-, and camera-conditioned models through native-interface adapters. Our Orbia benchmark provides a systematic basis for diagnosing 3D inconsistency and developing more persistent video world models.

</details>


### [14] [L2GSSL: Label-Efficient Local-to-Global Self-Supervised Learning for Volumetric Healthcare Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21356)
*Po-Cheng Cheng,Hung-Hsuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出局部到全局自监督学习框架L2GSSL，将3D CT预训练分解为层内与层间表征空间，在标签稀缺条件下显著优于体级对比方法，且具备跨数据集泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学CT体数据维度高、专家标注稀缺，需充分利用大量无标注体数据；现有体级自监督方法难以兼顾层内局部细节与体数据的全局上下文建模，同时受GPU显存限制。

Method: L2GSSL将预训练拆分为层内（intra-slice）与层间（inter-slice）两个表征空间：切片编码器学习视角不变的局部特征，全局编码器聚合切片token以建模体数据上下文；用stop-gradient将局部空间与全局目标隔离；采用逐切片执行与三阶段训练调度以适配有限显存。

Result: LIDC-IDRI肺结节恶性分类上，仅用10%标签达0.9229 AUROC、25%标签达0.9377，分别超过体级VICReg 0.0229与0.0294；全标签下最强监督基线仅高出0.0044。无外部适配迁移至LNDb时，25%标签微调模型取得0.8138 AUROC与0.7589 AUPRC，较最强自监督基线提升0.1130与0.0954。实验覆盖多数据源、胸腹解剖部位、多下游任务及两种骨干网络；病灶切片掩码实验表明预测依赖病灶内容。

Conclusion: 局部到全局分层自监督是高效利用无标注CT体数据的可行范式，在标签受限时收益最大，并具有跨队列泛化与对病灶内容的可解释依赖性。

Abstract: Healthcare institutions routinely accumulate high-dimensional computed tomography (CT) volumes, but only a fraction can be annotated by experts. We present L2GSSL, a local-to-global self-supervised learning framework that leverages unlabeled volumes by decomposing pretraining into intra-slice and inter-slice representation spaces. A slice encoder learns view-invariant local features, and a global encoder aggregates the resulting tokens to model volumetric context. The stop-gradient isolates the local space from the global objective, while slice-wise execution and a three-phase schedule support training under constrained GPU memory. On LIDC-IDRI lung nodule malignancy classification, L2GSSL reaches $0.9229$ AUROC with only $10\%$ of the labels and $0.9377$ with $25\%$, exceeding the volume-level VICReg by $0.0229$ and $0.0294$, respectively. With all labels, the strongest supervised baseline exceeds L2GSSL by only $0.0044$ AUROC, consistent with self-supervised pretraining providing its greatest value when annotations are limited. Without adaptation to the external LNDb cohort, a model fine-tuned on $25\%$ of LIDC-IDRI obtains $0.8138$ AUROC and $0.7589$ AUPRC, improving over the strongest evaluated self-supervised baseline by $0.1130$ and $0.0954$. Experiments across data sources, thoracic and abdominal anatomy, downstream tasks, and two backbones examine healthcare-data variety, while lesion-slice masking shows that the predictions depend on lesion-bearing content.

</details>


### [15] [Dusting the Phantom: Gaussian Allocation for Fading Dynamic Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21302)
*Minhyeok Roh,Minseo Lee,Jun Young Jeong,Byeonghyeon Lee,Lucas Yunkyu Lee,Jaesik Park,Eunbyung Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对动态高斯表示中快速运动小物体消失、高动态区域细节丢失的问题，指出瓶颈在于高斯原语的"分配位置与时序"，提出Seed Injection与Temporal Biased Relocation两种互补策略，在不增加高斯预算的前提下提升重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态高斯方法在实时体积视频上已较实用，但复杂运动会破坏重建：小而快的物体可能消失，高动态区域细纹理常丢失；简单延长训练或增加高斯数量仍无法恢复这类物体。作者认为核心瓶颈不是表示容量，而是高斯原语"何时何地"被分配——现有分配策略依赖当前高斯位置，忽略了被重定位高斯的时空目的地。

Method: 提出两种互补策略：(1) Seed Injection：利用时序外观变化与重建误差识别欠重建的动态区域，并通过多视角观测在几何合理的3D位置初始化新高斯，且独立于已有高斯位置；(2) Temporal Biased Relocation：利用时间分辨的优化信号引导被重定位高斯的时空放置。两者均在固定高斯预算下工作，不改变底层表示。

Result: 在多个动态场景数据集上的实验表明重建质量提升，尤其在高动态区域，能够恢复小而快速运动的物体以及细粒度外观细节。

Conclusion: 通过改进高斯原语的时空分配（而非增加容量），可在固定预算下有效提升动态高斯重建对快速小物体与精细纹理的恢复能力。

Abstract: Dynamic Gaussian representations have made high-quality, real-time volumetric video increasingly practical, yet challenging motion can still break their reconstruction: small fast-moving objects may disappear, and fine textures in highly dynamic regions are often lost. In particular, simply training longer or using more Gaussians may still fail to recover small, fast-moving objects. We argue that the key bottleneck is not representational capacity itself, but where and when Gaussian primitives are allocated. Existing allocation strategies rely on current Gaussian locations and often overlook the spatiotemporal destinations of relocated Gaussians. To address these limitations, we introduce two complementary strategies. First, Seed Injection identifies under-reconstructed dynamic regions using temporal appearance changes and reconstruction errors, and initializes new Gaussians at geometrically plausible 3D locations through multi-view observations, independent of existing Gaussian locations. Second, Temporal Biased Relocation uses time-resolved optimization signals to guide the spatiotemporal placement of relocated Gaussians. Both strategies maintain a fixed Gaussian budget without modifying the underlying representation. Experiments across multiple dynamic-scene datasets demonstrate improved reconstruction quality, particularly in highly dynamic regions, recovering small, rapidly moving objects and fine-grained appearance details.

</details>


### [16] [DLOSAC: Joint Spatial-Geometric Image Estimation via Delaunay-Guided Local Optimization RANSAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21215)
*Yu Liu,Meiqi Zhou,Zipei Fan,Guanyuan Feng,Xuan Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DLOSAC局部优化框架，通过Delaunay三角剖分联合建模几何一致性与空间分布信息，提升含噪匹配点下的图像几何估计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RANSAC类方法及其局部优化以残差为中心；即便有强制空间均匀性的启发式尝试，也因串行两阶段结构使分布优化与几何精化解耦，无法有效权衡二者。

Method: 在候选内点上构建Delaunay三角剖分显式编码邻域拓扑；几何模块引入基于边的（edge-based）一致性度量评估几何误差；空间模块由三角形面积与边属性导出分布描述子以量化内点聚集程度；最终联合边级几何约束、空间分布正则化与传统点级几何约束共同优化几何参数。

Result: 在六个公开真实数据集、三类几何估计任务上取得state-of-the-art性能。

Conclusion: 将空间分布正则化与几何一致性在同一局部优化框架内联合建模，可克服两阶段解耦的局限，实现分布优化与几何精化的有效权衡。

Abstract: Reliable image geometric estimation from noisy matching points is typically addressed using RANSAC, often combined with local optimization to refine inliers and model parameters. However, existing paradigms are predominantly residual-centric; even heuristic attempts to enforce spatial uniformity suffer from a sequential, two-stage nature that inherently decouples distribution optimization from geometric refinement, preventing an effective trade-off. In this paper, we propose DLOSAC, a local optimization framework that jointly incorporates both geometric consistency and spatial distribution information. We begin by constructing a Delaunay triangulation over candidate inliers to explicitly encode neighborhood topology. The geometric module introduces an edge-based consistency metric to evaluate geometric errors, while the spatial module derives distribution descriptors from triangle areas and edge attributes to quantify the clustering of inliers. Finally, we combine edge-based geometric constraints, spatial distribution regularization, and traditional point-level geometric constraints to improve the quality of geometric parameters. Extensive experiments on six publicly available real-world datasets, covering three types of geometric estimation tasks, achieve state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [17] [SpaceCache: Intermediate Feature Reuse for Efficient Semantic Segmentation on Point Cloud Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21207)
*Dongheon Yang,Beomseok Kang,Jae-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpaceCache 是一种免训练的特征缓存方法，通过跨帧复用点云 Transformer 中间层特征，实现最多1.65倍加速且 mIoU 下降小于1点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分割网络对点云流逐帧从零计算，尽管相邻帧高度重叠；作者观察到点云 Transformer 的中间特征在帧间对同一空间区域保持语义一致（尤见于深层），存在可复用的冗余计算。

Method: 免训练特征缓存：已观测区域的 token 跳过若干 Transformer 块并直接取用缓存特征，仅计算剩余 token，随后恢复完整 token 布局以做稠密预测；辅以轻量缓存管理（限制条目寿命、剔除低置信度条目）保证可靠性；与 token 合并不同，token 选择成本可忽略且缓存操作每编码阶段仅执行一次。

Result: 在 nuScenes、Waymo、ScanNet 等室内外基准上，配合 Utonia 与 LitePT-B 两种 Transformer 骨干，取得最高1.65倍加速、mIoU 下降小于1点，并稳定优于 token 合并方法。

Conclusion: 跨帧特征复用是加速点云流分割的有效且无需训练的手段，兼顾效率与精度，优于 token 合并类加速方案。

Abstract: Point-cloud streams are central to autonomous driving and robotics, yet existing segmentation networks process every frame from scratch, although consecutive frames largely overlap. We observe that the intermediate features of point-cloud transformers remain semantically consistent across frames for the same spatial region, especially in deep stages. Building on this, we present SpaceCache, a training-free feature cache that reuses these features across frames. Tokens of previously observed regions skip a sequence of transformer blocks and take their cached features, only the remaining tokens are computed, and the full token layout is restored for dense prediction. Lightweight cache management keeps reused features reliable by limiting each entry's age and evicting low-confidence entries. Unlike token merging, SpaceCache selects tokens at negligible cost and runs its cache operations once per encoder stage. On both outdoor and indoor benchmarks, such as nuScenes, Waymo, and ScanNet, with two transformer backbones of Utonia and LitePT-B, SpaceCache achieves up to 1.65x speedup with less than 1-point mIoU drop and consistently outperforms token-merging methods.

</details>


### [18] [DIAL: Dynamic and Informed Adjustment of the Native Stochastic Law for Reliable Weather Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21639)
*Yucheng Wang,Haonan Guo,Tianhao Zhao,Di Wang,Yu Wu,Zengmao Wang,Bo Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DIAL 通过在线调控冻结集合预报模型的固有随机性来修正集合离散度偏差，无需重训练或全链反向传播，在 FCN3 上将多尺度 CRPS 提升 8.27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集合天气预报在全局平均上看似可靠，但不同变量、空间尺度、预报时效和天气状态下存在离散度不足或过度的问题；训练阶段固定了随机行为、事后校准滞后、推理期引导通常逐成员打分而非整体优化集合，且将终端可靠性反馈反向传播穿越多步混沌预报代价高且不稳定。

Method: 提出 DIAL（动态知情地调整固有随机律），对冻结模型的原生随机性进行调控：从历史预报中学习预测各变量与尺度需要增大或减小离散度；在预报推进过程中估计潜在噪声变化对未来精度与可靠性的影响；融合两类预测，在每个预报步做小幅噪声更新，形成闭环，避免重训练与穿越预报全链的反向传播。

Result: 在 FourCastNet 3（FCN3）上、使用训练未见过的较晚年份 ERA5 数据评估：DIAL 相较学习式后处理与基于奖励的控制基线具有更好的尺度分辨可靠性；相较未修改的原生 FCN3 参考，主多尺度 CRPS 改善 8.27%。

Conclusion: 在冻结模型上于推理期闭环调控固有随机性，是一种不依赖重训练即可同时改善集合可靠性与概率预报技巧的有效途径。

Abstract: Ensemble weather forecasts can look reliable in global averages while hiding too little spread in some cases and too much in others. These errors vary across variables, spatial scales, lead times, and weather states. Adapting randomness during forecasting can correct these errors, but training fixes stochastic behavior beforehand and output calibration acts afterward. Inference guidance also acts during forecasting, but typically scores ensemble members separately rather than the ensemble as a whole. Reliability feedback arrives at the end, and tracing it backward through many chaotic forecast steps is expensive and unstable. We therefore introduce Dynamic and Informed Adjustment of the Native Stochastic Law (DIAL), which controls a frozen model's native stochasticity. DIAL learns from past forecasts to predict whether each variable and scale needs more or less spread. As a forecast develops, it estimates how possible noise changes will affect later forecast accuracy and reliability. It combines these predictions to make small noise updates at each step. This loop avoids retraining the weather model and full backpropagation through the forecast. We evaluate DIAL on FourCastNet 3 (FCN3) using later ERA5 years unseen during training. DIAL provides better scale-resolved reliability than learned postprocessing and reward-based control baselines. Relative to the unmodified Native FCN3 reference, DIAL achieves an 8.27% improvement in the primary multiscale continuous ranked probability score (CRPS).

</details>


### [19] [Audio-Visual Dog Identification and Tracking in Real-World Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21011)
*Aryahvishwa Babu,Kingsley Jin,Lillian Wang,Tuan M. Dang,Theron S. Wang,Jeorge Joshi,Annie Cao,Kenny Q. Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多模态“犬只指纹”框架，将视觉识别、多目标跟踪与犬吠声纹绑定，实现真实视频中个体犬的识别与持久档案维护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实视频中从吠声识别个体犬几乎未被探索，但该能力对长期行为分析、兽医监测与动物福利监测至关重要；真实场景存在多犬同时吠叫、遮挡、进出画面等困难，且既有研究将犬类声学与视觉身份信息割裂研究。

Method: 构建多模态犬只指纹框架：融合视觉识别、多目标跟踪与犬吠声纹（vocal fingerprinting）；将吠叫事件与跟踪到的犬只进行时间同步，并利用视觉外观与声学特征持续更新个体档案；同时发布含46段真实视频、213个人工标注吠叫事件的基准数据集。

Result: 在可见吠叫事件中，以IoU≥0.30为标准正确框定发声犬只的比例达61.83%；可见吠叫与画外吠叫检测的F1为91.33%。

Conclusion: 多模态融合可缓解遮挡、吠声重叠与画外吠叫带来的身份歧义，实现跨事件的犬只持久身份关联，为该方向提供首个真实场景基准。

Abstract: Identifying individual dogs from barks in real-world videos remains challenging and largely unexplored. Solving this problem would enable long-term behavioral analysis, veterinary monitoring, and animal welfare monitoring by attributing vocalizations and behaviors to the correct individual. Real-world videos are challenging because multiple dogs may bark simultaneously, become occluded, or move in and out of view. Prior studies show that canine vocalizations and visual appearance contain discriminative identity information, but these modalities have largely been studied independently. We present a multimodal dog-fingerprinting framework combining visual identification, multiple-object tracking, and canine vocal fingerprinting to associate bark events with individual dogs while maintaining persistent profiles. The framework synchronizes bark events with tracked dogs and updates profiles using visual appearance and vocal characteristics, enabling identification despite occlusion, overlapping vocalizations, and off-screen barking. We also introduce a benchmark of 46 real-world videos containing 213 manually annotated bark events. Our framework correctly localizes the active visible dog in 61.83% of visible events at IoU $\geq 0.30$ and achieves a 91.33% F1 score for visible versus off-screen barking detection.

</details>


### [20] [VesperMUSE: Multi-Modal Fusion Vision Transformer for Image Semantic Segmentation Under Diverse Light Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21990)
*Raul Balmez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VesperMUSE，一个面向低光照环境的统一多模态语义分割框架，通过物理启发的合成低光适配算法、热引导的低光增强以及模态无关的自注意力融合来恢复场景细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光照下语义分割因噪声、伪影、色彩失真和结构细节丢失而性能严重下降，而自动驾驶等系统的可靠导航与场景理解依赖分割性能，需要鲁棒的跨光照条件解决方案。

Method: 多层次处理光不足问题：(1) 物理启发的合成低光适配算法；(2) 以热成像引导的低光图像增强(LLIE)范式；(3) 多模态融合恢复本征场景细节。增强后动态融合主RGB特征与辅助热成像表示及突出几何不连续与材质边界的表面法线图；核心是一个高可扩展、模态无关的自注意力机制，可从任意数量辅助输入中提取全局上下文与细粒度局部特征，并将层次化多模态表示组合投影至统一组件，无需改动架构即可适配不同传感器配置。

Result: 大量评估表明VesperMUSE能成功恢复缺失的视觉线索，在极端与非结构化真实环境中提供灵活的最先进(SOTA)机器感知方案。

Conclusion: VesperMUSE通过物理启发的数据适配、热引导增强和可扩展模态无关融合注意力，实现了跨光照条件的鲁棒多模态语义分割，且对不同传感器配置具备即插即用的灵活性。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation is critical for the reliable navigation and scene understanding of autonomous systems, yet its performance degrades severely in low-light environments due to noise, artifacts, colour distortion, and the loss of structural details. To address this challenge, this work introduces VesperMUSE, a unified multi-modal framework designed to achieve robust image semantic segmentation across diverse illumination conditions. The architecture addresses the light deficiency problem on multiple levels: proposing a novel physics-informed synthetic low-light adaptation algorithm, approaching Low-Light Image Enhancement (LLIE) through a thermal-guided paradigm, and finally aiming to recover the intrinsic scene details for semantic segmentation through a multi-modal fusion approach. Following enhancement, the framework dynamically integrates the primary RGB features with auxiliary thermal representations and surface normal maps highlighting geometric discontinuities and material boundaries. At the core of this integration is a highly scalable, modality-agnostic self-attention mechanism that extracts both global context and fine local features from an arbitrary number of auxiliary inputs. By combining and projecting these hierarchical multi-modal representations into a unified component, the attention module allows the network to selectively attend to relevant cross-modal cues without requiring any architectural modifications when different sensor configurations are used. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that VesperMUSE successfully recovers missing visual cues, providing a flexible, state-of-the-art (SOTA) solution for machine perception in extreme and unstructured real-world environments.

</details>


### [21] [BridgeAfford: Bridging Query-Target Gap for Generalizable 3D Affordance Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21918)
*Yijie Su,Yakun Zhu,Yi Bin,Pengpeng Zeng,Liang Chen,Guoqing Wang,Jingkuan Song,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 BridgeAfford 框架，从交互区域对齐与视觉证据聚合两个角度缩小查询-目标语义鸿沟，提升语言引导 3D 可供性定位在未见物体上的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言引导 3D 可供性定位方法对未见物体泛化差，核心症结是"查询-目标鸿沟"：编码交互意图的查询特征与目标区域特征存在语义错配，导致定位不准；端到端训练使查询表征过拟合已见物体，难以适配未见物体的目标区域特征。

Method: BridgeAfford 从两方面互补地缩小语义鸿沟：(1) 交互区域对齐 IRA——训练时在几何相似的交互区域间施加跨物体对齐，使未见物体上相似结构产生与训练期相近的表征，便于目标区域识别；(2) 视觉证据聚合 VEA——借助 2D 可供性模型修正注意力分布，引导查询聚焦正确区域，获得更具目标感知性的查询。

Result: 在 LASO 与 PIAD 数据集上的大量实验表明，BridgeAfford 优于现有方法，在未见物体上优势尤为明显，验证了其稳健的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过跨物体交互区域对齐与 2D 视觉证据引导，可有效缓解查询-目标语义鸿沟，是提升 3D 可供性定位跨物体泛化的一种有效范式。

Abstract: Language-guided 3D affordance grounding aims to localize functional regions on 3D objects according to interaction intents. However, existing methods exhibit poor generalization to unseen objects. One challenge is the query-target gap: a semantic discrepancy between query features encoding interaction intents and target-region features, leading to inaccurate grounding. End-to-end training favors query representations tailored to seen objects, limiting their compatibility with shifted target-region features on unseen objects. To address this issue, we propose BridgeAfford, a framework that reduces the query-target semantic gap from two complementary perspectives. First, Interaction Region Alignment (IRA) promotes cross-object alignment among geometrically similar interaction regions during training. This enables structures on unseen objects that resemble previously observed ones to produce representations similar to those learned during training, facilitating target-region recognition. Second, Visual Evidence Aggregation (VEA) leverages a 2D affordance model to refine the attention distribution and guide the query toward the correct region, yielding more target-aware queries. Extensive experiments on the LASO and PIAD datasets demonstrate that BridgeAfford outperforms existing methods, particularly on unseen objects, highlighting its robust generalization ability.

</details>


### [22] [MYOPIA: Understanding the Limits of Tree-Based Reasoning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21573)
*Islam Mesabah,Sary Elmansoury,Gerrit Großmann,Peter Neigel,Sebastian J. Vollmer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MYOPIA 基准，系统诊断 VLM 通过决策树遍历分类时的精度损失，发现遍历在所有数据集-模型组合中均不如零样本提示，并揭示属性回忆与图像接地之间的差距等交互失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策树可将 VLM 分类变为可解释的根到叶问答序列，但这种贪心、不回溯的遍历方式带来的精度代价尚不明确，缺乏系统量化与失败机制分析。

Method: 构建 MYOPIA 基准：针对 GTSRB、MNIST、CIFAR-10、MSCOCO 生成四棵由 LLM 生成、人工精修的分类决策树，配套对齐提示词、诊断协议与评测框架；在 GPT-4o、Qwen-VL Max、LLaMA-3.2 11B Vision 上对比树遍历与零样本提示，采用配对 bootstrap 分析，并进行节点级审计（分裂熵、深度、分支因子、节点内接地差异）。

Result: 12 个数据集-模型组合中树遍历均劣于零样本提示，配对 bootstrap 一致支持零样本；标签-图像精度差距均值分别为 9.5、13.8、12.6 分（GPT-4o、Qwen-VL Max、LLaMA）；错误局部决策不可逆地排除正确类别，答案抽取引入模型相关的额外损失；节点审计显示分裂熵与较低节点精度呈弱但稳定的相关，深度、分支因子与节点内接地差异关联不稳定。

Conclusion: MYOPIA 为层级式视觉分类提供诊断框架，表明当前决策树遍历的可解释性以显著精度为代价；未来需设计优先考虑感知可回答性与早期错误恢复能力的树结构。

Abstract: Decision trees can make vision-language model (VLM) classification interpretable by replacing a single prediction with a sequence of root-to-leaf questions. However, the accuracy cost of this greedy, non-backtracking procedure remains unclear. We introduce \textbf{MYOPIA}, a benchmark comprising four LLM-generated, human-refined decision trees for GTSRB, MNIST, CIFAR-10, and MSCOCO, together with matched prompts, diagnostic protocols, and an evaluation harness. Across GPT-4o, Qwen-VL Max, and LLaMA-3.2 11B Vision, tree traversal underperforms zero-shot prompting in all 12 dataset--model pairs; paired bootstrap analyses consistently favor zero-shot. Our diagnostics characterize interacting failure modes associated with this gap. Models often recall tree-encoded class attributes more accurately than they ground the same attributes in images: the mean label--image accuracy gaps are 9.5, 13.8, and 12.6 points for GPT-4o, Qwen-VL Max, and LLaMA, respectively. Incorrect local decisions then irreversibly exclude the correct class, while answer extraction introduces additional model-dependent loss. A node-level audit of the four trees finds that split entropy is weakly but consistently associated with lower node accuracy, whereas depth, branching factor, and within-node grounding differences are less stable. \textbf{MYOPIA} therefore provides a framework for diagnosing hierarchical visual classification and motivates testing tree designs that prioritize perceptual answerability and recovery from early errors. Code is available at https://github.com/islammesabah/VLM_Classification.

</details>


### [23] [GRASE: Geometry-native Real-to-sim Agentic Scene Modeling from Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21498)
*Zhaorui Lin,Haohan Liu,Zhen Chen,Xiangru Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRASE提出一种智能体式（agentic）真实到仿真（real-to-sim）转换框架，将多视角视频观测作为贯穿建模全过程的"常驻参照"，通过闭环反馈迭代修正建筑外壳与资产几何/布局，生成忠于输入、跨视角一致且可用于仿真的可编辑3D场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有real-to-sim方法普遍采用一次性、松耦合的流水线范式：各阶段仅从前序阶段获得有限反馈，误差逐级累积且缺乏可靠纠正机制，导致最终场景不一致。作者希望打破"固化中间估计"的假设，用闭环反馈实现持续校正。

Method: GRASE以捕获的多视角视频作为常驻参考，不锁定中间估计：1）先从深度与多视角线索拟合建筑外壳及其开口（门窗等）；2）由智能体在共享坐标系中放置生成资产，并校验其空间关系；3）对各资产的几何与位姿进行迭代细化，以提升多视角渲染证据与观测的一致性。各步骤通过反馈耦合，仅定位并修正造成偏差的组件，同时保持跨视角一致。

Result: 实验中GRASE显著优于其他SOTA方法，包括直接使用GPT6-Astra进行的real-to-sim转换。生成场景对输入忠实、跨视角一致且适合仿真。

Conclusion: 将观测作为全程参照并以智能体闭环反馈替代一次性松耦合流水线，可有效抑制误差传播、实现局部化修正，是提升real-to-sim质量与一致性的有效范式。

Abstract: Real-to-sim conversion aims to turn a captured video of a real environment into an editable 3D scene while preserving the geometry and layout of what was observed. Existing methods often follow a one-pass, loosely coupled paradigm: each stage receives only limited feedback from previous stages, allowing errors to propagate without reliable correction and leaving the final scene inconsistent. We present GRASE, an agentic framework that closes this loop by treating the captured multi-view video observations as a standing reference throughout scene modeling. Instead of committing to intermediate estimates, GRASE revises its current hypothesis whenever rendered evidence disagrees with the capture, overcoming the fixed intermediate assumptions of conventional pipelines. Specifically, GRASE first fits the architectural shell and its openings from depth and multi-view cues. An agent then places generated assets in a shared coordinate frame while checking their spatial relationships. Finally, each asset's geometry and placement are refined iteratively to improve agreement across the observed views. By coupling these steps through feedback, GRASE localizes structural discrepancies and revises only the responsible components, while preserving cross-view consistency. The resulting 3D scenes are faithful to the input, consistent across views, and suitable for simulations. In our experiments, GRASE substantially outperforms other state-of-the-art methods, including a direct real-to-sim conversion using GPT6-Astra.

</details>


### [24] [Where, How, and When: Query-Guided Grounding for Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21422)
*Yogesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究多粒度查询引导的定位任务，涵盖物体级（QDETRv、TOViT）、关系级（少样本指代关系）和时刻级（MATR）三类grounding，推进了空间、关系与时间维度的视频理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频内容理解需要在多个粒度上推理，从识别物体到理解交互，再到定位特定动作发生的时间，现有方法缺乏统一的查询引导定位框架。

Method: 物体级：QDETRv将Detection Transformer扩展为查询引导的单样本未见物体定位，结合查询-上下文融合、时序一致性记忆模块和合成轨迹无监督预训练；TOViT基于语言对齐开放集视觉编码器并跨帧传播高置信度物体特征实现少样本检测。关系级：给定(subject, predicate, object)查询和少量共享谓词的支撑视频，用元学习关系网络（查询条件平移嵌入、全局语义聚合、局部定位聚合、基于置信传播的消息传递）优化T分块随机场。时刻级：MATR使用双阶段DTW进行融合前后对齐、可学习查询预测边界、带时序增强的自监督预训练，并扩展到文本到视频检索。

Result: MATR在文本到视频时刻检索上性能与SOTA相当或更优；各任务在对应基准上验证了查询引导定位的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过在空间、关系和时间三个维度上的grounding贡献，推进了查询引导的视频理解。

Abstract: Understanding video content requires reasoning at multiple levels of granularity, from identifying objects to interpreting their interactions and localizing when specific actions occur. This thesis studies three query-guided grounding tasks that answer where key objects are (object-level grounding), how they interact (relationship-level grounding), and when specific moments occur (moment-level grounding). For object-level grounding, we present QDETRv, a query-guided extension of the Detection Transformer for one-shot localization of unseen objects in videos, which combines query-context fusion, a memory module for temporal consistency, and unsupervised pretraining on synthetic object trajectories. We further propose the Temporal Object-Aware Vision Transformer (TOViT) for few-shot video object detection, which builds on a language-aligned open-set vision encoder and propagates high-confidence object features across frames. For relationship-level grounding, we introduce few-shot referring relationships in videos: given a (subject, predicate, object) query and a few support videos sharing the predicate, the entities are localized by optimizing a T-partite random field using a meta-learned relation network with query-conditioned translational embeddings, Global Semantic Aggregation, Local Localization Aggregation, and belief-propagation-based message passing. For moment-level grounding, we propose the Moment Alignment Transformer (MATR) for video-to-video moment retrieval, which uses dual-stage Dynamic Time Warping for pre- and post-fusion alignment, learnable queries for boundary prediction, and self-supervised pretraining with temporal augmentations; MATR also extends to text-to-video moment retrieval with performance comparable to, or better than, the state of the art. These contributions advance query-guided video understanding across spatial, relational, and temporal grounding.

</details>


### [25] [NICE Toolbox: Nonverbal Interpersonal Communication Exploration Toolbox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21786)
*Aleksandr Evgrashin,Carolin Schmitt,Timo Lübbing,Ashutosh Jha,Buket Naz Zeren,Sophie K. Bauer,Johannes Kopf-Beck,Anton K. G. Marx,Anamaria Semm,Gökce Ergün,Senya Polikovsky*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NICE Toolbox 是一个免费开源的 Python 框架，集成现有计算机视觉与语音处理方法，自动测量姿态、手势、注视、面部表情和语音等多模态人际交流信号，支持多相机、可替换检测算法、2D/3D 可视化、自定义数据评估与第三方导出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理学长期关注人类交流理解，人们通过姿态、手势、注视、面部表情与语音等多通道表达自我；但这些信号的测量与标注传统上依赖受训标注者的人工操作，耗时且难以规模化。

Method: 构建统一的 Python 软件包，将现有最先进的计算机视觉与语音处理算法整合到单一框架中，提供多相机系统支持、可互换的检测算法、检测输出的 2D/3D 可视化、基于用户自定义数据的评估以及向第三方软件的导出功能。

Result: 实现了一个免费、开源的 NICE Toolbox，可在单一平台内自动测量多模态人际交流信号，并具备可视化、评估与互操作能力。

Conclusion: 该工具的发布旨在使人际交流信号的数据探索具备可扩展性、可复现性与可及性，从而服务心理学研究。

Abstract: Understanding human communication is a long-standing goal in psychological research. People express themselves through many verbal and nonverbal channels: posture, gesture, gaze, facial expression, and speech. Measurement and annotation of these signals has traditionally required time-consuming manual work by trained human labellers. The Nonverbal Interpersonal Communication Exploration (NICE) Toolbox is a free, open-source Python framework that automatically measures these signals by combining existing state-of-the-art computer vision and speech-processing methods into a single software package. It includes support for multi-camera systems, interchangeable detection algorithms, 2D and 3D visualization of detector outputs, evaluation on the user's custom data, and export to third-party software. With the release of this tool, we aim to make data exploration of interpersonal communication signals scalable, reproducible, and accessible for psychological research.

</details>


### [26] [The Dormant Spiking Neuron: A State-Driven Mechanism for Efficient Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21748)
*Ruichen Ma,Yicai Chen,Pujun Zhou,Yue Zuo,Chongyang Li,Shuang Liu,Shaogang Hu,Guanchao Qiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Dormant Spiking Neuron（DSN）模型，利用膜电位低于负阈值时进入休眠的状态驱动门控，动态剪除亚阈值计算，实现40%以上的能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN的能效主要依赖突触前输入驱动的稀疏性，但突触后神经元的连续亚阈值动态是长期被忽视的计算开销来源。在ImageNet训练的ResNet-18中，多达44%的神经元处于深度抑制状态，放电概率极低却仍持续消耗计算。

Method: 引入状态驱动的动态门控：当膜电位低于预设负阈值时神经元进入休眠态，实现实例级计算剪枝；该机制直接嵌入神经元动力学，通过替代梯度与BPTT端到端训练，使网络自主学习鲁棒的休眠策略。

Result: 在静态与神经形态数据集上一致降低计算能耗40%至70%以上；在ImageNet上与同等代价的SOTA静态剪枝方法相比更优，并使基线SNN精度提升1.34%。

Conclusion: DSN建立了与输入驱动稀疏性正交的可训练状态驱动稀疏范式，可与现有方法协同获得增益，为神经形态计算的高效能推进提供有效途径。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) offer a compelling pathway toward energy-efficient artificial intelligence, fundamentally anchored in presynaptic input-driven sparsity. However, a substantial yet chronically overlooked source of computational overhead persists: the continuous subthreshold dynamics of postsynaptic neurons. Quantitative analysis reveals that up to 44% of neurons in an ImageNet-trained ResNet-18 model reside in a deeply inhibited state, imposing a persistent computational burden despite having a negligible probability of firing. To address this inefficiency, the Dormant Spiking Neuron (DSN) model is introduced. By incorporating a state-driven dynamic gate, the DSN allows a neuron to enter a dormant state when its membrane potential falls below a predefined negative threshold, dynamically pruning redundant computations at the instance level. Crucially, this dormancy mechanism is directly integrated into the neuronal dynamics and trained end-to-end via backpropagation through time using surrogate gradients, enabling the network to actively learn a robust dormancy policy that adaptively preserves critical information flow. Extensive experiments across static and neuromorphic datasets demonstrate that the DSN framework consistently reduces computational energy by 40% to over 70%. Notably, on the large-scale ImageNet dataset, the DSN outperforms state-of-the-art static pruning techniques at comparable reported cost reductions and simultaneously improves the corresponding baseline SNN accuracy by 1.34%. By establishing a trainable, state-driven sparsity paradigm orthogonal to conventional input-driven methods, the DSN unlocks synergistic efficiency gains, presenting an effective approach for advancing neuromorphic computing.

</details>


### [27] [Edit4D: Editable 4D Scene Generation with Textual Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21668)
*Chanwoo Kim,Tackgeun You,Hwasup Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Edit4D，一个可编辑的文本到 4D 场景生成框架，将 4D 场景分解为背景与动态角色的独立表征分阶段构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本驱动的动态场景生成方法通常生成整体场景视频，背景与动态角色耦合，导致难以进行对象级编辑与灵活的相机控制，且需同时保持空间几何与时间动态。

Method: 基于阶段化生成管线：先将用户指令分解为背景、动态角色及场景-角色合成三组阶段提示；用文本到图像与相机条件生成先验合成多视角场景图像，重建背景 3D Gaussian Splatting；再生成角色图像，经图像条件视频先验驱动动画，重建 4D mesh 并绑定 Gaussian 基元得到角色表征；随后通过文本引导分割与接触感知优化进行合成，最后做全局场景精修以提升协调性与渲染一致性。

Result: 通过整合阶段化组件，Edit4D 支持可控相机运动、灵活的对象级编辑，并可实际构建可编辑 4D 场景。

Conclusion: 将背景与角色解耦的分阶段表征与合成策略，可有效克服整体式生成的编辑性与相机控制瓶颈，为可编辑 4D 场景构建提供可行方案。

Abstract: We introduce Edit4D, an editable text-to-4D scene generation framework that constructs 4D scenes from separate background and dynamic character representations. Recent advances in generative models have enabled text-driven dynamic scene synthesis, but editable 4D scene generation remains challenging due to the need to preserve spatial geometry and temporal dynamics. Existing approaches typically generate holistic scene videos, where the background and dynamic characters are coupled, making object-level editing and flexible camera control difficult. Edit4D addresses these limitations through a stage-specific generation pipeline. Given a user instruction, Edit4D first decomposes it into stage-specific prompts for background, dynamic character, and scene-character composition. Using these prompts, we synthesize multi-view scene images with text-to-image and camera-conditioned generative priors and reconstruct a background 3D Gaussian splatting representation. We then generate a character image, animate it with an image-conditioned video prior, reconstruct a 4D mesh, and bind Gaussian primitives to obtain a character representation. Finally, both components are composed through text-guided segmentation and contact-aware optimization. A global scene refinement is then performed to improve harmonization and rendering consistency. By integrating stage-specific components, Edit4D enables controllable camera motion, flexible object-level editing, and the practical construction of editable 4D scenes. Project page: https://chanwo0kim.github.io/Edit4D/

</details>


### [28] [FusionAudit: Pathway-Conditioned Robustness Auditing for Native Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21667)
*Jiaming Zhang,Zherui Li,Siqi Guo,Junhao Dong,Kun Wang,Wei Yang Bryan Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PaRMA：面向VLM的路径条件化审计框架，通过像素扰动与渲染文本插入两类图像级干预暴露架构特异的视觉攻击面盲点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM从冻结编码器流水线转向原生多模态架构后，视觉通道既是感知输入又是渲染文本载体，会与用户提示竞争；现有单攻击、像素中心的评估把不同视觉攻击面压缩为单一标量，且忽视架构变化带来的脆弱性差异。

Method: 提出PaRMA证据驱动审计框架，在图像级定义两条路径条件化风险：像素扰动与渲染文本插入；以路径特异诊断替代任意攻击枚举；并实例化FusionAudit，含MANTIS（边际感知像素扰动）与Nat-Evid（宿主一致的语义插入）两种诊断驱动攻击，配合新的“原生性”分类体系评估18个开源权重VLM。

Result: 发现原生VLM存在“看见但不上当”特征（高度关注排版叠加文本却在视觉上予以拒绝）；识别深度融合模型中编码器级与答案级攻击的目标错配；证明传统单攻击排序（如PGD）对原生模型不稳定；诊断驱动攻击显著提升残余风险估计。

Conclusion: PaRMA提供了可证伪、证据驱动的审计方法，能揭示标准基准遗漏的脆弱性，说明需按架构路径分别评估视觉风险。

Abstract: As vision-language models (VLMs) transition from frozen-encoder pipelines to native multimodal architectures, the visual channel increasingly serves as both a perceptual input and a carrier of rendered text that competes with user prompts. Current single-attack, pixel-centric evaluations fail to capture this complexity: they collapse distinct visual attack surfaces into a single scalar and ignore how architectural shifts alter vulnerability. We introduce PaRMA, an evidence-based auditing framework that defines pathway-conditioned risk over two primary image-level interventions: pixel perturbation and rendered-text insertion. Instead of arbitrary attack enumeration, PaRMA uses pathway-specific diagnostics to expose architecture-specific blind spots. Notably, we uncover a see-but-not-deceived signature where native VLMs attend heavily to typographic overlays but visually reject them, and we identify an objective mismatch between encoder-level and answer-level attacks in deep-fusion models. To exploit these findings, we instantiate FusionAudit, introducing two diagnostic-motivated attacks: MANTIS (margin-aware pixel perturbation) and Nat-Evid (host-congruent semantic insertion). Evaluating 18 open-weight VLMs across a novel nativeness taxonomy, we demonstrate that legacy single-attack rankings (e.g., PGD) are unstable for native models. Our diagnostic-driven attacks substantially raise the estimated residual risk, showing that PaRMA provides a falsifiable, evidence-driven auditing methodology for uncovering vulnerabilities missed by standard benchmarks.

</details>


### [29] [RoboCloth: A Large-Scale Real Cloth Material Dataset for Neural Reflectance Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21090)
*Zhen Li,Haoran Qin,Francesco Di Sario,Daniel Rebain,Andrea Tagliasacchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RoboCloth数据集：500种真实布料在机器人控制的相机-光源下各采集约580帧HDR图像，配SfM精化位姿与稀疏几何，支撑神经材质重建；对应两阶段流程，共享MLP先验+冻结解码器拟合2048²潜在纹理，在自留材质与UBO2014上优于现有实测数据集与解析PBR拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实测反射率数据集多采集于固定、非标定的采集装置，难以覆盖宽视角-光照半球，且缺乏逐点结构化反射观测，限制了神经材质重建的可迁移性；作者希望提供柔性、可标定的机器人采集布料材质数据以学习布料材质先验。

Method: 构建500种真实布料的采集数据集，相机与光源均由机器人控制并完成标定；每种材质采集约580帧HDR图像，覆盖宽视角-光照半球；用structure-from-motion精化相机位姿并对齐机器人坐标系，得到逐材质稀疏表面几何与结构化逐点反射观测张量。重建采用两阶段流程：阶段1在本语料上训练共享MLP解码器学习布料材质先验；阶段2冻结解码器，为每个目标材质拟合2048×2048稠密潜在纹理。

Result: 在自数据集留出材质与独立基准UBO2014上评估：基于本数据集训练的解码器优于在现有实测反射率数据集上训练的解码器以及解析PBR拟合，且无需修改即可迁移到未见过的采集系统。

Conclusion: 机器人控制、可标定的布料材质采集与结构化逐点反射观测能有效支撑神经材质先验学习与跨系统迁移；数据集、标定文件、观测张量与训练/测试划分以CC BY 4.0发布于Hugging Face Hub。

Abstract: We present RoboCloth, a dataset of 500 real cloth materials captured under flexible, calibrated camera and lighting configurations, where both are robotically controlled during acquisition. Each material is imaged in approximately 580 high-dynamic range frames spanning a wide view-light hemisphere; camera poses are refined via structure-from-motion and aligned to the robot frame, yielding per-material sparse surface geometry and structured per-point reflectance observations ready for neural material training. We demonstrate the value of this data through a two-stage neural reconstruction pipeline: Stage 1 trains a shared MLP decoder on our corpus to learn cloth material priors; Stage 2 then freezes the decoder and fits a dense 2048x2048 latent texture to each target material. Evaluating on held-out materials from our own dataset and on the independent UBO2014 benchmark, we show that a decoder trained on our captures outperforms decoders trained on existing measured reflectance datasets and analytic PBR fitting, transferring to unseen acquisition systems without modification. The dataset, calibration files, observation tensors, and train/test splits are released under CC BY 4.0 on the Hugging Face Hub.

</details>


### [30] [Mind the Tail: Data Selection Through Sparse Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21007)
*Ziheng Zhang,Neha Hulkund,Colin Lee,Zheda Mai,Hui Shen,Ying Ding,Tanya Berger-Wolf,Sara Beery,Wei-Lun Chao,Jianyang Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文从概念支撑（concept support）视角研究数据选择，用稀疏自编码器提取图像概念激活，在选择过程中累积数据集级概念支撑并对该分布施加递减回报，结合局部支撑挑选样本，从而把训练资源从充分表征的概念转向长尾概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据选择会重新分配训练资源在物体、属性、颜色、纹理、背景等视觉概念上的支撑分布，不同分配策略会影响下游模型行为。仅由少量样本支撑的长尾概念被丢弃会损害测试时鲁棒性，而过度强调类别边界样本又会引入不稳定的训练信号，因此需要显式研究概念支撑分配与模型行为之间的关系。

Method: 使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）从单张图像中提取概念激活；在选择过程中为每个新加入样本累积数据集级概念支撑分布，并对该分布施加递减回报（diminishing returns）以降低已充分表征概念的权重、把注意力转向分布尾部；再引入局部支撑（local support）以筛选能够可靠增强支撑不足概念的样本。

Result: 在细粒度分类、虚假相关（spurious-correlation）分类、通用图像分类以及多模态预训练等基准上，方法相对强数据选择基线均取得一致提升；并揭示了方法所增强的概念与所诱发的行为变化之间的联系，包括对分布外数据和虚假上下文的鲁棒性提升。

Conclusion: 概念支撑作为一种超越单样本重要性的原则，对数据高效且鲁棒的学习具有实用价值。

Abstract: Data selection reallocates training support across visual concepts, including objects, attributes, color, texture, and backgrounds. Different allocation strategies may produce varied downstream model behavior, especially for tail concepts supported by only a few samples. Discarding them can hurt test-time robustness, while overemphasizing samples lying on category boundaries may introduce unstable training signals. This work explicitly investigates this relationship across fine-grained, spurious-correlation, and general image classification, as well as multimodal pretraining. Specifically, we use sparse autoencoders (SAEs) to extract concept activations from individual images. During selection, we accumulate the dataset-level concept support for each added sample. Applying diminishing returns to this distribution down-weights well-represented concepts and shifts the focus to the distribution tail. We then incorporate local support to find samples that reliably strengthen under-supported concepts. Empirical results show consistent improvements over strong data selection baselines across all benchmarks. We also reveal the connection between concepts our method enhances and the induced behavioral changes, including improved robustness against out-of-distribution and spurious contexts. This work establishes concept support, beyond individual sample importance, as a useful principle for data-efficient and robust learning.

</details>


### [31] [WorldCast: Exploring Intelligent Visual Generation in World State Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20731)
*Wenhao Yan,Haodong Li,Xiaotong Li,Yaxin Luo,Zhengrong Yue,Jialv Zou,Xu Huang,Haojin Yang,Nan Jia,Yan Feng,Peng Pei,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多模态生成能力的四级分类体系，并构建 WorldCast 基准（320 个手工设计案例、8 类任务、评估 12 个模型），聚焦"多步世界状态预测"这一高层任务；结果显示前沿系统初具真实世界视觉状态预测能力（GPT-Image-2.5 系统 72.8），但模型间差距悬殊，开源最佳仅 20.1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能够整合多模态观测并预测世界如何演变，近期多模态生成系统也展现出类似能力。作者将模型能力划分为层级后发现，尽管模型整体进步明显，更高层级的任务仍相对缺乏探索，因此需要系统刻画这一新兴方向并建立评测手段。

Method: 将多模态生成能力组织为四级层次结构以定位研究空白；选取"多步世界状态预测"（从多模态观测与多样化参考中预测连续未来世界状态）作为高层任务；构建 WorldCast 基准，含 8 个类别共 320 个人工设计案例，并评测 12 个代表性模型。该任务要求理解与生成能力深度纠缠，并依赖大量世界知识，将人类动作序列、物理变换与抽象视觉推理转化为连贯视觉输出。

Result: 三点关键发现：(a) 前沿模型快速推进智能世界建模边界，GPT-Image-2.5 System 得 72.8 分，在真实世界任务上呈现早期阶段的视觉状态预测能力；(b) WorldCast 揭示显著模型差距——Nano Banana Pro 仅 42.8 分，最佳开源模型 20.1 分，缺陷集中在复杂上下文处理、纠缠参考与重推理任务；(c) 强生成模型能有效利用其底层语言模型的能力。

Conclusion: 未来需要兼具动态规划与推理能力、并实现深度统一的多模态生成模型，才能忠实模拟真实世界状态。

Abstract: Humans interpret multimodal observations of the world and forecast how it will evolve. Recent multimodal generative systems have demonstrated similar capabilities: they can integrate multimodal context and synthesize visual outputs that depict changes in the world. To characterize this emerging trajectory, we organize model capabilities into a four-level hierarchy and discover that higher-level tasks remain relatively underexplored despite model progress. To bridge this gap, we explore model performance on multi-step world state prediction, a high-level task that requires models to forecast successive future world states from multimodal observations and diverse references. Such a task requires models to possess entangled understanding and generation capabilities, as well as extensive world knowledge to translate human action sequences, physical transformations, and abstract visual reasoning into coherent visual outputs. We introduce WorldCast, a benchmark comprising 320 manually designed cases across eight categories, and evaluate 12 representative models. The conducted experiments yield three key insights: (a) advances in frontier models are rapidly pushing the boundaries of intelligent world modeling, with the GPT-Image-2.5 System scoring 72.8 and demonstrating early-stage visual state prediction capabilities on real-world tasks; (b) WorldCast reveals substantial gaps between models: even Nano Banana Pro reaches only 42.8 and the best open-source model scores 20.1, with deficits in handling complex context, entangled references, and reasoning-heavy tasks; (c) strong generative models effectively draw on the capabilities of their underlying language models. These findings highlight the need for multimodal generative models that are capable of dynamic planning and reasoning while also being deeply unified to faithfully simulate real-world states.

</details>


### [32] [BD-HazardVLM: Probing Vision-Language Models for Latent Defensive-Driving Hazards in Bangladesh Road Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20627)
*Abdullah Al Maruf,Md. Sajedul Islam,Tanmoy Mridha,Irfan Hossain Bhuiyan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BD-HazardVLM孟加拉道路场景诊断基准（500图），评估三个VLM的零样本防御性驾驶推理，发现粗粒度危险识别与多数类基线相当，但细粒度推理（类别、风险时机、防御动作）严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型越来越多用于驾驶场景理解，但其在结构较弱的全球南方（Global South）交通环境中的防御性推理能力尚不清楚，缺乏针对地理多样化的安全性评测。

Method: 构建BD-HazardVLM基准，含500张孟加拉道路图像（375来自RSUD20K，125来自TFP-BD），标注危险存在性、主危险类别、风险时机、严重程度、防御动作、可见证据，以及幻觉陷阱和西方道路偏置陷阱；在统一零样本任务定义与结构化输出模式下评测Qwen2.5-VL-7B、InternVL3-8B、MiniCPM-V-4.6三个模型。

Result: 二分类危险存在准确率88.0%–89.2%，基本等同89.0%的多数类基线；类别准确率低于40%，风险时机准确率1.6%–12.4%，防御动作精确匹配低于2%。InternVL3-8B严重程度准确率最高（61.2%），但对58个高严重度样本召回为零；Qwen出现严重类别模式坍缩；所有模型潜在危险预判弱、自报陷阱识别近乎为零。

Conclusion: 对所评测模型而言，粗粒度危险识别并不代表可靠的细粒度防御性驾驶推理，凸显了开展地理多样化安全评测的必要性。

Abstract: Vision-language models are increasingly used for driving-scene understanding, yet their defensive reasoning under weakly structured Global South traffic remains unclear. We introduce BD-HazardVLM, a diagnostic benchmark of 500 Bangladesh road-scene images, comprising 375 from RSUD20K and 125 from TFP-BD, annotated for hazard presence, primary hazard category, risk timing, severity, defensive actions, visible evidence, and hallucination- and Western-road-bias traps. We evaluate Qwen2.5-VL-7B, InternVL3-8B, and MiniCPM-V-4.6 using a shared zero-shot task definition and structured output schema. Binary hazard-presence accuracy ranges from 88.0 percent to 89.2 percent, essentially matching the 89.0 percent majority-class baseline. Fine-grained reasoning is substantially weaker: category accuracy remains below 40 percent, risk-timing accuracy ranges from 1.6 percent to 12.4 percent, and exact defensive-action matching remains below 2 percent. Although InternVL3-8B achieves the highest severity accuracy at 61.2 percent, it has zero recall on the 58 high-severity cases. Qwen exhibits severe category mode collapse, while all models show weak latent-hazard anticipation and near-zero self-reported trap recognition. For the evaluated models, these findings show that coarse hazard recognition does not imply reliable fine-grained defensive-driving reasoning and motivate geographically diverse safety evaluation.

</details>


### [33] [CertEx: Exact Visual Retrieval at Scale by Certified Exclusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21325)
*Dongfang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"认证排除"（certified exclusion）原理：精确kNN只需证明被排除向量不可能是邻居，而证明可保守故可用低精度检查。据此实现GPU精确kNN方法Certex，在半精度存储下界投影并把舍入证书折叠进同一张量核点积，仅少数候选需精确距离，理论上保证返回精确kNN；在16个数据集（至1亿向量）上吞吐为H100上最快cuVS搜索的7.1–18.0倍且逐查询精确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像描述子与嵌入上的精确kNN是视觉检索、近重复检测、表示评估及LLM智能体记忆的基础需求。穷举搜索以全精度计算所有距离，代价高；近似索引则召回率低于1，无法满足必须精确的场景。因此需要一个既保持精确性又显著提速的方案。

Method: 核心思想为"认证排除"：不必高精度计算距离，只需给出每个被排除向量不可能成为邻居的保守证明，证明可在低精度下核验（类比视网膜低分辨率扫视全场景）。Certex在半精度下存储每个向量的下界投影，并将舍入证书（rounding certificate）折叠进同一个张量核点积运算中，使筛选检验与投影计算一次完成；仅通过检验的少量向量再计算精确距离。作者给出Certex对每个查询均返回精确kNN的正确性证明。

Result: 在单张H100上，于16个数据集（规模最高1亿向量）中，Certex对每个查询均精确，吞吐量为最快cuVS搜索的7.1至18.0倍；在要求逐查询精确的条件下，优于量化索引与图索引。

Conclusion: 通过将"排除证明"与"距离计算"解耦并以低精度保守检验实现，可在GPU上兼顾精确性与高吞吐，为必须精确的kNN检索提供了优于近似索引与穷举全精度搜索的实用方案。

Abstract: Exact $k$-nearest-neighbor (kNN) search over image descriptors and embeddings underlies visual retrieval, near-duplicate detection, the evaluation of learned representations, and increasingly the memory of LLM agents. However, exhaustive search computes every distance at full precision, whereas approximate indexes return recall below one. We observe that exact search only needs a proof that each excluded vector cannot be a neighbor. Because such a proof only has to be conservative, it can be checked in low precision, much as the retina examines the whole scene at low resolution. We call this principle certified exclusion and realize it in an exact kNN method for GPUs, which we call \certex. Specifically, \certex\ stores a lower-bounding projection of each vector in half precision and folds a rounding certificate into the same tensor-core dot product, so that only the few vectors that the test keeps need exact distances. We prove that \certex\ returns the exact kNN of every query. On sixteen datasets with up to 100M vectors, \certex\ is exact for every query at 7.1$\times$ to 18.0$\times$ the throughput of the fastest cuVS search on one H100 and outperforms quantized and graph indexes whenever every query must be exact.

</details>


### [34] [VLA-$Δ$: Learning Physical State Transition for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21306)
*Qi Lu,Jiarun Li,Ce Zhang,Miao Pan,Yi Pan,Xu Wang,Wenqi Zhang,Xuhong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VLA-Δ 以“当前状态与未来状态之间的物理变化”作为显式学习目标，构建大规模预训练数据与物理状态转移标注，无需额外推理开销即可在 LIBERO、RoboTwin 及真机上取得有竞争力甚至更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型大多直接将观测映射到示范动作，缺乏对中间物理状态变化的显式监督，容易过拟合观测—动作对应关系；已有方法虽引入语言/视觉/隐空间中的中间推理或未来状态预测，但并未显式监督物理状态转移本身。

Method: 提出 VLA-Δ 通用框架，把当前状态与未来状态之间的物理变化作为显式学习目标；为此构建大规模预训练数据集，并推导覆盖任务相关语义与视觉运动变化的物理状态转移标注，为连续动作生成提供更直接的监督；推理阶段直接由当前观测生成动作，不引入额外推理开销。

Result: 在 LIBERO 与 RoboTwin 上达到与最先进方法相当（competitive）的性能，真机实验中优于 π_{0.5}。

Conclusion: 显式监督物理状态转移是提升 VLA 策略泛化性与动作生成质量的有效途径，且可在不增加推理成本的前提下实现，代码已开源（ZJU-OmniAI/VLA-Delta）。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models transfer semantic knowledge acquired through vision-language pretraining to robot control, achieving strong performance across various benchmarks. However, most VLAs directly map observations to demonstrated actions without explicit supervision of intermediate physical state changes, making them prone to overfitting observation-action correspondences. Some methods introduce intermediate reasoning or future-state prediction in language, visual, or latent spaces to guide action generation, but do not explicitly supervise the physical state transition itself. We therefore propose VLA-$Δ$, a general framework that uses physical changes between current and future states as explicit learning targets. To support this objective, we construct a large-scale pretraining dataset and derive physical state-transition annotations covering task-relevant semantics and visual motion changes, providing more direct supervision for continuous action generation.
