During inference, the model generates actions directly from current observations without additional inference overhead. VLA-$Δ$ achieves performance competitive with the state of the art on LIBERO and RoboTwin, and outperforms $π_{0.5}$ in real-robot experiments. Our code is available at https://github.com/ZJU-OmniAI/VLA-Delta

</details>


### [35] [Do VLAs Really Follow Instructions? Diagnosing How-Level Instruction Following in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21251)
*Sangwu Park,Wonjoong Kim,Yeonjun In,Chanyoung Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文诊断了VLA模型对"How-level"（执行方式）指令的跟随能力，发现当前模型不仅无法受益，成功率反而下降；提出将训练episode与单一指令配对的假设，并用segment-level富化指令训练提升响应性，但未稳定提升成功率；进而提出SIMPLER-IN基准，显示该响应性无法泛化到高难度与分布外How-level指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA研究集中于"What-level"指令（指定做什么任务），而忽视了"How-level"指令（指定如何执行）；作者希望通过诊断揭示当前VLA在How-level指令跟随上的缺陷及其成因。

Method: 1）诊断实验：给现有VLA输入How-level指令，观察对任务成功率的影响；2）假设每个训练episode仅配一条指令限制了How-level跟随能力，改为segment-level富化指令进行训练；3）构建SIMPLER-IN基准，其中成功由指令中给出的条件定义，用于评测标准基准之外的How-level跟随能力。

Result: How-level指令未能帮助当前VLA，且降低任务成功率；segment-level富化指令训练使动作能对这类指令产生响应，但并不能持续提升任务成功率；SIMPLER-IN显示所获得的响应性无法扩展到高难度或分布外的How-level指令。

Conclusion: How-level指令跟随仍是开放挑战；仅靠数据层面的指令富化不足以实现稳健的How-level控制，需要在建模与评测层面进一步研究。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are expected to follow instructions like their language backbones, yet prior work has focused on What-level instructions that specify the task rather than How-level instructions that specify how to carry it out. Through our diagnoses, we find that How-level instructions do not help current VLAs and lower task success. We hypothesize that pairing each training episode with a single instruction limits How-level instruction following, and show that training with segment-level enriched instructions makes actions respond to such instructions without consistently raising task success. To measure How-level instruction following beyond standard benchmarks, we introduce SIMPLER-IN, where success requires conditions given only in the instruction. It shows that the gained responsiveness does not extend to demanding or out-of-distribution How-level instructions. How-level instruction following thus remains an open challenge.

</details>


### [36] [Trajectory Prediction Method for person under WSN with Hierarchical Envelope Adversarial Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21132)
*Wenqian Jiang,Qifeng Sun,Xiaosong Gao,Xin Zou,Xingrui Ni,Yuan Hu,Yongming Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出层次包络对抗域自适应紧-松耦合模型（HEADA-LTCTPM），通过包络样本图构建与局部/全局对抗域自适应，解决现有行人轨迹预测中社交耦合单一与域偏移处理不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行人轨迹预测模型有两个共性局限：一是仅考虑单一类型的社会耦合，引入冗余交互，且无法刻画不同场景、不同行人之间耦合性质的差异，难以自适应多样场景；二是域偏移处理不足，少数方法依赖基于统计准则的域分布对齐，对预定义统计度量强依赖，在复杂动态环境中局限明显。

Method: 1）包络样本构建机制：构造包络样本并将其扩展为图结构；2）对抗域自适应模块：融合局部与全局两种域自适应策略，动态适应跨场景的多样耦合关系；整体构成层次包络对抗域自适应与松-紧耦合的轨迹预测模型。

Result: 在两个代表性公开数据集上验证，并与六个相关基线算法全面对比；模型精度显著优于现有方法，ADE与FDE分别降低17.6%和19.1%，时间开销满足实际应用需求，验证了关键创新的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过包络样本图构建与层次化局部/全局对抗域自适应，可有效建模多样化的松-紧耦合关系并缓解域偏移，在保证实用效率的前提下显著提升行人轨迹预测的准确性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Pedestrian trajectory prediction holds significant applications across autonomous driving, intelligent surveillance, and smart cities. However, the complexity and unpredictability of pedestrian interactions make this task a persistent challenge. Current models face two common limitations: 1) Consider only a single type of social coupling. This introduces redundant interactions. More critically, it fails to account for the varying nature of trajectory coupled across different scenarios and between different pedestrians. Consequently, models cannot deeply adapt to diverse scenes. 2) Inadequate domain shift handling. Although very few methods address domain shift, they rely on statistical criterion-based domain distribution alignment. Such approaches exhibit strong dependency on predefined statistical metrics. This leads to significant limitations in complex and dynamic environments. To address these problems, a Hierarchical Envelope Adversarial Domain Adaptation with Loosely-Tightly coupled model (HEADA-LTCTPM) is proposed. First, an envelope sample construction mechanism was proposed. It constructs envelope samples and extends them into graph structures. Secondly, an adversarial domain adaptation module was proposed. This integrates both local and global domain adaptation strategies. It dynamically adapts to diverse coupled relationships across scenarios. These innovations collectively enhance prediction accuracy and robustness. In the experimental section, two representative public datasets were employed for validation and comprehensive comparisons with six relevant baseline algorithms were conducted. Results demonstrate that our model achieves significantly higher accuracy compared to existing methods, with the ADE and FDE metrics reduced by 17.6% and 19.1% respectively. The time overhead meets practical requirements, which verifies the effectiveness of our key innovations.

</details>


### [37] [PiCo: Physics-Informed Concept Probing for Geo-Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20973)
*Yihang She,Andrew Blake,Jianbo Qi,Srinivasan Keshav*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Physics-Informed Concept Probing (PiCo)，一种事后解释地理基础模型(GFM)预测的框架，通过在嵌入空间中构造物理概念向量来揭示模型的物理敏感性并支持假设检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GFM评估仅以下游任务预测性能为准，难以说明模型对哪些物理特征敏感、在预测中依赖哪些特征，限制了开发者验证行为假设的能力和用户为新任务选型的能力。

Method: PiCo借助三维辐射传输模型与游戏引擎耦合生成合成多光谱卫星观测，在GFM嵌入空间中识别物理概念向量；将嵌入投影到这些概念向量上得到低维表示，用于量化概念在预测中的重要度。在三个不同输入模态的GFM上、覆盖森林叶片光学与冠层结构的七个物理概念进行评估。

Result: 在地表覆盖估计中，RGB限制降低干物质敏感性，并显著恶化干植被与裸地的预测，验证了由敏感性分析导出的假设；在分层森林类型与树种分类中，概念表示保留70.0–93.8%的完整嵌入精度；各模型捕捉到相似的类间物理对比（如阔叶林与空地的结构差异、橡树与山毛榉的叶片含水量差异），但在对比强度与主导分类的概念上存在差异。

Conclusion: PiCo为假设检验与模型评估提供物理依据，且在前向模型可支持受控模拟、预训练嵌入可探测的场景下，可推广至地球观测之外的应用。

Abstract: Geo-foundation models (GFMs) are typically evaluated by downstream task predictive performance, revealing little about which physical features they are sensitive to or use in prediction. This limits developers' ability to test hypotheses about model behaviour and users' ability to select models for new tasks. We propose Physics-Informed Concept Probing (PiCo), a post-hoc framework for explaining GFM predictions. PiCo identifies concept vectors in a GFM's embedding space by generating synthetic multispectral satellite observations from a 3D radiative transfer model coupled with a game engine. These vectors reveal physical sensitivities that inform domain-based hypotheses about downstream performance. Projecting embeddings onto these concept vectors produces low-dimensional representations for quantifying concept importance in making predictions. We evaluate PiCo on three GFMs with different input modalities using seven physical concepts covering forest leaf optics and canopy structure. In ground-cover estimation, RGB restriction reduces dry-matter sensitivity and disproportionately degrades predictions for dry vegetation and bare ground, supporting a hypothesis derived from sensitivity analysis. For hierarchical forest type and tree species classification, concept representations retain 70.0-93.8% of full-embedding accuracy. Models capture similar physical contrasts between classes, such as structural differences between broadleaf forest and cleared ground and leaf-water-content differences between oak and beech, yet differ in the strength of these contrasts and the concepts driving classification. PiCo thus provides a physical basis for hypothesis testing and model assessment, with potential applicability beyond Earth observation wherever forward models enable controlled simulation and pretrained embeddings are accessible for probing.

</details>


### [38] [VELD-QA: A Large-Scale Dataset for Visual Evidence Grounding and Question Answering in Long Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20964)
*Zhivar Sourati,Marianne Menglin Liu,Rongguang Wang,Zheng Wang,Yazhe Hu,Lan Guo,Yuying Wang,Mengqing Guo,Bhavesh Dnyaneshwar Gore,Shubham Pathak,Ramapriya Ranganath,Sandeep Sharma,Tao Sheng,Sujith Ravi,Dan Roth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出VELD-QA——一个面向长文档视觉证据定位与问答的大规模基准，含15,000个人工撰写QA对、覆盖5,859份真实PDF（372,445页），并附带支撑页与视觉元素级证据标注，用于分离评估检索与解读能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长文档QA基准很少同时具备大规模人工撰写问题与细粒度视觉证据标注，导致难以诊断模型在证据定位（retrieval/localization）与视觉解读（interpretation/reasoning）两环节的失败来源。

Method: 构建VELD-QA基准：15,000个人工QA对，覆盖5,859份真实PDF、共372,445页，均衡涵盖图表、表格与复杂版面任务；人工标注支撑页与证据元素并描述所引用视觉内容，从而支持检索与解读的分离评估。实验上评估9个视觉语言模型在4种证据设置下的表现，以及7种检索增强与agentic系统。

Result: 检索类方法显著低于提供真实支撑页的oracle设置；全文档直接输入无法弥合该差距。仅靠定位不足够：oracle准确率最高仅81.7%，表明视觉解读与推理仍是持续瓶颈。将视觉元素标注作为输入富化后，性能差距缩小17–24%。

Conclusion: 证据定位与视觉解读是长文档多模态QA的两大瓶颈，且定位正确并不保证可答；细粒度视觉元素标注不仅能用于评测，还可作为输入富化手段提升性能，从而推动多模态文档QA的数据增强方向。

Abstract: Existing long-document QA benchmarks rarely combine large-scale human-authored questions with detailed visual evidence annotations, limiting diagnosis of evidence localization and interpretation failures. We present VELD-QA, a large-scale benchmark for Visual Evidence grounding and question answering in Long Documents. It contains 15,000 fully human-authored question-answer pairs across 5,859 real-world PDFs totaling 372,445 pages, balancing chart, table, and complex-layout tasks. Human annotations identify supporting pages and evidence elements and describe cited visual content, enabling separate retrieval and interpretation evaluation. We evaluate nine vision-language models under four evidence settings and seven retrieval-augmented and agentic systems. Our analysis identifies bottlenecks in evidence localization and interpretation. Retrieval-based methods substantially underperform the oracle setting with ground-truth supporting pages; full-document access does not close the gap. Localization alone is insufficient: oracle accuracy peaks at 81.7%, highlighting persistent visual interpretation and reasoning challenges. Enriching inputs with our visual-element annotations narrows the performance gap by 17-24%, demonstrating value beyond evaluation and motivating data enrichment for multimodal document QA. Our code is available at https://github.com/oracle-samples/veld-qa.

</details>


### [39] [Decision-FLIP: Budget-Free Structured Pruning for Gaussian Occupancy Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20917)
*Jingxing Li,Anurag Paul,Subhasis Das,Cindy Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Decision-FLIP 是一个无需预算的占用预测模型压缩框架：以无标注校准输入为准，用 Exact Mean-FLIP 度量每行"移除后体素 argmax 决策改变数"，再用带守护的 Otsu 分割自动确定保留行数，并将幸存行物理编译为更小检查点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 占用预测器依赖大型持久高斯库或手工固定规模的查询；常用剪枝代理（不透明度、激活频率、参数幅值）并不衡量"删除某行是否改变模型的体素决策"，因此缺乏与被裁单位决策影响直接相关的打分依据，且现有方法通常需要预先指定裁剪预算。

Method: 1) Exact Mean-FLIP 打分：对每条持久行计算将其从读出层移除后发生改变的体素 argmax 决策数目，作为该行重要性分数；2) 对 log 分数分布做带守护的 Otsu 二分割，自动选出幸存行数，无需目标预算，且在分割缺乏支撑时弃权；3) 将幸存行物理编译为更小检查点，使相机采样、注意力与聚合随库规模一并收缩；4) 对流式库，分数在每行生命周期（warp、death、refill）上积分。

Result: 在四种高斯库模型-数据集设定与一个持久查询迁移设定中，自动选定的裁剪移除 12–79% 的合格行，将依赖库/查询的延迟降低 7–45%（给定组件性能剖析）；受图像、体素、传感器等固定成本模块约束，主要端点上整模型节省最高 8.0%，峰值内存节省最高 16.6%。在匹配的一轮恢复对照下，语义 mIoU 变化不超过 0.18 点。同 K 对照表明排序而非库规模本身起决定作用：随机移除使 GaussianFormer-1 与 OccFormer 崩溃，而无标签 Mean-FLIP 在全部五个对照设定上匹配或优于有监督 Taylor 排序。

Conclusion: Decision-FLIP 以决策改变量作为剪枝准则，实现了无预算、仅需无标注校准输入的占用预测器编译压缩，在几乎不损失精度的情况下获得延迟与内存收益；其适用前提是可移除原语在检查点中具有稳定的行身份，且"无预算"指幸存行数无需预设，而非无参数。

Abstract: Occupancy predictors rely on large persistent banks of Gaussians or learned queries whose size is fixed by hand. Common pruning proxies, such as opacity, activation frequency, or parameter magnitude, do not measure whether removing a row changes the model's voxel decisions. We introduce Decision-FLIP, a budget-free compilation framework whose scoring and structure selection need only unlabeled calibration inputs. Exact Mean-FLIP scores each persistent row by the number of voxel argmax decisions that change when the row is removed from the readout; a guarded Otsu split of the log-score distribution selects the survivor count without a target and abstains when the split is unsupported; and the survivors are physically compiled into a smaller checkpoint, so camera sampling, attention, and aggregation shrink with the bank. For streaming banks, scores are integrated over each row's lifecycle through warp, death, and refill. Across four Gaussian-bank model-dataset settings and one persistent-query transfer, the automatically selected cuts remove 12-79% of eligible rows and cut bank/query-dependent latency by 7-45% across settings with component profiles, with whole-model savings of up to 8.0% on the primary endpoints bounded by fixed-cost image, voxel, and sensor modules and peak-memory savings of up to 16.6%. Under matched one-epoch recovery controls, semantic mIoU changes by at most 0.18 point. Same-$K$ controls show that the ranking, not only the bank size, matters: random removal collapses GaussianFormer-1 and OccFormer, while label-free Mean-FLIP matches or outperforms supervised Taylor ranking on all five settings where that control was run. The method applies when removable primitives keep a stable checkpoint-row identity; it is budget-free in survivor count, not parameter-free.

</details>


### [40] [MARS - A foundational map auto-regressor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20843)
*Qi Zhang,Suvam Bag,Rupanjali Kukal,Lucas Thai,Jacob Kovarskiy,Mike Bianco,Xiaojie Guo,Raphael Tang,Mikael Figueroa,Rishi Madhok,Nikolaos Karianakis,Fuxun Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个地图基础模型 MARS（Map Auto-Regressor），以自回归语言建模方式统一生成多折线路网与多边形建筑，并构建了 3.4M 规模的 MAP-3M 多类地图提取数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地图生成任务涉及大量非结构化的矢量化数据（点、折线、多边形），对常规逐像素生成模型构成挑战。传统方法采用多阶段流程（像素级分割＋矢量化后处理），误差与复杂度逐阶段累积。受自回归语言建模近期成功的启发，作者希望以端到端方式直接生成矢量化地图要素。

Method: 提出 MARS，将地图要素表示为序列化 token，用自回归模型统一建模多折线路网与多边形建筑；为此收集并构建 MAP-3M 数据集（约 3.4M 样本，据称是最大的多类地图提取数据集）。

Result: 在四个道路与建筑数据集上，MARS 的性能优于或持平多阶段基线方法；借助端到端自回归特性，还实现了 "Chat with MARS" 交互式人在回路生成与修正能力。数据集与演示页面已公开。

Conclusion: MARS 表明自回归序列建模范式可有效替代多阶段管线，统一处理异构矢量地图要素，并天然支持交互式编辑；MAP-3M 与 Demo 的开放将推动地图基础模型研究。

Abstract: Map generation tasks feature extensive non-structural vectorized data (e.g., points, polylines, and polygons) and thus pose significant challenges to common pixel-wise generative models. Conventional approaches use multiple stages, first segmenting these features at the pixel level and then performing vectorized post-processing, with errors and complexity compounding at each stage. Motivated by the recent success of auto-regressive language modeling, we propose the first map foundation model, named Map Auto-Regressor (MARS), that is capable of generating both multi-polyline road networks and polygon buildings in a unified manner. For training MARS, we collected to our knowledge the largest multi-class map extraction dataset totaling 3.4M examples, which we call MAP-3M. Across four road and building datasets, MARS outperforms or matches the performance of multistage baselines. Additionally, we develop a "Chat with MARS" capability that enables interactive human-in-the-loop map generation and correction, supported by the auto-regressive nature of our end-to-end approach. We release our MAP-3M dataset and project demo page at (1) https://huggingface.co/datasets/bag-lab/MAP-3M and (2) https://huggingface.co/spaces/bag-lab/MARS, respectively.

</details>


### [41] [Intern Lumina U2: A Multi-Codebook Diffusion Large Language Model for Omni-Visual Understanding and Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20818)
*Qian Yu,Zongyang Qiu,Juncheng Yan,Hongliang Li,Qi Qin,Keqi Wang,Siqi Luo,Junkui Ju,Yapeng Mi,Tiantian Lu,Tian Liu,Pengyu Xie,Chenhao Ji,Minghui Zhang,Yiang Zhao,Siyu Yan,Haiying Xu,Yanbo Wang,Jianghan Shen,Yuandong Pu,Jiayi Lei,Borui Zhang,Jinxin Shi,Bo Zhang,Yuhang Zang,Shenglong Ye,Lixin Gu,Hongjie Zhang,Linghan Liu,Xinyuan Chen,Yuewen Cao,Lihe Zhang,Xiaohong Liu,Junjun He,Qiangwei Liu,Qi Zhang,Yu Cheng,Tianfan Xue,Hui Xiong,Chang Xu,Yu Qiao,Yihao Liu,Bin Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Intern Lumina U2 是一个扩散语言模型，首次在完全离散空间中表示多码本视觉 token，实现文本与高容量视觉表示在同一骨干、同一优化空间中统一建模，并支持图像、视频、3D 输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态生成存在三类范式：单码本离散方法天然对齐文本建模但表示容量有限；连续与混合方法视觉表示更丰富，却在异构空间中用不同训练目标建模文本与图像，易引发模态间优化冲突与表示空间错位。核心问题是如何在单一骨干的共享优化空间内，高效地同时建模高容量视觉表示与文本内容。

Method: 以 AToken 的统一 4D 表示为基础，用一组来自互补码本的 codes 表示每个视觉位置：输入端在每个位置拼接各码本嵌入；输出端采用轻量级多码本自回归头，在每个位置内顺序预测各码，而扩散骨干并行处理所有位置。另提出稳定性感知采样策略，结合置信度平台引导的解掩码与漂移触发的缓存刷新机制。并在昇腾与 NVIDIA 平台上搭建全栈训练、推理与评测系统。

Result: 在视觉理解、图像生成和图像编辑上取得统一模型中的领先性能；单一骨干支持图像、视频与 3D 输入；跨平台验证了模型有效性；稳定性感知采样使图像生成加速 4.45 倍。

Conclusion: 全离散的多码本视觉表示可在保持与离散文本建模兼容的同时提升表示容量，为统一多模态生成提供了一条避免模态优化冲突的可行路径，并通过采样优化显著提升推理效率。

Abstract: Unified multimodal generation covers discrete, continuous, and hybrid paradigms. Discrete approaches with single-codebook visual tokenizers align naturally with text modeling but have limited representational capacity. Continuous and hybrid approaches retain richer visual representations, but they model text and images in heterogeneous spaces with different training objectives, potentially introducing optimization conflicts between modalities with misaligned representation spaces. These choices expose a central question: how to efficiently model high-capacity visual representations alongside textual content in a shared optimization space within a single backbone. We present Intern Lumina U2, to our knowledge the first diffusion language model to represent multi-codebook visual tokens in a fully discrete space. Each visual position is represented by a group of codes from complementary codebooks, increasing its representational capacity while preserving compatibility with discrete text modeling. At the input, the embeddings are concatenated at each position; at the output, a lightweight multi-codebook autoregressive head predicts the codes within each position sequentially, while the diffusion backbone processes all positions in parallel. Built on the unified 4D representation of AToken, Intern Lumina U2 supports image, video, and 3D inputs within a single backbone and achieves leading performance among unified models in visual understanding, image generation, and image editing. We further build a full-stack training, inference, and evaluation system on both Ascend and NVIDIA platforms to verify the cross-platform effectiveness of our model. We also improve the inference efficiency through a stability-aware sampling strategy, which combines confidence-plateau-guided unmasking approach with drift-triggered cache refreshing mechanisms, accelerating image generation by 4.45x.

</details>


### [42] [From Adults to Children: Benchmarking Foundation Models for Chest X-Ray Classification under Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21809)
*Sara Inácio,Hugo Proença,João C. Neves*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次在统一的成人→儿科评估设定下对多个基础模型进行基准比较，发现所有模型跨人群迁移均出现显著性能下降（超过10个百分点），且加深适配深度并不能改善跨人群泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在医学影像中潜力显著，但跨患者人群（如从成人到儿科）的鲁棒性尚不明确，缺乏统一基准。

Method: 将VinDr-CXR与PediCXR统一为五分类任务，评估6个基础模型和2个CNN基线；仅用成人数据适配，在成人域内与儿科域分别采用线性探测、部分微调和全量微调三种适配深度进行测试。

Result: 所有模型从成人迁移到儿科数据均出现显著性能下降，降幅超过10个百分点：成人测试集Macro-AUROC约92%，儿科域降至约78%；加深适配深度未显著改善跨人群泛化；强域内性能并不转化为更好的儿科表现。

Conclusion: 基础模型在成人→儿科跨人群泛化上存在明显不足，适配深度并非有效手段，域内性能不能作为跨人群鲁棒性的可靠代理指标。

Abstract: Foundation models have shown significant potential across diverse fields and have been applied to medical imaging, but their robustness across patient populations remains unclear. In this work, we investigate foundation models' ability to generalize from adult to pediatric chest X-ray classification. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first benchmark to compare multiple foundation models under a common adult-to-pediatric evaluation setting while also analyzing the effect of adaptation depth. We harmonize VinDr-CXR and PediCXR into a five-label classification task and evaluate six foundation models and two CNN baselines. All models are adapted using adult data only and tested in both the in-domain and pediatric domains using linear probing, partial fine-tuning, and full fine-tuning. The benchmark reveals a clear and consistent trend: all models suffer a substantial performance degradation when transferred from adult to pediatric data, with drops exceeding 10 percentage points. While Macro-AUROC values reach approximately 92% on the adult test set, performance decreases to around 78% on the pediatric domain. Another important finding is that increasing the depth of adaptation does not significantly improve cross-population generalization. Moreover, strong in-domain performance does not translate into better performance on pediatric data.

</details>


### [43] [Single-stage joint diffusion of sky images and clear-sky index for ultra-short-term irradiance forecasting: what the frame-to-irradiance stage discards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21795)
*Kanawut Suwandee,Suwichaya Suwanwimolkul*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出单阶段扩散模型联合去噪未来天空图像与晴空指数序列，并由辐照度头直接读取去噪预览，实现无需测量辐照度输入的辐照度概率预报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有天空图像辐照度预报为两阶段（视频预测+单独回归器），存在级联误差且需分别训练；同时站点可能只有相机而无辐照度计。

Method: 单一扩散过程中联合去噪未来天空图像与晴空指数序列；辐照度头读取未来天空的去噪预览而非噪声扩散状态；仅以天空图像与确定性协变量为输入，无回归阶段、无实测辐照度。

Result: 2023 SIRTA数据池化RMSE 104.1 W/m²，CRPS相对智能持续法的技巧分数+0.34，优于12个基线配置，跨2021–2022重训仍保持；在其自生成帧上单独回归器为150.6 W/m²，联合训练挽回约46 W/m²；确定性网络（另输入实测辐照度）点精度97.6 W/m²更优，但本模型在各天空条件三分位CRPS技巧均领先0.26–0.30。

Conclusion: 联合训练单阶段建模避免了级联信息损失，概率预报技巧更优，适用于无辐照度计站点。

Abstract: Sky-image irradiance forecasting is dominated by a two-stage design: a video-prediction model forecasts future sky frames, and a separately trained regressor maps those frames to irradiance. We propose a single-stage alternative that co-denoises the future sky-image and clear-sky-index sequences in one diffusion process, with an irradiance head that reads a denoised preview of the future sky rather than the noised diffusion state. The forecaster needs no regression stage and runs from sky images and deterministic covariates alone, with no measured irradiance at its input --- the situation at a site with a camera but no pyranometer. On 2023 SIRTA data the model reaches a pooled RMSE of $104.1$~W\,m$^{-2}$ and a skill score of $+0.34$ on the continuous ranked probability score (CRPS) against smart persistence, higher than any of twelve baseline configurations, and retains that skill when retrained on 2021--2022. On the model's own generated frames, a separately trained regressor reaches $150.6$~W\,m$^{-2}$ against the joint head's $104.1$: roughly $46$~W\,m$^{-2}$ that joint training recovers and a cascade discards. Point accuracy is competitive but not best: a deterministic network that also reads measured irradiance attains $97.6$~W\,m$^{-2}$. The model's CRPS skill leads that network's in every sky-condition tertile, by $0.26$ to $0.30$.

</details>


### [44] [MedPAO-VL: Interpretable and Protocol-Compliant Agent for Structured Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21794)
*Shrish Shrinath Vaidya,Gowthamaan Palani,Minmini Selvam,Gokulraja Srinivasaraja,Ganapathy Krishnamurthi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MedPAO-VL 多模态智能体，将胸片结构化报告生成从单体黑箱生成转变为可解释、迭代、符合规范的 plan-act-observe 循环，提升临床正确性与结构合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端方法为黑箱，易产生事实性幻觉与无依据发现，且不遵守领域专用结构化规范，导致可靠性与临床可用性不足。

Method: 多模态智能体框架：plan-act-observe 循环；先通过可解释视觉模块感知胸片并刻画诊断不确定性，再通过观察语义偏差进行迭代自我修正，使报告与视觉检测到的临床发现对齐。

Result: 专家评分准确度 3.5/5，结构评分 4.3/5，临床正确性与完整性均有提升。

Conclusion: 以病理特异可解释性与迭代精炼替代不透明的端到端映射，为自动化结构化放射报告生成提供透明、临床可行的替代方案。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable generalization capabilities of Large Language Models, generating clinically accurate and structured radiology reports from medical images remains a significant challenge. Current end-to-end approaches function as black boxes, prone to factual hallucinations and generating ungrounded findings. This lack of reliability and failure to adhere to domain-specific structuring protocols, fundamentally limits their clinical utility. We present MedPAO-VL, a multimodal agent that shifts the paradigm from monolithic generation to explainable, iterative, protocol-compliant structured report generation. Unlike deterministic models, our plan-act-observe loop functions as a goal-oriented agent: it first perceives the Chest X-Ray through an explainable vision module designed to capture inherent diagnostic uncertainty, then iteratively refines the generated report by observing semantic discrepancies and self-correcting its generation for better alignment with visually detected clinical findings. Evaluation of our framework demonstrates that this agentic reasoning improves clinical correctness and completeness, achieving an expert-rated accuracy score of 3.5/5 and a structural score of 4.3/5. By prioritizing pathology specific explainability and iterative refinement over opaque end-to-end mapping, MedPAO-VL offers a transparent and clinically viable alternative for automated structured radiology report generation.

</details>


### [45] [Beyond Source Prompts: Towards Visually Grounded AI-Generated Video Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21758)
*Kehao Zhou,Yixuan Li,Yuxuan Jiang,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VGCap，用视觉接地字幕（全局修订字幕+分段字幕）引导AI生成视频质量评估，在LGVQ与FETV上三个质量维度均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成视频（AIGV）易受多种失真影响，需专用质量评估方法；内容语义对AIGV质量评估很重要，通常以源提示词作为文本参考，但源提示词未必准确反映生成视频内容，造成意图描述与生成结果之间的偏差。

Method: 提出VGCap框架，以视觉接地字幕替代源提示词作为文本引导。引入两类字幕：一是基于多帧视觉证据对源提示词做最小修改得到的全局字幕；二是与提示词无关、描述局部时序内容的分段字幕。两类字幕互补，为空间质量、时序质量和文本-视频对齐评估提供文本证据。

Result: 在LGVQ和FETV数据集上，VGCap在三个质量维度上均优于代表性基线。关于字幕来源与时序组件的消融实验证实视觉接地字幕对各质量维度有效，并表明分段级特征对时序质量预测有益。

Conclusion: 以视觉接地字幕作为语义参考可缓解源提示词与生成内容不一致的问题，提升AIGV质量评估的空间、时序与对齐预测性能；分段级特征对时序质量评估尤为关键。代码将开源。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative artificial intelligence have accelerated video generation technologies. However, AI-generated videos (AIGVs) remain susceptible to various distortions, highlighting the need for dedicated video quality assessment methods. Content semantics play an important role in AIGV quality assessment, with source prompts typically used as textual references. However, these prompts may not accurately reflect the generated video content, resulting in a discrepancy between the intended textual description and the generated result. To address this limitation, this paper proposes VGCap, an AIGV quality assessment framework guided by visually grounded captions that align better with generated content. Specifically, we introduce two forms of captions: a global caption obtained by minimally revising the source prompt based on multi-frame visual evidence, and prompt-independent segment captions that describe localized temporal content. Together, these captions provide complementary textual evidence for assessing spatial quality, temporal quality, and text-video alignment. Experimental results on the LGVQ and FETV datasets show that VGCap outperforms representative baselines across all three dimensions. Further ablation studies on caption sources and temporal components confirm the effectiveness of visually grounded captions for each quality dimension, while highlighting the benefits of segment-level features for temporal quality prediction. The source of VGCap will be available at https://github.com/lolo123-123/VGCap.

</details>


### [46] [Bridging the Markerless Tracking Validation Gap: Intraoperative X-ray Workflow Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21696)
*Connor Daly,Salvatore Russo,Jinendra Ekanayake,Daniel S. Elson,Ferdinando Rodriguez y Baena*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一套临床可行的流程，利用术中C臂透视（常规level check图像）结合多模态基准阵列，将以多视角2D-3D CT-透视配准获得的椎体6-DoF位姿与RGB-D相机坐标系对齐，从而为无标记脊柱跟踪方法提供独立的地下（subsurface）真值。离体验证精度为2.06±0.29 mm、3.35±0.93°；并在3段手术序列、11个椎体、1500帧临床数据上首次对两种无标记配准策略进行独立真值评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于RGB-D传感器的无标记术中脊柱解剖跟踪是有望替代侵入性骨锚定基准点的方案，但缺乏带独立地下真值的临床数据集，无法验证这些方法。

Method: 构建临床可用流程：由常规术中C臂透视图像恢复每椎体6-DoF位姿（多视角2D-3D CT-透视配准），通过一个在两种模态中均可检测的多模态基准阵列与RGB-D相机坐标系共配准，形成标定后的变换链；无需额外辐射、器械或改变标准手术流程。

Result: 离体羊椎骨对光学跟踪验证显示端到端精度2.06±0.29 mm与3.35±0.93°；在3段手术序列、11个椎体、1500帧临床数据上评估了两种无标记配准策略，为临床场景下首次对独立地下真值的评估。

Conclusion: 该框架可作为可复用的基础设施，在真实手术数据上对新兴无标记跟踪方法进行基准评测。

Abstract: Markerless intraoperative tracking of spinal anatomy via RGB-D sensors is a promising alternative to invasive bone-anchored fiducials, yet no existing clinical dataset provides independent subsurface ground truth for validating these methods. We address this gap with a clinically viable pipeline that derives per-vertebra 6-DoF pose from routine intraoperative C-arm fluoroscopy, co-registered to the RGB-D camera frame through a multimodal fiducial array detectable in both imaging modalities. Critically, the pipeline requires no additional radiation, instrumentation, or deviation from standard surgical workflows, relying solely on the fluoroscopic images already acquired during routine level checks. A calibrated transform chain links the vertebral pose recovered via multi-view 2D-3D CT-to-fluoroscopy registration to the RGB-D coordinate frame, enabling direct comparison of any markerless registration estimate against a subsurface reference. Ex vivo validation against optically tracked ovine vertebrae confirms end-to-end accuracy of 2.06 +- 0.29 mm and 3.35 +- 0.93 degrees, sufficient to serve as independent ground truth for methods with substantially larger errors. We demonstrate the pipeline's utility by evaluating two markerless registration strategies on clinical data spanning 11 vertebrae across 1,500 frames from three surgical sequences: the first such evaluation against an independent subsurface reference in a clinical setting. The proposed framework provides reusable infrastructure for benchmarking emerging markerless tracking methods on real surgical data.

</details>


### [47] [SCMerge: Spatial-Consistency-Aware Token Pair Merging for Efficient Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21642)
*Junfeng Ren,Yi Gong,Yuan Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCMerge是一种免训练的token合并方法，在配对相似token时同时考虑特征相似性与空间一致性，并可直接插入现有ViT语义分割流程，在精度与效率间取得更优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT在语义分割上表现优异，但二次注意力复杂度限制其实际应用。现有token合并方法主要依赖特征相似性识别相似token，忽略空间一致性，可能在语义分割中引入分割错误。

Method: 提出SCMerge：联合考虑特征相似性与空间一致性来配对相似token；在编码器内对配对token进行合并，解码后将得到的token序列重构回原始patch网格；方法灵活，可免训练地插入现有基于ViT的语义分割流程。

Result: 在ADE20K、Pascal Context和Cityscapes基准数据集上的大量实验表明，SCMerge在分割精度与效率的权衡上持续优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 引入空间一致性可改进token合并质量，SCMerge作为一种免训练、即插即用的方案，能有效缓解ViT语义分割的效率和精度矛盾。

Abstract: Vision Transformer architectures have showcased impressive performance on semantic segmentation, yet their quadratic attention complexity hinders practical applications. Recent works have focused on merging tokens to reduce sequence length and thus improve transformer efficiency. However, existing token merging methods primarily rely on feature similarity to identify similar tokens, overlooking spatial consistency, which may introduce segmentation errors in semantic segmentation. In this work, we propose SCMerge, a training-free token merging method that jointly considers feature similarity and spatial consistency in pairing similar tokens. The pairwise tokens are then merged within the encoder, and the resulting token sequences are reconstructed onto the original patch grid after decoding. The proposed method is flexible and can be plugged into existing vision Transformer-based semantic segmentation pipelines. Extensive experiments on the benchmark datasets ADE20K, Pascal Context, and Cityscapes show that SCMerge consistently achieves a better trade-off between segmentation accuracy and efficiency than baseline methods.

</details>


### [48] [Anchor3DGS: Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting with Compact Anchor-Based Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21634)
*Sheng Ye,Jiangke Lin,Fudong Wang,Yi Yuan,Zhen-Hui Dong,Yong-Jin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Anchor3DGS，一个无位姿前馈3DGS框架，用信息熵引导的锚点式紧凑表示替代逐像素预测，以更少一个数量级的高斯原语实现SOTA新视角合成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈3DGS方法多采用像素对齐范式，每个输入像素预测一个高斯，使高斯数量与输入分辨率和视角数耦合；这导致纹理贫乏区域出现冗余原语、几何复杂区域覆盖不足。

Method: 以信息熵为引导在场景中布置一组3D锚点，集中于视觉复杂度高区域并保持空间覆盖；每个锚点对多视角图像特征做遮挡感知聚合，锚点间进行信息交换，再解码为一组局部高斯；另加轻量特征增强模块细化高斯泼溅的粗RGB输出。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上取得SOTA的新视角合成质量，同时高斯原语数量少一个数量级。

Conclusion: 锚点式、熵引导的紧凑表示可解耦高斯数量与输入分辨率/视角数，在无位姿前馈重建中以显著更低的原语预算获得更优渲染质量与覆盖均衡性。

Abstract: Recent feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) methods have enabled effective multi-view 3D reconstruction by predicting Gaussian primitives in a single forward pass. However, existing approaches predominantly adopt a pixel-aligned paradigm that predicts one Gaussian per input pixel, coupling the Gaussian count to the input resolution and number of views. This also leads to redundant primitives in textureless regions and insufficient coverage in geometrically complex areas. We present Anchor3DGS, a pose-free feed-forward 3DGS framework that replaces pixel-aligned prediction with a compact anchor-based representation. Guided by information entropy, our method places a set of 3D anchors in the scene, concentrating them in regions of higher visual complexity while maintaining broad spatial coverage. Each anchor performs occlusion-aware aggregation of multi-view image features, exchanges information with other anchors, and is decoded into a set of local Gaussians. A lightweight feature enhancement module further refines the coarse RGB output from Gaussian splatting and improves rendering fidelity. Extensive experiments on diverse benchmark datasets demonstrate that our Anchor3DGS achieves state-of-the-art novel view synthesis quality while only using an order of magnitude fewer Gaussian primitives.

</details>


### [49] [When Models Misread Ink: Auditing VLMs for Arabic Calligraphy Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22018)
*Niaz Mohaiman Abtahi,Evan Maksud,Reyana Shahid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文审计12个视觉语言模型在阿拉伯书法六分类任务上的细粒度识别能力，发现最佳数字提示准确率仅60.9%（闭源/API）与47.2%（开源），核心失效模式是"默认标签坍缩"（单一标签占33–63%预测），提示策略改善效果不均且往往只是转移而非消除默认偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯书法是穆斯林社群中具有高文化价值的视觉领域，但现有VLM评测极少检验其对具体书法体传统的细粒度辨识能力。作者希望提供一个紧凑、文化扎根的压力测试基准，用以衡量哪些视觉传统能被自动化系统真正"读懂"，并检验VLM鲁棒性。

Method: 构建清洗后的1,195张图像、六类书体的基准数据集；对12个VLM（含闭源/API与开源权重模型）进行审计式评测；采用三种提示范式：纯数字标签提示、视觉线索提示、以及"Diwani优先"提示，比较不同提示对预测分布的影响。

Result: 闭集设定下，闭源/API模型最佳数字提示准确率为60.9%，开源权重模型为47.2%。主要失效为默认标签坍缩：每次数字提示运行中单一标签占预测的33–63%，而多数类基线仅18.8%。12个模型中有8个默认标签为Thuluth；GPT-4o将52.9%的图像标为Thuluth，而该类别真实占比仅18.1%。提示效果不均：视觉线索对部分模型提升超10个百分点，但对另一些模型反而有害；突出Diwani往往只是把默认标签"挪位"而非去除。

Conclusion: 阿拉伯书法识别可作为VLM鲁棒性的紧凑、文化接地压力测试，并可用于度量哪些视觉传统对自动化系统而言是可辨识的；模型表现受提示设计强烈且不稳定影响，默认类别偏置是系统性瓶颈。

Abstract: Arabic calligraphy is a high-stakes cultural visual domain for Muslim communities, yet VLM evaluations rarely test its fine-grained script traditions. We audit 12 VLMs on a cleaned 1,195-image six-way benchmark using numeric, visual-cue, and Diwani-first prompts. In this closed-set setting, the best numeric-prompt accuracy is 60.9% for closed/API models and 47.2% for open-weight models. The main failure is default-label collapse: every numeric run is dominated by a single label taking 33-63% of predictions, against an 18.8% majority-class baseline. For 8 of 12 models the default is Thuluth; GPT-4o labels 52.9% of all images as Thuluth although it is only 18.1% of the set. Prompting helps unevenly: visual cues move accuracy by more than 10 points for some models but hurt others, and foregrounding Diwani often relocates the default rather than removing it. Arabic calligraphy recognition is therefore a compact, culturally grounded stress test for VLM robustness and for measuring which visual traditions become legible to automated systems.

</details>


### [50] [Visual Sanad: Auditing Vision-Language Models for False Acceptance of Qur'anic Citations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22009)
*Niaz Mohaiman Abtahi,Reyana Shahid,Evan Maksud*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Visual Sanad 基准，检验视觉语言模型（VLM）在视觉框架变化下是否会误接受错误的《古兰经》经文归属，发现闭源模型引用核验能力较强、开源模型倾向于一律判为有效，去偏提示只能移动判定阈值而非提升鉴别力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 越来越多用于解读宗教、教育等文化敏感视觉内容，但其在引用核验（citation verification）上的可靠性研究不足；尤其当同一引用—参考文献对以不同视觉样式呈现时，模型是否会被外观"权威感"误导尚未被系统检验。

Method: 构建受控基准 Visual Sanad：100 节英文《古兰经》经文 × 4 种事实状态（每节）× 6 种视觉条件（从纯文本到装饰性、权威框架式排版），共 2,400 张渲染图像；所有引用—文献对在渲染前均经来源核查与人工审核。开展 24 次完整模型—提示组合实验。

Result: 出现明显能力分界：商用 API 服务的 VLM 从近乎完美的引用核验到中等程度的误接受不等；近期开源权重 VLM 常表现出弱鉴别力，在多数图像上默认输出 VALID。去偏提示（de-biased prompting）可降低误接受率，但多是通过将判定阈值移向 INVALID，而非形成强引用鉴别能力。

Conclusion: Visual Sanad 揭示了一种根植于文化的多模态事实性失效：流畅的 VLM 可能将展示出的宗教引用视为"自证有效"，除非其具备强引用核验能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used to interpret religious, educational, and culturally sensitive visual content, yet their reliability on citation verification remains underexplored. We introduce Visual Sanad, a controlled benchmark for testing whether VLMs falsely accept incorrect Qur'anic verse attributions when the same quote-reference pair is rendered under different visual framings. The benchmark contains 2,400 rendered images built from 100 English Qur'an verses, four factual states per verse, and six visual conditions ranging from plain text to decorative and authority-framed layouts. All quote-reference pairs were source-checked and human-audited before rendering. Across 24 full model-prompt runs, we observe a sharp capability split. Proprietary API-served VLMs range from near-perfect citation verification to moderate false acceptance, whereas recent open-weight VLMs often show weak discrimination and default to VALID on most images. De-biased prompting can reduce false acceptance, but often by shifting the decision threshold toward INVALID rather than by producing strong citation discrimination. Visual Sanad therefore exposes a culturally grounded multimodal factuality failure: fluent VLMs may treat displayed religious citations as self-authenticating unless they have strong citation-verification ability.

</details>


### [51] [Data-Efficient ConvNeXt Pretraining for High-Resolution Sentinel-1 GRD SAR Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21993)
*Yuki Natsume,Sang-Ho Yun,Chai Kiat Yeo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SparK-SAR：基于稀疏卷积掩码预训练（SparK+ConvNeXt）的Sentinel-1 GRD自监督框架，在有限算力下以27.8M参数在四个Copernicus-Bench S1GRD任务上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: S1GRD标注数据稀缺，限制监督学习；而该领域的自监督预训练被ViT掩码图像建模主导，需大量数据与算力。

Method: 利用卷积归纳偏置：ConvNeXt-RS编码器将stem步长由4x减半为2x，在相同参数量下于16x步长处获得14x14特征图、4倍空间token；轻量ConvNeXt-512重建解码器采用PixelShuffle上采样；并刻画标准SparK UNet-768解码器在S1GRD上FP16混合精度预训练的不稳定性，下游微调采用逐层学习率衰减（LLRD）。

Result: 相比UNet-128基线，单GPU预训练显存降低7.5–8.5%且精度相当；微调后27.8M编码器较无预训练基线在EuroSAT-S1、BigEarthNet-S1、DFC2020-S1、Flood-S1上分别提升+4.7、+5.0、+5.8、+1.4 pp，并在四项任务中三项优于139M参数的冻结Copernicus-FM ViT（约1/5参数量）。数据扩展实验显示分类任务在SSL4EO-S12 v1.1的10–25%即饱和，稠密预测在25–50%仍持续提升。

Conclusion: 卷积式稀疏掩码预训练在S1GRD上具备高参数与算力效率，是ViT掩码建模范式的可行替代；下游收益随任务类型呈差异化数据效率，辅以CKA表征分析与地理去碰撞实验佐证。

Abstract: Labeled Sentinel-1 Ground Range Detected (S1GRD) datasets remain scarce, limiting supervised deep learning for land-surface remote sensing, while self-supervised pretraining for this domain has been dominated by Vision Transformer (ViT)-based masked image modeling requiring substantial data and compute. We propose SparK-SAR, a sparse convolutional masked pretraining framework built on SparK and ConvNeXt, designed to exploit convolutional inductive biases for S1GRD imagery under constrained compute. SparK-SAR makes three core technical contributions: (i) ConvNeXt-RS, a domain-adapted encoder that halves the stem stride (4x to 2x) to produce 14x14 feature maps at stride 16x, providing 4x more spatial tokens at identical parameter count; (ii) a lightweight ConvNeXt-512 reconstruction decoder with PixelShuffle upsampling that reduces pretraining VRAM by 7.5-8.5% per GPU relative to a UNet-128 baseline while matching its accuracy; and (iii) an empirical characterization of FP16 mixed-precision pretraining instability in the standard SparK UNet-768 decoder on S1GRD data, complemented by a finding that finetuning with layer-wise learning rate decay (LLRD) improves downstream performance of pretrained encoders. On four Copernicus-Bench S1GRD tasks, our finetuned 27.8M-parameter ConvNeXt-RS encoder improves over a non-pretrained baseline by +4.7, +5.0, +5.8, and +1.4 pp on EuroSAT-S1, BigEarthNet-S1, DFC2020-S1, and Flood-S1 respectively, and outperforms the Copernicus-FM ViT (139M parameters, frozen) on three of four tasks at roughly 5x fewer parameters. A pretraining data-scaling study reveals task-dependent data efficiency: classification saturates with as little as 10-25% of the SSL4EO-S12 v1.1 corpus, while dense prediction continues to improve significantly up to 25-50%. The study also includes a CKA representation analysis and a geographic de-collision experiment.

</details>


### [52] [Seeing is Believing: Benchmarking and Mitigating the Interference of Language Priors on Visual Perception in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21902)
*Li Ma,Yan Zhuang,Jiawen Deng,Fuji Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ViSiP-Bench 基准与 MAPS 方法，用于更精确地度量并缓解多模态大模型（MLLMs）中语言先验压过视觉证据的问题，发现先验依从普遍存在且不随规模改善，MAPS 可在不损害通用能力的前提下削弱过度先验依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准假设各模型共享同一套语言先验，且仅以准确率衡量指标；但实际上跨模型的先验一致性低于 80%，且超过 15% 的样本在无图像时也能答对，导致准确率会高估模型对先验的抗性，无法真实反映视觉感知被先验覆盖的程度。

Method: 1) 构建 ViSiP-Bench：以罕见但真实的场景为基础，覆盖六类先验；2) 设计模型感知的动态先验诱导协议（model-aware dynamic prior induction），为每个模型单独归纳其先验；3) 提出三个可连续刻画的指标替代单一准确率；4) 提出 MAPS：识别各模型最强的先验，并基于事实性知识进行微调，以软化过度先验依赖。

Result: 在 24 个模型上的评测显示：先验依从普遍存在；不随模型规模增大而改善；大模型倾向于要么强烈覆盖先验、要么坚定遵循先验。

Conclusion: 仅靠准确率评估会系统性高估模型的先验抗性，需以模型感知的先验诱导与多维指标进行度量；MAPS 能针对性缓解过度先验依赖，同时保持通用能力。

Abstract: Language priors in MLLMs can override visual evidence, impairing visual perception. Existing benchmarks assume shared priors across models and rely solely on accuracy. However, cross-model prior agreement falls below 80%, and over 15% of items can be answered correctly without any image, causing accuracy to overestimate prior resistance. To address these limitations, we introduce ViSiP-Bench, a benchmark grounded in rare-but-real scenarios across six prior categories, with a model-aware dynamic prior induction protocol and three metrics that more precisely and continuously measure prior resistance than accuracy alone. Evaluating 24 models reveals that prior adherence is pervasive, does not improve with scale, and larger models tend to either strongly override their priors or firmly adhere to them. We therefore propose MAPS, which identifies each model's strongest priors and fine-tunes on factually grounded knowledge to soften excessive prior reliance without disrupting general capabilities.

</details>


### [53] [Decoupled Safety Control: A Safety-Control Algorithm for Training-Free Safety Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21569)
*Huilin Zhou,Ruoxi Cheng,Yuhang Wang,Yuming Liu,Minghao Sun,Ruolong Ma,Lan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出解耦安全控制（DSC），把免训练安全引导重构为显式的状态依赖控制过程，使干预由当前生成状态决定而非直接继承单一不安全条件，从而在更强抑制不安全内容的同时保持良性生成质量与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练安全引导多依赖"耦合式安全控制链"：同一不安全条件同时承担"避免什么、当前不安全信号、干预构造、干预何时生效"四种角色。当有害与良性语义在生成状态中纠缠时，该耦合不可靠，易残留不安全内容，或不必要地损害良性生成质量与提示保真度。

Method: DSC 作为免训练安全引导的插件式算法，将安全引导重新表述为显式的状态依赖控制过程：执行何种干预由当前生成状态推断，而非由单一不安全条件直接继承，从而解耦"条件—信号—干预—激活时机"的耦合链。

Result: 在标准安全基准上，DSC 可增强代表性潜空间与文本空间安全防护方法的表现：不安全内容抑制更强，同时保持良性生成质量，并维持高效推理。

Conclusion: 解耦安全控制链、以状态驱动干预，是提升免训练扩散安全引导有效性与保真度权衡的可行方向，且具备即插即用、无需改权重、推理高效的实用性。

Abstract: Diffusion models achieve strong performance in text-to-image generation, but they can still produce unsafe content. Training-free safety guidance offers a practical inference-time alternative that intervenes during sampling without modifying model weights. However, many existing safeguards still rely on a coupled safety-control chain. In this chain, the same unsafe condition simultaneously specifies what should be avoided, indicates the current unsafe signal, constructs the intervention, and determines when that intervention remains active. This coupling can work when unsafe factors are explicit, but becomes less reliable once harmful and benign semantics are entangled in the current generation state, often leaving residual unsafe content or unnecessarily degrading benign-generation quality or prompt fidelity. We address this problem with Decoupled Safety Control (DSC), a plug-in safety-control algorithm for training-free safety guidance. DSC reformulates training-free safety guidance as an explicit state-dependent control process, so the executed intervention is determined from the current generation state rather than inherited directly from a single unsafe condition. Experiments on standard safety benchmarks show that DSC improves representative latent- and text-space safeguards, achieving stronger unsafe-content suppression while preserving benign-generation quality and maintaining efficient inference.

</details>


### [54] [Rethinking Classifier-Free Guidance for TI2I Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21550)
*Andreas Floros,Elena Koritska,Teofilo de Campos,Christoph Vogel,Chu-Tak Li,Anuj Sharma,Alexandros Neophytou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了文本-图像到图像（TI2I）流匹配中的无分类器引导（CFG）机制，发现CFG在流建模的噪声阶段对编辑隐式有害，并提出在早期流步骤进行简单校正的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CFG在生成建模中通常被视为保证高质量输出的关键组件，但作者认为其在TI2I建模中的作用缺乏充分理解，需要重新审视。

Method: 作者观察到CFG机制在流建模的噪声阶段会阻碍指令跟随与可控性，据此提出在早期流步骤中引入一个简单的校正，从而在无额外开销的情况下实现更丰富的对齐-保持权衡，并在像素空间和潜空间的TI2I流上验证。

Result: 所提方法在像素空间与潜空间TI2I流上均展示了有效性。

Conclusion: CFG在TI2I编辑的噪声阶段隐式有害；通过早期流步骤的简单校正即可改善可控性与指令跟随，且不增加计算成本。

Abstract: We study Text-and-Image-to-Image (TI2I) flow matching methods for controllable image editing. In a broader generative modeling context, Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG) is established as a key component responsible for high quality outputs. However, we argue that its role in TI2I modeling is poorly understood. Specifically, we make the surprising observation that the CFG mechanism is implicitly harmful for editing in the noisy phase of flow modeling, hindering instruction following and controllability. We propose a simple correction in the early flow steps, enabling a richer alignment-preservation tradeoff at no extra cost. The effectiveness of our approach is demonstrated on both pixel-space and latent TI2I flows.

</details>


### [55] [RLDF: A Region-Level Deepfake Dataset and Benchmark for Partial-Face Manipulation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21438)
*Shukesh Reddy,Abhijit Das*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个区域级深伪检测数据集RLDF，包含超200万张局部换脸图像，涵盖7个面部区域、8种换脸方法，并给出12个SOTA检测器的基准审计，揭示其在区域级评估下AUC下降10.5%、准确率下降10%的泛化缺陷，并提出mask-guided cross-attention基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深伪检测模型均基于"整体换脸"或"整体合成"的篡改图像构建，缺乏针对篡改仅局限于局部面部子区域场景的检测能力评估，因此提出开放问题：现成检测器能否有效检测区域级篡改。

Method: 提出区域感知的部分换脸合成模型（region-aware partial swap synthesis），利用8种换脸方法生成超200万张局部换脸图像；覆盖7个面部区域（4个离散区域：眼、鼻、嘴、眉；4个复合区域：眼+鼻、眼+鼻+嘴、眼+鼻+嘴+眉、全脸）；定义3种评估协议，在5个标准跨数据集评估、DF40及RLDF上对12个SOTA检测器进行基准测试；并提出mask-guided cross-attention模型作为基线。

Result: 现有检测器在跨局部面部区域时无法一致泛化，区域级评估下整体AUC下降10.5%、准确率下降10%；基准测试系统性地定位了现有检测器在何处、为何表现不佳。

Conclusion: 区域级深伪检测是被忽视但重要的评估维度，现有方法存在显著泛化缺口；所提mask-guided cross-attention基线可有效检测区域级篡改，数据集、代码与检查点已开源以推动后续研究。

Abstract: Existing deepfake detection models are built upon manipu- lated images that are tampered with either exclusively fully swapped or fully synthesised faces, which leads us to an open question: Can off-the-shelf deepfake models detect manipulation effectively while tampering is confined to a local facial sub-region? To fill this gap, we introduce RLDF (Region-Level Deepfake), the first dataset for region-level deepfake evaluation. Employing the proposed region-aware partial swap synthesis model, we generate over 2M par- tial face-swap images by using eight face-swapping meth- ods. Altogether, we considered seven face regions, among which there are four discrete regions (eye, nose, mouth and eyebrow) and four composite facial regions (eye and nose; eye, nose and mouth; eye, nose, mouth and eye- brow; and full face). In addition, we define three evalu- ation protocols and benchmark 12 state-of-the-art detectors across the five popular standard cross-dataset evaluations, DF40, and the proposed RLDF, providing a comprehen- sive audit of where and why existing detectors underper- formed. Our evaluation reveals that existing detectors fail to generalize consistently across partial facial regions, re- sulting in an overall drop of 10.5% AUC and 10% accu- racy under region-level evaluation. Further, we propose a mask-guided cross-attention model as a baseline approach that can effectively detect region-level manipulation. The proposed dataset, code, and checkpoints are available at https://github.com/RLDF-Dataset/RLDF.

</details>


### [56] [ManiFusion: Unlocking the High-Throughput Generation via Superposition in Manifold Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21433)
*Minkyu Kim,Baekseung Kim,Junhoo Lee,Jangho Kim,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ManiFusion：通过"融合空间计算"在单次去噪评估中并行处理多样本，实现吞吐导向的扩散模型加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型采样需迭代去噪、计算开销大；现有加速方法主要降低单样本延迟，该方向收益趋于饱和，需探索正交的吞吐导向路径。

Method: 提出骨干无关框架 ManiFusion：在去噪骨干前将多样本融合、之后反融合分离；给出多共享融合表示同时保留各自生成轨迹引导信息的几何视角；并用轨迹融合调度（TFS）随采样阶段动态调整融合因子。

Result: 方法可泛化至 U-Net 与 Transformer 骨干、像素与潜空间等扩散家族；可与现有加速方法组合，在最小质量损失下达到先前方法无法实现的吞吐区间。

Conclusion: 吞吐导向的多样本融合计算是扩散加速的有效正交方向，ManiFusion 兼具骨干通用性与可组合性。

Abstract: Diffusion models achieve high-quality image generation but remain computationally expensive due to iterative denoising across many timesteps. Existing acceleration methods mainly reduce per-sample latency, but gains along this axis are becoming saturated. We instead explore an orthogonal throughput-oriented direction by processing multiple samples within a single denoising evaluation. To this end, we propose ManiFusion, a backbone-agnostic framework for multi-sample generation through fused-space computation. ManiFusion jointly processes multiple samples by fusing them before the denoising backbone and separating them afterward through unfusion. We provide a geometric perspective on how multiple samples can share a fused representation while retaining the information needed to guide their individual generation trajectories. To better align fusion allocation with denoising stages, ManiFusion uses Trajectory-Fusion Scheduling (TFS), which dynamically adjusts fusion factors throughout sampling. ManiFusion generalizes across diffusion families, including both U-Net and transformer backbones in pixel and latent spaces. We further show that ManiFusion composes with existing acceleration methods and reaches throughput regimes unattainable by prior approaches with minimal quality degradation.

</details>


### [57] [CLAD: Keypoint Matching Using Global Line Correspondences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21409)
*Masaki Satoh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLAD利用线对应的全局几何上下文来辅助关键点匹配，缓解局部描述子在重复纹理场景下的歧义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SIFT、ORB等传统描述子由局部图像块计算，在重复纹理或弱纹理场景中不同位置的图像块外观相似，导致匹配不可靠。

Method: 提出Contextual Line-Assisted Descriptor（CLAD），用向量表示每个关键点到已匹配直线的邻近程度，从而编码关键点与场景中直线的空间关系；并用HOME（Hough空间的极值一维匹配）检测清晰与微弱的线结构，建立足够的线—关键点关联以表征该上下文。

Result: 在TUM RGB-D SLAM数据集和室外视频序列上评估，CLAD给出几何一致的匹配，可补充传统局部描述子的匹配结果；在重复场景中借助全局结构提升匹配可靠性，且计算轻量、易于实现。

Conclusion: 将全局线几何上下文与局部描述子结合，可有效提升重复/弱纹理场景下的关键点匹配可靠性。

Abstract: Traditional keypoint descriptors, such as SIFT (Scale-Invariant Feature Transform) and ORB (Oriented FAST and Rotated BRIEF), are computed from local image patches. They may be unreliable in scenes with repetitive patterns or weak textures, where patches at different locations can look similar. We propose the Contextual Line-Assisted Descriptor (CLAD), a keypoint matching method that addresses this problem by using global geometric context from line correspondences. CLAD represents each keypoint with a vector whose elements indicate its proximity to matched lines. It can therefore use the keypoint's spatial relationships to the lines in the scene. We use HOME (Hough-space One-dimensional Matching of Extrema) to detect both clear and weak linear structures and to establish enough line-to-keypoint associations to represent this context. Evaluations on the TUM RGB-D SLAM dataset and outdoor video sequences show that CLAD provides geometrically consistent matches that complement those obtained with traditional local descriptors. In repetitive scenes, where local descriptors may not distinguish similar patches, CLAD uses global structure to improve matching reliability. The method is computationally lightweight and practical to implement.

</details>


### [58] [SATOR: Backpropagation-Free 3D Test-Time Adaptation via Sample-Adaptive Token Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21367)
*Inseok Kong,Geunyoung Jung,Jiyoung Jung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SATOR：一种无源、免反向传播的3D点云测试时自适应框架，用样本自适应token路由替代预定义的purge-size搜索，在三个3D损坏基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免反向传播的token剔除式3D TTA方法依赖预定义的purge-size候选集合，需对每个样本多次前向传播来搜索最优剪枝规模，推理成本高。

Method: 1）从互补的注意力图与几何关系图计算token异常分数；2）基于Otsu可分性直接从样本导出保留/剔除token的划分阈值，无需评估候选规模；3）keep-ratio校准路由，仅当剪枝预测的熵（经保留率惩罚）低于原始预测熵时才选用剪枝预测。

Result: 在ModelNet40-C、ScanObjectNN-C与ShapeNet-C三个3D损坏基准上取得SOTA精度。

Conclusion: 通过样本自适应地推导划分阈值与路由决策，SATOR在消除搜索开销的同时保持并提升了对3D分布偏移的自适应性能。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) mitigates the performance degradation of source-pretrained models on unlabeled test data under distribution shifts. Recent backpropagation-free token-purging methods address 3D point cloud distribution shifts by filtering unreliable tokens. However, their reliance on predefined purge-size candidates requires multiple forward passes per sample and increases inference cost. We propose SATOR (Sample-Adaptive TOken Routing), a source-free and backpropagation-free 3D TTA framework that eliminates this search by deriving both the partitioning threshold and routing decision directly from each sample. SATOR computes token abnormality scores from complementary attention and geometric relation graphs. It then derives a sample-specific partition into retained and pruned tokens via Otsu-based separability, without evaluating candidate sizes. Keep-ratio-calibrated routing then selects the pruned prediction only when its entropy, penalized by the keep ratio, is lower than that of the original prediction. Experiments on ModelNet40-C, ScanObjectNN-C, and ShapeNet-C demonstrate that SATOR achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on three 3D corruption benchmarks.

</details>


### [59] [CoDeFont: One-Shot Font Generation via Multi-Scale Factor-Specific Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21364)
*Rejoy Chakraborty,Prasun Roy,Saumik Bhattacharya,Umapada Pal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoDeFont，一个与文字系统无关的一次性字体生成（OFG）框架，通过多层风格-内容解耦与渐进式协调，从单张参考字形中更准确地迁移字体风格并保持字符结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单张参考字形所含风格信息有限，难以准确捕捉字体风格；对结构复杂的文字系统（如孟加拉文）这一挑战更为突出。现有方法在风格-内容分离与信息利用的层次性上不足。

Method: 在多个空间层级上独立聚合并互补的风格与结构信息，得到因子专属表示；引入对比目标显式促使风格嵌入与内容嵌入分离；重建阶段通过多层风格条件化（multi-level style conditioning）渐进注入解耦表示，使风格信息在不同解码分辨率上参与生成，同时保持字形结构。框架与具体文字系统无关。

Result: 在结构差异显著的孟加拉文与拉丁文上，针对已见与未见字体风格进行评测，定量与定性分析均一致优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 多层风格-内容解耦结合渐进式协调可有效提升OFG在多样书写系统中的泛化能力与生成质量，验证了该框架的通用性与有效性。

Abstract: One-shot Font Generation (OFG) aims to synthesize a target glyph by transferring the stylistic characteristics of a single reference glyph while preserving the structure of the target character. However, accurately capturing font style from a single reference image remains challenging due to the limited stylistic information available in a single glyph, and this challenge is further amplified for complex scripts. We propose CoDeFont, a script-agnostic OFG framework that addresses this challenge through multi-level style-content factorization and progressive style-content reconciliation. CoDeFont independently aggregates complementary style and structural information across multiple spatial levels to obtain factor-specific representations, while a contrastive objective explicitly encourages separation between style and content embeddings. During reconstruction, the disentangled representations are progressively injected through multi-level style conditioning, enabling stylistic information to be incorporated at different decoding resolutions while preserving the underlying glyph structure. We evaluate CoDeFont on structurally distinct Bengali and Latin scripts across seen and unseen font styles. Extensive quantitative and qualitative analyses demonstrate consistent improvements over existing state-of-the-art methods, highlighting the effectiveness of the proposed framework for OFG across diverse writing systems.

</details>


### [60] [Semantic Latent Alignment for Flow Matching with Invertible Bi-Lipschitz Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21332)
*Felix Nützel,Mischa Dombrowski,Luca Hagen,Bernhard Kainz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种保持编码器/解码器冻结的可逆双Lipschitz潜在空间适配器，在离线对齐语义教师后固定，用于类条件流匹配生成，兼顾语义组织与原有压缩/重建几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练潜在空间在压缩、重建与下游生成器可学习坐标之间存在权衡；继续训练代价高，且缺乏新的对齐目标时往往无法改善下游生成。

Method: 保持编码器与解码器固定，仅改变二者之间的坐标：使用可逆的双Lipschitz适配器，离线对齐到语义教师（semantic teacher），并在类条件流匹配训练中冻结；可逆性保证与预训练自编码器兼容，双Lipschitz约束限制对齐对潜在几何的扭曲幅度。

Result: 在两个医学影像数据集上，受约束的潜在适配提升了特征指标、随机种子稳定性与反事实编辑强度，同时在下游分类上保持竞争力；相比之下，无约束适配虽仍可逆，却可能高度各向异性且敏感，说明控制几何畸变的重要性。

Conclusion: 受约束的事后适配（post-hoc adaptation）是一种参数高效、免调参的替代方案，可取代在生成训练中联合修改表示的做法。

Abstract: Pretrained latent spaces trade compression, reconstruction, and the learnability of the coordinates a downstream generator has to work in, which complicates adapting these spaces to different domains or tasks: continued training is expensive, and without a new alignment objective it often fails to improve downstream generation. We instead leave the encoder and decoder fixed and change only the coordinates between them, using an invertible bi-Lipschitz adapter aligned offline to a semantic teacher and frozen during class-conditional flow matching. Invertibility preserves compatibility with the pretrained autoencoder, while the bi-Lipschitz constraint limits how far alignment can distort its latent geometry. This provides a simple way to improve semantic organization while retaining the compression and reconstruction properties of the original latent space. On two medical imaging datasets, constrained latent adaptation improves feature metrics, seed stability and counterfactual edit strength, while remaining competitive on downstream classification. In contrast, unconstrained adaptation can become highly anisotropic and sensitive despite remaining invertible, highlighting the importance of controlling geometric distortion. Our results suggest that carefully constrained post-hoc adaptation offers a parameter-efficient and tuning-free alternative to jointly modifying the representation during generative training.

</details>


### [61] [TRACER: A Multimodal Harness for Patient Care in Hospitals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21266)
*Zane Durante,Paul Tang,Keshigeyan Chandrasegaran,Amit Kaushal,Shrinidhi Kowshika Lakshmikanth,Feolu Kolawole,Yu Jun Zheng,Yifan Geng,Shobhit Agarwal,Kevin Schulman,Juan Carlos Niebles,Ehsan Adeli,Arnold Milstein,Li Fei-Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRACER：无需训练的层次化多模态框架，将连续视频转为分钟级动作记录并与临床记录融合为小时级报告，LLM agent 通过工具检索该层次结构，实现动作检测与长时行为问答，并在大规模患者队列中发现谵妄与目标性活动的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 住院期间患者行为随时间演变，连续视频与临床记录互为补充；核心难点在于既要表征整段住院期间的行为，又要保留对细粒度视觉与临床证据的可访问性，同时避免在受保护健康数据上训练。

Method: 提出 TRACER，一个 training-free 多模态 harness：从视频构建分钟级动作记录，与临床记录结合生成小时级报告，形成层次化结构；为 LLM agent 配备工具，用于查询该层次、查看相关视频片段、检索临床记录。构建两个基准：397 段带标注的 1 分钟片段（15 类原子动作）；144 道经临床医生参与设计的多选题，基于两名 ICU 患者共 264 小时视频与临床记录。

Result: 动作检测准确率最高 84.8%，问答准确率 61.0%（同骨干最强基线为 49.3%）；在约 60,000 摄像头小时、280 名患者中，住院期间谵妄评估阳性者的目标性活动显著少于阴性者（p < 0.0001）。

Conclusion: 将连续视频与临床记录结合，可在不针对受保护健康数据训练的情况下，支持对个体患者及患者队列的行为随时间变化的理解。

Abstract: Continuous video and clinical records provide complementary information for understanding how patient behavior evolves over a hospital stay. A central challenge is how to represent behavior across the entire stay while preserving access to fine-grained visual and clinical evidence. We introduce TRACER, a training-free multimodal harness that constructs minute-level action records from video and combines them with clinical records into hourly reports. The harness equips an LLM agent with tools to query this hierarchy, inspect relevant video, and retrieve clinical records. To evaluate TRACER, we construct two benchmarks. The first contains 397 annotated one-minute clips spanning 15 atomic actions; the second poses 144 clinician-informed multiple-choice questions, grounded in 264 hours of video and clinical records from two ICU patients. TRACER achieves up to 84.8% accuracy for action detection and 61.0% QA accuracy versus 49.3% for the strongest baseline using the same backbone. Across 280 patients spanning approximately 60,000 camera-hours, TRACER identifies significantly less purposeful activity in patients with a positive delirium assessment during their stay than in those without (p < 0.0001). Our results show that combining continuous video with clinical records can support understanding of patient behavior over time and across patient cohorts without training on protected health data.

</details>


### [62] [VisualTrust: Video Reasoning under On-Screen Claim Framing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21159)
*Huachao Zhu,Junjielong Xu,Sicheng Li,Liang Yang,Mingtao Chen,Zhichao Hu,Li Jiang,Pinjia He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出VisualTrust反事实基准（5,063例/14类断言-事实类别），揭示多模态大模型存在"断言顺从"现象——屏幕误导性文字可覆盖冲突视觉证据，无断言准确率90.1%在误导断言下跌至51.9%。并提出免训练框架DEAR，通过证据仲裁与阅读解耦，将误导下的准确率提升至81.8–85.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准将"屏幕文字描述视频内容"与"屏幕文字框定视频含义"混为一谈，仅测试模型能否读取文字，而非是否应信任该文字。作者关注多模态大模型在屏幕断言与视觉证据冲突时倾向于顺从断言（claim deference）这一安全与可靠性风险，且缺乏针对性评测手段。

Method: 1) 构建VisualTrust反事实基准：每例含同一视频的三个版本（无断言、准确断言、仅改动单一可视觉验证事实的误导断言），误导断言所支持的选项设为陷阱答案；2) 评测7个开源与4个专有模型，分析准确率与答案翻转率；3) 提出DEAR（Decoupled Evidence Arbitration and Reading）免训练智能体：独立核查模块报告冲突证据，选择器在不预设模态优先级的情况下权衡证据并对被扣留信息实施遮蔽（blind），阅读器在全新上下文中基于所得视频作答，实现仲裁与阅读解耦。

Result: 11个模型平均准确率由无断言时的90.1%降至误导断言下的51.9%；在初始正确的回答中，42.8%翻转为陷阱答案，仅0.6%转为其他错误，说明错误高度定向；无断言下的高准确率不能保证抗误导能力。DEAR在三个专有模型、500个视频上，将误导断言下准确率从47.2–64.0%提升至81.8–85.2%，陷阱答案选择率从34.4–50.8%降至11.6–15.0%，同时保持无断言准确率不降。

Conclusion: 模型对屏幕断言的顺从是系统性、定向性缺陷，而非一般性能力不足，需以反事实冲突设计进行专门评测。将证据仲裁与阅读解耦、并在阅读阶段遮蔽被扣留证据的免训练框架，可显著缓解该问题且不损害基线性能，为可信视频问答提供可行路径。

Abstract: On-screen text can describe what a video shows, or it can frame what the video is supposed to mean. Current benchmarks treat both alike, testing whether a model can read the text rather than whether it should trust it. We study claim deference, multimodal large language models' tendency to let an on-screen claim override conflicting visual evidence. We introduce VisualTrust, a counterfactual benchmark with 5,063 cases across 14 claim-fact classes. Each case pairs a question with three versions of the same video: one with no claim, one with an accurate claim, and one with a misleading claim that alters a single grounded fact. The option the misleading claim supports is the trap answer. Across seven open-weight and four proprietary models, mean accuracy falls from 90.1% without a claim to 51.9% with a misleading claim. Among initially correct responses, 42.8% flip to the trap answer and only 0.6% become other errors. Strong no-claim accuracy does not ensure resistance. We also introduce DEAR (Decoupled Evidence Arbitration and Reading), a training-free agent for video question answering that separates evidence arbitration from reading. An independent check reports conflicting evidence, a selector weighs it without a fixed modality priority and blinds what it withholds, and the reader answers from the resulting video in a fresh context. On 500 videos across three proprietary models, DEAR raises accuracy under misleading claims from 47.2--64.0% to 81.8--85.2% and reduces trap-answer selection from 34.4--50.8% to 11.6--15.0%, while preserving no-claim accuracy.

</details>


### [63] [CoViG: Coarsened Vision GNN for Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21321)
*Martial Guidez,Stefan Duffner,Christophe Garcia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoViG通过在ViG中间块的Grapher组件中引入MinCUT池化进行图粗化，在ImageNet上以约51%的FLOPs取得与原始ViG相当的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉图网络（ViG）将图像视为节点集并在k-NN图上做图卷积，在图像分类上精度出色，但计算开销较高；而图神经网络领域的优化方法（如粗化）尚未被引入视觉图模型。

Method: 借鉴GNN优化思路，提出CoViG，将图粗化与ViG结合：在中间ViG块的Grapher组件内增加MinCUT池化步骤，简化中间图、减少特定块的节点数，从而降低推理时的计算量。并在ImageNet上对多种ViG架构进行评测。

Result: 在ImageNet上，多种ViG架构下CoViG达到与原ViG模型相当的精度，FLOPs仅约为原来的51%。

Conclusion: 图粗化（MinCUT池化）是一种有效的ViG计算降本手段，可在几乎不损失精度的前提下显著减少推理开销。

Abstract: Although Graph Neural Networks are widely used in various domains, they remained mostly unexplored in computer vision. Convolutional Neural Networks and Vision Transformers were leading the field, until the quite recent introduction of Vision Graphs (ViG) that treat images as a set of nodes and apply graph convolution on their k-NN graphs. ViGs show impressive accuracy on image classification but their computational cost remains relatively high. Inspired by optimization methods for GNNs, we present CoViG, a novel approach combining graph coarsening and ViGs. Our method introduces an additional MinCUT pooling step within the Grapher component of intermediate ViG blocks. By simplifying the intermediate graphs and thus decreasing the number of nodes of specific blocks, our approach considerably reduces the computational cost of ViG during inference. We evaluated our approach on ImageNet with several ViG architectures, and we obtained accuracies equivalent to the original ViG models with only about 51\% of FLOPs.

</details>


### [64] [ReState: Noise-Level-Aware State Correction for Training-Free Object Removal in Few-Step Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21287)
*Donghwan Lee,Sanghyun Jo,Sihyun Kim,Ziseok Lee,Kyungsu Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出免训练的目标移除方法 ReState，修正少步蒸馏扩散模型中掩码区域的预测状态，而非仅重定向注意力，在多个骨干与基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统免训练目标移除依赖将自注意力从目标区域引开，但在少步蒸馏扩散模型上常残留物体形状伪影。作者识别出"时间错配"问题：掩码内的结构化预测在首次前向即已形成，而短轨迹后续修正机会少，且注意力重定向主要作用于较晚步骤，因此无法及时纠正。

Method: 在四个蒸馏骨干上做幅度匹配的单次前向干预实验，发现有背景导向的状态修正比注意力重定向对最终结果影响更大。据此提出 ReState：用噪声水平感知的权重，将掩码处的干净预测向从输入中检索到的背景证据校正，且该权重在最后一次前向时衰减为零；同时保留排除目标的注意力作为互补约束，且不增加去噪器评估次数。

Result: 在两个配对基准和两个少步骨干上，ReState 在免训练方法中取得最佳 masked PSNR、SSIM 和 LPIPS，PSNR 平均比最强基线提升 3.17 dB；在 OBER-Test 上三项指标均超过 50 步 attention-forcing 参考方法，延迟降低 6.7 倍；同一配置无需调参即可迁移到另外两个蒸馏骨干。

Conclusion: 少步蒸馏扩散模型的目标移除瓶颈在于早期形成的掩码内预测状态，而非注意力路由；直接进行噪声水平感知的背景导向状态修正，是一种高效、免训练、可迁移的解决方案。

Abstract: Training-free object removal commonly redirects self-attention away from the target region, but on few-step distilled diffusion models this often leaves object-shaped residuals. We identify a temporal mismatch: a structured prediction forms inside the mask at the first forward, and the short trajectory offers few later chances to revise it, while attention rerouting acts mainly in later steps. Across four distilled backbones, magnitude-matched single-forward interventions show that background-directed state correction affects the final result more than attention rerouting. We therefore propose ReState, which corrects the masked clean prediction toward background evidence retrieved from the input using a noise-level-aware weight that vanishes at the final forward. ReState retains object-excluding attention as a complementary constraint and adds no denoiser evaluations. Across two paired benchmarks and two few-step backbones, ReState achieves the best masked PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS among training-free methods, improving PSNR by 3.17 dB on average over the strongest baseline. On OBER-Test, it also outperforms the 50-step attention-forcing reference on all three metrics at 6.7x lower latency. The same configuration transfers to two additional distilled backbones without retuning. Project page: https://tdrlee.github.io/ReState

</details>


### [65] [AdaLoop: Thinking with the Vision Encoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21323)
*Xinran Nicole Han,Yiyang Lu,Katelyn Gan,Todd Zickler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AdaLoop 提出面向问题的视觉循环计算框架：在冻结预训练视觉编码器权重的前提下，让视觉计算依问题自适应，并复用编码器变换进行多轮迭代，同时提出 VisRO 数据集与生成流程用于以视觉为中心的推理评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 普遍将视觉编码器视为静态特征提取器：图像仅被编码一次且与问题无关，后续推理全部交给语言模型。但不同问题需要不同的视觉操作与处理深度，这种一次性的、问题无关的编码方式限制了模型的视觉推理能力。

Method: 提出 AdaLoop 框架，实现问题条件化的视觉循环（question-conditioned visual recurrence）：视觉计算依据问题自适应调整，并在多次迭代中复用编码器自身的变换，且保持预训练视觉权重冻结。另构建 VisRO 程序化数据集与生成流水线，用于训练与评估以视觉为中心的推理任务。

Result: 在 VisRO 的六个任务上，在训练数据与优化器更新次数匹配的条件下，AdaLoop 优于标准微调与问题条件化单次前向基线；该提升在三个 VLM 模型家族、同分布与分布外样本上均成立。

Conclusion: 预训练视觉编码器可通过任务导向的迭代式视觉计算参与推理过程，而不仅是静态特征提取器，为 VLM 中视觉端的计算分配提供新思路。

Abstract: Most vision-language models (VLMs) treat the vision encoder as a static feature extractor: it encodes an image once, independently of the question, and leaves subsequent reasoning to the language model. Yet different questions may require different visual operations and processing depths. We present AdaLoop, a framework for question-conditioned visual recurrence in VLMs. AdaLoop adapts visual computation to the question and reuses the encoder's transformations across multiple iterations, with the pretrained vision weights frozen. We also introduce VisRO, a procedural dataset and generation pipeline for training and evaluating vision-centric reasoning. On six VisRO tasks, AdaLoop outperforms standard fine-tuning and question-conditioned single-pass baselines with matched training data and optimizer updates. These gains hold across three VLM families on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution examples. Our findings suggest that pretrained vision encoders can contribute to reasoning through task-directed, iterative visual computation.

</details>


### [66] [ZoomCap: Evidence-Routed Visual Zooming for Recoverable Visual Information Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21320)
*Hao Shi,Jiafeng Wang,Yuzhuo Fu,Tao Zhang,Jian Bai,Juelu Zhang,Qing Cui,Chao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ZoomCap：将细粒度描述建模为可恢复的视觉信息迁移，用草稿路由检索/OCR/覆盖缺口恢复/自适应缩放；并提FactCycle事实级评分，监督可迁移且提升下游预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长描述质量常被长度掩盖：单次长输出仍漏小目标、文字与空间关系，且把不确定观察写成确定断言。需要区分"信息是否真的被转移"而非"文本是否够长"。

Method: 形式化为有限观察预算下的可恢复视觉信息迁移：草稿描述用于路由条件知识检索、OCR敏感阅读、覆盖缺口恢复与自适应视觉缩放（仅对最复杂合格区域分配更深裁剪），由grounded几何推导空间关系，并融合非重复证据。评测端提出FactCycle，将描述分解为原子主张，在源图像（真实性）与重建（可实现性）两个盲锚上打分，逐条区分编造与生成器遗漏；辅以人工与多评委比较。

Result: 对比Qwen3.5-397B-A17B，以体积超10倍小的教师获得81.9%人工平局校正偏好分；FactCycle统计每图迁移主张52.4条 vs. ScaleCap 31.5。持续预训练中ZoomCap-450K使11基准均值提升1.31–2.38点；Ling-Tiny-VL-v3在60B tokens下文档/OCR监控套件+1.4（感知+1.5）；医学预训练2.64M图像使五个VQA基准+3.04点（同图Gemini-3.1-Pro描述仅+1.76）。

Conclusion: 细粒度描述的收益取决于证据可恢复与事实可验证，而非长度；事实级筛选与自适应证据获取能以小模型产出高质量、可复用监督，并稳定迁移到通用与领域预训练。

Abstract: Detailed captions transfer visual information into vision-language model supervision, but quality is easily conflated with length: long single-pass outputs still miss small objects, text, and spatial relations or promote uncertain observations into confident claims. We formulate fine-grained captioning as recoverable visual information transfer, in which a teacher acquires missing evidence under a finite observation budget and converts it into reusable supervision. ZoomCap uses a draft caption to route conditional knowledge retrieval, OCR-sensitive reading, coverage-gap recovery, and adaptive visual zooming, allocating deeper crops only to the most complex eligible region, deriving spatial relations from grounded geometry, and fusing non-duplicate evidence. We also introduce FactCycle, which scores caption-derived atomic claims on two blind anchors--source image (truthfulness) and reconstruction (realizability)--separating fabrication from generator loss claim by claim; human and multi-judge comparisons complement this fact-level view. Against Qwen3.5-397B-A17B captions, ZoomCap achieves an 81.9% human tie-adjusted preference score with a teacher over 10x smaller, and FactCycle counts 52.4 transferred claims per image vs. 31.5 for ScaleCap. The evidence also transfers: in continual pre-training, ZoomCap-450K improves the 11-benchmark average by 1.31-2.38 points over the strongest competing corpus and Ling-Tiny-VL-v3 gains 1.4 points on a production document/OCR monitor suite (1.5 on perception) at 60B tokens; in medical pre-training, 2.64M ZoomCap-captioned images improve five VQA benchmarks by 3.04 points (vs. 1.76 for same-image Gemini-3.1-Pro captions).

</details>


### [67] [Loud and Clear: Making Visual Evidence Count in Multimodal Document RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21318)
*Eunchan Jo,Seounghyun Jang,Sangwoo Mo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 多模态文档 RAG 的瓶颈不在检索而在生成器如何"读"页面：通过满足 Loud（用满视觉 token 容量）与 Clear（突出证据区域）两项需求，提出免训练、免标注的 LoC 方法，在 SciEGQA、ViDoRe-v3、MMLongBench-Doc 上以相同检索器与生成器超越 ColQwen3 达 8.88/3.09/4.22 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作集中在检索环节，但即使检索到正确页面，证据仍未被子充分利用。作者通过实验证明：页面的视觉 token 预算从 1K 提升到 32K 可带来 15.22 分提升；固定 16K 预算下加入真值裁剪区域还能再提升 3.75 分。说明生成器侧的页面输入方式是独立于检索的瓶颈。

Method: 将生成器对页面的需求抽象为两项：Loud——充分利用视觉 token 容量（提升 token 预算或视觉保真度/分辨率，整页缩放优于重复与切块）；Clear——精炼页面使证据突出（抑制周边内容、叠加查询相关区域的 OCR 文本）。据此实现 LoC：高分辨率整页图像 + 查询相关区域的 OCR 文本联合输入，无需真值标注与额外训练。

Result: 在相同检索器与生成器条件下，LoC 在 SciEGQA、ViDoRe-v3、MMLongBench-Doc 上优于八个先前方法，较最强基线 ColQwen3 分别提升 8.88、3.09、4.22 分；在相同视觉 token 预算下亦优于其 page-only 变体。

Conclusion: 多模态文档 RAG 的瓶颈已越过检索环节，转向"检索到的页面如何呈现给生成器"。同时满足 Loud 与 Clear 的简单输入重构即可显著提升准确率，提示后续工作应重视生成器侧的页面表征设计。

Abstract: Multimodal document retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has focused largely on retrieval, yet its answers depend on how well a vision-language model reads the retrieved pages. We find that evidence in retrieved pages remains underused even when the correct pages are retrieved. On SciEGQA oracle pages, five prior methods give the generator 0.7K to 2.7K visual tokens per query on average under a common image processor, yet raising the page-only budget from 1K to 32K improves accuracy by 15.22 points. At a fixed 16K budget, adding ground-truth crops improves accuracy by 3.75 points over page-only input. We study what the generator needs from retrieved pages through two requirements: Loud, making full use of visual token capacity, and Clear, refining the page so that its evidence becomes prominent. For Loud, increasing either the visual token budget or visual fidelity, the image resolution those tokens preserve, improves accuracy with the other held fixed, and full-page scaling outperforms repetition and tiling at matched budgets. For Clear, suppressing surrounding content improves accuracy without enlarging selected regions, and adding OCR of these regions to page images outperforms either input alone. We implement both as Loud and Clear (LoC), which combines higher-resolution pages with OCR from query-relevant regions without ground-truth annotations or additional training. LoC outperforms eight prior methods on SciEGQA, ViDoRe-v3, and MMLongBench-Doc, improving over the strongest, ColQwen3, by 8.88, 3.09, and 4.22 points with the same retriever and generator. It also outperforms its page-only variant under the same visual token budget. These results locate a bottleneck beyond retrieval: how the retrieved pages are given to the generator.

</details>


### [68] [GeoPano: Towards Geometrically Accurate 3D Reconstruction from a Single Panorama](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21297)
*Jing OU,Zidong Cao,Liaoyuan Fan,Zhuoxiao Li,Shuai Zhang,Yinrui Ren,Dongli Wu,Haoang Li,Hui Xiong,Wufan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoPano：单视图全景前馈式3D重建框架，通过Pano-Attention聚合器与混合监督，抑制跨视图几何不一致，从单张全景图恢复几何精确且一致的点云。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全景3D重建可为机器人与AR/VR提供完整360°场景几何，但ERP图像存在球面畸变；常见做法是将全景分解为无畸变透视视图，但独立处理会引入跨视图几何不一致问题。

Method: 提出GeoPano前馈框架，包含两项创新：(1) 全景感知注意力聚合器，采用精简注意力架构并引入Pano-Attention机制，显式利用全景固有球面几何引导跨视图交互以抑制几何不一致；(2) 混合监督策略，在透视空间约束细粒度局部细节，同时在球面空间约束全局结构一致性。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在重建精度、几何一致性与视觉保真度上显著优于此前SOTA方法。

Conclusion: GeoPano验证了显式建模球面几何并联合透视-球面监督可有效解决全景重建中的畸变与一致性问题，实现单视图全景的高质量点云重建。

Abstract: Panoramic 3D reconstruction enables the holistic recovery of complete 360 $^\circ$ scene geometry, offering significant benefits for robotics and AR/VR applications. However, directly estimating geometry from ERP images is inherently challenged by spherical distortions. To reduce spherical projection distortions, a common strategy is to decompose panoramas into distortion-free perspective views, but independently processing these views often introduces cross-view geometric inconsistencies. To address these challenges, we introduce GeoPano, a feed-forward framework for single-view panoramic 3D reconstruction that recovers a geometrically accurate and consistent point cloud from a single panorama. Our approach is driven by two key innovations: First, we propose a panoramic-aware attention aggregator, featuring a streamlined attention architecture equipped with a novel Pano-Attention mechanism, which explicitly exploits the intrinsic spherical geometry of the panorama to guide cross-view interactions, thereby suppressing geometric inconsistencies. Furthermore, we formulate a hybrid supervision strategy to jointly enforce fine-grained local details in perspective space and global structural coherence in spherical space. Experiments demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms previous SOTA approaches in reconstruction accuracy, geometric consistency, and visual fidelity.

</details>


### [69] [Seeing Both the Forest and the Trees: Reusing Holistic 3D Priors for Part-Decomposed Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21850)
*Yiqun Zhao,Binbin Huang,Haobin Duan,Zibo Zhao,Shenghua Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STRUCTPARTS：双坐标系交错机制使部件分解3D生成同时继承整体先验的部件内几何细节与全局结构连贯性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部件级3D资产生成需同时具备部件内几何细节与整体结构连贯性，现有图像条件3D生成模型依托锚定空间位置的结构化3D表示已能兼顾二者，但输出为单一融合网格，无法分解为部件。核心问题是：部件分解模型如何继承整体式先验的这两项属性。

Method: 提出STRUCTPARTS框架，核心技术为双坐标系交错机制：每个形状隐变量同时在部件局部与物体全局参考系下解释，并通过全局-局部交错Transformer处理，在部件尺度复用先验的几何细节、在整体尺度复用其结构连贯性。结构输入采用分段网格脚手架（粗网格+面级分割掩码），完全保持先验的原生3D表示；仅需轻量微调。

Result: 在PartObjaverse-Tiny上，STRUCTPARTS以远低于先前部件分解模型的训练成本达到相当或更优性能，并天然支持部件网格细化与部件级编辑。

Conclusion: 通过在原生结构化表示上引入双坐标系交错与分段网格脚手架，部件分解生成可从同一预训练权重中同时涌现部件内细节与部件间连贯性，实现低成本的部件级生成、细化与编辑。

Abstract: Generating 3D assets with explicit parts requires two properties at once: geometric detail inside each part and structural coherence across the whole object. Recent image-conditioned 3D generation models adopt a structured 3D representation that anchors tokens to explicit spatial positions, capturing both properties, but their output remains a single fused mesh. We ask how a part-decomposed generation model can inherit both properties of these holistic priors, and answer with STRUCTPARTS, a framework whose core technical contribution is a dual-frame coordinate interleaving mechanism: each shape latent is interpreted under both a part-local and an object-global reference frame, and processed through a global-local inter-leaved transformer that simultaneously reuses the prior's geometric detail at the part scale and its structural coherence at the whole-object scale. As its structural input, STRUCTPARTS uses a segmented mesh scaffold, a coarse mesh paired with a face-level segmentation mask kept entirely within the prior's native 3D representation. With only lightweight finetuning, both intra-part detail and inter-part coherence emerge from the same pretrained weights. On PartObjaverse-Tiny, STRUCTPARTS matches or surpasses prior part-decomposed models at a fraction of their training cost, while naturally supporting part-mesh refinement and part-level editing.

</details>


### [70] [Teaching DINOv3 About Partial 3D Geometry: A Self-Supervised Geometry-Aware Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21802)
*Viktoria Ehm,Dongliang Cao,Riccardo Marin,Daniel Scholz,Weikang Wang,Florian Bernard,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.CV
