TL;DR: 提出基于LoRA自监督学习精炼DINO特征的方法，生成对部分性鲁棒的特征描述子，用于部分形状匹配并达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分形状匹配是3D视觉中重要但研究不足的问题，现实中形状常只能被部分观测。现有特征描述子在部分形状场景下表现不佳：传统表示难以处理部分形状边界，或过度依赖形状的空间位置；已有的DINO特征本身并不适合处理部分观测。

Method: 采用基于LoRA的自监督学习对DINO特征进行精炼，使其能够生成对部分性（partiality）鲁棒的特征描述子；并将该特征集成到现有的部分形状匹配流程中。

Result: 相比传统特征或视觉基础模型特征，该方法在部分形状匹配上显著提升性能；集成到现有流程后在部分形状匹配与左右（left-right）预测基准上取得state-of-the-art结果。

Conclusion: 通过LoRA自监督精炼DINO特征可有效增强描述子的部分性鲁棒性，为部分形状匹配提供了一种有效且可即插即用的解决方案。

Abstract: Partial shape matching is a crucial yet underexplored problem in 3D vision, with significant relevance to real-world scenarios where shapes are often only partially observed. Existing feature descriptors face difficulties in this setting, as traditional representations either struggle with the boundaries of partial shapes or heavily depend on the shape's spatial position. While existing approaches have employed DINO features for partial shape matching, these features are not inherently suited for handling partial observations. In this work, we propose a method to refine DINO features using LoRA-based self-supervised learning, enabling the generation of feature descriptors that are robust to partiality. Our features substantially improve performance on partial shape matching compared to traditional or vision foundation features. Additionally, when integrated into existing partial shape matching pipelines, we achieve state-of-the-art results on partial shape matching and left-right prediction benchmarks.

</details>


### [71] [Mixture of Multi-Sequence Feature Difference for Contrast-Enhanced MRI Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21703)
*Jonghun Kim,Junmo Kwon,Hyunjin Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于特征差异混合模块（MoFD）的非对比序列CE-MRI合成方法，用于降低对比剂风险与成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CE-MRI对脑肿瘤诊断与分割至关重要，但需注射对比剂，存在健康风险并增加扫描成本；现有合成方法多用通道级拼接融合多序列信息，未能充分利用序列间的显式差异。

Method: 从非对比序列合成CE-MRI；设计Mixture of Feature Difference (MoFD) 模块，采用动态加权策略，自适应选择并聚合来自各单一源序列的特征及其显式差异特征。

Result: 在UCSF-PDGM与UPenn-GBM数据集上，定量与定性评估均优于基线（图像保真度与感知质量）；成功重建肿瘤区域，基于合成CE-MRI的分割性能与真实CE-MRI相当；消融显示对抗学习提升感知质量，而回归变体具有更高结构保真度。

Conclusion: 多序列特征差异的动态加权融合可有效提升CE-MRI合成质量，兼具临床可用性与成本效益，感知质量与结构保真度之间存在权衡。

Abstract: Contrast-enhanced magnetic resonance imaging (CE-MRI) is crucial for accurately diagnosing and segmenting brain tumors. However, the acquisition of CE-MRI requires injecting contrast agents, which poses potential health risks and increases scan costs. Here, we propose a CE-MRI synthesis method from non-contrast sequences, reducing both risk and cost. Unlike existing methods that rely on channel-wise concatenation to fuse multi-sequence information for synthesis, we introduce a Mixture of Feature Difference (MoFD) module. The proposed module uses a dynamic weighting strategy to adaptively select and aggregate informative features from both individual source sequences and explicit differences among them. Quantitative and qualitative evaluations demonstrate that our proposed method outperforms baselines in terms of image fidelity and perceptual quality on the UCSF-PDGM and UPenn-GBM datasets. Our method successfully reconstructs tumor regions and achieves segmentation performance comparable to that obtained with real CE-MRI. Our ablation studies show that although adversarial learning improves perceptual quality, a regression-based variant of our method achieves higher structural fidelity.

</details>


### [72] [FilterFusion: Prior-Guided Adaptive Filter Fusion for Efficient Video Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21505)
*Wenlong Yang,Weihang Yuan,Canran Jin,Jiahao Li,Henrik Turbell,Lifeng Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FilterFusion：先验引导的多帧视频去噪框架，用低分辨率控制器预测引导信号，自适应融合一组可解释滤波分支，仅109K参数，在CRVD上34.72dB PSNR/0.9330 SSIM、0.18 GFLOPs，编译C实现在单CPU线程1080p下达17.9ms/帧（55.9 FPS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通信系统场景下的真实视频去噪需同时应对传感器噪声、压缩伪影与运动不一致等混合退化，并满足严格时延预算。纯深度模型精度高但计算开销大，经典滤波高效鲁棒却自适应不足，亟需兼顾质量、效率与速度的方案。

Method: 提出先验引导的多帧去噪框架FilterFusion：将时序感知的轻量学习特征与可解释去噪分支库相结合；模型预测低成本引导信号，在原始分辨率上自适应混合少量去噪响应；低分辨率控制器显式注入时序方差与空间方差先验；合成路径仅含高斯模糊、中值滤波、加权求和等简单规整算子，便于编译为C实现。

Result: 参数量仅109K，网络计算量0.18 GFLOPs；CRVD真实传感器噪声数据集上PSNR 34.72 dB、SSIM 0.9330；合成路径的单线程CPU C实现在1080p下17.9ms/帧（55.9 FPS），超过实时，且比需每帧2秒以上的轻量CNN基线快122–252倍。

Conclusion: 通过低分辨率控制与显式滤波合成的组合，实现了质量—效率—速度的良好权衡，为实时视频去噪提供了可落地的工程路径。

Abstract: Real-world video denoising for communication systems must handle mixed degradations such as sensor noise, compression artifacts, and motion-induced inconsistency while remaining deployable under tight latency budgets. Purely deep models often achieve high accuracy but come with significant computational cost, whereas classical filters are robust and efficient but insufficiently adaptive. We present FilterFusion, a prior-guided multi-frame denoising framework that combines temporally-aware learned features with an interpretable bank of denoising branches. The model predicts a low-cost guidance signal and uses it to adaptively mix a compact set of denoising responses at the original resolution. The lightweight design uses only 109K parameters and explicitly injects temporal variance and spatial variance cues into the low-resolution controller. On CRVD (real sensor noise), it reaches 34.72 dB PSNR and 0.9330 SSIM with only 0.18 GFLOPs (network-only). Because the filter-bank operations are simple and regular (Gaussian blur, median, weighted sum), a straightforward compiled C implementation of the synthesis path achieves 17.9ms per frame (55.9 FPS) on a single CPU thread at 1080p---exceeding real-time and 122--252 times faster than lightweight CNN baselines that require over 2 seconds per frame. The results demonstrate a strong quality-efficiency-speed trade-off and a practical route to real-time denoising through low-resolution control and explicit filter-based synthesis.

</details>


### [73] [Cross-Prompt Inference Mitigates Rare-Class Suppression in Imbalanced VLM Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21470)
*Emanuel Ben Baruch,Sivan Biham,Alon Shoshan,Yochai Zvik,Avi Ben-Cohen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在类别不平衡数据上微调的视觉-语言模型存在"匹配提示惩罚"：推理时改用与微调不同的描述性提示（跨提示推理）可稳定恢复稀有类召回，其机制是减少对微调引入计算的依赖、回归预训练成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调后的VLMs在类别不平衡数据上会丧失稀有类召回，甚至低于其自身零样本水平。作者希望探究推理阶段提示选择这一简单设计是否能够挽回被抑制的稀有类知识，并给出机制解释。

Method: 在六个数据集、多种VLM上系统比较"匹配提示"（复用微调时的描述性提示）与"跨提示推理"（改用另一描述性提示）；通过控制非描述性提示变化、分析不平衡程度与视觉混淆度的影响做归因；并引入Tuning Contribution（TuCo）将模型计算分解为预训练部分与微调部分，考察跨提示推理对二者贡献的重分配，以及削减微调贡献后的效应变化。

Result: 跨提示推理一致性地恢复稀有类召回，但对常见类召回存在不同程度的权衡；该效应源于训练-评估提示交互而非单纯提示质量，且非描述性提示替换不产生该效应；效应在类别不平衡与视觉混淆导致显著稀有类压制时最强，无压制时微弱或缺失；TuCo分析显示跨提示推理降低对微调诱导计算的依赖、转向预训练成分，且削减微调贡献会显著削弱跨提示优势。

Conclusion: 被不平衡微调压制的稀有类知识并未丢失，而是在不同推理提示下仍可被访问；推理提示的选择是与训练提示相交互的关键变量，提示层面的干预可作为抑制微调负迁移的轻量手段。

Abstract: When a vision-language model is finetuned on class-imbalanced data, rare-class recall can fall well below the model's own zero-shot performance. In this regime, we uncover a surprising matched-prompt penalty whereby the standard choice of reusing the descriptive finetuning prompt at inference can yield lower rare-class recall than using a different descriptive prompt. We call this inference-time strategy cross-prompt inference. We evaluate diverse VLMs across six datasets and find that cross-prompt inference consistently recovers rare-class recall, with varying trade-offs in frequent-class recall. The effect reflects a train-eval prompt interaction rather than prompt quality alone, and does not arise with non-descriptive prompt changes. It is strongest when imbalance and visual confusability produce substantial rare-class suppression, and is weak or absent when such suppression does not occur. Using Tuning Contribution (TuCo), which decomposes a model's computation into pretrained and finetuning parts, we find that cross-prompt inference reduces reliance on finetuning-induced computation and shifts the model toward its pretrained component. Reducing that finetuning contribution also substantially reduces the cross-prompt advantage. These results show that rare-class knowledge suppressed by imbalanced finetuning can remain accessible under a different inference prompt.

</details>


### [74] [Perception or Reasoning? Diagnosing and Tackling Reinforcement Learning Bottlenecks in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21380)
*Zeyuan Yang,Zhuohao Chen,Jiaben Chen,Yao Zhang,Maohao Shen,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出PARA（感知与推理解耦归因）指标，量化各模型-基准对的感知余量与推理余量，发现现有RL对推理余量利用更充分；据此提出GRAD——组相对注意力蒸馏目标，用组内最佳rollout直接引导弱rollout的视觉注意力，在2个规模、7个基准上稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习显著提升了语言模型推理能力，但在视觉任务上的收益不稳定。根本难点在于视觉任务失败来源混杂：同一基准对某模型可能受限于感知，对另一模型却受限于推理，导致RL增益难以归因与针对性优化。

Method: 提出PARA归因度量：对每个模型-基准对，分别以"更准确的视觉信息"与"更强的推理器"替换，比较当前错误被挽回的比例，从而估计感知余量与推理余量；在4个VLM、17个多模态基准上验证。进而分析现有RL目标（奖励级与token级信号）对视觉感知的影响，发现二者仅间接作用于视觉注意力，故设计GRAD：组相对注意力蒸馏，以组内最佳rollout的视觉注意力作为教师信号直接监督较弱rollout。

Result: PARA整体与既有基准的预期特性吻合，同时揭示显著的模型特异性差异；关键发现是感知余量越大RL增益越小，推理余量解释的可挽回错误越多RL增益越大。GRAD在两个模型规模、七个基准上一致提升性能，并与现有RL方法互补叠加。

Conclusion: 视觉RL增益不一致的根源在于感知余量未被有效利用，而非缺乏可学习的感知空间；将组内相对优势施加于注意力层面（GRAD）可弥补这一缺口，为视觉推理RL提供了对感知更直接的优化路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has brought strong gains to language model reasoning, but its improvements on visual tasks remain less consistent. One difficulty is that visual task failures can arise from different sources: the same benchmark may be limited by perception for one model and by reasoning for another. We introduce Perception and Reasoning Attribution (PARA), which measures the remaining perceptual and reasoning headroom of each model--benchmark pair by comparing how many current errors are recovered with more accurate visual information versus a stronger reasoner. Across 4 vision-language models and 17 multimodal benchmarks, PARA broadly agrees with the expected characteristics of existing benchmarks while revealing substantial model-specific variation. More importantly, it shows that RL gains become smaller as perceptual headroom increases, but larger when reasoning explains more of the recoverable errors, suggesting that current RL exploits reasoning headroom more effectively than perceptual headroom. Motivated by this gap, we analyze how existing RL objectives affect visual perception and find that reward- and token-level signals influence visual attention only indirectly. We therefore propose GRAD, a group-relative attention distillation objective that uses the best rollout in each group to directly guide the visual attention of weaker rollouts. GRAD consistently improves performance across two model scales and seven benchmarks, and further complements existing RL methods.

</details>


### [75] [RIGen: Beyond Action Following with Reciprocal Interaction in Generative Gameplay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20811)
*Mengfei Li,Yi Zhang,Xingtong Ge,Lunjie Zhu,Fangyu Lin,Peng Li,Guanglu Song,Yu Liu,Wei Xue,Wenhan Luo,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RIG（Reciprocally Interactive Generation）框架，将持久状态与可配置规则、上下文交互推理、动作条件化音视频生成三者结合，实现具有持久后果与自主世界行为的生成式游戏交互，并基于Xonotic数据集构建了交互式生成FPS原型RIG-Arena。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成式游戏仅能捕捉局部动态，缺乏持久历史、游戏状态仅被部分观测，且需进行上下文相关的行为决策，因此仅靠音视频预测难以维持长期交互：动作缺乏持久后果，生成世界无法按自身状态与规则自主行动。

Method: RIG框架由三部分构成：(1) 持久化状态与可配置规则；(2) 上下文交互推理，用于行为决策；(3) 动作条件化的音视频生成。三者联合使"交互后果"与"角色行为"共同决定生成内容，并保持游戏状态与可见结果一致。为此构建Xonotic第一人称射击游戏数据集，训练音视频生成器以学习动态与交互，并经进一步后训练得到交互式原型RIG-Arena。

Result: 实验表明，RIG-Arena在战斗中产生更可靠的战斗结果，并实现了玩家与生成世界之间的双向（互惠）交互。

Conclusion: 将显式状态与规则、上下文推理和动作条件化生成相结合，可突破纯音视频预测在长期一致性与自主世界行为上的局限，为可交互、后果持久的生成式游戏提供可行路径。

Abstract: Generative gameplay requires more than immediate responses to player actions.Actions should have persistent consequences, while the generated world should act according to its own state and rules, shaping the player's situation and subsequent choices. Video generation captures local dynamics, but limited history, partially observed game state, and the need for contextual behavior decisions make such interactions difficult to sustain through audiovisual prediction alone. We introduce \rig (Reciprocally Interactive Generation), a framework for reciprocal interaction in generative gameplay. \rig combines persistent state and configurable rules, contextual interaction reasoning, and action-conditioned audiovisual generation. Together, these components make interaction consequences and actor behavior jointly determine generated content while maintaining consistency between game state and visible outcomes. We construct a Xonotic gameplay dataset to train an audiovisual generator for learning dynamics and interactions. Further post-training enables \rigarena, an interactive generative first-person shooter (FPS) prototype. Experiments demonstrate more reliable combat outcomes and reciprocal interaction between the player and the generated world. Project page: https://murphylmf.github.io/RIGen/.

</details>


### [76] [ReasonUni: Reasoning-Augmented Reconstruction Advances Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20781)
*Feize Wu,Zhiyuan Yan,Yuhuan Hong,Yuwei Niu,Zongjian Li,Zheyuan Liu,Bin Lin,Jinbo Yan,Jie Qin,Xudong Mao,Limeng Qiao,Guanglu Wan,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReasonUni，一种推理增强的重构式后训练框架，将I2T理解与T2I生成从"感知层面"的耦合扩展到"推理层面"；并设计树状强化学习方法TreeGRPO，在统一目标下联合优化两个模块，在多项基准上同时提升理解与生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于重构的后训练方法将I2T与T2I耦合，但方法仍以感知为中心，对结构、意图与变换等推理能力的监督有限。作者希望把这种耦合从感知扩展到推理，使理解与生成获得真正的双向增益。

Method: 给定原始图像与编辑指令，统一多模态模型先推理"编辑什么、如何编辑"并描述目标状态，再以该理解输出为条件生成目标图像。为联合优化两个模块，提出TreeGRPO：一种树状结构的强化学习方法，对每条推理路径采样多条推理路径与多张候选图像，聚合视觉奖励，在统一目标下同时更新理解与生成模块。

Result: 在多项基准上的实验表明，理解与生成任务均获得一致性提升，验证推理增强的重构能为UMM带来真实的相互增益。

Conclusion: 将重构式后训练从感知层面推进到推理层面，并以树状RL统一优化理解与生成，是一种有效的UMM后训练范式，可实现两个方向任务的协同增益。

Abstract: Image-to-text (I2T) understanding and text-to-image (T2I) generation are dual tasks in unified multimodal models (UMMs). Reconstructive post-training couples these tasks, but existing reconstruction-based methods remain perception-centric and provide limited supervision for reasoning about structure, intent, and transformation. We propose ReasonUni, a reasoning-augmented reconstructive post-training framework that extends this coupling beyond perception. Given an original image and an editing instruction, the UMM first reasons about what and how to edit and describes the target state, then generates the corresponding target image conditioned on this understanding output. To jointly optimize both modules, we introduce TreeGRPO, a tree-structured reinforcement learning method that samples multiple reasoning paths and candidate images per path, then aggregates visual rewards to update both modules under a unified objective. Experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate consistent gains in both understanding and generation, showing that reasoning-augmented reconstruction yields genuine mutual gains for UMMs.

</details>


### [77] [Towards automatic digital garment assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20651)
*Alejandro Rodriguez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两种基于学习的方法，仅以二维矢量纸样为输入，自动完成缝合定义与样板摆放，实现服装的自动数字化装配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从现有二维纸样生成数字服装仍耗时费力：尽管纸样已普遍以数字形式存在，但在服装CAD软件中装配仍需手工定义缝合操作并排布样板以进行悬垂仿真。

Method: 缝合模型在片段（segment）级别运作，采用带多预测头的Transformer编码器，支持多对多、有序的片段对应关系，并可处理贴边、多层服装所需的搭接缝，以及缝份、线迹类型等构造属性；互补的样板（panel）级模型预测身体区域归属、层与摆放坐标以初始化悬垂仿真。两者均在4,035个专业装配服装实例数据集上评估。

Result: 缝合模型在63.3%的测试服装上精确恢复全部缝合与贴边拓扑；排布模型在66.2%的测试服装上满足全部样板的摆放准则。两方法已集成进服装CAD软件，可自动装配预测成功的服装。

Conclusion: 以二维矢量纸样为唯一输入的学习式流水线可端到端自动化缝合与排布，并首次纳入缝份、线迹类型等此前被忽视的构造属性，具备集成到实际CAD工作流的可行性。

Abstract: Creating digital garments from existing two-dimensional patterns remains a time-consuming process. Although patterns are widely available in digital form, their assembly in garment CAD software involves manual definition of sewing operations and arrangement of the pattern panels for drape simulation. We present two learning-based approaches that automate both tasks using the 2D vector pattern as their sole input. Our automatic sewing model operates at the segment level, leveraging a Transformer encoder with multiple prediction heads. Our novel architecture supports many-to-many, ordered segment correspondences, as well as hems, overlapping seams needed for multilayer garments, and construction properties such as seam allowances and stitch types, required for the finished virtual garment but neglected by previous methods. A complementary panel-level model predicts body-region assignments, layers, and placement coordinates to initialize the drape simulation. We evaluate both models on a curated dataset of 4,035 professionally assembled garment instances. The sewing model exactly recovers all seam and hem topologies of 63.3\% of test garments, while the arrangement model meets the specified placement criteria for all panels in 66.2\% of test garments. We also integrate both methods into garment CAD software, demonstrating automatic assembly of successfully predicted garments.

</details>


### [78] [Blame Errant Words, Not the Canvas: Defect-Aware Spatial Credit Assignment for Visual Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20794)
*Sujie Hu,Huaisong Zhang,Pujing Yang,Qian Wang,Xiaomin Li,Xinhang Leng,Chen Li,Jing Lyu,Xiu Li,Ling Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TextDART缺陷感知区域奖励模型与SCANNER区域化策略反馈，实现细粒度文本生成奖励，在文本准确性与字形保真上达SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习视觉文本生成存在两个耦合局限：基于识别的奖励忽视可见字形缺陷；整图标量反馈掩盖了各文本区域质量不均。核心原则为"归咎错误单词而非整幅画布"，以保留局部质量差异。

Method: 1) TextDART：缺陷感知的区域定位奖励模型，定位单个文本区域并独立评估其语义正确性与字形质量，提供区域精确反馈；2) SCANNER：将区域判断转化为空间差异化策略反馈，对每个文本目标在采样图像间归一化奖励，并将区域优势投射到对应latent token上。

Result: 在现有基准及新构建的PolyText-Bench上，方法在文本准确性和字形保真度上达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 以区域级奖励替代整图标量反馈可有效提升文本渲染准确性与字形质量，并贡献了PolyText-Bench基准。

Abstract: Recent reinforcement learning approaches have improved visual text generation, yet generated images still suffer from incorrect wording and malformed glyphs. We identify two coupled limitations: recognition-based rewards often overlook visible glyph defects, while whole-image scalar feedback obscures the uneven quality of individual text regions. These limitations motivate our central principle: $\textit{Blame errant words, not the canvas}$, to effectively preserve local quality distinctions. First, we introduce $\mathbf{TextDART}$, a defect-aware region-targeted reward model that localizes individual text regions and independently evaluates their semantic correctness and glyph quality, providing fine-grained, region-precise feedback. Building on TextDART, we propose $\mathbf{SCANNER}$, which turns these regional judgments into spatially differentiated policy feedback. It normalizes rewards for each text target across sampled images and casts the resulting regional advantages onto the corresponding latent tokens. Extensive experiments on existing benchmarks and our newly established $\textbf{PolyText-Bench}$ demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance in text accuracy and glyph fidelity. Project page: https://sujiehu.github.io/textdart-scanner

</details>


### [79] [Probing Negation Understanding in Vision-Language Models With Object Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20852)
*Beth Pearson,Michael Wray,Martha Lewis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文通过对比模型注意力与人类注视，研究视觉语言模型（VLMs）如何处理否定，发现模型并非通过类人注意力机制处理否定，且存在肯定/否定性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 否定是语言理解的基本要素，需确保VLMs能有效处理否定；但模型处理否定的内部机制尚不清楚，尤其是注意力是否承担该功能。

Method: 分析不同VLM的注意力模式，将模型的对象级注意力与人类眼动注视进行对比（二元与多元设定，合成与真实数据集）；开展逐层（layerwise）分析与逐头（headwise）消融；并用下游任务评估肯定与否定问题的表现差异。

Result: 模型整体上不具备类人的否定注意力模式，暗示其依赖注意力之外的机制；PaliGemma2-3B在否定下将对象注意力转移到替代对象，但逐头消融表明这是副产物而非否定处理机制；LLaVA-1.5-7B存在对左侧对象的空间偏置；所有模型在肯定与否定问题上均存在性能差距。

Conclusion: VLM的否定处理并非由注意力机制主导，需更深入理解否定的底层处理过程以缩小肯定/否定的性能差距。

Abstract: Negation is a fundamental aspect of language understanding and it is important vision-language models (VLMs) are able to process it effectively. We investigate how VLMs process negation by analysing attention patterns of different models. We draw comparisons between human gaze and attention in models when processing affirmative vs negated statements. We compare object level model attention to human eye-fixations in both binary and multary settings, evaluating with both synthetic and realistic datasets. We find models generally do not follow human-like attention patterns when resolving negation, suggesting they rely on mechanisms outside of attention. While layerwise analysis shows that PaliGemma2-3B shifts object attention to the alternative under negation, our headwise ablations suggest this is a byproduct rather than a negation processing mechanism. Additionally, we find that LLaVA-1.5-7B displays a spatial bias towards left-side objects. Our evaluation of models on downstream tasks shows that all models have a discrepancy between affirmative and negated questions highlighting the need to better understand the underlying processing of negation to close this performance gap. Code available at https://github.com/otmive/negation_attention

</details>


### [80] [Beyond the Dominant Label: Recovering Coexisting Lesion Evidence with Multiple Instance Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22091)
*Lubin Gan,Jing Zhang,Xinming Zhang,Chengpeng Xu,Siying Wu,Xiaoyan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出DomSet-MIL框架，将全切片图像中"仅标注最严重诊断"的弱监督场景形式化为dominant-label多示例学习，并通过感知标注机制的标签建模，在提升主诊断预测的同时更好恢复共存病灶证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全切片图像中常存在并存病灶，但弱监督仅提供最严重的那一个诊断标签。标准多类MIL将其视为完整的one-hot描述，导致当低严重度病灶与主导病灶共存时，其证据被抑制，临床上有意义的次要发现被丢弃。

Method: 提出标签感知的DomSet-MIL框架，在bag与instance层面统一对齐标注机制：(1) Dominant-Label Censored Likelihood，对报告诊断建模而不将未报告病灶声明为不存在；(2) Severity-Ordered Residual Routing，按严重度从高到低分配patch证据，并为次要发现保留残差支持；(3) top-q池化，将局部路由分数与bag预测关联。

Result: 在自然与受控实验设置下，DomSet-MIL在主导标签预测上达到state-of-the-art性能，同时更有效地恢复潜在的共存病灶证据。

Conclusion: 对主导标签机制的显式建模可在不牺牲临床有意义次要发现的前提下提升诊断预测，说明correctly处理标注censoring机制对弱监督病理MIL具有重要价值。

Abstract: Whole-slide images may contain coexisting lesions, yet weak supervision typically provides only the most severe diagnosis. We formulate this setting as dominant-label multiple instance learning, where the observed label is the maximum-severity element of a latent lesion set. Standard multiclass MIL treats it as a complete one-hot description and therefore suppresses lower-severity evidence whenever it co-occurs with a dominant lesion. We propose DomSet-MIL, a label-aware framework that aligns the annotation mechanism across bag and instance levels. Dominant-Label Censored Likelihood models the reported diagnosis without declaring unreported lesions absent. Severity-Ordered Residual Routing assigns patch evidence from high to low severity and preserves residual support for secondary findings, while top-$q$ pooling links local routing scores to bag prediction. Experiments across natural and controlled settings show that DomSet-MIL achieves state-of-the-art performance in dominant-label prediction while more effectively recovering latent coexisting-lesion evidence. These findings demonstrate that modeling the dominant-label mechanism can improve diagnostic prediction without sacrificing clinically meaningful secondary findings.

</details>


### [81] [Memory as Weights: Internalizing Long-Term History for Streaming Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22070)
*Zhuoming Liu,Jinhong Lin,Lin Zhang,Yiwu Zhong,Yin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MaLoW，将流式视频历史内化为持续更新的轻量 LoRA 权重，而非回填上下文，并构建 MaLoW-Instruct 数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式 VLM 记忆机制依赖压缩、检索或缓存历史观测再回注输入，导致历史记忆与当前观测争夺有限上下文；需要让历史不占上下文而被携带。

Method: 用循环 HyperNetwork 将流入的视频观测增量地固化到固定尺寸 LoRA 权重中，历史以权重形式表示、当前观测仍作为输入；为此构建 381K QA 对、56K 视频的 MaLoW-Instruct 训练集。

Result: 在 OVO-Bench、StreamingBench、StreamArena 上表现强劲；OVO-Bench 后向子集上较先前记忆方法提升 3.9%–11.8%（不同视频骨干），延迟开销适中；迁移至 VLA 模型使 Simpler WidowX 成功率提升 9.3%。

Conclusion: 以参数化记忆替代上下文回注可缓解流式场景的记忆-观测竞争，且具跨任务泛化性，代价为少量延迟。

Abstract: Streaming vision-language models (VLMs) must continuously integrate information from the past while remaining responsive to current observations and user queries. Existing memory mechanisms typically compress, retrieve, or cache historical observations and reintroduce them into the input context, forcing historical memory and current observations to compete for limited context. We propose MaLoW, a streaming memory framework that instead internalizes historical context into evolving lightweight parameter updates. MaLoW uses a recurrent HyperNetwork to incrementally consolidate incoming video observations into fixed-size LoRA weights, allowing historical information to be carried forward without revisiting the full video stream. In doing so, MaLoW represents history as weights, while keeping the current observation as input. To support training, we further construct MaLoW-Instruct, a large-scale streaming video dataset containing 381K question-answer pairs over 56K videos. We conduct extensive experiments to demonstrate that MaLoW achieves strong performance on OVO-Bench, StreamingBench, and StreamArena. On the backward split of OVO-Bench, MaLoW outperforms prior memory approaches by 3.9% to 11.8% across different video backbones while incurring only modest latency overhead. Beyond streaming video understanding, MaLoW also generalizes to vision-language-action models, improving the action success rate on Simpler WidowX by 9.3%.

</details>


### [82] [Beyond Generation: Using Multimodal LLMs as Detectors of LLM-Generated Deepfake Facial Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21760)
*Olivia Salamon,Virginia N. L. Franqueira,Shujun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将六种MLLM用于合成图像取证基准测评（400张图像，三阶段评测），发现模型可凭结构/物理不一致性检测假图（GPT-5.2成对比较峰值97.9%，整体平均82.5%），但Gemini自检测实验显示SynthID水印使检测准确率下降约10%，推测水印嵌入抑制了部分可见伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像生成模型日益逼真，传统取证技术难以应对，因而探索MLLM能否作为一种取证手段识别合成媒体，并厘清其判断依据是物理不一致性还是数字水印等伪影。

Method: 构建400张图像数据集（50张真实照片，350张由Gemini 3、Gemini 2.5、Flux.2 Pro、GPT-Image-1、GPT-Image-1.5、Seedream 4.0、Seedream 4.5七个模型生成），对GPT-4o、GPT-5、GPT-5.2、Gemini 2.5、Gemini 3、Seed 1.6六种MLLM进行三阶段评测：单图分类、成对比较、以及针对Gemini的"自检测"实验（含水印与干净对照组）。

Result: 多种MLLM取得较高检测准确率，GPT-5.2在成对比较中针对特定生成器峰值达97.9%，整体平均82.5%，主要依赖图像内部结构与物理不一致性；但自检测实验中，不可见SynthID水印使Gemini检测准确率较干净对照组下降10%。

Conclusion: MLLM无需依赖水印即可检测结构性伪影，但不同代际模型性能差异显著；对比实验C5的结果提示SynthID嵌入可能抑制检测所依据的部分结构伪影。

Abstract: As image generation models become increasingly capable of producing highly realistic content, traditional forensic techniques are struggling to keep up. This paper investigates whether MLLMs can instead be used as a form of forensic analysis by identifying synthetic media. Specifically, the study aims to determine whether these models detect fake images by recognising physical inconsistencies, or whether they rely on digital artefacts such as embedded watermarks.Six MLLMs: GPT-4o, GPT-5, GPT-5.2, Gemini 2.5, Gemini 3, and Seed 1.6, are benchmarked using a dataset of 400 images. Fifty images are authentic photographs, while the remaining 350 are generated using seven leading image models: Gemini 3, Gemini 2.5, Flux.2 Pro, GPT-Image-1, GPT-Image-1.5, Seedream 4.0, and Seedream 4.5. The evaluation is structured in three stages: single-image classification, pairwise comparison, and a focused "self-detection" experiment on Gemini. Several MLLMs achieved a high detection accuracy, with GPT-5.2 reaching a peak of 97.9% in the pairwise experiment, against specific generators and an overall mean pairwise accuracy of 82.5% by identifying structural or physical inconsistencies within images. However, results from the self-detection experiment indicate that the presence of invisible SynthID watermarks leads to a 10% reduction in Gemini's detection accuracy compared to the clean control group. One possible explanation is that SynthID embedding reduces some of the visible artefacts the models rely on for detection.These results imply that MLLMs can detect structural artefacts without relying on watermarks, but performance varies substantially across model generations. The findings from comparison C5 also suggest that SynthID embedding may suppress some of the structural artefacts used during detection.

</details>


### [83] [FeaTok: Feature-Loss Tokenization for Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22060)
*Zhang Xiaofeng,Samuel Lavoie,Nicolas Beltran-Velez,Jianhao Yuan,Felix Friedrich,Jakob Verbeek,Aaron Courville,Michal Drozdzal,Adriana Romero-Soriano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 FeaTok，用重建预训练特征替代重建像素来训练离散图像 tokenizer，从而获得更易被生成器建模的 token 表示，在类别条件生成与文本条件生成上均超越离散与连续基线且采样算力大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散图像生成器学习的是 tokenizer 输出的 token 分布，因此 token 表示应当"易于生成器建模"，而不仅是能忠实重建图像。然而现有 tokenizer 普遍以像素重建为目标训练，侧重低层保真度，可能破坏对生成有利的表示结构。

Method: 受特征预测式自监督学习启发，提出 feature-loss tokenization（FeaTok）：离散 token 的训练目标由重建像素改为重建预训练特征。并在 SigLIP、SigLIP2、DINOv2、DINOv3 四种特征骨干上做了消融对比。

Result: 四种骨干的消融均显示 FeaTok 生成性能更优。类别条件生成中推进了质量—算力 Pareto 前沿：256×256 下 gFDr6 相较最强离散基线最多降低 29%；以少 38 倍的采样算力超越最强无引导连续基线 RAEv2。人类偏好奖励模型得分优于先前方法且未使用奖励训练；预训练后仅需 500 步微调即在三个基准上超过连续与离散的文本条件基线。

Conclusion: tokenizer 的训练目标应与生成器的建模需求对齐：以特征重建替代像素重建可显著提升离散生成的效率与质量，并具备良好的骨干通用性与微调迁移性。

Abstract: Discrete image generators learn the distribution of tokens produced by an image tokenizer. These token representations should be easy for generators to model, not merely enable faithful image reconstruction. Yet these tokenizers are commonly trained to reconstruct pixels, which prioritizes low-level fidelity and may disrupt representation structure useful for generation. Motivated by feature-predictive self-supervised learning, we introduce feature-loss tokenization (FeaTok), which trains discrete tokens by reconstructing pretrained features rather than pixels. Ablations across SigLIP, SigLIP2, DINOv2, and DINOv3 show better generative performance with FeaTok. In class-conditional generation, FeaTok advances the quality-compute Pareto frontier, reducing gFDr6 at 256x256 by up to 29 percent over the strongest discrete baselines and outperforming the strongest unguided continuous baseline (RAEv2) with 38 times less sampling compute. FeaTok also achieves higher human-preference reward-model scores than prior art without reward-based training and surpasses continuous and discrete text-conditional baselines on three benchmarks with only 500 fine-tuning updates after pre-training.

</details>


### [84] [Can World and Action Modeling Benefit Each Other under Unified Causal Training?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21095)
*Yue Su,Bokai Lin,Xinkai Wang,Chubin Zhang,Xiangnan Wu,Kuan Zhang,Weiyang Jin,Jiwen Yu,Yiming Li,Cewu Lu,Lixin Yang,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一因果训练框架 OneWM，将动作同时作为世界预测的条件与策略学习的目标，验证世界建模与动作生成之间存在互惠收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界建模与动作建模关系密切，但在统一因果训练下二者的互惠收益尚不明确，本文旨在厘清这一双向作用。

Method: 构建 OneWM 框架，在共享的视频-动作架构中，把动作既作为世界预测的条件，又作为策略学习的目标，实现统一因果训练；在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、RoboCasa365 具身测试平台及真实世界策略实验中评估策略学习与世界建模两方面表现。

Result: 学习指定动作条件下的视觉后果可提升策略性能及在场景变化下的泛化能力；反之，动作学习在同一架构下提升了多视角世界预测的保真度。两个问题均得到肯定回答。

Conclusion: 在统一因果训练下，世界建模与动作生成可相互促进，支持二者双向互惠的结论。

Abstract: World and action modeling are closely related, yet their reciprocal benefits under unified causal training remain unclear. We introduce OneWM, a simple framework for unified causal training that treats actions as both conditions for world prediction and targets for policy learning within a shared video-action architecture. Using this framework, we investigate whether learning forward dynamics conditioned on specified actions improves action learning, and whether learning to generate actions in turn improves action-conditioned world modeling. We use LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and RoboCasa365 as embodied testbeds to evaluate both policy learning and world modeling, complemented by real-world policy experiments. Learning the visual consequences of specified actions improves policy performance and generalization across changing scenes. In turn, action learning improves multi-view world-prediction fidelity under the same architecture. Together, these results answer both questions positively, supporting reciprocal benefits between world modeling and action generation under unified causal training.

</details>


### [85] [RoPE-Flow: Monocular Vision-based Robot Pose Estimation via 2D Graph-Conditioned 3D Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21086)
*Liangjing Shao,Wanhao Liu,Zhiwei Fang,Rulin Zhou,Quanlu Zhang,Changjing Liu,Beilei Cui,Yiming Huang,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RoPE-Flow：基于流匹配的端到端相机-机器人位姿估计框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目图像相机-机器人位姿估计对视觉导航与控制很重要，但从2D观测恢复3D结构特征仍困难。现有方法要么依赖耗时的迭代渲染，要么采用复杂2D-3D配准，存在投影歧义、远端关节误差累积以及不同关节学习不均衡的问题。

Method: 提出端到端流匹配框架：1）从预测的2D热图出发，用关键点中心特征提取器（Keypoint-centered Feature Extractor）从以关键点为中心的多尺度patch中提取位置特征与上下文表示；2）引入图引导条件生成器（Graph-guided Condition Generator），嵌入可学习邻接图，基于关键点间相关性生成流匹配条件；3）设计骨骼长度损失与射线-深度损失，保证拓扑一致性与尺度稳定性，并采用关节级加权缓解不同关节预测难度差异。

Result: 在多种类型机器人与多相机设置下，位姿估计性能优于现有SOTA方法；并构建新的跨场景基准，验证了RoPE-Flow在零样本跨场景机器人位姿估计上的优异表现。

Conclusion: RoPE-Flow通过流匹配结合关键点中心特征与图引导条件生成，配合拓扑与尺度约束损失，有效缓解投影歧义与关节不均衡问题，兼具强泛化能力与跨场景零样本迁移性能。

Abstract: Camera-to-robot pose estimation from a monocular image is significant for vision-based robot navigation and control. However, recovering 3D structural features from 2D observations remains a challenging task. Existing camera-to-robot pose estimation methods either rely on time-consuming iterative rendering or involve sophisticated 2D-3D registration which suffers from projection ambiguity, accumulated errors for distal joints and imbalanced learning for different joints. In this work, we propose RoPE-Flow, an end-to-end flow matching-based framework for camera-to-robot pose estimation. From the predicted 2D heatmaps, we propose a Keypoint-centered Feature Extractor to obtain positional features along with contextual representations from keypoints-centered multi-scale patches. Moreover, we introduce Graph-guided Condition Generator, which embeds a learnable adjacency map to generate condition for flow matching based on correlation among keypoints. Besides, we introduce Bone Length Loss and Ray-Depth Loss for topological consistency and scale stability, with joint-wise weighting to mitigate varied prediction difficulty across joints. The proposed method outperforms state-of-the-art methods for pose estimation across different types of robots with mutiple cameras. Besides, we establish a novel cross-scene benchmark to demonstrate the outstanding performance of RoPE-Flow on zero-shot cross-scene robot pose estimation. Meanwhile, animated visualizations are shown on the website: ropeflow.netlify.app

</details>


### [86] [EigDiff: Eigensample Diffusion - or how to significantly reduce training dataset size with marginal performance variation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20999)
*Andrei Jarca,Vlad Hondru,Florinel-Alin Croitoru,Radu Tudor Ionescu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EigDiff利用CLIP空间PCA得到的特征向量作为参考点，构建兼具代表性（exemplars）与多样性的训练子集，在多个数据集和扩散模型上将训练数据最多减少14倍、子集选择加速1607倍，性能仅略有差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型训练代价高昂，需要大规模数据学习多步去噪。现有工作主要聚焦于蒸馏扩散步数，却忽视了训练数据本身存在的冗余。

Method: 在预训练CLIP隐空间中做PCA得到latent eigenvectors，以缓解eigenimages对仿射变换的敏感性；将特征向量作为参考点，选取嵌入最接近特征向量的样本（exemplars）并结合多样性图像，构成代表性训练子集用于训练扩散模型。

Result: 在多种扩散模型与多个数据集上的实验表明，EigDiff可将训练数据减少至多14倍，子集选择加速至多1607倍，且性能差异极小。

Conclusion: 通过特征样本（eigensamples）选择数据可在保持扩散模型生成性能的同时显著降低训练数据规模与选样开销，是一种有效的数据高效训练策略。

Abstract: Diffusion models are notoriously expensive to train, requiring large-scale datasets to learn how to gradually reverse the noise from images over multiple small denoising steps. Current approaches focus on distilling the number of diffusion steps, neglecting potential redundancies of the training data. In this paper, we explore the potential of harnessing eigensamples to train diffusion models on significantly smaller datasets, while preserving model performance. To mitigate the sensitivity to affine transformations of eigenimages, we harness the latent space of a pre-trained CLIP model. We next compute latent eigenvectors by performing principal component analysis (PCA) in CLIP space. Further, we employ eigenvectors as reference points to guide the selection of a representative training set comprising comprising both exemplars (images whose embeddings are closest to eigenvectors) and diverse images. Our comprehensive experiments with different diffusion models applied on multiple datasets indicate that our approach (EigDiff) can reduce training data by up to 14x and accelerate subset selection by up to 1607x, with marginal performance differences.

</details>


### [87] [Pixels to Tokens: Token Space Efficient Active Learning for Low-Budget Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20881)
*Utku Cicek,Mahip Singh,Ziyao Shang,Kimathi Kaai,C Thomas,Pedro Pablo Guerrero Vela,Alexander Wong,Sirisha Rambhatla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TEAL是首个基于ViT表征的像素级主动学习框架，在冻结ViT的token格点上做多样性选择并用解码器网格做不确定性精修，单像素/图标注下大幅超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向ViT的主动学习多停留在patch/region级，标注成本仍高；将其推广到像素级极低预算场景极为困难：仅一张图一个像素查询时，需同时定位最有信息量的位置并从极稀疏信号中训练可靠解码器头。此外，将token嵌入插值到稠密像素分辨率会引入伪影。

Method: 采用冻结ViT主干；在原生token格点上直接进行基于多样性的选择，避免token嵌入插值伪影；对多层token描述子施加两级MaxHerding策略选出代表性候选；再在更细的解码器网格上用margin-based不确定性做精修。

Result: 在CamVid、Cityscapes、ADE20K、Pascal Context上，极端标签稀缺下持续优于先前基线：单像素/图时较最强基线最高提升+24.36 mIoU；10像素/图时可达全监督性能的97%。所有基线统一使用DINOv3主干后增益仍保持。

Conclusion: 将token空间选择与解码器网格上的不确定性精修相结合，使ViT token几何成为极低预算主动分割的有效基础；增益来源是方法设计而非主干差异。

Abstract: While foundation models and vision transformers (ViTs) have redefined visual representations, current active learning (AL) strategies for these backbones largely operate at the patch or region level, where annotation requirements remain high due to extensive pixel labeling. Extending ViT-based AL to the pixel-level, low-budget regime is uniquely challenging: with as little as one pixel query per image, a method must simultaneously identify the most informative locations and train a reliable decoder head from an extremely sparse signal. We introduce TEAL (Token-space Efficient Active Learning), the first pixel-level AL framework built around ViT representations. TEAL uses a frozen ViT backbone and performs diversity-based selection directly on its native token lattice, avoiding the artifacts introduced by interpolating token embeddings to dense pixel resolutions. Our framework applies a two-level MaxHerding strategy over multi-layer token descriptors to select representative candidates, followed by margin-based uncertainty refinement on a finer decoder grid. Across CamVid, Cityscapes, ADE20K, and Pascal Context, TEAL consistently outperforms previous baselines under extreme label scarcity, improving over the strongest baseline by up to +24.36 mIoU with a single labeled pixel (px) per image, and reaching up to 97% of fully supervised performance with just 10 px. To rigorously establish the source of the gains, we further show that the improvements persist when all baselines are equipped with the same DINOv3 backbone, demonstrating that coupling token-space selection with uncertainty refinement on the decoder grid makes ViT token geometry an effective foundation for extremely low-budget active segmentation.

</details>


### [88] [FocusOPD: Structure-Guided On-Policy Distillation for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21250)
*Xinrui Lin,Shumin Wang,Suqi Song,Fan Li,Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FocusOPD通过将特征对齐监督限制在深层Transformer块与早期去噪步，显著提升多教师on-policy蒸馏的能力迁移效果与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多教师on-policy蒸馏（OPD）可让rectified-flow扩散Transformer学生融合多个教师能力，但仅匹配输出并不保证内部表征对齐；现有方法不清楚特征监督应在何处、何时施加最有效。

Method: 分析教师-学生在各Transformer块与去噪时间步上的特征失配分布，发现在早期去噪的深层块失配最集中且蒸馏后仍残留；据此在基础OPD目标上增加受限的逐token方向性特征对齐，仅作用于深层与早期去噪步。

Result: 在两个rectified-flow骨干和多种OPD目标下提升组合生成、文本渲染与偏好评估表现：GenEval 0.953→0.967，PickScore 23.797→23.944，LAION-Aesthetic 6.052→6.199。消融表明选择性深层-早期监督比全网全轨迹对齐更有效且更高效。

Conclusion: 输出匹配不足以保障表征对齐，诊断式定位失配区域并施加稀疏特征监督可高效提升蒸馏质量。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation enables rectified-flow models with diffusion-transformer architectures to combine task-specific capabilities within a single student. However, matching model outputs does not guarantee that their internal representations are aligned, and it remains unclear where and when additional feature supervision is most useful. We analyze the teacher-student feature mismatch across Transformer blocks and denoising timesteps, and find that the mismatch is concentrated in deeper Transformer blocks during early denoising and persists after OPD distillation, indicating ineffective distillation. Motivated by this observation, we introduce FocusOPD, which augments the base OPD objective with token-wise directional feature alignment restricted to deep layers and early denoising steps. Across two rectified-flow backbones and multiple OPD objectives, FocusOPD improves capability transfer in compositional generation, text rendering, and preference-based evaluation. On the primary benchmark, it improves over DiffusionOPD from 0.953 to 0.967 on GenEval, from 23.797 to 23.944 on PickScore, and from 6.052 to 6.199 on LAION-Aesthetic. Ablations show that selective deep and early supervision is more effective and efficient than alignment over the full network and trajectory.

</details>


### [89] [A large-scale climate-aware satellite image dataset for domain adaptive land-cover semantic segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21238)
*Songlin Liu,Linwei Chen,Li Zhang,Jun Hu,Ying Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个气候感知的遥感土地覆盖语义分割跨域数据集CASID，覆盖亚洲四个气候带，并给出全面分析与基线评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有土地覆盖语义分割数据集忽视了不同气候区之间地理区域的显著多样性，导致遥感图像存在域间差异（domain gap），使分割模型跨区域泛化性能严重下降。因此亟需构建能体现气候差异的多域数据集，并研究气候对分割任务的影响。

Method: 首次系统研究气候对土地覆盖语义分割的影响，构建大规模气候感知卫星图像数据集CASID：包含980幅5000×5000像素卫星图像，采自亚洲30个不同区域，覆盖超24,500平方公里，涵盖温带季风、亚热带季风、热带季风、热带雨林四个气候带，划分为四个子数据集/域。此外，从全局图像特征、图像纹理、类别分布、光谱值、目标形状等维度对四个气候带样本进行对比分析，并在CASID上对最新语义分割与无监督域适应方法开展大量实验，建立基线。

Result: CASID成为首个具有多域、气候感知特征的土地覆盖语义分割数据集；跨气候带分析揭示了在全局特征、纹理、类别分布、光谱与形状上的显著差异，解释了域间差距的来源；对现有分割与无监督域适应方法的实验结果为后续研究提供了稳健基线。

Conclusion: 气候是影响土地覆盖外观与分布的重要因素，构建气候感知多域数据集有助于提升分割模型的泛化能力。CASID公开可用（https://github.com/Linwei-Chen/CASID），可作为跨气候域自适应土地覆盖分割研究的基准平台。

Abstract: A few well-annotated datasets for land-cover semantic segmentation have recently been introduced to advance the field of earth observation technologies. However, these datasets overlook the significant diversity among geographic areas with different climates, which can greatly impact and diversify land cover. Consequently, this leads to a domain gap in remote sensing images and severe performance degradation of the segmentation models. To enhance land-cover semantic segmentation with improved generalization ability, we conducted the first investigation into the impact of climate on this task. In this paper, we present a unique large-scale Climate-Aware Satellite Images Dataset (CASID) specifically designed for domain adaptive land-cover semantic segmentation. It consists of 980 satellite images with a size of 5000 x 5000 pixels, collected from 30 different regions around Asia, covering over 24,500 square kilometers. These images are gathered from four distinct climate zones, namely temperate monsoon, subtropical monsoon, tropical monsoon, and tropical rainforest. It includes four sub-datasets/domains, each representing one of the aforementioned climate zones. This characteristic makes CASID the first climate-aware land-cover semantic segmentation dataset with multiple domains. Additionally, we provide a comprehensive analysis of the samples from the four climate zones, emphasizing differences in global image features, image texture, category distribution, spectral value, and object shape. These analyses offer valuable insights for subsequent research in this field. Moreover, we conduct extensive experiments to evaluate the latest semantic segmentation and unsupervised domain adaptation methods on the CASID dataset. These results serve as a robust baseline for future research endeavors. Our dataset will be made publicly available at https://github.com/Linwei-Chen/CASID.

</details>


### [90] [UltraDiff: Transferring High-Fidelity Priors to Compressed Latent Spaces for High-Resolution Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21202)
*Jingjing Ren,Haitian Zheng,Haoyu Chen,Connelly Barnes,Zhifei Zhang,Yuqian Zhou,Zhe Lin,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 UltraDiff，将高分辨率 LDM 的“压缩率—质量”权衡重构为联合潜空间扩散学习 + 边缘分数蒸馏，在 f32 效率下逼近 f8 质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 难在：LDM 的潜压缩因子 f 决定计算成本与生成保真度的权衡。激进压缩（f32）效率高但信息损失与歧义严重，标准 f8 质量好但代价高。目标是在保留 f32 效率的同时达到 f8 级质量。

Method: 1) 将问题重构为联合潜空间扩散学习，再做边缘分数蒸馏；2) 提出 MixTeacher，双分支 DiT 建模 f8–f32 联合分布，用共享注意力以高保真 f8 先验消解压缩空间歧义；3) 提出在原生 f32 空间做向量场蒸馏，理论上迫使学生学习教师轨迹的条件期望，从而滤除压缩伪影。

Result: 在 1K–4K 设置下，UltraDiff 质量接近 f8 水平并保持 f32 效率；单张 A100、20 步采样下 6.2 秒生成 2K 图像，比 f8 基线快 40 倍以上。

Conclusion: 联合分布建模结合原生压缩空间的向量场蒸馏，可有效缓解极端潜压缩的歧义与伪影，是高分辨率生成中兼顾效率与保真度的可行方案。

Abstract: High-resolution synthesis with Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs) faces a fundamental trade-off between computational cost and generative fidelity. This trade off is largely governed by the latent compression factor f (e.g. f8 denotes f=8 and f32 f=32). While aggressive compression (f32) significantly improves efficiency, it suffers from severe information loss and ambiguity compared to standard f8 models. We propose UltraDiff, a framework that reformulates this challenge as joint latent diffusion learning followed by marginal score distillation. We introduce MixTeacher, a dual branch DiT that models the joint f8-f32 distribution, utilizing shared attention to resolve the ambiguity of the compressed space with high fidelity f8 priors. Crucially, we propose vector field distillation in the native f32 space. Theoretically, this compels the student to learn the conditional expectation of the teacher trajectory, effectively filtering out compression artifacts. Across the evaluated 1K to 4K settings, UltraDiff approaches f8 level quality while preserving f32 efficiency. With 20 sampling steps on a single A100 GPU, it generates a 2K image in 6.2 seconds over 40 times faster than our f8 baseline.

</details>


### [91] [ControlFlow3D: Distilling Multi-View Knowledge into Latent Flow Matching for Point Cloud Upsampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21190)
*Yuang Liu,Zhi Zuo,Zhengkai Zhao,Lirui Zhang,Pan Gao,Hao Feng,Zhengzhe Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ControlFlow3D 通过训练期从多模态基础模型蒸馏知识、测试期不依赖多视图图像的方式实现点云上采样，并构建了 31 倍于先前规模的 ShapeNetPU 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点云上采样受稀疏、欠约束观测限制存在几何歧义；多视图图像可缓解该问题，但现有跨模态方法未能充分利用视觉线索，且测试期难以获得多视图图像。

Method: 在冻结 3D 编码器定义的结构化隐空间做条件流匹配；将多视图图像 token 与 3D 隐 token 通过无监督软 KMeans 联合聚类为共享原型，原型以速度控制引导流轨迹，并用一致性损失渐进把图像知识迁移到基础网络；最终由基于 Mamba 的几何解码器以线性复杂度输出稠密坐标。

Result: 在多个基准上验证了方法有效性，并展现出较强泛化能力；构建 ShapeNetPU 基准，含 3.5 万物体，规模为先前数据集的 31 倍。

Conclusion: 训练期多模态蒸馏、测试期纯点云推理的范式可有效克服几何歧义，无需测试时多视图输入即可获得强上采样性能。

Abstract: Point cloud upsampling is essential for 3D applications but remains challenging due to sparse, under-constrained observations. Multi-view images can resolve geometric ambiguity, but existing multi-modal methods do not fully exploit visual cues. We propose ControlFlow3D, a framework that distills knowledge from pretrained foundation models into a lightweight generation network during training, enabling strong performance without multi-view images available at test time. Specifically, our method performs conditional flow matching in a structured latent space defined by a frozen 3D encoder. Multi-view image tokens and 3D latent tokens are jointly clustered into shared prototypes via unsupervised soft KMeans; the resulting prototypes guide the flow trajectory through velocity control and are aligned by a consistency loss that progressively transfers image knowledge into the base network. A Mamba-based geometry decoder then maps latent features to dense coordinates with linear complexity. We also construct ShapeNetPU, a 35K-object multi-modal benchmark that is 31 times larger than prior datasets. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our method and its strong generalization capability.

</details>


### [92] [Sparse Attention for Video Generation Acceleration via Growing Sparsity and Reduced Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21129)
*Yun Dai,Yuyun Chen,Jiarui Wen,Huiping Zhuang,Cen Chen,Ziqian Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架GSRS，通过随去噪进度增长的稀疏比例与复用早期搜索结果的缩减搜索空间，加速DiT视频生成1.55×–2.21×且质量接近全注意力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer（DiT）在视频生成中占主导，但自注意力二次复杂度导致推理开销高。现有稀疏注意力方法在去噪全过程使用固定稀疏比，且难以准确判断哪些计算可跳过。

Method: 通过注意力熵与平均注意力距离分析注意力动态，发现注意力随去噪步数逐渐集中，且早期选中的块在后续步骤仍占据大部分注意力权重。据此设计免训练框架GSRS：早期保留更多计算、后期逐步减少；后续搜索限制在早期候选集内以降低搜索开销；采用Top-p自适应稀疏控制，并借助在FlashAttention计算中精确计算注意力权重的评估方案实现近零额外开销。

Result: 在HunyuanVideo与Wan 2.1上实验，实现1.55×–2.21×加速，同时生成质量与全注意力相当。

Conclusion: 注意力随去噪进程逐步集中的特性可转化为“渐进增长稀疏比+缩小搜索空间”的加速策略，GSRS以免训练方式在保持生成质量的同时显著降低视频DiT推理成本。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) dominate video generation but suffer from high inference cost due to the quadratic complexity of self-attention. Sparse attention mitigates this by skipping unnecessary computation in the attention matrix, but existing methods apply a fixed sparsity ratio throughout denoising and struggle to accurately determine which computations to skip. To address these limitations, we analyze attention dynamics through attention entropy and mean attention distance, and find that attention becomes increasingly concentrated across denoising steps. We further observe that blocks selected early continue to capture a large fraction of the total attention weight at later steps. These findings suggest two acceleration opportunities: the sparsity ratio should grow as denoising progresses, and earlier search results can be reused to reduce the search space. Based on these findings, we propose Growing Sparsity and Reduced Search (GSRS), a training-free framework that retains more computation early and progressively reduces it later, while restricting subsequent searches to an early candidate set to reduce search overhead. GSRS adopts Top-p selection for adaptive sparsity control, supported by a precise evaluation scheme that computes exact attention weights within the FlashAttention computation at negligible overhead. Experiments on HunyuanVideo and Wan 2.1 validate that GSRS achieves 1.55$\times$--2.21$\times$ speedup while maintaining generation quality comparable to full attention.

</details>


### [93] [Can Visual Generation Expand What Unified Multimodal Models Know?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21459)
*Wenkai Li,Wenyi Hong,Zhuoyi Yang,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出KTP协议，用图像生成训练注入新知识，再用纯文本问答检验其可及性；发现视觉监督能直接但非均衡地迁移到语言，并提出文本对齐与交错图文对齐两种方法改善知识访问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型（UMMs）接受的图像生成监督暴露了文本未完整记录的视觉信息，由此产生核心问题：学习生成图像能否扩展预训练模型的知识边界？现有研究缺乏可控手段来检验视觉监督中知识向语言能力的迁移。

Method: 设计知识迁移探测（KTP）协议：通过图像生成训练注入新知识，并以纯文本问答测试其可及性；覆盖七类知识与三个UMM；在此基础上依次引入文本对齐（Textual Alignment）与交错图文对齐（Interleaved Image–Text Alignment），分别通过共享知识与交错响应连接生成与问答。

Result: 观察到从视觉监督到语言存在直接但不均衡的迁移；对选定实体的引导可在无问答监督下提升其他实体的知识访问，交错对齐带来额外增益；能被正确复现于图像中的知识往往在文本侧更易访问，但两种访问形式之间仍存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 视觉生成可作为超越文本监督的知识来源；需联合研究训练数据设计与模型架构，使该类知识获得更广泛的可及性。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) receive image-generation supervision that exposes them to visual information not fully documented in text. Can learning to generate images expand what pretrained models know? We introduce Knowledge Transfer Probing (KTP), a controlled protocol that supplies new knowledge through image-generation training and tests its accessibility through text-only question answering. Across seven knowledge categories and three UMMs, we observe direct but uneven transfer from visual supervision to language. Motivated by these observations, we successively introduce Textual Alignment and Interleaved Image--Text Alignment, which connect generation and answering through shared knowledge and interleaved responses, respectively. Guidance on selected entities can improve knowledge access on other entities without QA supervision, with additional gains from interleaved alignment. Further analysis shows that knowledge correctly reproduced in images is often more accessible through text, although substantial gaps remain between the two forms of access. These findings support visual generation as a source of knowledge beyond textual supervision, and motivate joint investigation of training data design and model architecture to make this knowledge more broadly accessible.

</details>


### [94] [Pathology foundation models for training-free case retrieval in salivary gland tumors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22087)
*Maximilian J. Roesel,Niklas Kiermeyer,Philipp Keyl,Frederick Klauschen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究探索无需任务特定训练，利用病理基础模型进行基于内容的检索，以辅助罕见肿瘤的诊断；在137例唾液腺肿瘤病例上，检索精度和相似度排序均达到可用水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理模式识别依赖长期训练形成的参照框架，面对罕见癌症亚型时仍显不足；既往诊断档案中往往存有相关先例，但难以定位最相关病例。作者因此希望验证：现成的病理基础模型能否在零训练条件下实现病例级检索，将个人经验扩展为科室集体经验。

Method: 病理学家以全切片图像（WSI）中的感兴趣区域（ROI）检索数字化档案，返回形态学相似病例及其ICD-O编码与对应WSI；ROI可手动选取，也可由门控注意力多示例学习（MIL）模型自动选取。在含12个ICD-O形态类别的137例不平衡唾液腺肿瘤数据集上，采用留一病例法（leave-one-case-out）评估Atlas2、Atlas2-B、Atlas2-S与UNI2h四种病理基础模型的检索表现，并提出改进的Chamfer相似度用于区分Top-5中正确与错误匹配（以AUC衡量）。

Result: Top-1/3/5检索准确率：Atlas2为62.5%/76.7%/81.3%，Atlas2-B为52.3%/71.5%/77.6%，Atlas2-S为54.4%/65.2%/73.7%，UNI2h为50.2%/66.6%/72.9%。在样本量≥10的五个类别上，宏平均F1从最小模型Atlas2-S的69.0%到最大模型Atlas2的76.6%。改进Chamfer相似度对Top-5匹配的区分能力：Atlas2的AUC为0.754，显著优于Atlas2-B（0.679，p=0.020）与Atlas2-S（0.653，p=0.003），但与UNI2h（0.697，p=0.196）差异不显著。

Conclusion: 该方法无需额外训练或人工标注，即可用病理基础模型使科室档案可检索，为基于病例的鉴别诊断提供低门槛路径，并将个体参照框架扩展为科室集体经验；检索质量与模型规模及架构相关，改进Chamfer相似度可作为匹配可靠性排序的补充指标。

Abstract: In pathology, pattern recognition relies on a frame of reference built through extensive training and experience, which may still fall short when encountering rare cancer subtypes. Archives of previously diagnosed tumors often contain relevant precedents, yet identifying the most relevant cases remains challenging. Here we explore whether content-based retrieval with current pathology foundation models facilitates rare cancer diagnostics without any task-specific training. Pathologists can query a digitized archive using a region of interest (ROI) from a whole slide image (WSI) and retrieve morphologically similar cases, along with their ICD-O codes and corresponding WSIs. The ROI can be selected manually or automatically using a gated attention-based multiple instance learning model. We evaluate our approach by leave-one-case-out on an imbalanced dataset of 137 salivary gland tumor cases spanning 12 ICD-O morphology categories, comparing four pathology foundation models (Atlas2, Atlas2-B, Atlas2-S, and UNI2h). Top-1/3/5 retrieval was 62.5%/76.7%/81.3% for Atlas2, 50.2/66.6/72.9% for UNI2h, 52.3%/71.5%/77.6% for Atlas2-B and 54.4/65.2/73.7% for Atlas2-S. Macro-averaged F1 ranged from 69.0% for the smallest model Atlas2-S to 76.6% for the largest model Atlas2 across the five classes with at least ten cases. Our modified Chamfer similarity separated correct from incorrect matches among the Top-5 more reliably for Atlas2 (AUC 0.754) than for Atlas2-B (0.679, p = 0.020) and Atlas2-S (0.653, p = 0.003), but not UNI2h (0.697, p = 0.196). Requiring no additional training or manual annotation, this approach makes departmental archives searchable with pathology foundation models, providing a low-barrier route to case-based differential diagnosis and extending the individual frame of reference to the collective experience of the department.

</details>


### [95] [Retrofitting Recurrent Vision-Language Models from Pretrained Backbones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22075)
*Madhav Iyengar,Andreas Geiger,Hilde Kühne*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将深度循环（depth recurrence）改造引入预训练视觉语言模型的语言主干，可减少参数量而保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作证明深度循环可被回填进预训练语言模型，本文希望把该范式用于视觉理解，以降低预训练VLM的参数占用。

Method: 对VLM语言主干剪枝部分层，并对剩余层子集做循环；每步循环用线性适配器将块输出与前面层的token表示融合并投影回块内。为弥补剪枝损失，采用三阶段恢复训练：语言-only预训练恢复语言主干、视觉-语言投影器对齐、视觉指令微调全量微调；第一与第三阶段以预训练非循环模型输出作蒸馏。

Result: 在TinyLLaVA-1.4B主干上、且不引入新视觉语言数据，循环化模型在同等恢复数据下优于未剪枝基线，语言参数少23.3%；优于把循环LM转为VLM的方案；消融显示循环间适配器与更大循环块有益；推理类基准提升，基础视觉理解与基线持平。

Conclusion: 深度循环可用于VLM回填以实现参数高效，且在三阶段恢复与蒸馏下不损失（甚至提升推理）性能。

Abstract: Recent work shows that depth recurrence can be retrofitted into pretrained language models. In this work, we adapt this paradigm for visual understanding, using depth recurrence to reduce the parameter footprint of a pretrained vision-language model (VLM). To this end, we prune layers from the language backbone of the VLM and loop over a subset of the remaining layers. At each step of the loop, a linear adapter merges the block's output with the token representations from the preceding layers and projects the fused representation back into the block. As the pruning impacts the original model performance, we propose a three-stage recovery training: first, we leverage a language-only pretraining to recover the performance of the language backbone, followed by an alignment of the vision-language projector, and a full fine-tuning of the model via visual instruction tuning. In the first and the last stage, we distill the outputs of the pretrained non-recurrent model into the recurrent model, which further improves the recovery. We evaluate the proposed approach on the TinyLLaVA-1.4B backbone without introducing any new vision-language data during retrofitting. Our experiments demonstrate that the retrofitted recurrent model outperforms the unpruned baseline trained with the same recovery data while storing 23.3% fewer language parameters. We further show that the proposed retrofitting recurrence outperforms the setup of converting a recurrent LM into a VLM on the same data. Additionally, our ablations show how design choices, such as the adapter between recurrences and a larger recurrent block, lead to the improved performance of our approach. Finally, we find that our proposed model gains on reasoning-oriented benchmarks, whereas its performance on basic visual understanding benchmarks remains on par with the baseline.

</details>


### [96] [Flow-CLIP: Image-Conditioned Residual Scoring for CompositionalImage-Text Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21937)
*Minghong Wei,Enguang Zhu,Pu Cao,Lu Yang,Qing Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Flow-CLIP通过图像条件化的token–patch证据变化精化全局图文对齐，提升组合式匹配与属性–物体绑定能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP类视觉–语言预训练在图文检索与零样本识别上进展显著，但句级对齐不足以支撑组合式匹配：不同描述可在物体与属性上相同、仅在元素绑定关系上不同，导致模型依赖语言先验而误判。

Method: 提出Flow-CLIP，包含三部分：(1) GCC（Global Compatibility Calibration）学习句级相似度度量；(2) ICER（Image-Conditioned Evidence Refinement）将视觉patch上下文注入token query，并以局部匹配证据的变化量调整全局分数；(3) CIMR（Cross-Image Margin Ranking）跨匹配/不匹配图像比较同一正–负caption对，在图像支持正确描述时增大排序间隔。

Result: 在组合式匹配与属性–物体绑定基准上，Flow-CLIP相较预训练CLIP及已学习的全局对齐方法提升了绑定准确率；跨图像排序监督缓解了对语言先验的依赖。

Conclusion: 局部证据精化可作为全局对齐的有效补充，跨图像排序监督可抑制语言先验偏见；代码将开源。

Abstract: Vision--language pre-training has enabled substantial progress in image--text retrieval and zero-shot recognition by learning transferable cross-modal representations. However, sentence-level alignment remains insufficient for compositional matching, where captions can describe the same objects and attributes while differing in how these elements are bound. To address this limitation, we propose Flow-CLIP, which refines global image-text alignment through image-conditioned changes in token--patch evidence. Global Compatibility Calibration (GCC) learns a sentence-level similarity metric, while Image-Conditioned Evidence Refinement (ICER) incorporates visual patch context into token queries and uses the resulting change in local matching evidence to adjust the global score. We further introduce Cross-Image Margin Ranking (CIMR), which compares the same positive--negative caption pair across matched and mismatched images, encouraging a larger ranking margin when the image supports the correct description. Experiments on compositional matching and attribute--object binding benchmarks show that Flow-CLIP improves binding accuracy over pretrained CLIP and learned global alignment through local evidence refinement, while cross-image ranking supervision mitigates reliance on language priors. Code will be released at link.

</details>


### [97] [Anvil: Scaling Embodied Spatial Intelligence through Knowledge Recirculation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21882)
*Weilin Ruan,Bohan Zhou,Jinghao Li,Yangkai Wei,Wenqian Li,Peiran Dong,Yingjie Xu,Leyi Wu,Haojian Huang,Binghui Xie,Han Yang,Kaiwen Zhou,Yinchuan Li,Ying-Cong Chen,James Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Anvil统一框架（数据生成Anvil-Gen、基准Anvil-Ben、模型Anvil-MOPD），用9B学生模型整合异构空间知识，在七个公开基准上平均超Qwen3.5-9B与RynnBrain-8B分别9.29与11.03个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身空间智能不能只靠视觉识别，还需定位实体、推理关系并以动作相关形式表达知识；但监督信号在几何与语义输出间碎片化，联合训练会使不同阶段所需知识相互混淆。

Method: (1) Anvil-Gen：异构数据飞轮，结合按形式生成、跨阶段一致性校验与失败驱动的回收，覆盖11种Ground–Relation–Act形式；(2) Anvil-Ben：场景对齐监督与场景关联评测，区分阶段能力与误差传播；(3) Anvil-MOPD：9B学生模型，从专家教师蒸馏异构空间知识，采用语义优先故障路由将监督对准学生最早的决定性失败，并用阶段归一化控制不同长度原生输出的损失尺度。构建5.8M源数据记录（160K图像），580K留作评测、5.22M用于训练。

Result: 在七个公开基准上平均超越Qwen3.5-9B和RynnBrain-8B达9.29与11.03个百分点，VABench-Point达80.67%；对照实验支持定向覆盖、选择性迁移与反馈引导修复三者为互补框架组件。

Conclusion: 通过数据生成、场景对齐评测与故障路由蒸馏的一体化设计，可有效缓解空间监督碎片化与跨阶段知识混淆，显著提升具身空间智能的定位、关系推理与动作表达能力。

Abstract: Embodied spatial intelligence requires more than visual recognition: models must localize entities, reason about their relations, and express this knowledge in action-relevant forms. Yet joint training can conflate the knowledge needed at different stages because supervision is fragmented across incompatible geometric and semantic outputs. We introduce Anvil, a unified framework for data generation, training, and benchmarking for embodied spatial intelligence. (1) Anvil-Gen is a heterogeneous data flywheel that combines form-specific generation, cross-stage consistency verification, and failure-driven recollection across 11 Ground--Relation--Act forms. (2) Anvil-Ben provides scene-aligned supervision for learning diverse embodied spatial capabilities and scene-linked assessments that distinguish stage competence from error propagation. (3) Anvil-MOPD is a 9B student that consolidates heterogeneous spatial knowledge through distillation from specialist teachers. Semantic-first-fault routing targets teacher supervision to the student's earliest decisive failure, while stage normalization controls the loss scale across native outputs of different lengths. We construct 5.8M source-grounded records spanning 160K images, reserving 580K spatial records for held-out evaluation and using the remaining 5.22M records for training. On average across seven public benchmarks, Anvil-MOPD outperforms Qwen3.5-9B and RynnBrain-8B by 9.29 and 11.03 percentage points, respectively, while reaching 80.67% on VABench-Point. Controlled studies support targeted coverage, selective transfer, and feedback-guided repair as complementary framework components.

</details>


### [98] [International Multi-Centre Validation of Unsupervised Domain Adaptation for Precise Discrimination between Normal and Abnormal Retinal Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21719)
*Zhanhan Tu,Nikhil Reddy,Helen J. Kuht,Ha-Jun Yoon,Gail D. E. Maconachie,Ravi Purohit,Prateek Pani,Line Kessel,Jinu Han,Jing Jin,Shui-Hua Wang,Karthik Bharadwaj,Viral Sheth,Michael Hisaund,Rebecca J. McLean,George Garratt,Chetan Kantibhai Patel,Kanmin Xue,Callum Hunt,Zisen Zhou,Ian De Silva,Seema Teli,Sonal Shah,Vanessa Rodwell,Aarti Patel,Irene Gottlob,Yu-Dong Zhang,Zedong Zheng,Girish Varma,Mervyn G. Thomas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出并评估一种无监督域适应模型，在无需目标设备标注数据的情况下跨不同OCT系统分类正常与异常视网膜发育，验证集准确率87.75%–96.06%，最佳模型AUC达0.994。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视网膜发育停滞可导致儿童视觉损害，但OCT扫描分级在不同设备与中心之间难以标准化，限制了临床评估与大规模影像数据利用。

Method: 回顾性多中心研究，数据来自4国5个中心及UK Biobank，涵盖4种OCT设备（含台式与手持式）。采用基于ResNet50的域对抗神经网络（DANN），以源设备标注图像与目标设备无标注图像训练，评估6组源-目标配对，并与7名临床分级者比较；另以Heidelberg Spectralis数据集作外部验证。

Result: 验证集准确率87.75%–96.06%，敏感度88.68%–98.38%，特异度78.00%–98.02%。最佳模型（TM OCT1→HH OCT1）准确率96.06%，AUC 0.994（95% CI 0.981–0.999）。部分模型在未见过的Heidelberg设备上仍保持判别能力。

Conclusion: 无监督域适应可在无目标设备标注数据条件下实现跨设备OCT分类，有望支持更一致的视网膜发育评估及既有影像数据集的大规模分析。

Abstract: Arrested retinal development can cause childhood visual impairment, but grading optical coherence tomography scans is difficult to standardise across devices and centres. We developed and evaluated an unsupervised domain adaptation model to classify normal and abnormal retinal development across different OCT systems. This retrospective multicentre study used OCT scans from five centres in four countries and the UK Biobank. Data covered four OCT devices, including table mounted and handheld systems. A ResNet50 based Domain Adversarial Neural Network was trained using labelled images from a source device and unlabelled images from a target device. We evaluated six source to target pairings and compared model performance with seven clinician graders. A separate Heidelberg Spectralis dataset was reserved for external evaluation. Across validation sets, accuracy ranged from 87.75% to 96.06%, sensitivity from 88.68% to 98.38%, and specificity from 78.00% to 98.02%. The best performing model, adapted from TM OCT1 to HH OCT1, achieved 96.06% accuracy and an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 0.994, with a 95% confidence interval from 0.981 to 0.999. Several models also maintained discrimination on the unseen Heidelberg device. Unsupervised domain adaptation enabled OCT classification across devices without labelled target device data. The approach may support more consistent assessment of retinal development and large scale analysis of existing imaging datasets.

</details>


### [99] [Digital Typhoon Dataset V3: Benchmarking for Multi-spectral Extension and Analyzing Systematic Bias in Intensity Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21654)
*Asanobu Kitamoto,Victor Le Louarn,Noelia Falczuk,Ngoc Anh Tong,Anja Veleva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发布Digital Typhoon Dataset V3，新增多光谱卫星数据，评估半球数据划分策略，并对气压/风速回归进行基准测试，发现多通道输入未显著优于单通道，且强度回归存在对强台风的系统性低估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建近50年最长时序的台风卫星影像数据集，用于长时程时空数据的机器学习基准测试；并考察多光谱多通道数据、数据划分策略及模型结构对回归性能的影响，同时揭示强度回归中的系统性偏差问题。

Method: 在V3中增加多光谱卫星数据以支持多通道模型；设计并比较面向北、南半球的数据划分策略；在多种模型架构（如ResNet-18）、图像分辨率与光谱通道（如Channel 1）组合下，对气压回归与风速回归开展基准实验；并测试逆密度加权等面向类别不平衡的加权策略（受BalancedMSE启发）以缓解强度回归偏差。

Result: 在现有数据构建与实验设定下，多通道输入在气压回归上未明显优于单通道；ResNet-18、全分辨率图像与Channel 1在单通道气压回归中表现最佳；WP与AU数据集性能随回归目标不同而异，气压回归在WP上RMSE更低，风速回归在AU上更低；不平衡加权策略不足以消除对强台风强度的低估偏差。

Conclusion: 基准性能解读需考虑数据集与任务的特异性；多光谱多通道并未自动带来增益；强度回归的系统性低估偏差仍需更有效的方法解决；数据集已公开，供后续长时序台风研究使用。

Abstract: This paper presents the Digital Typhoon Dataset V3, a new version of the longest typhoon satellite image dataset spanning nearly 50 years, designed to benchmark machine learning models on long-term spatio-temporal data. The latest addition to Dataset V3 is multispectral satellite data, enabling multi-channel models. We investigate data-splitting strategies and present benchmarking results for the Northern and Southern Hemispheres. Experimental results show that, under the current dataset construction and experimental setting, multi-channel inputs do not clearly outperform single-channel inputs for pressure regression. Among the evaluated model architectures, image types, and spectral channels, ResNet-18, full-resolution images, and Channel 1 generally provide the best single-channel performance for pressure regression. Performance also differs between the WP and AU datasets by regression target, with lower RMSE on WP for pressure regression and on AU for wind regression. These results highlight the importance of considering dataset-specific and task-specific characteristics when interpreting benchmark performance. Additionally, we analyzed systematic bias in intensity regression, which leads to underestimation of intensity for strong typhoons. We tested imbalance-aware weighting strategies, including inverse-density weighting inspired by BalancedMSE, and showed that these approaches are not sufficient to remove the bias. The dataset is publicly available at http://agora.ex.nii.ac.jp/digital-typhoon/dataset/ and https://github.com/kitamoto-lab/digital-typhoon/.

</details>


### [100] [When Should a Low-Light Pipeline Generate? Auditable Risk Control for Selective Generative Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21335)
*Qiyi Xie,Wei Chen Ng,Suyang Xi,Xicheng Wang,Ziyao Li,Bohong Xu,Wenyi Liu,Jian Ding Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VetoNet：面向选择性生成式低光增强的可审计区域路由框架，以"认证否决"为核心，仅在无分布假设的感知(LPIPS)风险预算允许时覆盖冻结的修复专家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式低光增强器能恢复合理纹理但可能虚构结构，修复网络保留结构但部分区域增强不足，且两种优势在空间上并不均匀，因此需要决定"在哪里"覆盖基专家。

Method: 将选择性生成增强建模为决策问题：先度量冻结的"修复-生成"专家对的互补性与路由方向，再仅用部署可得信号预测区域覆盖的效用，并借助无分布假设的伤害预算认证(perceptual LPIPS risk budget)来决定是否允许覆盖，证据不足即否决切换。

Result: 在多组专家对与多域实验中，VetoNet 的路由行为随证据强度与可靠性自适应：专家互补性可预测时取得统计显著的认证增益；专家相对优势变化时相应反转路由方向；证据弱或存在跨域偏移时保守否决切换。

Conclusion: 将选择性生成增强视为可审计的区域路由/否决决策，并配合分布无关的风险认证，可在保证感知风险可控的前提下获得增益，同时具备自适应与保守性。

Abstract: Generative low-light enhancers recover plausible texture but may hallucinate structure; restoration networks preserve structure but can leave regions under-enhanced, and neither advantage is spatially uniform. Therefore, we formulate selective generative enhancement as a decision problem: determine where overriding a frozen base expert is beneficial and refuse to switch when the evidence is insufficient. We propose VetoNet, an auditable regional routing framework centered on a certified veto. It first measures the complementary strengths and routing direction of a frozen restoration generative pair, then predicts the utility of regional overrides from deployment-available signals alone and admits an override only when a distribution-free harm-budget certificate says the perceptual (LPIPS) risk budget allows it. Experiments across multiple expert pairs and domains show that VetoNet adapts its routing behavior to the strength and reliability of the available evidence. When expert complementarity is sufficiently predictable, it achieves statistically significant certified gains; when the relative strengths of the experts change, it correspondingly reverses the routing direction; and when routing evidence is weak or affected by cross-domain shift, it conservatively vetoes switching.

</details>


### [101] [SCCL-DT: Semantic-Conflict-Aware Contrastive Learning with Dual-Rule Teacher Distillation for Zero-Shot Chest X-Ray Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22094)
*Ting Liu,Haoran Lai,HaobinChen,Ming Liu,S Kevin Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出语义冲突感知对比学习（SCCL），通过EMA统计自适应阈值并硬掩码假负样本，再经双向蒸馏得到可部署的SCCL-DT，在五个胸片基准上零样本AUC达0.850，优于最强基线0.837。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准图文对比预训练将批次内所有非对角图文对视为负样本，但放射科中不同患者常有重复发现，实例不匹配并不等于语义不兼容，由此产生"语义冲突"式假负监督。固定阈值随相似度分布漂移会失准，软重加权又会残留排斥梯度。

Method: 用EMA统计跟踪模态特定的相似度分布并推导自适应阈值，在softmax归一化前对疑似假负候选做硬掩码；以OR（包含）与AND（共识）规则构建两个SCCL教师，通过标准双向跨模态排序蒸馏整合为单一可部署学生模型SCCL-DT。作者还用训练轨迹审计验证在线掩码所选配对在报告来源发现一致性上更富集（仅为代理分析，不构成临床等价性证明）。

Result: 在五个公开胸片基准上，SCCL-DT零样本平均AUC为0.850，对比中最强基线为0.837。轨迹审计显示被掩码的配对在报告派生发现一致性上更富集。

Conclusion: 将批次内假负问题显式建模为语义冲突，并采用自适应分布跟踪阈值与硬掩码，可有效改善胸片零样本诊断；教师-学生蒸馏使方法保持单模型部署的实用性。作者指出代理审计不等同于临床等价性，代码将公开。

Abstract: Contrastive vision-language pre-training supports zero-shot chest X-ray diagnosis, but standard objectives treat every off-diagonal image-report pair in a batch as a negative. In radiology, recurring findings across patients mean that instance mismatch need not imply semantic incompatibility, creating false-negative supervision that we term a semantic conflict. A training-trace audit shows that pair-observations selected by the online mask used during training are enriched for report-derived finding agreement, although this proxy analysis does not establish clinical equivalence. Fixed thresholds can become miscalibrated as similarity distributions evolve, while soft reweighting leaves residual repulsive gradients. We therefore propose Semantic-Conflict-Aware Contrastive Learning (SCCL), which tracks modality-specific similarity distributions with exponential-moving-average (EMA) statistics, derives adaptive thresholds, and hard-masks flagged candidates before softmax normalization. Inclusive (OR) and consensus (AND) rules define two SCCL teachers, whose bidirectional cross-modal rankings are consolidated through standard distillation into a single deployable student, SCCL-DT. Across five public chest X-ray benchmarks, SCCL-DT achieves a mean zero-shot AUC of $0.850$, compared with $0.837$ for the strongest evaluated baseline in our comparison. Code will be made publicly available soon.

</details>


### [102] [FALCON: FPGA-Accelerated Latency-Certifiable Occupancy Networks for Camera-Based Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22143)
*Haohua Que,Mingkai Liu,Handong Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FALCON在AMD Kria KR260嵌入式FPGA上实现相机3D占用前端，以分层方式显式给出延迟保证：PL计算可解析周期界定、DPU推理用10万帧测量包络界定、看门狗在超时帧输出上一帧并标记stale。30万帧代理推理全部在100ms+≤10.2ms内交付，尾延迟比仅1.5–2.0倍，显著优于Jetson Orin(4.4×)与L4(23.6×)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 感知系统（如行人制动）的正确性要求取决于最坏情况每帧延迟，而非平均延迟；商用边缘加速器因动态调度、OS抖动、热降频与资源争用只优化均值，延迟可测但不可界定。作者因此主张把延迟保证做成"分层且显式"的工程属性。

Method: 在KR260（空闲4.3W、推理峰值6.6W）上构建相机3D占用前端：1）PL计算采用可解析周期界；2）DPU推理以100k帧迹线拟合测量包络；3）看门狗强制输出交付，超期帧发布上一帧并置stale标志；4）DPU不支持的两类算子——视图变换gather与双线性resize——实现为固定trip-count的PL IP，并与软件参考数值对齐。截止期实验以每帧6次ResNet-50 DPU推理的固定工作量代理代替完整网络。

Result: 三次10万帧强制运行共30万代理帧均在100ms截止期加最多10.2ms（三次中最差）内交付。完整六相机FlashOcc管线在板上约1.3s/帧，尚未达到10Hz。Occ3D-nuScenes验证集上mIoU 29.88，为DPU无法运行的FP32基线的92.8%。10万帧ResNet-50探针下KR260尾延迟中位数1.5倍以内（六推理代理2.0倍），而Jetson Orin为4.4倍、最差数据中心GPU（L4）达23.6倍。

Conclusion: 贡献在于证明"可界定延迟"可作为边缘FPGA上的显式设计目标，并以尾延迟分布而非均值来评价。当前限制同样明确：全网络仅~0.77FPS，代理与真实占用网络的时效性差距未被覆盖，mIoU仍低于FP32基线。部署配方、HLS源码与原始时序迹线已开源。

Abstract: A perception system that must brake for a pedestrian does not need a low average latency. It must deliver an answer before the deadline on every frame, the worst case included. Commodity edge accelerators optimize the mean through dynamic scheduling, OS jitter, thermal throttling, and contention, so their per-frame latency can be measured but not bounded. We build FALCON, a camera-based 3D occupancy front-end on the AMD Kria KR260, an embedded FPGA drawing 4.3 W idle and up to 6.6 W under inference, and make the latency guarantee layered and explicit: programmable-logic (PL) compute is cycle-bounded analytically; inference is a measured envelope over 100k-frame traces; and a watchdog enforces output delivery, publishing the previous output flagged stale when a frame is late. The deadline runs use a fixed-work proxy of six ResNet-50 DPU inferences per frame, not the full occupancy network: across three 100k-frame enforced runs, all 300k proxy frames delivered an output within the 100 ms deadline plus at most 10.2 ms, the worst of the three runs. The full six-camera FlashOcc pipeline composes to about 1.3 s per frame on the board and is not yet a 10 Hz system. The two operations the DPU cannot run, a view-transform gather and a bilinear resize, become fixed-trip-count PL IPs numerically matched to their software references. On the full Occ3D-nuScenes validation set the board reaches mIoU 29.88, 92.8% of an FP32 baseline the DPU cannot run. On a ResNet-50 probe over 100k frames the KR260 tail stays within 1.5 times its median (2.0 times for the six-inference proxy), where Jetson Orin reaches 4.4 times and the worst datacenter GPU run (NVIDIA L4) 23.6 times. The deployment recipe, HLS sources, and raw timing traces are released.

</details>


### [103] [EVA-Cap: Optimizing Audiovisual Video Captioning via Event-Centric Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22186)
*Jiapeng Shi,Qiuxia Hou,Jiaqi Leng,Junke Wang,Yanhao Zhang,Haonan Lu,Ziyi Ye,Zuxuan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EVA-Cap框架，将音视频视频描述分解为原子音视频事件，把无结构文本监督转化为以事件为中心的对齐，并通过SFT+GRPO训练，显著提升事件级细粒度对齐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Omni Language Models（OLM）做音视频视频描述时把任务当作无结构文本生成，忽略了视频内在的语义结构，导致细粒度音视频对齐次优。

Method: 将caption分解为原子音视频事件，把非结构化文本监督转为事件中心对齐；构建事件中心质量控制的EVA-Data数据集；先SFT再用GRPO微调，GRPO阶段围绕三个事件中心对齐目标优化：全局事件完整性、事件内归因（intra-event attribution）、事件间同步（inter-event synchronization）。

Result: 在事件中心评测上显著优于现有方法（ChronusAV平均+9.0%），在通用音视频描述基准上保持竞争力（WorldSense +3.3%）。

Conclusion: 事件中心的结构化监督与强化学习优化能有效提升音视频细粒度对齐，同时不牺牲通用描述能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in Omni Language Models (OLMs) provide a unified solution for audiovisual video captioning. Nevertheless, existing approaches predominantly treat captioning as an unstructured text generation process, neglecting the inherent semantic structure of videos and leading to suboptimal fine-grained audiovisual alignment. To bridge this gap, we propose EVA-Cap, a novel framework that decomposes captions into atomic audiovisual events and transforms unstructured text supervision into event-centric alignment. We train EVA-Cap via Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) on a self-curated dataset, EVA-Data, which is built with event-centric quality control. During the GRPO phase, the model is explicitly optimized against three event-centric alignment targets: global event completeness, intra-event attribution, and inter-event synchronization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EVA-Cap significantly outperforms existing methods in event-centric evaluation (e.g., +9.0% average gain on ChronusAV), while achieving competitive performance on general audiovisual captioning benchmarks (e.g., +3.3% on WorldSense).

</details>


### [104] [LAMP: Layerwise Adaptive Mixing of Predictors for Training-Free Few-Shot Vision-Language Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22182)
*Zhipeng Ye,Feng Jiang,Qiufeng Wang,Hao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LAMP：一种免训练的少样本分类框架，在类别分数空间对多层编码器的全局与区域核预测器进行自适应混合（LAMP-F进一步校准文本锚定原型），在11个数据集上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本适配通常只使用编码器最后一层的全局表示，中间层互补信息被浪费；而融合多层特征面临维度不一致、各层有效性随任务变化两大难题。

Method: 每视图单次前向获取所有层描述子；全局预测器与经支持集校准的区域核预测器分别独立拟合同一标签残差；由有标签验证数据在类别分数空间中学习混合权重，保持原生特征维度、无需学习投影。LAMP-F在固定核残差预测上利用缓存响应精确更新文本锚定类原型。

Result: 在ViT与ResNet骨干、11个数据集上验证：LAMP在4/8/16 shot下取得已发表免训练ResNet-50方法中最高平均精度，16 shot达78.73%；五档支持预算平均较仅全局特征基线在ViT-B/16、ResNet-50上分别提升1.14与1.59个百分点，LAMP-F提升至1.96与2.54个百分点，16 shot精度分别为83.57%与79.10%。

Conclusion: 消融表明多层集成是主要增益来源，并考察了池化、校准、验证标签与计算开销；结论是融合层特定预测可有效利用冻结编码器的互补特征来提升少样本分类。

Abstract: Few-shot adaptation often relies on an encoder's final global representation, leaving complementary information in intermediate layers underused. Combining these features is challenging because their dimensions can differ and their usefulness varies across tasks. We propose LAMP, Layerwise Adaptive Mixing of Predictors, a training-free framework that combines global and regional information from multiple encoder layers. A single forward pass per view supplies all descriptors. Global and support-calibrated regional kernel predictors independently fit the same label residuals. Labeled validation data select their mixture in class-score space, preserving native feature dimensions without learned projections. LAMP-F further adapts text-anchored class prototypes, using a cached response to update the fixed-kernel residual predictions exactly. Experiments cover eleven datasets and ViT and ResNet backbones. LAMP achieves state-of-the-art mean accuracy among the compared published training-free ResNet-50 methods at 4, 8, and 16 shots, reaching 78.73% at 16 shots. Averaged across five support budgets, LAMP improves over a paired global-feature-only baseline by 1.14 and 1.59 percentage points on ViT-B/16 and ResNet-50; LAMP-F raises these gains to 1.96 and 2.54 points. LAMP-F reaches 16-shot accuracies of 83.57% and 79.10%, respectively.
