Abstract: Typology-aware tokenizers are motivated by the claim that segmentation which respects morpheme boundaries preserves information that frequency-driven segmentation destroys. Studies routinely establish that it helps while assuming the boundaries must be linguistically correct. We separate them. Tagalog marks voice with infixes (s-um-ulat 'wrote', s-in-ulat 'was written'), so the root is not contiguous and no sequence of BPE merges can recover it. We build MorphAustro-Tok, a tokenizer that emits the root as a contiguous unit followed by a positional infix token, and compare it against a corpus-matched BPE baseline and, at two of three fertility levels, against a scrambled-morpheme control that preserves morph count, invertibility and per-type consistency while randomising the pseudo-infix. Across 33 runs, MorphAustro-Tok improves Tagalog machine translation over matched BPE by 0.84, 1.49 and 3.40 mean sentence-level chrF++ points. Token count does not explain the gain, since it grows as segmentation coarsens, and neither does capacity: at the coarsest level the scrambled arm carries more parameters and scores lower. Scrambling the analysis moves the gain by less than between-seed dispersion at both controlled levels, so we detect no contribution from morphological correctness at this scale.

</details>


### [141] [Reference-Guided Turn-Taking Style Transfer for Full-Duplex Spoken Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20763)
*Yuxuan Chen,Yang Xiao,Jack Sun,Chak Sun,Brick Li,Sam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文识别出决定全双工语音语言模型"何时开口"的单一后层残差流方向——initiative vector，它无需训练即可从模型自身对话中提取，既能读出也能调节模型的说话比例与轮次切换风格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工语音模型每帧都要决策"是否取得发言权"，这构成其对话人格，但每组权重只固定编码一种人格；人类说话者的人格是可灵活切换的。现有控制手段多为外部干预，无法指出决定"说多少话"的具体内部量。

Method: 从模型自身对话的残差流激活中提取单一后层方向（initiative vector），无训练、无标注；用于两项功能：一是读取（readout）发言决策并提前预测发言起始，二是在生成时按比例缩放该方向以干预行为；并用短参考录音进行风格迁移与特异性检验。

Result: 该方向可提前最多800 ms预测发言起始；缩放后说话比例从6–12%升至42–61%（不干预为21–30%），跨对话、权重与语料一致；短参考录音即可迁移说话者轮次风格，替换为他人参考时93–98%情况下匹配变差，而最佳统一剂量与不干预无差别。

Conclusion: 轮次切换风格并非冻结在权重中，而是可读、可调、可按个体校准的一个方向。

Abstract: Full-duplex spoken language models decide at every frame whether to take the floor, defining a conversational personality -- yet each set of weights encodes exactly one, which human speakers bracket rather than share. Existing controls mostly act from outside; none identifies the quantity that sets how much the model speaks. We identify it: a single late-layer residual-stream direction, the initiative vector, extracted from the model's own conversations without training or annotation. The same direction reads the decision, anticipating onsets up to 800 ms ahead, and shifts it: scaled during generation, it moves speech ratio from 6-12% to 42-61% (no steering: 21-30%), consistently across conversations, weights, and corpora. A short reference recording suffices to transfer a speaker's turn-taking style to the model, and the transfer is reference-specific: swapping in another speaker's reference worsens the match in 93-98% of cases, whereas the best uniform dose is indistinguishable from no steering. Turn-taking style is not frozen into the weights but a direction that can be read, shifted, and calibrated per person. Audio examples are available at https://elpsykongloo.github.io/initiative-vector/.

</details>


### [142] [Does Personalization Break Trust? Open-Set Rejection Calibration Under Few-Shot On-Device Intent Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20652)
*Vaibhav Prajapati,Pushp Garg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 揭示少样本LoRA个性化会损害域外（OOS）拒识能力，并给出用Mahalanobis距离替代softmax置信度的免训练修复方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧虚拟助手普遍用少样本LoRA进行个性化，但现有评测几乎只关注是否学会新命令，忽略了助手能否拒识不匹配任何意图的输入（OOS拒识），这一盲区可能导致适配后误响应。

Method: 在5个公开意图分类基准、每个5个随机种子上评估朴素少样本LoRA个性化对OOS拒识AUROC的影响；做评分器无关性检查以区分置信度评分器自身失准与真实效应；用嵌入空间中已知意图区域边界的收缩量（池化Spearman rho）做机制归因；提出以Mahalanobis距离替代softmax置信度进行零额外训练的拒识打分，并与两种OOD打分基线对比。

Result: OOS拒识AUROC在全部5个基准上均下降（平均降幅0.055–0.258），而域内准确率保持不变；评分器无关检查显示部分下降源于标准置信度评分器在适配下的失准，但任何评分器下仍存在较小的真实退化；该退化与已知意图区域嵌入间隔收缩显著相关（rho=0.61）；Mahalanobis打分在5个数据集中的3个上恢复大部分损失质量且无准确率代价，优于两个OOD打分基线，并在不同样本数、LoRA秩及第二个基座编码器上泛化。

Conclusion: 少样本LoRA个性化存在被忽视的OOS拒识退化风险，部分是评分器假象、部分是真实的嵌入间隔收缩；Mahalanobis距离打分是部署廉价的实用缓解手段，但并非在所有数据集上都有效，作者如实报告局限并开源代码以保障可复现性。

Abstract: Always-on virtual assistants increasingly personalize on-device to new user-specific commands via few-shot fine-tuning (e.g., LoRA), but this adaptation is evaluated almost exclusively on whether it learns the new commands, not on whether the assistant can still recognize an input that matches none of its intents - the out-of-scope (OOS) rejection problem. We show that naive few-shot LoRA personalization degrades OOS-rejection AUROC (area under the ROC curve) on every one of five public intent-classification benchmarks (mean drop $0.055$--$0.258$, five seeds each), while in-scope accuracy is preserved. A scorer-invariant check shows part of this drop is an artifact of the standard confidence scorer's own miscalibration under adaptation, though a smaller, genuine effect remains under any scorer; we trace it to a measurable shrinkage of the embedding margin around known-intent regions (pooled Spearman rho=0.61$). A deployment-cheap fix is scoring rejection by Mahalanobis distance instead of softmax confidence without extra training/retraining recovers most of the lost quality on 3 of 5 datasets at no accuracy cost, beats two further OOD scoring baselines, and generalizes across shot count, LoRA rank, and a second base encoder. We report honestly where the fix falls short and release all code for reproducibility.

</details>


### [143] [CoTS: Competitive Token Selection for Latent Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20642)
*Jie Yu,Yuxi Sun,Zhaoxu Li,Chunxiao Liu,Xudong Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出无需训练的竞争性token选择框架CoTS，用top-2候选概率竞争动态连续分配潜在干预强度，在六个推理基准上优于标准CoT与SOTA无训练潜在推理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在推理（软嵌入/隐状态替代采样token）能保留多样推理路径，但各解码步对潜在推理的需求不同：高置信步宜离散承诺，歧义步宜保留多种可能。现有方法仅通过全局软激活或基于不确定性阈值的硬切换处理，将潜在干预当作全有或全无的决策，无法连续调节强度。

Method: CoTS将潜在干预从二值模式选择重构为token级的连续分配：利用两个最可能候选之间的概率竞争（差值/分离度）衡量当前离散选择的稳定性——次优候选是最强替代，其与首选的距离直接反映稳定性。当某一候选明显占优时，保持表示接近采样出的离散token以维持稳定轨迹；竞争加剧时提升软表示的贡献以保留可行替代。该方法无需人工调阈值或混合系数，也不增加额外前向计算。

Result: 在六个推理基准实验中，CoTS一致优于标准CoT及当前最先进的无训练潜在推理方法。

Conclusion: 以top-2概率竞争实现的自适应、连续潜在干预是有效且免调参、零额外前向开销的方案，为潜在推理中离散承诺与软探索的平衡提供了新范式（代码已开源）。

Abstract: Recent latent reasoning methods move beyond discrete Chain-of-Thought (CoT) by replacing sampled tokens with continuous soft embeddings or hidden states, enabling richer intermediate representations and preserving alternative reasoning paths. However, the need for latent reasoning varies across decoding steps: confident predictions favor discrete commitment, whereas ambiguous steps benefit from preserving multiple plausible alternatives. Existing approaches typically handle this variation through either global soft activation or hard switching based on uncertainty thresholds, treating latent intervention as an all-or-nothing decision rather than continuously adapting its strength. To address this limitation, we propose Competitive Token Selection (CoTS), a training-free framework that reframes latent intervention from binary mode selection into continuous allocation at the token level. CoTS uses the probability competition between the two most likely candidates to dynamically balance discrete commitment and soft exploration at each reasoning step. The second candidate represents the strongest alternative to the model's preferred continuation, so the separation between the two directly reflects the stability of the current discrete choice. When one candidate clearly dominates, CoTS keeps the representation close to the sampled discrete token to preserve a stable reasoning trajectory; as the competition strengthens, it increases the contribution of the soft representation to retain plausible alternatives. This competition-based allocation requires neither manually tuned switching thresholds nor mixing coefficients, and introduces no additional forward passes. Experiments across six reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that CoTS consistently outperforms standard CoT and state-of-the-art training-free latent reasoning methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/OkinoYu/CoTSReasoning.

</details>


### [144] [Real-time Chatbot Romance Scam Detection in Stage Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20631)
*Yi-cheng Liu,Guan-ru Lai,Yi-cheng Lai,Lyn Chao-ling Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一个基于 LINE Messaging API 的实时聊天机器人，利用大语言模型与社会科学七阶段理论检测"杀猪盘"（romance scam）所处阶段，并在检测到诈骗后给出阶段解释与防范建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上的恋爱诈骗（romance scam）日益严重，且人工智能技术进步使伪造身份更加容易，亟需实用的实时检测与干预手段。

Method: 通过 LINE Messaging API 构建聊天机器人；以社会科学领域的七阶段理论作为诈骗进程框架，借助大语言模型对对话进行阶段识别；检测到恋爱诈骗后，再由大语言模型生成阶段说明与防范建议。

Result: 摘要未给出定量评估结果（如准确率、用户实验数据），仅说明系统实现了实时阶段检测与解释、建议生成功能。

Conclusion: 工作贡献在于提供一个可落地的实际应用，能够在真实场景中用于降低恋爱诈骗风险。

Abstract: Romance scam has become a serious problem via social media, and the improvement of artificial intelligence enable to fake identify easily. In the study, a chatbot has developed to detect romance scam in real-time through Line Messaging API. Using the seven stage theories in the social science field, the stages of romance scam can be detected through large language model. In addition, once a romance scam is detected, the chatbot provides explanations of the stage and prevention advices through large language model. The contribution of the work provides a practical application, and can be used in real scenario for reducing the risk of romance scam.

</details>


### [145] [RenoLayout: Unified Floorplan Renovation and Furniture Layout for Constraint-Aware Residential Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21366)
*Jingguo Liu,Yuanqing Liu,Chong Zhang,Yue Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 RenoLayout 框架，将住宅设计建模为设计点引导的条件生成任务：输入既有住宅状态与设计点集合，输出可执行的 Geometry-as-Code 程序；构建含 123 个细粒度设计点的 Design Map、基于前后差异的自动标注方法、三阶段 CPT–SFT–RL 训练流程，以及首个统一住宅设计基准（200 个真实改造案例）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有户型生成方法多以空建筑边界为输入，而改造需在既有户型上推理；且户型改造与家具布置被分开处理，忽略二者固有空间依赖。实际住宅设计需保留不可变结构、改造可编辑空间元素，并在设计目标与物理约束下优化家具布局。

Method: 将住宅设计形式化为设计点引导的条件生成任务；提出 RenoLayout，把设计前住宅状态与设计点集合翻译为可执行 Geometry-as-Code 程序。构建 Design Map（123 个细粒度设计点，覆盖结构改造与家具布局）；提出 Difference-Grounded Design-Point Annotation，从改造前后设计对中自动派生设计点集，并用 VLM 做一致性验证；采用 CPT–SFT–RL 三阶段训练，逐步提升空间推理、指令遵循与约束满足能力。同时构建 RenoLayout Benchmark（200 个真实改造前后案例，含完整家具布局及结构、建筑设备关键约束）。

Result: 摘要为方法/资源介绍型，未给出定量实验指标；主要产出为 RenoLayout 框架、123 点 Design Map、自动标注流水线与 200 案例的首个统一住宅设计基准。

Conclusion: 将户型改造与家具布局统一为结构保留约束下的条件生成问题，以代码化表示与分阶段训练实现可控监督，并为统一住宅设计提供首个评测基准。

Abstract: Residential design aims to transform existing homes into functional living spaces while satisfying diverse design preferences and physical constraints. However, existing floorplan generation methods typically assume empty building boundaries as input, whereas renovation requires reasoning over existing floorplans. Furthermore, floorplan renovation and furniture layout are commonly addressed as separate tasks, overlooking their inherent spatial dependency. Consequently, practical residential design must preserve immutable structural components while transforming editable spatial elements. It must also optimize furniture placement under design objectives and physical constraints. To address these challenges, we formulate residential design as a design-point-guided conditional generation task and propose RenoLayout, a framework that translates a pre-design residential state together with a design-point set into an executable Geometry-as-Code program. To enable controllable supervision, we construct a Design Map comprising 123 fine-grained design points covering structural renovation and furniture layout. We further introduce Difference-Grounded Design-Point Annotation to derive design-point sets automatically from before--after design pairs with VLM-based consistency verification. RenoLayout is trained using a three-stage CPT--SFT--RL pipeline, progressively improving spatial reasoning, instruction following, and constraint compliance. We also introduce the RenoLayout Benchmark, a set of 200 real-world before--after renovation cases with complete furniture layouts. To the best of our knowledge, it is the first benchmark for unified residential design. These cases include design-critical structural and building-service constraints.

</details>


### [146] [Emotion Is Not External Knowledge: Internal Attention Signals for Affective State Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21347)
*Wenqiu Tang,Zhen Wan,Yihui Wang,Takahiro Komamizu,Ichiro Ide*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出自定向内部注意力增强（SIAB），通过因果定位稀疏情感头并在答案位置增强其已关注的语义token，实现跨任务复用的推理期内情感状态路由，无需领域标注头或外部指定目标span。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在多轮交互中需维护隐式对话状态（如情感状态），这类状态由稀疏上下文线索推断且在话题切换后仍需保留。既有注意力干预方法依赖有标注的领域内头画像和外部指定的目标span，难以跨不同情感证据分布迁移。作者追问：LLM是否在推理时暴露出可用于路由此类证据的内部注意力信号。

Method: SIAB：在合成最小对比对上用因果定位（激活修补）识别稀疏情感头；在答案位置增强这些头本就关注的content token。编辑的头集与目标token均由模型自身行为导出，因而同一头集可跨任务复用。在ECC与GoEmotions、3个LLM、多种提示设置上评测，并与PASTA式外部监督结合；辅以机制分析（稀疏情感富集头、层级邻近性对可加性的影响）。

Result: 相对prompt匹配基线，ECC上最高+10.3 macro-F1、GoEmotions上+5.5；与PASTA式外部监督结合后，最高+13.84准确率点与+12.51 macro-F1点。机制分析显示增益可归因于稀疏的情感富集头，且层级邻近性影响可加性。

Conclusion: LLM内部存在可因果定位、可复用的稀疏情感注意力头，可作为推理期路由隐式情感证据的自监督信号，避免依赖领域标注头画像与外部指定span，并能与外部监督方法叠加增效。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly operate in multi-turn interactions that require maintaining latent conversational states, not only explicit facts. We study affective state as an implicit state inferred from sparse contextual cues that remain relevant after topic shifts, and ask whether LLMs expose internal attention signals for routing such evidence at inference time. Prior attention interventions rely on labeled in-domain head profiles and externally specified target spans, limiting transfer across affective-evidence distributions. We propose Self-targeted Internal Attention Boosting (SIAB), which localizes sparse affective heads via causal localization (in detail activation patching) on synthetic minimal pairs and boosts, at the answer position, the content tokens these heads already attend to; both edited heads and target tokens are derived from the model's own behavior, enabling the same head set to be reused across tasks.We evaluate on ECC and GoEmotions across three LLMs and multiple prompting settings. Across settings, SIAB improves prompt-matched baselines by up to +10.3 macro-F1 on ECC and +5.5 on GoEmotions. Combined with PASTA-style external supervision, it achieves up to +13.84 accuracy points and +12.51 macro-F1 points, while mechanistic analyses trace the gains to sparse, emotion-enriched heads and show that layer proximity affects additivity.

</details>


### [147] [Stop Training Against Soft Users: Mentalized Adversarial Self-Play for Proactive Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21156)
*Zanwu Liu,Dan Zhang,Xiaodi Wang,Yongbo Mao,Xiaoyang Guo,Yonglin Wu,Peiyue Jiang,Yangyang Wei,Zecheng Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MASP——一种"心智化对抗自博弈"框架，将主动对话建模为系统与"理性抵抗型用户"的部分可观测博弈，把对理性抵抗的鲁棒性内化为训练目标，在多个主动对话基准上提升成功率与理性约束成功率、缩短平均轮数，并对"难缠用户"表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动对话规划器（搜索、学习策略、用户感知规划）通常基于固定或过度顺从的模拟用户进行训练与评估，导致高模拟成功率可能源于"软用户偏差"而非真实鲁棒对话能力；作者希望把对理性抵抗用户的鲁棒性从评测条件转变为训练目标。

Method: MASP将主动对话视为系统与理性抵抗用户之间的部分可观测博弈；用基于BDI的隐状态刻画用户演化的心智状态；将"奖励侧教师心智化"与"策略侧学生心智化"分离；对系统策略与用户策略交替进行自博弈更新。

Result: 在多个主动对话基准上，与常用的SFT与仅规划器（Planner-only）方法相比，MASP显著提升成功率（SR）与理性约束成功率（RCSR），降低平均轮数（AT），且在使任务目标更难达成的"难缠用户"上表现更好。

Conclusion: 通过对抗自博弈与理性抵抗用户建模，可将鲁棒性直接纳入训练目标，缓解模拟用户顺从性带来的评估失真，为主动对话智能体提供更强的多轮引导与适应能力。

Abstract: Proactive dialogue agents must guide multi-turn interactions toward task goals while adapting to users whose intentions and attitudes evolve. Existing planners improve system side action selection through search, learned policies, or user aware planning, but are often trained and evaluated against fixed or overly compliant simulators; as a result, high simulated success can reflect soft user bias rather than robust dialogue ability. We introduce MASP, a Mentalized Adversarial Self Play framework that treats proactive dialogue as a partially observable game between a system and a rationally resisting user. MASP represents the user's evolving mental state with a BDI based latent state, separates reward side teacher mentalization from policy side student mentalization, and alternates self play updates for the system and user policies. This turns robustness to rational resistance into a training objective rather than only an evaluation condition. Across diverse proactive dialogue benchmarks, compared with the widely used SFT and Planner-only approaches, MASP substantially improves success rate (SR) and rationality constrained success (RCSR), reduces average turns (AT), and performs better against hard users who make the task goal more difficult to achieve.

</details>


### [148] [Explaining the Roles of Experts in Mixture-of-Experts LLMs for Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22127)
*Sekh Mainul Islam,Pepa Atanasova,Isabelle Augenstein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出将大语言模型个性化应用于MoE架构，利用稀疏专家路由降低多义性，并通过crosscoder学习可解释的个性化隐变量，实现对个性化组件的定位、理解与因果控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM个性化方法主要针对稠密模型，其神经元具有多义性（单个神经元编码多个无关概念），导致难以定位个性化所对应的模型组件，从而阻碍对个性化机制的理解与控制。

Method: 在三个开源权重MoE LLM上基于大规模个性化数据集进行适配；在个性化与非个性化模型的成对激活上训练crosscoder，学习可解释的个性化隐变量，并据此识别编码不同个性化特征的专家；通过因果干预（隐变量恢复、专家路由增强）验证其作用。

Result: 个性化MoE模型仅激活少量参数即可达到与稠密LLM相当的竞争性能；相比活跃参数量相近的稠密模型具有更低的多义性；同族模型内个性化隐变量与非个性化模型共享更少；因果干预表明这些隐变量足以恢复个性化模型行为，增强对应专家可使非个性化模型向个性化模型偏移，并揭示不同个性化特征存在不同路由路径。

Conclusion: MoE架构的稀疏路由能有效缓解多义性，为个性化的可解释性与可控性提供可行路径，实现了个性化机制从"黑箱"到可定位、可干预的转变。

Abstract: Personalization of Large Language Models (LLMs) is increasingly important for user-centric applications, yet prior methods mainly adapt dense LLMs, whose polysemantic neurons, each encoding multiple unrelated concepts, hinder localizing the components behind personalization, and thus understanding and controlling it. We propose to personalize Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) LLMs, which sparsely route each token to a few specialized experts, reducing polysemanticity and enabling such localization. We adapt three open-weight MoE LLMs on a large-scale personalization dataset and train crosscoders on paired activations of the personalized and non-personalized models to learn interpretable personalized latents, from which we identify the experts encoding distinct personalization features. The personalized MoE LLMs perform competitively with dense LLMs while activating only a fraction of their parameters, exhibit lower polysemanticity than dense LLMs with similar active parameters, and, within the same model family, yield personalized latents that are less shared with the non-personalized model. Causal interventions show that these latents suffice to recover the personalized model's behavior and that boosting their associated experts moves the non-personalized model toward it, revealing distinct routing paths for different personalization features.

</details>


### [149] [Learning to Read Out: Unembedding Dynamics in Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21025)
*Matteo He,William F. Shen,Alex Iacob,Andrej Jovanovic,Xinchi Qiu,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文指出以输出行为判定能力出现时间会混淆"模型编码"与"读出表达"，提出参数轨迹交叉编码追踪readout，发现隐藏态早于readout表达任务信息，且该间隙受readout学习率调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展性研究通常以模型何时生成正确token来判定能力获得时间，但该预测须经过仍在学习的unembedding矩阵（readout）。作者希望厘清行为时间线究竟反映模型何时学到信息，还是readout何时学会表达，从而避免将二者混为一谈。

Method: 提出parameter-trajectory crosscoding：对每个checkpoint中每个token的readout行拟合同一个稀疏字典，仅需checkpoint权重（区别于基于激活的crosscoder）。用其在Pythia预训练中定位readout重组窗口并检验WordNet词类可解码性；同时从头预训练仅在readout学习率上不同的小模型做因果验证。

Result: 在Pythia预训练0.4%–1.1%的狭窄早期窗口内，readout发生重组，WordNet词类可由其行解码；此时隐藏态上的线性探针已能解码模型输出尚未体现的任务信息。提高readout学习率使readout更早发育、间隙更快闭合，而最终loss几乎不受影响。

Conclusion: 训练早期（许多发展性转变被定年的阶段），行为时间点可能反映readout学会表达任务信息的时刻，而非模型真正习得该信息的时刻；评估能力获得时间需区分编码与读出。

Abstract: When does a language model acquire a capability? Developmental studies usually answer this by tracking when models start producing the correct token. But that prediction passes through the unembedding matrix, or readout, which is itself still being learned during pretraining. We show that a model's hidden states can encode task information before its own readout expresses it, so timelines read from outputs conflate what the model encodes with what its readout expresses. To track the readout directly, we introduce parameter-trajectory crosscoding, which fits one sparse dictionary to every token's readout row at every checkpoint. Unlike crosscoders on activations, it needs only checkpoint weights. We use it to locate a narrow early window, 0.4-1.1% of the way through Pythia's pretraining, in which the readout reorganizes and WordNet word categories become decodable from its rows. By then, linear probes on the hidden states already decode task information that the model's own outputs do not yet show. To test whether such gaps depend on how fast the readout learns, we pretrain small models from scratch that differ only in the readout's learning rate. We find that raising it makes the readout develop earlier and the gap close sooner, with little effect on final loss. Together, these results imply that early in training, where many developmental transitions are dated, a behavioral date can reflect when the readout learns to express task information and not when the model learns it.

</details>


### [150] [Same Context, Different Agents: A Behavioral Study of Semantic Layers, Orchestration and Compaction in Data Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20959)
*Zhivar Sourati,Marianne Menglin Liu,Zheng Wang,Rongguang Wang,Sai Ashish Somayajula,Tao Sheng,Kyu J. Han,Sujith Ravi,Dan Roth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统分析三种上下文工程技术（语义层、编排、压缩）在数据智能体中的单独与组合效果，发现其收益取决于智能体行为分组：只有"过早作答型"在编排下能把收益转化为准确率提升，而"调查型"智能体反而可能被同类技术损害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构数据问答需要融合文件、数据库、文档与网络证据，数据智能体依赖上下文质量。现有研究仅单独验证单一上下文工程技术（CET）的效果，未揭示智能体行为如何影响其成效，缺乏对多种CET单独及组合作用机制的全面理解。

Method: 在5个基准的1,514个长时程任务上，用7个大型语言模型对三种CET（语义层、编排、压缩）进行单独与组合的系统性评估，从行为、准确率与成本三个维度展开分析。

Result: 无CET的数据智能体呈现三种行为分组：调查型、过早作答型（未检查数据即作答）与过早放弃型。每种CET均能带来其已知收益（更多调查、更短提示等），但只有当CET与智能体行为分组匹配时才能转化为准确率提升（如编排之于过早作答型）。对调查型智能体，同类CET反而有害：语义层抑制其探索，编排导致过度自信使准确率下降5个百分点，压缩导致步骤重复。多个CET组合的增益与损失均小于各自之和。

Conclusion: CET的效果取决于智能体行为，应按智能体行为、任务需求及准确率-成本权衡来选择CET，而非默认叠加使用。

Abstract: Answering questions over heterogeneous data means combining evidence from files, databases, documents and the web. Data agents automate this, but their success depends on context. Three context-engineering techniques (CETs) help by shaping it: semantic layers describe the data, orchestration splits work among agents, and compaction condenses interaction history. Existing studies validate each CET alone, leaving open how agent behavior shapes its effect. We present a comprehensive analysis of the three CETs, alone and combined, with seven large language models (LLMs) on 1,514 long-horizon tasks from five benchmarks, covering behavior, accuracy and cost. We find that data agents without CETs form three behavior groups: investigators, premature answerers that answer without inspecting data, and early stoppers that give up. Each CET delivers its known benefit, from more investigation to smaller prompts, but only the behavior group it matches turns that benefit into accuracy, such as premature answerers under orchestration. For investigators, the same CETs can hurt: the semantic layer stops them from looking, orchestration makes them overconfident, costing 5 percentage points, and compaction makes them repeat steps. Combining CETs yields smaller gains and losses than their sum. We derive guidance for choosing CETs by agent behavior, task demands and accuracy-cost trade-offs.

</details>


### [151] [PEDBench: Can Speech Models Detect Phonetic Errors?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20830)
*Zhonghao Shi,Zackary Rackauckas,Jasmine Yip,Savannah Halpern,Simon Campos,Lauren Brace,Emma Yang,Tiantian Feng,Xuan Shi,Shrikanth Narayanan,Teresa Satterfield,Ying Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出首个音素级错误检测基准PEDBench，涵盖自然、受控与合成三类语音，评测13个SOTA语音模型，发现其检测能力与精确率均不足，并验证合成数据微调可改善词级检测但仍存在仿真-真实域差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音模型在音素识别上进展显著，但因缺乏高质量评测数据，其细粒度音素错误检测（替代、删除、插入）的可靠性尚不明确，需要系统性基准加以诊断。

Method: 构建PEDBench基准，包含儿童真实发音错误录音（N=334）、演员脚本化错误发音（N=1,188）及基于音素条件TTS的合成错误发音（N=1,083），全部标注由两名受过音素训练的人员转写并核验；在此基础上评测13个SOTA语音模型，并用合成数据微调自研语音语言模型。

Result: 13个模型即使在声学变异较小的受控（演员/合成）条件下也无法可靠检测音素错误，且精确率低，常将正确发音误判为错误；基于合成数据微调的语音语言模型在词级PED上优于商业与开源基线，但仍存在仿真到真实的域差距。

Conclusion: 现有模型在音素级错误检测上仍显著不足，PEDBench为诊断与改进提供了统一评测平台；合成数据微调是有效方向但需缩小sim-to-real差距，作者将公开代码、配方及演员与合成数据集。

Abstract: Phonetic error detection (PED) aims to identify phoneme-level substitutions, deletions, and insertions and is essential for educational and clinical speech assessment and intervention. Despite recent advances in speech models for phoneme recognition, their ability to reliably detect fine-grained phonetic errors remains unclear because of a lack of high-quality evaluation data. To address this gap, we introduce PEDBench, the first PED benchmark spanning a continuum from natural to controlled speech. It comprises real-world recordings of spoken errors made by children (N=334), scripted error productions by human actors (N=1,188), and synthetic error productions generated using phone-conditioned text-to-speech (N=1,083). All phonetic annotations were transcribed and verified by two phonetically trained annotators. Our evaluation of 13 state-of-the-art speech models shows that they fail to reliably detect phonetic errors, even under controlled conditions with acted and synthetic speech, where acoustic variability is reduced. The models also exhibit low precision, frequently misclassifying correctly pronounced phonemes as errors. Our speech-language models, fine-tuned on synthetic data, outperform commercial and open-source baselines on word-level PED, although a sim-to-real domain gap remains. We plan to publicly release our code and recipes, along with our acted and synthetic datasets, to support the development of more accurate PED systems.

</details>


### [152] [PRISM: Transfer and Limits of Activation Interventions in Tool-Calling Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20808)
*Giovana Kerche Bonás,Thales Sales Almeida,Marcelo da Silva Reis,Rodrigo Nogueira,Marcos Meideiros Raimundo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 PRISM 框架，利用量化(NF4)标签与跨语言迁移来评估和迁移激活干预，以暴露并缓解模型"文本拒绝但工具调用不安全"的隐藏漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献显示语言模型常出现"文本层面拒绝、但仍执行不安全工具调用"的不一致行为，这种隐藏脆弱性难以被常规评估发现，需要系统性框架来暴露并改进对齐。

Method: PRISM 框架，考察激活干预在精度（NF4 量化）、语言与模型规模三个维度上的迁移；使用 NF4 副本产生的行为标签估计干预方向，并通过配对良性激活将安全干预映射到更大模型。

Result: 极少场景下 NF4 标签即可稳定估计干预；五语言 NF4 监督方向可零样本迁移至未参与训练的阿拉伯语，使 Llama-3.1-8B 的不安全违规率下降 21.95 个百分点；干预亦成功迁移至最大 70B 的模型。

Conclusion: 来自压缩模型与多语言数据的辅助监督能有效揭示并缓解跨模型家族的危害性工具调用行为；代码与交互式结果已开源。

Abstract: Recent literature shows that unsafe tool calls can often accompany textual refusals in language models. To investigate this hidden vulnerability, we introduce PRISM, a framework for evaluating the transfer of activation interventions across precision, languages, and scales. Our findings demonstrate that leveraging lower-resource settings (specifically quantized NF4 labels and cross-lingual transfer) offers a robust pathway to expose and improve model alignment. Notably, using behavioral labels from an NF4 copy enables stable intervention estimation even with very few scenarios. Furthermore, these alignment improvements transfer effectively across languages: five-language NF4-supervised directions successfully transfer to held-out Arabic, reducing unsafe violations in Llama-3.1-8B by 21.95 percentage points without target-language tuning. By mapping paired benign activations, we also successfully transfer these safety interventions to much larger models (up to 70B parameters). Ultimately, PRISM highlights how auxiliary supervision from compressed models and multilingual data can uncover and mitigate hazardous tool-calling behaviors across different model families. We release all experimental code and supporting artifacts at https://github.com/GiKerche/prism, alongside an interactive explorer of recorded model responses and experimental results at https://gikerche.github.io/prism/.

</details>


### [153] [AbridgeKV: Compressing Long Reasoning into the KV Cache of Learned Pause Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20641)
*Jack Cai,Lijie Yang,Vasilis Kontonis,Dimitris Papailiopoulos,Shivam Garg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AbridgeKV 训练模型将已完成生成文本块压缩为少量可学习 pause token 的 KV 条目，从而在长链推理下把生成的 KV cache 压缩到约四分之一而不损失精度与输出长度，并在 vLLM 中实现单序列 3.5 倍更小显存、饱和 H100 上 3.2 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链思维链解码是访存受限的：生成 token 的 KV cache 随测试时计算线性增长。直接驱逐 cache 条目不能解决问题，因为未在此类被裁剪的 cache 上训练过的模型会产生更长的输出，常常达到长度上限。

Method: 改为训练模型压缩自身输出：将每个已完成的生成文本块压缩为少量可学习 pause token 的 KV 条目（pause token 为不产生输出的额外输入 token，嵌入可训练）。pause token 编码完成后释放该文本块原始 cache，后续 token 改为关注 pause token 的 KV。三个关键设计：(1) pause token 的 KV 条目位于其所替代文本块内部的相对位置；(2) 按各层条目价值测量结果在层间分配 cache 预算；(3) 在相对较大的块上执行压缩。

Result: 在 AIME、HMMT、AMC、BRUMO、CMIMC、SMT 共 446 道竞赛题、32K token 生成设置下，保留四分之一生成 cache 即可在准确率与输出长度上匹配同等训练的全 KV 模型；匹配预算下最强已发表驱逐方法输出显著更长，最接近的训练式压缩器在同数据重训后掉 10 分。在 vLLM 部署中单序列 cache 缩小 3.5 倍，饱和 H100 上完成一题比全 KV 快 3.2 倍。

Conclusion: 通过让模型端到端学习压缩自身生成文本的 KV 表示，可在长链推理中大幅降低 KV cache 显存与延迟，同时保持准确率和输出长度，为长测试时计算的部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought decoding is memory-bound: the KV cache of the generated tokens grows linearly with the test-time compute being spent. Evicting cache entries does not fix this, because a model never trained on a cache with entries removed generates longer, often up to the length limit. We instead train the model to compress its own output. AbridgeKV compresses each completed block of generated text into the KV entries of a few learned pause tokens. These are extra input tokens with trained embeddings that produce no output. Once the pause tokens have encoded the block, AbridgeKV frees the block's original cache; subsequent tokens attend to the pause tokens' KV entries in its place. Three novel choices make this work: the pause tokens' KV entries take positions inside the block they replace, the cache budget is allocated across layers according to the measured value of each layer's entries, and compression operates on relatively large blocks. On 446 competition problems from AIME, HMMT, AMC, BRUMO, CMIMC and SMT with 32K-token generations, keeping a quarter of the generated cache matches an identically trained full-KV model in accuracy and output length; the strongest published evictor at a matched budget writes far longer, and the closest trained compressor, retrained on the same data, loses 10 points. Served in vLLM, the cache is 3.5x smaller per sequence and a saturated H100 finishes a problem 3.2x faster than with full KV.

</details>


### [154] [Exploring the Design Space of Permutation Diffusion LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20635)
*Zhe Xu,Mingfu Liang,Kavosh Asadi,Jean-Pascal Billaud,Tianyi Chen,Yanhui Guo,Christina Huang,Baekjin Kim,Jimmy Kim,Yufei Li,Daqi Sun,Jiachen Sun,Dian Yu,Zaiwei Zhang,Kaushik Rangadurai,Frank Shyu,Sandeep Pandey,Xi Liu,Luke Simon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型下的置换生成问题，比较了三种基于扩散语言模型的参数化方案（硬分配、软分配、直接排序分数），并在Amazon三个领域的重排序任务上评估其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 置换生成需要同时满足文本条件排序与候选到输出位置的一对一全局分配约束。不同参数化方案在预测与解码的不同阶段施加约束，因而对应不同的训练目标与采样流程，但缺乏系统性对比。

Method: 通过三种扩散LLM参数化方式实现：(1) 在打乱的候选索引序列上做硬分配预测；(2) 在双随机矩阵上做软分配预测；(3) 直接预测排序分数后排序。在Amazon三个领域、候选集规模10和20的重排序任务上进行评估。

Result: 在单次函数评估下，硬分配与软分配变体的Recall@1与受约束掩码扩散基线相当；直接排序分数预测无需匈牙利分配或Sinkhorn归一化即可保证合法置换。增加函数评估次数并不稳定提升排序质量，其效果强烈依赖状态表示与训练目标。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在采用语言模型作为去噪器的置换扩散中，扰动方式、预测参数化、目标函数与解码器应进行联合设计。

Abstract: Permutation generation with language models requires both text-conditioned ranking and a global one-to-one assignment between candidates and output positions. We study this problem through three diffusion-LLM parameterizations: hard-assignment prediction over shuffled candidate-index sequences, soft-assignment prediction over doubly stochastic matrices, and direct ordering-score prediction followed by sorting. The three families place permutation constraints at different stages of prediction and decoding and therefore support different training objectives and sampling procedures. We evaluate these designs on recommendation reranking across three Amazon domains with candidate sets of size 10 and 20. At one function evaluation, the hard- and soft-assignment variants achieve Recall@1 comparable to a constrained masked-diffusion baseline, whereas direct ordering-score prediction guarantees valid permutations without Hungarian assignment or Sinkhorn normalization. Additional function evaluations do not consistently improve ranking quality, and their effect depends strongly on the state representation and training objective. These results show that corruption, prediction parameterization, objective, and decoder should be designed jointly for permutation diffusion with language-model denoisers.

</details>


### [155] [Jev-as-a-Judge for RAG Claim Verification: Confidence-Gated Cascading Between a Non-Generative Verifier and a Frontier LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20634)
*Alden Do Rosario,Kirollos Atef*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在495条人工标注claim上，非生成式决策模型与前沿LLM在二分类指标上无显著差异（-0.56分，187倍成本差），但二者错误结构不同：廉价模型对不支持claim的放行率23.2% vs 14.1%；路由级联可提质降本，然随机升级对照显示节省来自升级比例而非置信度挑选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG中"证据是否支持该claim"本质是1比特判断，但主流流水线用文本生成器回答并为附带文字付生成价格；同时仅用传统单一指标评估此类判定组件，可能掩盖部署时真正重要的错误结构（如漏放不支持claim）。

Method: 构建within-item配对比较：非生成式决策模型（返回声明枚举中的类型化取值+原生概率、不输出文本）vs 按工程部署方式配置为二分类的前沿LLM；同一495条LLM-AggreFact人工标注claim。报告配对差异、系统间不一致率与不支持claim放行率，并做置信度阈值路由级联、样本外阈值选择、以及同预算随机升级对照。

Result: (1) 传统指标上二者统计不可区分：配对差-0.56分（Jev减Astra），成本差187倍；(2) 该打平几乎不含部署信息：系统在15.2%的claim上不一致，廉价模型放行23.2%的标注为不支持的claim，前沿模型为14.1%；(3) 级联达74.10 @ USD 2.92/千条，对比73.83 @ USD 8.93（阈值样本外选定），但同预算的随机升级对照（样本内阈值）表现相当；相对廉价模型，级联成本高61倍仅换0.83分，95% CI [-3.36, 4.86]，且级联是两者中更宽松的守卫。

Conclusion: 准确率相等掩盖了错误的不相等；判定组件应按其放行不支持claim的比率评估，而非仅看传统headline指标。级联的节省主要来自升级比例而非置信度选择的正确性，成本-收益在此区间并不划算。全部预测、提示、定价与代码已开源。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation asks one question at volume: does this evidence support this claim? The answer is one bit, yet most pipelines obtain it from a text generator and pay generation prices for the prose wrapped around it. We compare a non-generative decision model, which returns a typed value from a declared enumeration together with a native probability and no text, against a frontier LLM configured as an engineer would deploy it for binary classification. Both answer the same 495 human-labelled claims from LLM-AggreFact, so every comparison is within-item. We report three things. First, the two systems are statistically indistinguishable on the metric this task is conventionally scored by, a paired difference of -0.56 points, Jev minus Astra, at a 187-fold cost difference. Second, that tie carries almost no information about deployment: the systems disagree on 15.2% of claims, and the cheaper one admits 23.2% of the claims annotators marked unsupported against the frontier model's 14.1%. A component whose job is to catch unsupported claims should be judged on that rate, and the conventional headline hides it. Third, because the errors differ they compose: routing the claims the cheap model is confident about and escalating the rest reaches 74.10 at USD 2.92 per thousand claims against 73.83 at USD 8.93, with the threshold chosen out of fold. But a matched random-escalation control at in-sample thresholds, spending the same budget by coin flip, does as well, so what buys the saving is the escalation share and not the probability that picks what to escalate. Measured against the cheap model it is built from, the cascade costs 61-fold more for 0.83 points, 95% CI [-3.36, 4.86], and it is the more permissive guard of the two. Equal accuracy conceals unequal errors. All predictions, prompts, pricing and code are released.

</details>


### [156] [Beyond Downstream QA: Rethinking How We Evaluate Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22209)
*Sebastian-Antonio Toma,Jean de Kernier,Mike Ply,Oliviu Matei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 FEB-30 基准，通过"范式反转"构造 30 条事实演化轨迹并生成文档，实现无需人工标注的确定性真值，通过直接检视记忆库来分离记忆正确性与检索/生成质量，揭示现有下游问答准确率评估范式的不完备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 agent 记忆基准以下游问答准确率作为记忆质量的代理，将记忆正确性与检索、生成质量混为一谈，导致假阳性（正确回答掩盖损坏的记忆）与假阴性（错误回答归咎于正确记忆的检索失败），无法诊断记忆系统本身是否真正完成了事实的创建、更新、强化与替代。

Method: 提出范式反转（paradigm inversion）：先定义已知的事实演化轨迹，再由轨迹反向生成多作者制品（文档、邮件、对话），从而获得确定性真值、免除人工标注；每条轨迹规定事实在多次摄入中应如何演化，可在每个摄入步骤后直接检视记忆库。基准含 30 条轨迹，覆盖 6 个诊断类别与 3 个难度层级。

Result: 在代表性记忆系统上，内在评估与外在评估平均相差 16 个百分点，44% 的轨迹超过显著分歧阈值；诊断分解显示系统对记忆操作（create、update、reinforce、supersede）的正确分类仅 20%，该失败在外在评估中完全不可见；失败模式按类别与难度存在系统性差异。

Conclusion: 下游问答准确率不足以衡量记忆质量，需以内在下游解耦的诊断式评估替代；FEB-30 及文档生成流水线与评分框架开源，以支持可复现的 agent 记忆系统评估。

Abstract: Agent memory benchmarks evaluate whether an agent's responses reflect correct knowledge, measuring downstream question-answering accuracy as a proxy for memory quality. We show this evaluation paradigm is incomplete: it conflates memory correctness with retrieval and generation quality, producing both false positives where correct responses mask broken memory and false negatives where incorrect responses blame correct memory for retrieval failures. We introduce FEB-30, a benchmark of 30 fact-evolution trajectories spanning 6 diagnostic categories and 3 difficulty levels, constructed via paradigm inversion: documents are generated from known fact trajectories, yielding deterministic ground truth without human annotation. Each trajectory defines how a fact should evolve across multi-author artifacts (documents, emails, conversations), enabling direct inspection of the memory store after each ingestion step. Applying FEB-30 to a representative memory system, we demonstrate that intrinsic and extrinsic evaluation diverge by an average of 16 percentage points, with 44% of trajectories exceeding meaningful divergence thresholds. Diagnostic decomposition reveals that the system correctly classifies memory operations (create, update, reinforce, supersede) only 20% of the time, a failure entirely invisible to extrinsic evaluation, and that failure modes differ systematically by category and difficulty. We release the benchmark, document generation pipeline, and scoring framework to support reproducible evaluation of agent memory systems.

</details>


### [157] [Hy-Memory-30B-A3B: Attributed Reinforcement Learning for Long-Term Memory Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22196)
*Tianxiang Fei,Jiazheng Kang,Mao Zheng,Xingyan He,Xiang Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出紧凑型长期记忆写入模型 Hy-Memory-30B-A3B，通过记忆在线策略蒸馏与记忆强化学习，跨 Mem0、MemOS、Hy-Memory 三种框架统一执行写入协议，在四个评测框架（含未见的 EverMemOS）上均排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部记忆系统依赖模型执行写入路径（决定保留什么、如何精确表示、新证据如何更新持久状态），但现有框架通用大模型加框架专用提示，服务成本高，且针对单一协议特化的写入器无法迁移到其他框架，缺乏可跨框架统一操作的模型。

Method: 1) Memory On-Policy Distillation：在学生模型自身生成的状态上教授各框架专属写入协议；2) Memory RL：用稠密分块探针、可归因的跨块奖励、过度存储控制与块级 GRPO 优化下游可回答性；3) 跨窗口携带策略写入的记忆库，保持训练与部署一致。

Result: 在固定的 20% 基准子样本上，于四个评测框架（含留出的 EverMemOS）全部排名第一，宏平均 55.49%，对比 Claude Opus 5 的 54.62%。

Conclusion: 紧凑的专用记忆写入模型可跨多个记忆框架统一运行，在降低服务成本的同时取得优于强通用大模型的记忆写入质量，并具备对未见框架的泛化能力。

Abstract: External-memory systems extend language-model applications beyond a context window, but their quality depends critically on the model executing the write path: deciding what to retain, how precisely to represent it, and how new evidence should update persistent state. Existing frameworks typically place a general-purpose large language model behind framework-specific prompts. This raises serving cost, and a writer specialized for one protocol does not transfer directly to another; a unified model that can operate across established memory frameworks remains largely unexplored. We present Hy-Memory-30B-A3B, a compact long-term-memory writer trained across Mem0, MemOS, and Hy-Memory. Memory On-Policy Distillation teaches framework-specific write protocols on student-generated states; Memory RL then optimizes downstream answerability using dense per-chunk probes, attributed cross-chunk rewards, over-storage controls, and chunk-level GRPO. Carrying policy-written banks across windows keeps training aligned with deployment. On fixed 20% benchmark subsamples, Hy-Memory-30B-A3B ranks first on all four evaluated harnesses, including held-out EverMemOS, with a 55.49% macro average versus 54.62% for Claude Opus 5.

</details>


### [158] [PremiseSQL: Evaluating Premise Critique and Abstention in Text-to-SQL Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22158)
*Shuo Lin,Jinzhe Li,Chenlu Guo,Yanhan Li,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 PremiseSQL 基准与评估框架，考察 NL2SQL 模型对"错误前提"查询的识别与拒答能力（premise critique ability），发现现有 LLM 默认过度信任前提、很少质疑，批判性提示虽有改善但带来误判与正常 SQL 生成抑制的代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Text-to-SQL 系统普遍假设用户查询有效且可回答，导致模型对前提有缺陷（flawed premise）的查询仍生成可执行但误导性的 SQL，缺乏对错误前提的识别与弃答能力。

Method: 构建 PremiseSQL 基准，包含覆盖四类前提失效的 1,800 条缺陷查询及其对应的 1,800 条原始查询；设计评估框架，在三种提示设置下测量模型内在与提示诱导的前提感知行为。

Result: 前沿 LLM 实验显示：模型默认高度"信任前提"，极少批判缺陷前提；更强的批判导向提示可提升缺陷查询的检测与弃答率，但同时增加对原始查询的误判为无效，并抑制正常 SQL 生成。

Conclusion: NL2SQL 中的前提批判能力是被忽视的关键维度，当前模型在该能力上存在信任偏差与误判的权衡，需在检测缺陷前提与保持正常生成之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have substantially improved Text-to-SQL performance, yet existing NL2SQL systems largely assume that user queries are valid and answerable. As a result, models often generate executable SQL even for queries with flawed premises. We introduce premise critique ability in NL2SQL, namely the ability to recognize flawed queries and abstain from generating misleading SQL. To evaluate this capability, we propose PremiseSQL, a benchmark containing 1,800 flawed NL2SQL queries across four categories of premise failures, along with their 1,800 original queries. We further design an evaluation framework to measure intrinsic and prompt-induced premise-aware behavior across three prompting settings. Experiments on frontier LLMs show that current models are strongly premise-trusting by default and rarely critique flawed premises. Stronger critique-oriented prompting improves detection and abstention on flawed queries, but also increases false invalid judgments and suppresses SQL generation on originals. The code is available at https://github.com/vegeet/PremiseSQL.

</details>


### [159] [Intern-Decision: Efficient Multimodal Decision Making with Autoregressive Masked Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22131)
*Tong Zhu,Zhe Xu,Zefeng He,Yunkai Chai,Xuyang Hu,Dehong Zheng,Yun Luo,Yafu Li,Qi Zhang,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Intern-Decision 系列（0.8B/2B/4B）多模态决策模型，直接从文本与可选图像输出结构化选择与候选概率；4B 模型在七个评测集上取得 90.02% 平均准确率，延迟低至 44.16 ms，并具备可用校准与置信度路由能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式智能体需在有限动作/标签/序数结果中反复决策，自由形式生成带来额外时延并复杂化模型与环境的接口，因此需要紧凑、低时延、可直接输出结构化决策与概率的专用决策模型。

Method: 采用自回归掩码语言建模目标：在完整助手 JSON 骨架中为每个字段放置一个决策占位符，真值符号仅出现在监督信号中；所有字段在一次因果前向传播中、经骨干网络原生语言模型头并行评测；模型支持文本与可选图像输入，规模为 0.8B、2B、4B。

Result: 4B 模型在七个评测套件上无加权平均准确率 90.02%（Jev v1.13.0 为 88.74%）；289 token、三字段请求在单张 RTX 4090 上平均延迟 44.16 ms，较小模型约 33–34 ms；96 例已知分布试点中，温度缩放将多类 Brier 从 0.628 降至 0.550、ECE 从 0.213 降至 0.089；111 条 Jevbench-Hard 上基于置信度的交接将准确率由 73.87%（82/111）提升至 87.39%（97/111），42 条被路由至 Atria-Dawn-Preview。

Conclusion: 紧凑决策模型可作为大型智能体系统中的实用组件，兼具高精度、低延迟、可校准概率与置信度驱动的协同路由能力。

Abstract: Interactive agents repeatedly choose among a finite set of actions, labels, or ordinal outcomes. Generating a free-form response for each choice adds latency and complicates the interface between a model and its environment. We present Intern-Decision, a family of 0.8B, 2B, and 4B multimodal decision models that return structured choices and candidate probabilities from text and optional images. Our autoregressive masked language modeling objective places one decision placeholder per field in a complete assistant JSON skeleton. Ground-truth symbols appear only in supervision, while all fields are evaluated in a single causal forward pass using the backbone's native language-model head. The 4B model achieves a 90.02% unweighted average accuracy across seven evaluation suites, compared with 88.74% for Jev v1.13.0. For a fixed 289-token request with three fields on one RTX 4090, mean latency is 44.16 ms for the 4B model and approximately 33--34 ms for the smaller models. On a separate 96-case known-distribution pilot, temperature scaling reduces expected multiclass Brier from 0.628 to 0.550 and expected calibration error from 0.213 to 0.089. A confidence-based handoff study on 111 public Jevbench-Hard items improves accuracy from 82/111 (73.87%) to 97/111 (87.39%) by routing 42 items to Atria-Dawn-Preview. We also describe qualitative game, visual interface, and browser demonstrations. These results support compact decision models as a practical component of larger agent systems.

</details>


### [160] [In the Guise of Correction: How Framing and Cultural Adaptation Erode LLM Safety on Real-World Criminal Cases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21789)
*Mohsinul Kabir,Md. Tariquzzaman,Karam S. R. Elradie,Polydoros Giannouris,Yuechen Jiang,Shaoxiong Ji,Sophia Ananiadou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GUISE是一个基于真实案例与UNODC ICCS分类构建的犯罪协助场景基准，跨越三种语言框架与五种文化，采用双层评估（有害性+可操作提升）揭示：语言框架本身即可驱动不安全顺从，文化适配提示比直译更具风险，且最不安全模型的响应呈递增的盎格鲁中心语域泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全基准多依赖合成提示，分类体系与真实风险关联松散，且缺乏对严重犯罪类别的聚焦分析；同时缺乏跨语言、跨文化的细粒度诊断资源。

Method: 构建GUISE基准：场景取自真实案例，按UNODC ICCS犯罪分类组织；每个场景以三种语言框架渲染，并在五种文化下评估；提出双层评估框架，同时判定响应的有害性与操作层面的可执行提升。

Result: (i) 仅框架变化即驱动不安全顺从，平均不安全顺从率从直接请求的28.9%升至寻求纠正的66.4%（峰值97.3%，p<0.05），模型在纠正被刻意扭曲的细节时给出可执行的犯罪步骤；(ii) 文化适配提示在四种非英语语言中均显著高于同一直译（+7~+18点），基於翻译的基准会遗漏该风险；(iii) 最不安全模型的响应带有强烈盎格鲁中心语域特征，且泄漏程度随响应有害性单调上升（ρ=0.46~0.63，p<0.001）。

Conclusion: GUISE提供了有据可依的资源与细粒度诊断手段，用于强化跨语言、跨文化的拒答对齐；代码与数据将公开以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Existing safety benchmarks rely largely on synthetic prompts with loosely grounded taxonomies and offer little focused analysis of severe crime categories. We introduce GUISE, a benchmark of criminal-assistance scenarios sourced from real-world cases and organised under a taxonomy from the UNODC International Classification of Crime for Statistical Purposes (ICCS). Each scenario is rendered under three linguistic framings and evaluated across five distinct cultures. Using our proposed two-tier evaluation framework, which judges both harmfulness and operational uplift, we find that: (i) framing alone drives unsafe compliance, with the mean unsafe compliance rate rising from 28.9% under direct requests to 66.4% under correction seeking (peaking at 97.3%; p < 0.05), as models correcting deliberately distorted details supply executable criminal steps; (ii) culturally adapted prompts elicit significantly more unsafe compliance than literal translations of the same requests in all four non-English languages (+7 to +18 points), a risk that translation-based benchmarks miss; and (iii) unsafe responses from the least-safe models carry strong Anglocentric registers, and the extent of this leakage increases monotonically with response harmfulness ($ρ$ = 0.46 to 0.63 across languages, all p < 0.001). GUISE offers a grounded resource and fine-grained diagnostics for strengthening refusal alignment across languages and cultures. We publish our code and data to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [161] [ContextLift: Breaking the Spurious Correlation Behind Conditional Emergent Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21633)
*Florian Mai,David Kaczér*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ContextLift 通过反事实数据增广打破"微调语境—错位回答"的虚假相关，在同等数据与算力下将域线索触发的条件性错位率大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在窄域错位数据上微调会使模型广泛错位；交叠通用安全数据是最有效的训练期防御，但一旦提示与微调语境相似，错位立即复现。作者认为这种条件性错位本质是虚假相关：训练混合中微调域语境只与错位回答共现，模型因而以语境而非请求内容作为行为依据。

Method: 提出 ContextLift 反事实增广：无需额外模型、标签或领域知识，将一半交叠的安全数据行复制，并从随机训练提示中逐字提取一句语境句拼接上去，同时保持其对齐回答不变，从而在同一语境下同时呈现对齐与错位回答，解耦语境与回答的关联。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-7B 上分别用不良安全、法律、医疗建议数据微调：通用交叠在域线索下仍残留 18.9%、41.9%、58.2% 的错位回答；ContextLift 在相同数据与算力预算下降至 2.6%、6.1%、12.8%。窄任务性能与良性微调任务不受影响，且在错位样本仅占微调集 10% 时同样有效。

Conclusion: 条件性错位可被解释并通过反事实语境增广有效缓解；ContextLift 是一种低成本、通用、无需领域知识的训练期安全防御方法。

Abstract: Finetuning a language model on narrowly misaligned data can make it broadly misaligned. Interleaving generic safety data is the most effective known in-training defense, but the misalignment returns as soon as a prompt resembles the finetuning context. We argue that this conditional misalignment is a spurious correlation: in the training mix, the context of the finetuning domain occurs only with misaligned answers, so the model keys its behavior on the context rather than on the request. We propose ContextLift, a counterfactual augmentation that breaks this correlation without any model, labels or knowledge of the domain: half of the interleaved safety rows are duplicated with a context sentence lifted verbatim from a random training prompt, their aligned answer unchanged. On Qwen2.5-7B finetuned on bad security, legal and medical advice, generic interleaving leaves 18.9%, 41.9% and 58.2% misaligned answers under a domain cue; ContextLift reduces this to 2.6%, 6.1% and 12.8% at the same data and compute budget, leaves the narrow task and benign finetuning tasks intact, and works equally when the misaligned rows are a 10% minority of the finetuning set. Code: https://github.com/davidkaczer/contextlift

</details>


### [162] [Priming Base Models for Agentic Post-Training: An Open Midtraining Recipe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22019)
*Soumye Singhal,Joseph Jennings,Ka Shun Shum,Alexander Bukharin,Khushi Bhardwaj,Zirui Liu,Venkat Srinivasan,Jonathan Li,Yash Jain,Kezhi Kong,Markus Kliegl,Olivier Delalleau,Sanjeev Satheesh,Mostofa Patwary*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并公开了一套面向智能体后训练的"中间训练（midtraining）"配方，在两个不同规模的 Nemotron 模型上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型普遍在预训练与后训练之间插入中间训练阶段，但该阶段如何构建鲜有公开资料；同时预训练上下文短且缺乏智能体交互，SFT 数据多为合成数据且过长会损害后续 RL 可训练性，中间阶段可弥补这一空隙。

Method: 在256K上下文下训练数千亿token，数据为多样化自然软件工程数据（打包仓库、pull request、提交历史、构建日志）混合少量智能体轨迹；在Nemotron 3.5 Lightning与Super上开展消融实验，并采用基于短固定SFT运行的评测协议。

Result: Lightning上用500B中间训练token在相同完整SFT后，SWE-bench Verified 60.7→64.5、Multilingual 55.8→62.8、Pro 32.0→35.5；Super上用280B token使DeepSWE 11.7→15.7、SWE-bench Verified 72.9→74.3；并提升跨智能体框架的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 中间训练能有效为基座模型注入长上下文软件工程与智能体能力，且基座模型代理指标与后训练结果相关性不可靠，需以短固定SFT运行作为评测协议。

Abstract: Frontier models, general-purpose and domain-specific alike, now include a midtraining stage between pretraining and post-training, but little has been published about how this stage is built. We present an open midtraining recipe that primes base models for agentic post-training, and we show its effectiveness on two Nemotron models of different sizes. Pretraining typically runs at short context and sees almost no agent interactions, while SFT data is mostly synthetic, and very long SFT stages can hurt the model's later RL trainability. Our midtraining stage sits between the two. It trains at 256K context for hundreds of billions of tokens on a diverse mixture of natural software-engineering data like packed repositories, pull requests, commit histories, and build logs together with a small share of agentic traces. On Nemotron 3.5 Lightning, 500B midtraining tokens improve SWE-bench Verified from 60.7 to 64.5, SWE-bench Multilingual from 55.8 to 62.8, and SWE-bench Pro from 32.0 to 35.5 after identical full SFT. On the larger Nemotron 3.5 Super, 280B midtraining tokens improve DeepSWE, a harder benchmark of long-horizon tasks, from 11.7 to 15.7 and SWE-bench Verified from 72.9 to 74.3 after identical full SFT. Midtraining also improves robustness across agent harnesses on both models. We report the ablations behind the recipe and an evaluation protocol built on short fixed SFT runs, since base-model proxy metrics did not correlate reliably with post-training results.

</details>


### [163] [HyCo: Hybrid Drafting for Cost-Efficient Mobile-Cloud Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21436)
*Hanbin Bae,Heejin Choi,Joohyung Lee,Byoung Jin Choi,Sangjun Park,Hoon-Young Cho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HyCo是一种设备-云协同的推测解码方案：将服务端模型原生多token预测（MTP）头部署在移动NPU上负责"深度"草稿，一篇小模型（SLM）单次前向产生"备选分支"，服务端仅返回MTP头所需的隐藏状态、输入嵌入与KV条目。在Galaxy S26 Ultra（LTE/5G）上相较服务端MTP自推测，每输出token的GPU时间在Spec-Bench降低38.6%、32K摘要降低29.2%；精确保持版本分别节省27.7%与11.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备-云推测解码可减少服务端计算，但用小语言模型（SLM）在移动NPU上生长深层草稿树需多次前向，端侧开销高。作者希望在不牺牲质量的前提下，降低服务端GPU时间与端到端时延。

Method: 1) 将草稿深度交给服务端模型的原生MTP头，并在设备端执行；2) SLM仅做一次前向以产生备选分支（宽度来源）；3) 服务端不执行顺序解码步骤，只回传隐藏状态、输入嵌入及MTP头所需的KV条目；4) 端侧采用量化、受限词表与1K token窗口以适配NPU；5) 引入ε阈值规则，放松对验证器采样分布的精确保持。

Result: 单客户端LTE/5G实测：相较服务端MTP自推测，每输出token服务端GPU时间在Spec-Bench降38.6%、32K摘要降29.2%；同一接受规则下，相较纯SLM草稿系统，多次运行平均多节省9.0个百分点且交付速度提升1.9倍；质量上对比Cloud+MTP判分差+0.2（配对95% CI: -0.9~+1.3），探索性翻译任务SpecEdge-ε略优；不放松分布时HyCo exact分别节省27.7%与11.9%。

Conclusion: HyCo通过"服务端MTP头负责深度 + SLM单次前向负责分支 + 轻量回传"的端云分工，在基本不损质量的前提下显著降低服务端计算并提升交付速度；ε阈值放松可进一步换取效率，但会偏离验证器的精确采样分布，需按质量要求取舍。

Abstract: Device-cloud speculative decoding reduces server computation, but growing a deep draft tree with a small language model (SLM) requires repeated passes on a mobile neural processing unit (NPU). We present HyCo, which assigns depth to the server model's native multi-token prediction (MTP) head, executed on the device, and alternative branches to one SLM pass. The server returns the hidden state, input embedding, and key-value entries needed by the head instead of executing its sequential steps. Quantization, a restricted vocabulary, and a 1K-token window fit both drafters to the NPU. An ε-threshold rule additionally relaxes exact preservation of the verifier's sampling distribution. In single-client runs with a Galaxy S26 Ultra over LTE/5G, HyCo reduces server GPU time per output token relative to server-side MTP self-speculation by 38.6% on Spec-Bench and 29.2% on 32K-token summarization. With the same acceptance rule, three Spec-Bench runs per system show 9.0 percentage points more saving and 1.9x delivery speed than an SLM-only drafter. HyCo differs from Cloud+MTP by +0.2 judge-score points (paired 95% CI: -0.9 to +1.3); exploratory translation favors SpecEdge-ε. Without relaxation, HyCo exact saves 27.7% and 11.9% on the two datasets.

</details>


### [164] [OpCredit: Operation-Aware Policy Learning for Agentic Knowledge Graph Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21345)
*Yucheng Li,Mingyuan Li,Xiaxia Wang,Gong Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OpCredit 是一个面向 Agentic KGQA 的操作级策略学习框架，用分支结果信用（BOC）与前沿转移信用（FTC）两种互补信号对单个操作进行信用分配并监督相应决策片段，在 CWQ、WebQSP、GrailQA 三个数据集和三种骨干模型上取得一致的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic KGQA 依赖可执行操作序列，但现有轨迹级奖励掩盖了单个操作的贡献：成功轨迹中可能包含无效操作，失败轨迹中也可能含有有用操作，因此需要操作级别的信用评估。

Method: 提出 OpCredit 操作感知策略学习框架，融合下游结果信用与即时转移信用：BOC 复用常规 rollout，在匹配的导航上下文下比较不同操作之后观测到的终局任务奖励；FTC 度量操作引起的、以答案为条件的前沿势能变化。两种信号在训练中联合监督对应的决策片段，推理阶段仅使用学到的策略和标准 KG 接口。

Result: 在 ComplexWebQuestions、WebQSP 和 GrailQA 三个基准上，基于三种骨干模型的实验均显示 KGQA 性能的一致提升。

Conclusion: 通过将轨迹级奖励细化为操作级信用，OpCredit 能更精确地评估与监督每一步操作，从而稳定提升 Agentic KGQA 的效果，且不增加推理阶段的额外负担。

Abstract: Agentic knowledge graph question answering(KGQA) relies on sequences of executable operations, but trajectory-level rewards obscure their individual contributions. Successful trajectories may contain ineffective operations, while failed trajectories may contain useful ones. To assess individual operations, we propose OpCredit, an operationaware policy-learning framework that combines downstream outcome credit with immediate transition credit: Branch Outcome Credit (BOC) reuses ordinary rollouts to compare terminal task rewards observed after different operations under matched navigation contexts; Frontier Transition Credit (FTC) measures operation-induced changes in answer-conditioned frontier potential. The two complementary signals jointly supervise the corresponding decision spans during training, while inference uses only the learned policy and standard KG interface. Experiments on ComplexWebQuestions, WebQSP, and GrailQA across three backbones demonstrate consistent improvements in KGQA performance.

</details>


### [165] [QuasiLeak-Bench: Evaluating LLM-Based Privacy Detectors on Policy-Conditioned Quasi-Evidence Group Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21333)
*Yufan Zhu,Yangfan Jiang,Yang Fei,Shizheng Hou,Shubhankar Mohapatra,Xiaokui Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 QuasiLeak-Bench，一个面向"策略约束下准证据组恢复"的隐私评测基准，衡量检测器能否完整还原准证据组并识别策略禁止的披露行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言消息中单个准标识符虽弱，但组合后可联合暴露个体隐私属性（即"准证据组"），而现有隐私基准只关注显式标识符、属性推断或任务驱动披露，对这一联合推断场景缺乏度量；同时是否构成隐私泄露还取决于发送者指定的披露策略。

Method: 构建 QuasiLeak-Bench：采用逆向构造流水线，先挑选弱准标识符，将其组合为经验证可联合推断目标属性的准证据组，再围绕该证据生成文档，并辅以自动化质量控制以获得大规模可靠真值；任务设定为在给定策略条件下恢复完整准证据组并标记被策略禁止的披露。

Result: 现有隐私检测器迁移效果差；本地可部署的 LLM 检测器显著落后于闭源模型，主要原因是整体漏检大多数准证据组；但在训练集上微调的 4B 模型在留出文档上超过了最强的提示式模型。

Conclusion: 准证据组层面的隐私推断是可学习的新任务，小规模微调模型即可超越强提示基线，说明该基准既揭示了现有检测器的系统性缺陷，也为后续方法提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Natural language messages often contain quasi-identifiers that are weak in isolation but can jointly reveal an individual's private attributes. We call such a jointly revealing set of cues a quasi-evidence group. Existing privacy benchmarks focus on explicit identifiers, attribute inference, or task-driven disclosures, leaving inference from quasi-evidence groups largely unmeasured. In practice, whether exposing such a quasi-evidence group violates privacy also depends on sender-specified disclosure policies. We introduce QuasiLeak-Bench, a benchmark for policy-conditioned quasi-evidence group recovery, which evaluates whether detectors recover complete quasi-evidence groups and flag the disclosures the given policy prohibits. To obtain reliable ground truth at scale, a reverse-construction pipeline first selects weak quasi-identifiers, composes them into quasi-evidence groups verified to jointly reveal a target attribute, and generates documents around this evidence, followed by automated quality control. Experiments show that existing privacy detectors transfer poorly to QuasiLeak-Bench, and locally deployable LLM-based detectors fall far behind proprietary models, mainly because they miss most quasi-evidence groups altogether. However, a 4B model fine-tuned on our training split surpasses the strongest prompted model on held-out documents, showing that the task is learnable.

</details>


### [166] [From Long Context to Selective Memory:A Survey of Memory-Centric Compression for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21165)
*Chaogui Gou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该综述将长上下文压缩重构为"推理期显存/内存管理"问题：区分"计算活跃内存"与"计算非活跃内存"，把卸载KV缓存、持久前缀缓存、预计算KV状态视为激活路径上的中间状态，并将写、卸载、召回、检索注入、物化、驱逐、整合、遗忘等操作统一为信息生命周期的状态转移，配以统一的预算化成本模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文窗口不断扩大，但推理期的计算、存储与信息选择压力并未消除。现有提示压缩、KV缓存管理、高效服务、检索增强与智能体记忆等工作各自孤立讨论，导致"存储—计算"之间信息流动缺乏统一理解，尤其缺少对"哪些信息应参与昂贵推理、何时、以何种形式参与"的系统性刻画。

Method: 综述/框架构建类工作：(1) 提出二分概念——计算活跃内存（直接参与模型计算）与计算非活跃内存（处于计算路径之外，可存储、检索、更新、遗忘）；(2) 将卸载KV缓存、持久前缀缓存、预计算KV状态定位为激活路径上的中间状态；(3) 以信息生命周期刻画八类状态转移：写入、卸载、召回、检索并注入、物化、驱逐、整合、遗忘；(4) 引入预算化成本模型，把上述操作纳入单一成本账目；(5) 统一短期上下文压缩、长期记忆管理及其间的状态转移。

Result: 形成一个统一框架，将此前分散的提示压缩、KV-cache管理、高效服务、RAG与智能体记忆纳入同一内存管理视角；指出核心挑战并非单纯削减信息量，而是在统一成本约束下决定信息参与推理的时机与形态；并归纳了评估维度、基准缺口与开放问题。

Conclusion: 未来研究应从静态、逐点（pointwise）压缩转向动态、可治理、生命周期感知的记忆管理，使长上下文处理成为可预算、可调度的推理期资源分配问题。

Abstract: Long-context capability has become essential for large language models (LLMs) to handle complex tasks, yet continuously expanding the context window does not remove the computational, storage, and information-selection pressures incurred at inference time. Existing work on prompt compression, KV-cache management, efficient serving, retrieval augmentation, and agent memory is usually discussed in isolation, leaving the flow of information between storage and computation insufficiently understood. This survey reframes long-context compression as a memory-management problem for allocating inference-time resources. We distinguish compute-active memory, which directly participates in model computation, from compute-inactive memory, which lies outside the computation path but can be stored, retrieved, updated, or forgotten, and we characterize offloaded KV caches, persistent prefix caches, and precomputed KV states as intermediate states on the activation path. On this basis, we formulate writing, offloading, recall, retrieval-and-injection, materialization, eviction, consolidation, and forgetting as state transitions within an information lifecycle, and introduce a budgeted cost model that places these operations in a single cost account. The resulting framework unifies short-term context compression, long-term memory management, and the transitions between them, and shows that the central challenge is not merely reducing information volume but deciding which information should participate in costly inference, when, and in what form. We further summarize evaluation dimensions, benchmark gaps, and open challenges, and argue that future research should move from static, pointwise compression toward dynamic, governable, lifecycle-aware memory management.

</details>


### [167] [Automatic Sentence Completion Using Bengali Audio Speech with a Transformer-Based Early Speech Recognition System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21164)
*Md Asif Akram,Arif Faisal,Sakir Hossain Faruque,Sharun Akter Khushbu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个面向孟加拉语的痴呆症辅助系统：基于语音与文本特征检测痴呆症迹象，并用带注意力机制的Bi-LSTM实现对话中句子自动补全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 痴呆症严重损害沟通能力，患者难以表达想法或完成句子，家属与照护者难以理解其需求；同时孟加拉语等低资源语言中的痴呆症分析研究严重不足。

Method: 采集痴呆与非痴呆受试者录音，提取反映认知衰退的关键声学特征；用基于Transformer的模型将语音转写为文本以做语言分析；分类任务上用多种ML与DL模型分别基于声学与文本表征评估，其中LSTM架构表现较强；句子补全任务上构建带注意力机制的Bi-LSTM序列学习模型，对对话中未完整表达的孟加拉语句子进行顺序补全。

Result: LSTM类模型在建模语音与语言数据的上下文模式方面展现出较强能力；Bi-LSTM+注意力模型可实现孟加拉语部分句子的自动补全。

Conclusion: 研究填补了低资源语言（孟加拉语）痴呆症分析的空白，并构建了面向认知障碍人群的句子自动补全框架，为改善患者与照护者的沟通提供了集成化辅助方案。

Abstract: Dementia severely impairs communication abilities and turns everyday conversations into challenging experiences. Individuals affected by dementia frequently struggle to express their thoughts or complete sentences, making it difficult for family members and caregivers to understand their needs. This study proposes a novel approach to assist dementia patients by developing an integrated system that detects signs of dementia through Bangla speech and text analysis and provides sentence auto-completion support during conversations. To achieve this, recorded voices from individuals with and without dementia were analysed, and key acoustic features were extracted to capture vocal patterns associated with cognitive decline. A transformer-based model was employed to transcribe voice signals into text for linguistic analysis. In the classification task, several Machine Learning (ML) and Deep Learning (DL) models were evaluated using both acoustic and textual representations, and the LSTM model architectures demonstrated strong capabilities for modelling contextual patterns in speech and language data. For the sentence auto-completion task, a sequential learning model based on a Bidirectional LSTM (Bi-LSTM) network with an attention mechanism was developed to sequentially complete partially expressed Bangla sentences during conversation. This research bridges the gap in dementia analysis in an underrepresented language and implements a sentence auto-completion framework for cognitive disorders.

</details>


### [168] [From Observation to Intervention: Distilling Self-Generated In-Context Demonstrations via Executable Causal Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21149)
*Minming Ye,Min Liu,Haoang Chi,Ciheng Wu,Naiyang Guan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文指出，以“输出正确”为依据筛选自生成ICL示例会陷入观测等价，无法区分忠实因果理由与事后伪理由；据此提出以“迁移不变性”为判别准则，实现ECI2D方法，将语言理由抽象为可执行程序并施加do-算子进行因果干预，仅保留满足迁移不变性的示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自生成ICL方法以“理由是否能导出正确输出”作为筛选标准，但忠实因果理由与虚假事后理由会产生相同输出，单从观测上统计不可区分（观测等价），导致示例筛选不可靠。

Method: 受独立因果机制启发，提出忠实理由应满足“迁移不变性”，即在逻辑相似样本上仍有效。将该准则实现为ECI2D：把自然语言理由抽象为可执行程序，在逻辑相似样本上施加do-算子的因果干预，仅保留体现迁移不变性的示例。

Result: 在数学、逻辑与常识推理基准上，ECI2D持续优于基于观测的ICL，且保留的示例数量远少；当测试分布发生偏移时，性能提升超过7个点。

Conclusion: 以观测正确性筛选示例不足以识别忠实因果理由；引入干预式迁移不变性检验可显著提升自生成ICL的示例质量与分布偏移下的鲁棒性，同时大幅降低示例开销。

Abstract: Self-generated In-Context Learning (ICL) enables LLMs to synthesize their own few-shot demonstrations, often outperforming manual prompts. However, existing methods filter demonstrations by observation, assuming a rationale is high-quality if it leads to the correct output. We show such filtering suffers from \textit{observational equivalence}, whereby both faithful causal rationales and spurious post-hoc rationales can yield same outputs and are statistically indistinguishable from observation alone. Inspired by independent causal mechanisms, we argue that faithful rationales imply \textit{transfer invariance}, in which causal rationales should remain effective across logically similar samples. We instantiate this criterion as \textbf{E}xecutable \textbf{C}ausal \textbf{I}ntervention for \textbf{D}emonstrations \textbf{D}istillation (ECI2D), which abstracts language rationales into executable programs and applies the causal interventions ($do$-operator) on logically similar samples, retaining only those demonstrations that imply transfer invariance. Experiments on math, logical and commonsense reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that ECI2D consistently outperforms observation-based ICL while retaining far fewer demonstrations, with gains exceeding 7 points as the test distribution shifts.

</details>


### [169] [Compression Does Not End at Selection: Post-Selection Positional Geometry for Sparse KV Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21126)
*Wanyang Hong,Zhaoning Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文研究稀疏 KV 缓存中保留 token 的"位置几何"问题，提出 RoPERelay 通过紧凑、保序的 RoPE 坐标重定位缓存键相位，在不重新选择、不重预填、不增加缓存预算的前提下提升长上下文问答质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏 KV 压缩仅保留部分历史状态，但保留状态仍沿用完整历史中的原始坐标。在 RoPE 下，被淘汰 token 留下的位置空档会持续干扰 query-key 匹配，从而限制被保留证据的利用效率。作者由此提出：内容选择之后，位置组织应被视为独立的优化维度。

Method: 在固定选择器产出的缓存上（post-selection）研究位置几何。提出 RoPERelay：为缓存键分配紧凑且保序的 RoPE 坐标；通过逆旋转再重旋转（inverse rotation + re-rotation）迁移已缓存键的相位；并同步当前 query 与后续生成。方法复用已有 K/V，无需重新选择、历史重预填或额外缓存预算。

Result: 在 2 个模型、6 种稀疏选择器、3 类长期记忆任务共 36 组对比中，32 组答案质量提升。Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct + BM25 在 LongMemEval-S 上准确率由 23.6% 提升至 35.4%。实测完整请求时间开销约 1%–3%。

Conclusion: 位置组织是稀疏 KV 缓存内容选择之后一个独立且有效的优化维度；紧凑保序的 RoPE 坐标重定位可在低开销下显著改善长上下文推理的答案质量。

Abstract: Sparse KV compression reduces long-context inference costs by retaining selected historical states, yet these states typically keep their coordinates from the full history. Under Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE), gaps left by evicted tokens continue to affect query-key matching, potentially limiting the use of retained evidence. We study post-selection positional geometry on a fixed selector-produced cache, examining how positional organization affects the use of retained information and which coordinate and continuation rules improve answer quality. To reorganize this geometry, we propose RoPERelay, which assigns compact, order-preserving RoPE coordinates, relocates cached key phases through inverse rotation and re-rotation, and synchronizes the current query and subsequent generation. It reuses existing K/V without renewed selection, historical re-prefill, or additional cache budget. Across 36 comparisons spanning two models, six sparse selectors, and three long-term memory tasks, RoPERelay improves answer quality in 32 cases. For Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct with BM25 on LongMemEval-S, accuracy increases from 23.6% to 35.4%. Measured complete-request time overhead is approximately 1%-3% in the evaluated configurations. These findings establish positional organization as a distinct optimization dimension for sparse KV caches after content selection.

</details>


### [170] [Beyond Context Length: Characterising the Difficulty of Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22067)
*Shiwen Qin,Cameron Barker,Henry Gouk,Elliot J. Crowley,Shay B. Cohen,Mirella Lapata,Miao Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出长上下文评测不应只看上下文长度，而应从证据范围、证据分散度与推理深度三个属性刻画难度，并构建LURCH基准（6,550题，含证据与推理图）量化验证三属性与模型性能强负相关、难度可叠加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文评测多以"上下文长度"作为难度主指标，但无法解释等长上下文下任务难度的巨大差异：有的可从单一局部片段求解，有的需整合全文分散证据。因此需要可量化的、超越长度的长上下文推理难度度量。

Method: 提出三个难度属性：证据范围（须找到的必要信息量）、证据分散度（信息在上下文中分布的广度）、推理深度（推导答案所需的依赖步骤数）。据此构建多领域问答基准LURCH，共6,550个样例，附带支撑证据与推理图，使三属性均可量化；并对闭源与开源模型进行评测。

Result: (1) 在固定上下文长度下，三属性均与模型性能强负相关；(2) 随上下文长度增加，证据分散样本性能急剧下降，而证据局部化样本相对稳定；(3) 难度跨属性叠加：三项均中等偏高的题目比仅单项极端值更难。

Conclusion: 证据范围、证据分散度与推理深度可作为长上下文推理难度的有效度量，与上下文长度互补，为长上下文模型评测提供更细粒度的难度刻画框架。

Abstract: Long-context language models support ever longer context windows, and evaluations typically treat context length as the main indicator of difficulty. However, context length alone cannot explain why models fail on long-context tasks that require reasoning over complex information. Contexts of equal length can pose very different challenges: a task may be solvable from a single local passage, or it may require integrating evidence distributed across the full context. We quantitatively characterise this difficulty through three properties: evidence scope, how much necessary information must be found; evidence dispersion, how widely that information is scattered within the context; and reasoning depth, how many dependent steps are needed to derive an answer from it. We construct LURCH, a multi-domain question-answering benchmark of 6,550 instances with supporting evidence and reasoning graphs, enabling all three properties to be quantified. Evaluations of proprietary and open-weight models show that: (1) at fixed context length, all our three properties are strongly negatively correlated with model performance; (2) as context length increases, performance degrades sharply on samples with dispersed evidence, whereas it remains relatively stable on samples with localised evidence; and (3) difficulty compounds across properties, as questions with moderately high values on all three are harder than those with an extreme value on just one. These findings establish evidence scope, dispersion and reasoning depth as measures of long-context reasoning difficulty that complement context length.

</details>


### [171] [PRISM: PRIvileged Sibling-guided Multi-Agent Topology Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20628)
*Phuong Tuan Dat,Qi Li,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PRISM，用特权信息学习从同一执行验证图语料中恢复每查询偏好信号，训练LLM多智能体协作拓扑生成器，六个基准上优于所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统的效果关键取决于协作拓扑，现有方法从任务查询生成拓扑，并通过执行验证图语料做后训练。但语料只列出能解决查询的图，从不给出排序，因此持续SFT缺乏逐查询偏好，生成器会偏向语料中出现频率最高的拓扑而非最适合当前查询的结构，导致任务间此消彼长。

Method: PRISM引入特权信息学习：教师模型通过零初始化的低秩分支读取一个同样解决了该查询、但部署时不可用的“兄弟图”，在学生自采样轨迹上提供逐决策目标，学生模型对齐这些逐决策目标，从而从同一语料中恢复逐查询信号。

Result: 在六个多样化基准上，PRISM优于所有评估的拓扑设计基线；匹配教师（matched-teacher）对照实验支持特权查询信息的贡献。

Conclusion: 通过特权信息蒸馏可缓解拓扑生成器后训练中逐查询偏好缺失的问题，提升跨任务的拓扑适配能力。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) based on large language models (LLMs) have become a powerful way to address complex problems, and their effectiveness depends critically on the collaboration topology that decides which agents participate and how they communicate. Recent work learns to generate this topology from the task query, fitting the generator to corpora of execution-verified graphs. The post-training method for such generators remains a limitation: a corpus lists the graphs that solved a query but never ranks them, leaving continued supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with no per-query preference to exploit. Without such a preference, the generator drifts toward the topologies the corpus contains most often rather than those that suit each query, so a gain on one task can come at a cost on another. We propose PRISM, which recovers per-query signal from the same corpus by learning with privileged information: a teacher reads, through a zero-initialized low-rank branch, a sibling graph that also solved the query and is unavailable at deployment, and the student matches its per-decision targets on trajectories the student samples itself. Across six diverse benchmarks PRISM improves over every topology-design baseline evaluated, and matched-teacher controls support the contribution of privileged query information. Code is available in this link.

</details>


### [172] [A Jury, Not an Electorate: Label-Free Reliability Estimation for Ensembles of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21085)
*Alessandro Rozza,Davide Mambelli,Steven Motta,Juri Taresch*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 将语言模型集成视为"陪审团"而非"选民"，用EM从无标签的答案一致/分歧模式中推断各专家可信度，决策规则等价于对数几率加权的线性打分；在1560题基准上达到与标签拟合权重相当的效果，并优于最佳单模型和最强无标签基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集成方法通常为每个专家分配固定声誉（多由标签学习得到），但专家答案本身是"可靠性未知的证词"，关键在于谁可信而非多数票。作者希望验证：专家可靠性能否仅从专家间一致与分歧的模式中推断，从而摆脱对标注的依赖。

Method: 构建陪审团式概率模型：每题存在潜在真实答案，每位专家以未知可靠度报告该答案；仅用无标签的答案组合配置，通过期望最大化（EM）估计各专家可靠度。理论分析表明其决策规则可化简为对专家答案分布的 log-odds 加权线性得分。

Result: 在1560题基准上，由最多3B参数规模的九个专家组成的陪审团，其表现与同一融合规则但使用标签拟合权重的方法相当，监督并未带来统计显著优势；同时在全基准上优于匹配自一致性的最佳单模型及最强的无标签替代方法。

Conclusion: 集成中真正可改变的不是"陪审团如何投票"，而是"如何学习信任谁"；专家可靠度可从无标签的共识结构中辨识，实现与标签监督相当的集成效果，为免标签模型集成提供了可行路径。

Abstract: An ensemble of language models is a jury, not an electorate. The answers are testimony from witnesses of unknown reliability, and the question is not what the majority said but who is credible. Most ensemble methods assign each expert a fixed reputation, often learned from labels. We ask whether it can instead be inferred from the pattern of agreement and disagreement among the experts. We model the experts as a jury: each question has a latent true answer, each expert reports it with unknown reliability, and those reliabilities are estimated by Expectation Maximisation (EM) from unlabelled answer configurations alone. We show that its decision rule reduces to a log-odds weighted linear score over the experts' answer distributions. What changes is not how the jury votes, but how it learns whom to trust. On a 1560-question benchmark, the jury, with nine experts of up to 3B parameters, performs comparably to the same fusion rule with label-fitted weights, showing no statistically significant advantage from supervision. Instead, it outperforms the best single model with matched self-consistency and the strongest label-free alternative on the full benchmark.

</details>


### [173] [SHIP: Structured and Hierarchical Insight with Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21048)
*Kai Chen,Guisi Ni,Albert Cheu,Kassem Fawaz,Yuzheng Hu,Ryan McKenna,Peter Kairouz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将"差分隐私洞见"重新定义为可复用的表示，用于回答分析性查询，从而为效用评估提供统一基准；提出SHIP方法，自动把查询映射到属性、构建层次化混合分类体系，并发布DP合成表；实验显示其在QA性能与结构保真度上优于现有私有洞见基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从文本数据中提取洞见同时保护用户隐私日益重要，但现有方法产出的私有洞见形式多样、面向不同分析目的，导致其效用难以评估与比较；同时回答细粒度、跨属性统计的查询需要现有表示可能遗漏的信息。

Method: 形式化定义差分隐私（DP）洞见为可复用表示，用于回答分析性查询；提出SHIP（Structured and Hierarchical Insight with Privacy），自动将查询映射到属性、构建层次化混合分类体系，并发布可用于进一步分析的DP合成表；同时设计以QA性能和结构保真度为主的评估协议。

Result: 实验表明SHIP在QA性能与结构保真度两项指标上均显著优于现有私有洞见基线，同时识别出对结构化表示仍具挑战性的查询类型。

Conclusion: 将DP洞见统一为可复用表示可支撑可比较的效用评估；SHIP通过属性映射、层次化混合分类体系与DP合成表有效捕捉细粒度与跨属性信息，但部分查询类型仍是结构化表示的难点。

Abstract: Extracting insights from textual data while protecting user privacy is increasingly important. Existing methods produce private insights in diverse forms for different analytical purposes, making their utility difficult to assess and compare. In this work, we formulate differentially private (DP) insights as reusable representations for answering analytical queries, providing a common basis for utility assessment. Answering these queries, however, requires fine-grained details and cross-attribute statistics that existing representations may omit. To capture this information, we introduce Structured and Hierarchical Insight with Privacy (SHIP), which automatically maps queries to attributes, builds a hierarchical hybrid taxonomy, and releases a DP synthetic table for further analysis. We also develop an evaluation protocol based primarily on QA performance and structured fidelity. Our experiments demonstrate SHIP's strong performance on both measures relative to existing private insight baselines, while identifying queries that remain challenging for structured representations.

</details>


### [174] [Can LLMs Anticipate Their Cruxes?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21006)
*David Reber,James Reber,Ari Holtzman,Victor Veitch*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出从语言模型决策中将"crux"（决定结论走向的分歧子问题）形式化为rollout分布的性质，比较三种计算成本递增的抽取策略，实证表明只有多rollout比较才能完整识别crux。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型输出冗长，其推理理由难以直接用于实际决策。作者希望抽取"改变该子问题答案就可能改变最终推荐"的关键crux，以理解模型的决策依据，并探究低成本近似是否足够。

Method: 将crux定义为模型rollout之间出现分歧、且其取值改变可能影响结论的子问题，属分布级而非单条rollout的属性。比较三种策略：(1) 先验地让模型直接列出crux；(2) 先采样一次完整响应后再从中抽取crux；(3) 生成多条rollout并跨rollout比较推理以提取crux，用廉价reranker调用替代judge（约省86倍算力）使该过程可行。

Result: 跨多个领域与模型的实证评估显示：先验crux识别与事后真正重要的因素关联很弱；单rollout有所改善但不足以覆盖完整crux集合；多rollout比较能识别更全面的crux。reranker方案在算力上比judge便宜约86倍且仍可用。

Conclusion: 低成本近似不足以可靠提取决策crux；高风险决策及理解语言模型决策空间时，多rollout流程不可替代。

Abstract: To use language models to help us make decisions we would like to understand the rationale behind the language model's outputs. In principle, the language model provides its rationale as part of its text output. However, the sheer volume of text makes this hard to use in practice. In this paper, we are interested in extracting the cruxes that lead a model to recommend one action over another. We take a crux to be a sub-question that a model's rollouts disagree on, where changing its value may have altered the conclusion. A crux is therefore a property of the distribution of a model's rollouts, not of any one rollout. We consider three strategies of increasing computational cost. First, we just ask a model to identify, a priori, what the cruxes of a decision will be. Second, we have the model actually go through the process of generating a response and then extract the cruxes that arose in its reasoning. Finally, we generate many responses and extract the cruxes by comparing the reasoning across the rollouts. We make this last procedure computationally tractable with cheap calls to a reranker, an estimated 86 times less compute than a judge. We then conduct an extensive empirical evaluation to assess whether the computationally inexpensive options are sufficient. They are not. Across domains and models, we find that a model's a priori assessment of the cruxes has little relation to the factors that are actually identified as important a posteriori. Using a single rollout helps, but is still inadequate for identifying the full set of cruxes. Accordingly, for high-stakes decisions (and to understand the space of language model decision making) the many rollout procedure is unavoidable.

</details>


### [175] [One Bias Score, Two Factors: How Mitigations Affect Option Bias in Multiple-Choice Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20993)
*Hyunjong Ok,Minjae Park,Jaeho Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: The paper unifies selection bias and order sensitivity through the pooled SD of selection rates, which decomposes exactly into a flip rate and a positional skew; it uses these two label-free factors to distinguish what different mitigations actually do.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
