Motivation: Selection bias (letter preference) and order sensitivity (answer changes across orderings) have been studied separately; the paper asks whether they are two facets of one phenomenon and whether mitigations for them act on the same or different mechanisms. Prior work also reports only accuracy, which cannot separate instability from incorrectness.

Method: Define pooled SD of option-letter selection rates across questions and orderings. Show that under balanced design (each option appears equally often at each position), pooled SD equals flip rate × positional skew, where flip rate = fraction of questions whose chosen option changes across orderings, and positional skew = SD of selection rates among those questions. Evaluate across 41 checkpoints and 12 benchmarks, attributing flip rate to accuracy/uncertainty and skew to an estimated option-symbol prior; compare prior correction, reversal aggregation, and their combination.

Result: Flip rate tracks accuracy and output uncertainty; positional skew tracks the estimated option-symbol prior. Prior correction mainly reduces skew; reversal aggregation mainly reduces flip rate; both shrink the accuracy range across correct-answer letters similarly, but aggregation yields fewer model-rank reversals. Prior correction before aggregation reduces both factors and further narrows that range. Aggregation partly lowers flip rate by settling questions on the same wrong option.

Conclusion: The two factors are mechanistically distinct and should be reported jointly to characterize mitigation effects, together with accuracy, so that stability is not conflated with correctness.

Abstract: Reordering multiple-choice options can change a model's answer, an effect studied as selection bias (a preference for certain option letters) and as order sensitivity (changes in answers across option orderings). We connect these perspectives through the pooled selection-rate standard deviation (pooled SD), which measures how unevenly option letters are selected across questions and orderings. When every option of each question appears equally often at each position, the pooled SD is exactly the product of two label-free factors: the flip rate and the positional skew. The flip rate is the fraction of questions whose chosen option changes across orderings; the positional skew is the standard deviation of option-letter selection rates among those questions. Across 41 checkpoints and 12 benchmarks, the flip rate tracks accuracy and output uncertainty, while the positional skew tracks the estimated option-symbol prior. Prior correction for selection bias mainly reduces the positional skew. Aggregating each ordering with its reversal, a mitigation for order sensitivity, mainly reduces the flip rate. Both reduce the range of accuracy across correct-answer letters by similar amounts, but aggregation leaves fewer model-rank reversals. Applying prior correction before aggregation reduces both factors and further narrows this accuracy range. However, aggregation lowers the flip rate partly by settling questions on the same wrong option. We therefore recommend reporting both factors to distinguish mitigation effects, alongside accuracy to distinguish stability from correctness.

</details>


### [176] [ToolBottleneckBench: Measuring Tool-Agent Performance under Controlled Pressures and Fixed Harness Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21098)
*Zichuan Xu,Yuyang Dong,Makoto Onizuka,Chuan Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ToolBottleneckBench 在固定 harness 预算下，让注册工具目录、可见工具菜单、认证工作流与返回记录编码这四类压力逐一单独变化，测量工具智能体（tool agent）的性能，并用精确评分器记录每次失败的首个未满足义务及触发的 harness 限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具智能体的成功分数同时受模型能力与运行它的 harness 预算影响，二者混杂导致性能归因不清；需要一个在受控压力与固定预算下分离这些因素的评测基准。

Method: 构建合成企业级任务并保持任务不变，仅变化一种压力（注册工具目录 / 可见工具菜单 / 认证工作流 / 返回记录编码）；采用精确评分器记录失败 episode 的首个未满足义务与终止该 episode 的 harness 上限；在 21 种模型与 harness 配置上进行对比，并测试将 completion cap 提高 16 倍以及若干修复措施的效果。

Result: 三种作用于模型的压力中，只有"可见工具菜单"带来大幅性能损失，且该损失与 completion cap 处的截断相关；将上限提高 16 倍可在截断严重的配置中消除大部分损失。多数失败终止于 harness 限制，而失败记录对"被施加的压力"的辨识能力并不优于简单的工具调用次数统计。有效修复均改变了哪些工具能到达模型或由谁发起调用，仅两种修复（且都不改善模型行为）优于所有配置切换。

Conclusion: 报告压力效应时必须同时说明测量时的 harness 预算；策略与基准设计应关注工具可见性与调用发起方的控制，而非单纯切换模型（配置切换）。基准已开源发布。

Abstract: The success score of a tool agent reflects both its model and the budgets of the harness that runs it. We present ToolBottleneckBench, a benchmark that measures tool-agent performance under controlled pressures and fixed harness budgets. Synthetic enterprise tasks stay fixed while one pressure varies: the registered tool catalog, the visible tool menu, the certified workflow, or the encoding of returned records. An exact grader records the first unmet obligation of each failed episode and the harness limit, if any, that ended it. Across 21 model and harness configurations, the visible menu is the only one of the three pressures that act on the model with a large penalty, and this penalty tracks truncation at the completion cap. Raising the cap sixteenfold removes most of the penalty in the truncation-heavy configurations. Most failures end at a harness limit, and the failure record identifies the assigned pressure no more reliably than a count of tool calls. The repairs that help change which tools reach the model or who issues the calls, and only two repairs, neither improving model behavior, beat every configuration switch. We recommend reporting pressure effects with the harness budgets under which they were measured. We release the benchmark at https://github.com/zichuanxu/tool-bottleneck-bench.

</details>


### [177] [Beyond Invariance: Factor-wise Selective Treatment of Shifts for Multimodal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21403)
*Guimin Hu,Qiwei Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FaShift 提出因子级分布偏移框架，按分布变化、干预效应与跨环境稳定性诊断表示因子，并对其分别抑制、保留或衰减，在多模态情感数据集上取得最优 ID 与 OOD 表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过压制环境相关变化来追求不变性，对所有偏移信息统一处理；但偏移信息对预测的作用不同（无关、稳定有用、环境依赖），因此需要回答哪些偏移信息应被抑制、保留或衰减。

Method: 对多模态表示进行因子分解，针对每个潜在因子评估三类互补属性：分布变化、干预效应、跨环境稳定性；据此将因子划分为非预测因子、稳定预测因子、预测有用但不稳定因子，并分别施加抑制、保留、衰减操作。

Result: 在 MOSI、MOSEI、CH-SIMS 上 ID 准确率与 F1 均最优；相比报告 OOD 结果的最强基线，MOSI 的 OOD 准确率/F1 提升 1.5/3.6 点，MOSEI 提升 1.7/2.7 点，CH-SIMS 取得最佳已报告 OOD 准确率与 F1。

Conclusion: 按因子的偏移性质进行差异化处理优于统一不变性约束，验证了选择性抑制、保留与衰减偏移信息的有效性。

Abstract: Distribution shifts pose a fundamental challenge to robust multimodal learning. Existing methods typically promote invariance by suppressing environment-dependent variations, implicitly applying a uniform treatment to shifted information. However, shifted information can have different predictive consequences: some contributes little to prediction, some remains reliably informative, and some exhibits environment-dependent predictive effects. This raises a fundamental question: which shifted information should be suppressed, preserved, or attenuated? To address this question, we propose FaShift, a factor-wise shift framework that diagnoses latent representation factors according to their distributional changes, intervention effects, and cross-environment stability. By factorizing multimodal representations and evaluating these complementary properties, FaShift distinguishes non-predictive factors, stably predictive factors, and predictively useful but unstable factors. It then selectively suppresses, preserves, or attenuates their contributions, rather than enforcing uniform invariance. Experiments on MOSI, MOSEI, and CH-SIMS show that FaShift achieves the best ID accuracy and F1 across all three datasets. Compared with the strongest competitors reporting OOD results, it improves OOD accuracy/F1 by 1.5/3.6 points on MOSI and 1.7/2.7 points on MOSEI, while achieving the best reported OOD accuracy and F1 on CH-SIMS.

</details>


### [178] [The Right Byte First: How UTF-16 Helps Multilingual Boundary Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22071)
*Hyunsik Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出UBE编码（3字节UTF-8码点改写成2字节UTF-16低字节优先），使首符号携带8个低位码点比特，从而大幅降低字节级语言模型边界预测器所需的lookahead。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层次化字节级语言模型的边界预测器依赖编码方式与lookahead才能判断下一码点。UTF-8中大量文字的码点占3字节，24位中有8位用于标记长度与续字节，首字节仅携带4个高位码点比特，导致同一左上下文后"延长字素簇的符号"与"下一字母"首字节可能相同，难以区分边界。

Method: 提出Universal Byte-Level Encoding（UBE）：保留UTF-8的一、二字节编码，将三字节码点改写为UTF-16的两字节、低字节优先形式；并设置同长度的big-endian变体作为对照。在匹配的FineWeb-2语料上训练小型因果预测器，在FLORES-200上以UTF-8、UBE及big-endian变体分别评测单位置lookahead下的字素边界错误率，并考察保留父分词器子词切分的目标场景与训练效率。

Result: 单位置lookahead下，13种婆罗米系文字、17种语言在码点边界的平衡字素边界错误率：UTF-8为8.76%，big-endian变体8.62%，UBE仅0.06%。当目标保留父分词器在字素边界的子词切分时，UBE相对UTF-8的平均错误下降：韩语72.91%、中文35.29%、另外八种文字67.32%–99.55%。其中七种主Unicode区块落在单一256码点页内的文字，big-endian变体与UTF-8差距在3.0%以内，说明增益源于字节顺序而非长度。韩语、中文下UBE仅用25%和37.5%的训练量即低于UTF-8的最终平均错误。

Conclusion: 字节顺序决定了字节级边界预测器所需的lookahead量：低字节优先的UBE让首符号承载更多可区分信息，在极少lookahead与更少训练量下即可准确判定字素边界，且其优势来自字节序而非编码长度。

Abstract: Many hierarchical byte-level language models group bytes into patches using a learned boundary predictor whose view of the next codepoint depends on encoding and lookahead. In UTF-8, codepoints of many scripts take three bytes: 8 of 24 bits mark length and continuation, and the first byte carries only 4 high codepoint bits. After the same left context, a mark extending a grapheme cluster and a letter starting the next can thus share their first byte. Universal Byte-Level Encoding (UBE) keeps one- and two-byte UTF-8 encodings but writes these codepoints as two UTF-16 bytes, low byte first, so its first symbol carries 8 low codepoint bits that can distinguish such continuations. We train small causal predictors on matched FineWeb-2 text and evaluate on FLORES-200 under UTF-8, UBE, and UBE's same-length big-endian variant. At one-symbol lookahead, balanced grapheme-boundary error at codepoint boundaries across 17 languages in 13 Brahmic scripts is 8.76% with UTF-8, 8.62% with the big-endian variant, and 0.06% with UBE. When the target instead retains a parent tokenizer's subword cuts at grapheme boundaries, UBE lowers the mean error relative to UTF-8 at the same lookahead by 72.91% in Korean, 35.29% in Chinese, and 67.32-99.55% in eight more scripts. In the seven of them whose main Unicode block fits on one 256-codepoint page, the big-endian variant stays within 3.0% of UTF-8, so the gain follows byte order, not length. In Korean and Chinese, UBE's mean error falls below UTF-8's final mean error after only 25% and 37.5% of training. Which byte comes first thus affects how much lookahead a byte-level boundary predictor needs.

</details>


### [179] [Learning Speech Attribute Representations via Multi-Teacher Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21941)
*Maryem Bouziane,Salima Mdhaffar,Yannick Estève*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出多属性多教师蒸馏框架，让单一共享SSL编码器同时保留语义、说话人、语种、情感四类话语级表征，性能接近专用单任务学生模型，并揭示不同属性在编码器不同深度自发涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有后训练方法通过知识蒸馏可让自监督语音编码器学习单一话语级属性，但尚不清楚同一编码器能否同时保留多种互补的话语级表征，因此探究单编码器的多属性共存能力。

Method: 构建多属性多教师蒸馏框架：以共享SSL骨干网络加属性特定分支为结构，从四个冻结教师模型分别蒸馏语义、说话人、语种和情感表征，并学习层聚合策略。

Result: 在多语种语义检索、说话人验证、语种识别和语音情感识别任务上，模型性能接近各自专用的单任务学生模型，且仅需一个共享编码器；层聚合策略显示不同属性自然出现在SSL编码器的不同深度。

Conclusion: 单一共享SSL编码器可有效承载多属性话语级表征，实现多任务统一建模；不同语音属性在编码器深度上的分层涌现为语音表征组织方式提供了新见解。

Abstract: Recent post-training approaches have shown that self-supervised speech encoders can be adapted to learn a single utterance-level speech attribute through knowledge distillation. In this work, we investigate whether a single speech encoder can simultaneously preserve multiple complementary utterance-level speech representations. We propose a multi-attribute multi-teacher distillation framework that learns semantic, speaker, language, and emotion representations from four frozen teacher models using a shared SSL backbone and attribute-specific branches. Experiments on multilingual semantic retrieval, speaker verification, language identification, and speech emotion recognition show that the proposed model achieves performance close to dedicated single-task students while requiring only a single shared encoder. Furthermore, the learned layer aggregation strategies reveal that different speech attributes naturally emerge at different depths of the SSL encoder, providing new insights into the organization of speech representations.

</details>


### [180] [TEM: Temporal State Tracking Memory for Large Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21900)
*Pengfeng Song,Yunkai Zhuang,Liye Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TEM：无训练、按版本记录事实并标注时间状态的记忆框架，在多次更新信息的问答上显著优于会话感知RAG，且证据更紧凑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话中信息不断变化，新事实可能取代旧值，但旧值对后续问题仍可能相关；现有检索式记忆难以区分同一事实的多个版本及其时间状态。

Method: TEM（Temporal State Tracking Memory）为免训练框架：将事实存为带版本、可溯源至来源的记录；检索相关版本后按来源时间排序，并为历史记录打时间标签再交给答案模型；事件记忆与原始对话轮次补充结构化事实缺失的细节。

Result: 60个角色、600会话、420题的评测中，四个开放权重模型整体关键实体命中率75.71–97.62%，历史居住地子集96.67–100.00%；在相同且按来源排序的事实上添加历史标签使整体命中率提升17.14–55.95个百分点；每条答案仅94–118 token，比会话感知RAG少48.75–56.73%。在1,540道LoCoMo问题的固定证据预算混合记忆测试中，两个backbone均超过会话感知RAG，保守差值分别为7.92与10.58个百分点。

Conclusion: 回答反复更新信息的问题时，应保留事实版本并向生成器显式暴露其时间状态；TEM以紧凑证据实现该目标且无需训练。

Abstract: An agent's memory must remain useful as information changes across conversations. A new fact may replace an earlier value without making that value irrelevant to a later question. We present Temporal State Tracking Memory (TEM), a training-free framework for tracking these changes. TEM stores facts as versioned records linked to their sources. It retrieves relevant versions, places them in source order and labels historical records before passing the evidence to the answer model. Event memory and original dialogue turns provide details missing from structured facts. We evaluated TEM on 420 questions drawn from 600 sessions involving 60 personas. Across four open-weight models, key-entity hit rates reached 75.71-97.62% overall and 96.67-100.00% on the historical-residence subset. Adding historical labels to otherwise identical, source-ordered facts increased overall hit rates by 17.14-55.95 percentage points. The evidence was compact: 94-118 tokens per answer, or 48.75-56.73% less than session-aware retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). A further evaluation on 1,540 LoCoMo questions tested hybrid memory under a fixed evidence budget. TEM exceeded session-aware RAG with both backbones; conservative score differences were 7.92 and 10.58 percentage points. The results support preserving fact versions and exposing their temporal status to the generator when answering questions about repeatedly updated information.

</details>


### [181] [TRACER: Trajectory-Aware Tool Retrieval for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21267)
*Tanish Kher,Sarvesh DK,Abedelkadir Asi,Sparsha Chennarayapatna Sundaresh,Dezhi Hong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TRACER是一种工具检索前端：将历史工具调用序列索引为trie树，并用学习的经验先验与上下文先验混合排序后续工具候选，从而仅在每一步暴露少量工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代智能体从工具注册表中选工具，但暴露整个注册表token开销高，且注册表规模增大后反而损害选择性能。

Method: 构建trie结构索引已观测到的工具调用序列，对序列的下一个工具（continuation）候选进行排序，排序分数由数据驱动的经验先验与上下文先验经学习得到的混合权重构成；作为检索前端接入智能体流程。

Result: 在零售+电信混合τ²环境上，GPT-5.4工具选择准确率提升4.7个百分点，每步输入token减少80%，仅暴露128个工具中的5个；自主整轮执行中达到全注册表基线相同的任务成功率且每步输入token减少61%；跨6个环境、4个模型共24组中21组优于全注册表选择，平均增益6–10个百分点，3个例外最多落后2.2个百分点。

Conclusion: 按需检索少量候选工具可在显著降低token成本的同时提升工具选择准确性，并分析了两种运行模式下的失败案例以指明后续方向。

Abstract: Modern agents select tools from registries, but exposing entire registries incurs high token costs and can impair selection as they grow. We present TRACER, a retrieval front-end that indexes observed tool-call sequences in a trie and ranks continuations using a learned blend of empirical and contextual priors. On a mixed retail-and-telecom $τ^2$ environment, TRACER improves GPT-5.4's tool-selection accuracy by 4.7 points while reducing input tokens per step by 80%, exposing just 5 of 128 tools. In autonomous full-turn execution, it matches GPT-5.4's full-registry task success with 61% fewer input tokens per step. Across six environments and four models, TRACER improves tool-selection accuracy over full-registry selection in 21 of 24 model-environment pairs, with average gains of 6 to 10 points; the three exceptions trail by at most 2.2. We analyze failures in both regimes to identify directions for future work.

</details>


### [182] [What Should We Remove? Evaluating the Reliability and Costs of RLVR Data Decontamination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21229)
*Esther Xin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统审计16个开源RLVR数学语料（共1,673,514条训练样本）与6个基准（1,949题）的去污染清洗策略，基于594个双盲复核的训练-基准配对评估8种精确、词面与语义匹配策略。结果表明语义余弦阈值0.80下59例抽样移除中52例（88%）为误删，且预注册阈值网格（0.80–0.98）无一达到90%保守精确率目标；而现有判官裁定策略在留出语料上精确率98.1%、候选帧召回98.2%，抽样6例移除中零确认误删。作者主张以清洗可靠性与数据损失成本替代单纯重叠计数作为评估标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR数学语料普遍进行基准重叠筛查，但清洗策略的可靠性与数据损失代价缺乏系统检验；既有工作多以重叠计数为核心指标，忽视误删（假移除）风险与阈值选择的校准问题。

Method: 构建包含594个独立双盲复核的训练-基准配对的标注集，校准集与留出集的基准题目和训练样本族完全不相交；对80个抽样训练项的全部83个候选匹配逐条复核以测量条目级误删；在预注册的0.80–0.98语义阈值网格上评估精确率-召回率表现，并与现有判官裁定（judge-adjudicated）策略对比。

Result: 余弦阈值0.80下59例抽样移除中52例（88%）被确认为误删（非总体比率估计）；预注册阈值网格内无任何语义阈值达到90%保守精确率目标；判官裁定策略在留出语料上精确率98.1%、候选帧召回率98.2%，抽样6例移除中零确认误删、1例未决，但不足以证明零风险。

Conclusion: 在评估所用候选帧内，判官裁定策略相较语义阈值方案具有更优的精确率-召回率权衡；召回率不衡量候选帧之外的污染。作者发布复核标签、逐策略决策与覆盖率核算，以推动以清洗可靠性与成本而非重叠计数为标准的评估范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) math corpora are routinely screened for benchmark overlap, but the reliability and data-loss costs of cleaning policies remain underexamined. We audit sixteen open RLVR math corpora comprising 1,673,514 corpus-specific training items against six benchmarks containing 1,949 questions. We evaluate eight policies based on exact, lexical, and semantic matching using 594 independently and blindly dual-reviewed training-benchmark pairs. Calibration and held-out sets have disjoint benchmark questions and training-example families. A separate review of all 83 candidate matches for 80 sampled training items measures item-level false removal. At cosine threshold 0.80, 52 of 59 sampled removals (88%) were confirmed false removals; this is not a demonstrated population rate. No semantic threshold in our preregistered grid (0.80-0.98) meets the 90% conservative-precision target on calibration. The existing judge-adjudicated policy achieves 98.1% resolved precision and 98.2% candidate-frame recall on held-out corpora. It has zero confirmed false removals among six sampled removals, with one unresolved case; this does not establish zero risk. Within the evaluated candidate frame, this policy offers a more favorable precision-recall balance than the semantic-threshold alternatives tested. Recall does not measure contamination outside the candidate frame. We release reviewed labels, per-policy decisions, and coverage accounting to support evaluation of cleaning reliability and cost rather than overlap counts alone.

</details>


### [183] [When Judges Agree and Are Wrong: Consistency-Accuracy Dissociation as a Blind Spot for Label-Free Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21128)
*Xiang Zhang,Lovedeep Gondara,Jekaterina Novikova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文直接检验"一致性可作正确性代理"这一无标签评估的核心假设，发现在高一致性（≥0.8）区域仍有60%的错误答案，其中77.7%表现为完全答案塌缩，该"高一致-错误"象限无法被任何基于一致性/一致度的评估信号识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自一致性过滤、多数投票聚合、基于一致度的可靠性打分等无标签评估方法，隐含假设"一致性≈正确性"（在缺少或难以获取真实标签时）。作者希望直接检验该假设是否成立。

Method: 使用4个金融问答数据集（含多项选择与自由作答两类）、10个一致性维度、2个前沿模型家族；在单样本层面（而非聚合层面）测量一致性与准确率的联合分布；考察不同一致性阈值 t（0.50–1.00）的稳健性，并定位该象限在扰动维度与领域的特定交集中的分布及其稳定性。

Result: 60%的错误答案落在高一致性区域（一致性≥0.8），其中77.7%呈"完全答案塌缩"（所有扰动变体给出完全相同的错误答案）；该象限对阈值 t 稳健（t=0.50至1.00均存在），跨模型家族普遍存在，且集中在特定扰动维度与领域的交集处。

Conclusion: 基于一致度/一致性的评估信号存在系统性盲区：模型可能在"看起来最可靠"时实际出错。作者刻画了该象限的位置与稳定性性质，并讨论其对以一致度信号替代人工标签的评估流水线的启示，警示此类无标签评估的可靠性风险。

Abstract: Label-free evaluation methods, such as self-consistency filtering, majority-vote aggregation, and agreement-based reliability scoring, rely on an implicit assumption that consistency can serve as a proxy for correctness when ground-truth labels are unavailable or expensive to obtain. In this paper, we test this assumption directly. Using four financial QA datasets (multiple-choice and free-form), ten consistency dimensions, and two frontier model families, we measure the joint distribution of consistency and accuracy at the level of individual examples rather than in aggregate. We find that 60% of all incorrect answers fall in the high-consistency region (consistency $\geq$ 0.8), and that 77.7% of these show complete answer collapse, i.e. identical wrong answers across all perturbation variants. This high-consistency/incorrect quadrant cannot be detected by any evaluation signal that is built on agreement alone, as the model appears maximally reliable while being wrong. We show that the phenomenon is robust to the choice of consistency threshold t (it persists from t=0.50 to t=1.00), is shared across model families, and is concentrated in specific intersections of perturbation dimension and domain. We map where this quadrant occurs, analyze its stability properties, and discuss the implications for evaluation pipelines that use agreement-based signals in place of human labels.

</details>


### [184] [Behavioral Forgetting Is Not Privacy in Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21799)
*Tyler Lizzo,Larry Heck*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 现有机器遗忘评估只在指定基准分布上度量遗忘，不足以支撑面向隐私的遗忘；应以跨访问路径（如跨语言查询）是否仍可恢复信息来评估遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器遗忘的目标是从大模型中移除特定信息，但既有评测多在单一基准分布上衡量"行为层面的遗忘"，不能保证信息真正不可访问，因而需要更贴近隐私目标的评估标准。

Method: 以跨语言查询为受控案例研究：在多个多语言模型家族上，用不同语言和文字系统查询语义等价的信息，比较遗忘效果。

Result: 在不同语言/文字脚本下查询语义等价信息时，遗忘程度差异显著；仅一条访问路径即可暴露行为遗忘与信息不可访问之间的真实差距。

Conclusion: 行为遗忘不足以作为隐私导向遗忘的充分标准；应同时评估访问路径泛化性与对抗可恢复性，并辅以分布内遗忘。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove targeted information from large language models, but existing evaluations often measure forgetting only on a designated benchmark distribution. We argue that such behavioral forgetting is an insufficient standard for privacy-oriented unlearning, and that evaluation should instead test whether forgetting persists across the access paths through which the same information may be recovered. We demonstrate this with a controlled case study of cross-lingual querying: across multiple multilingual model families, forgetting varies substantially when semantically equivalent information is queried in different languages and scripts. A single access path is therefore enough to expose a real gap between behavioral forgetting and information inaccessibility, motivating broader evaluation of access-path generalization and adversarial recoverability alongside in-distribution forgetting.

</details>


### [185] [Bias Mitigation in Audio-Enabled LLMs through Adversarial Training and Distillation Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21763)
*Adnan Al Ali,Jindřich Libovický,Anne Lauscher*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出对抗式"遗忘"与蒸馏两种方法，消除音频大语言模型（AuLLM）基于性别属性的感知偏见，在保持其他基准任务性能的同时实现性别无关或"感知但中性回应"的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频大语言模型可直接以语音交互并编码非语言线索，但依据这些线索推断出的属性（如性别）可能导致有偏行为；实践中既有希望完全去除性别推断的场景，也有希望保留（无偏）性别感知的场景，需要不同强度的干预手段。

Method: 采用对抗式方法对模型的性别感知进行unlearning，使其不再从语音线索推断性别；作为较温和的方案，进一步将性别无关模型蒸馏到学生模型，使学生保留性别感知，但在未被显式询问时其回应与性别无关教师模型保持一致。方法在三款AuLLM上验证。

Result: 在三款AuLLM上，性别遗忘（unlearning）部分取得满意效果且未损害其他基准任务性能；蒸馏部分仅取得部分满意效果。

Conclusion: 对抗式遗忘可有效去除性别感知而不牺牲任务性能，蒸馏方案作为保留感知、抑制偏见的折中尚不完善；两类方法分别适用于性别推断不必要或需无偏感知的不同应用情境。

Abstract: Audio-enabled large language models (AuLLMs) allow users to interact with them simply by talking to them, without the need for an intermediate transcription. This expands the model's accessibility and capabilities by encoding non-verbal cues. However, using non-verbal cues may lead to biased behaviour based on the attributes inferred (or assumed) from them. In this work, we focus on the gender attribute and show that models' perception of it can be unlearned using adversarial methods without a loss in performance on other benchmarked tasks. Such models are useful in situations where gender inference is unwanted. However, in other practical settings, gender perception (without bias) may be desired.As a less radical measure, we distil the gender-agnostic model, creating a model that perceives gender but responds similarly to the gender-agnostic teacher, unless explicitly asked.We apply our newly proposed methods to three AuLLMs and obtain satisfactory results in the gender-unlearning part and partially satisfactory results in the distilling part.

</details>


### [186] [Retrieved but Not Read: Understanding Why Search-Augmented LLMs Fail to Use Available Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21731)
*Owen Walters,Lihu Chen,Francesca Toni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究检索增强LLM为何在有相关证据时仍给出过时答案，发现模型常仅凭搜索摘要作答而不打开原始页面，这一行为与更高的过时答案率和不可靠引用相关；分层分析揭示证据"可得"与"有效利用"之间存在鸿沟，即使证据影响内部预测，模型最终仍可能偏好过时信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: web search虽能让LLM获取最新信息，但模型在相关证据可得时仍会输出过时答案，其成因尚不明确，需要系统研究证据的可得性与有效利用之间的差异。

Method: 构造取值在模型训练截止后发生变化的时序事实；在一个部署了搜索的闭源模型和六个受控检索条件下的开源权重模型配置上进行评测；结合受控实验与逐层（layer-wise）分析，对比检索证据对内部预测的影响与最终答案的输出。

Result: 模型经常不打开源页面而仅依据搜索摘要作答，伴随更高的过时答案率和不可靠引用率；受控实验与分层分析显示证据可得性与有效利用之间存在差距，即便检索证据影响了内部预测，最终答案仍可能偏向过时信息。

Conclusion: 检索增强LLM对检索证据的利用存在"可得却未有效使用"的问题，需推动关于搜索增强模型可靠利用检索证据的后续研究。

Abstract: Web search allows LLMs to access up-to-date information, but it remains unclear why they sometimes produce outdated answers even when relevant evidence is available. To study this problem, we construct temporal facts whose values changed after the models' training cutoffs. We evaluate one proprietary model under deployed web search and six open-weight model configurations under controlled retrieval. We find that models often answer from search snippets without opening the source pages, which is associated with higher rates of outdated answers and unfaithful citations. Our controlled experiments and layer-wise analysis reveal a gap between the availability of evidence and its effective use. Even when retrieved evidence influences internal predictions, models may still favour outdated information in their final answers. We hope these findings will help advance research on the reliable use of retrieved evidence in search-augmented LLMs.

</details>


### [187] [PremiseCheck: Efficient Fact-Checking of Human and AI Peer Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21046)
*Dingjie Song,Zhiling Yan,Ziqian Zhang,Yuxuan Zhang,Lichao Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出ReviewFact基准，用于衡量评审意见中的事实性错误检测，并提出PremiseCheck框架：由LLM在不读全文的情况下将评论改写为显式前提，再由廉价判定模型逐条核对，从而在接近前沿LLM的准确率下大幅降低成本与延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投稿量增长使同行评审承压，人类或AI撰写的评审意见可能错误陈述论文内容；现有前沿LLM核查准确但需为每次核查通读全文，成本高；廉价判定模型虽能判断陈述是否被文本支持，却难以处理混合多个隐含断言的整段评论。因此需要统一的错误定义基准，并在准确率之外同时衡量成本与延迟。

Method: 构建ReviewFact基准：在NLP、机器学习与自然科学三个领域，以统一定义标注评审意见中的事实性错误，并同时测量成本与延迟。提出PremiseCheck框架：先由LLM在不阅读论文的情况下把每条评论改写为显式前提（分解隐含断言），再由决策模型（decision model）逐条对照论文核查每个前提。

Result: 前沿LLM核查评论效果良好但需通读全文；廉价判定模型在混合多个隐含断言的整段评论上表现明显不足。单独核查每条评论时，PremiseCheck在三个领域均达到与GPT-5.5相当的准确率，成本约为其一半；在NLP评审上成本约为四分之一，延迟不到一半。

Conclusion: 将评论分解为显式前提再用廉价判定模型逐条核验，可在保持与前沿LLM相当准确率的同时显著降低核查成本与延迟，为大规模评审意见的事实性核查提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Peer review is under strain from the growth of submissions, and review comments written by humans or AI can misstate what a paper says. We introduce ReviewFact, a benchmark that labels such factual errors in review comments under one definition across NLP, machine learning, and the natural sciences, and that measures cost and latency alongside accuracy. A frontier LLM checks comments well but must read the whole paper for each check, while cheap decision models, which score whether a statement holds in a text, fall far short on whole comments that mix several implicit assertions. PremiseCheck is a framework that combines the two. An LLM rewrites each comment as explicit premises without reading the paper, and a decision model checks each premise against the paper. When each comment is checked on its own, PremiseCheck reaches accuracy comparable to GPT-5.5 across all three fields at about half its cost, and on NLP reviews at a quarter of its cost and less than half its latency.

</details>


### [188] [Hybrid Generative-Tagging Decoding for Efficient Product Attribute-Value Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21329)
*Keshav Kolluru,Manas Gupta,Maroof Ahmad,Suchitra Sathyanarayana*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出混合式生成-标注解码架构，融合标注源文本片段与开放式生成，在vLLM中实现，覆盖率提升10%、正确属性值对多63%、延迟降44%且无精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商产品目录构建需要高质量属性-值抽取。现有两类方案各有缺陷：标注（tagging）模型直接标注源文本span，速度快、有据可依，但无法产出需重格式化的值，也不能识别标签空间外的稀有属性名；生成式模型逐token自回归生成属性名和值，虽规避上述问题，但推理慢且属性覆盖受限。因此需要一种同时兼顾速度、接地性与开放覆盖的方案。

Method: 提出混合生成-标注解码（hybrid generative-tagging decoding）：单一模型架构同时包含标注头与生成头。对高频且值可从源文本复制的属性，由标注头直接从源文本抽取span；生成头则专注剩余部分——标签空间外的稀有属性及不可复制值（含隐含值、需归一化的值）。该混合解码器集成到vLLM推理框架中。

Result: 属性覆盖率提升10%，正确属性-值对抽取量增加63%，精度无损失，同时延迟降低44%。

Conclusion: 将标注的高效、可接地性与生成式的开放性结合，是提升电商属性抽取覆盖率和吞吐效率的有效路径，且不牺牲精度。

Abstract: Attribute-value extraction is important for building high-quality product catalogs in e-commerce. Existing systems either rely on generative or tagging strategies for extracting the attribute names and their corresponding values. Tagging models directly label spans in the source, making them fast and grounded, but they cannot produce values that require reformatting or generate rare attribute names outside their label space. Generative models avoid these drawbacks by generating both attribute names and values token by token, but this auto-regressive nature makes them slow and limits attribute coverage. We introduce hybrid generative-tagging decoding, a novel model architecture that combines tagging source spans and open-ended generation. For frequent attributes whose values are copyable, a tagging head extracts values directly from the source. This lets the generative head focus on the remainder - rare attributes outside the tagger's label space and non-copyable values, including implicit or normalized values. Integrated into vLLM inference, the hybrid decoder raises attribute coverage by 10% and extracts 63% more correct attribute-value pairs at no precision cost, while reducing latency by 44%.

</details>


### [189] [VALCON: Benchmarking and Enhancing Value Consistency in Large Language Models via Dual-Role Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21304)
*Junru Wu,Jingting Zheng,Yuqi Ren,Deyi Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文指出当前LLM价值对齐评估只看输出合规性，忽视模型在"生成者"与"评估者"角色间切换时的价值一致性，提出VALCON框架构建三个基准套件揭示跨角色不一致，并通过机制分析与表征干预予以缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有价值对齐评估依赖输出合规性，忽略LLM在生成与评估双角色间切换时的价值一致性问题；而现代LLM智能体与对齐流水线常由同一模型既生成动作又评判可接受性，故该一致性日益关键。

Method: 提出角色驱动的数据构建框架VALCON，通过双角色变换将价值一致性操作化为三个基准套件（VAB-Consistency、VAB-Boundary、VAB-Challenge）：在相同原则下系统配对模型的生成行为与评估判断，合成目标评估数据以暴露隐藏的价值分歧；随后进行机制探查，并提出表征引导的干预方法。

Result: 在28个LLM上实验发现普遍存在跨角色不一致：输出高度合规的模型常无法使自身判断与其生成保持一致；机制探查揭示该行为不对称伴随角色依赖的表征解耦；所提表征干预能有效缓解一致性失败。

Conclusion: 本文将价值一致性确立为独立的对齐维度，并提供"评估—分析—缓解"的完整流水线，代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Current value alignment evaluations for large language models (LLMs) heavily rely on output compliance, overlooking whether LLMs maintain value consistency when alternating between generator and evaluator roles. Such consistency is increasingly important because modern LLM agents and alignment pipelines often rely on the same LLM to both generate actions and evaluate their acceptability. To mitigate this limitation, we propose VALCON, a role-driven data construction framework that operationalizes value consistency into three benchmark suites (VAB-Consistency, VAB-Boundary, VAB-Challenge) via dual-role variation. By systematically pairing an LLM's generative behavior with its evaluative judgments under identical principles, VALCON synthesizes target evaluation data that exposes hidden value discrepancies. Experiments on 28 LLMs reveal pervasive cross-role inconsistency: models with highly compliant outputs frequently fail to align their judgments with their own generations. To understand and address this gap, we perform mechanistic probing that uncovers a role-dependent representational decoupling associated with this behavioral asymmetry. Guided by these insights, we propose a representation-directed intervention that effectively mitigates consistency failures. Our work establishes value consistency as a distinct alignment dimension and provides a complete evaluation-analysis-mitigation pipeline. Code and data are available at https://github.com/tjunlp-lab/VALCON.

</details>


### [190] [FGSS-BO: Feedback-Guided Hypothesis Management for LLM-Based Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21246)
*Hao Chen,Ziyang Ling,Ronald X. Xu,Mingzhai Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出FGSS——一种结构化假设管理接口，将LLM的搜索方向显式化为可验证、可执行的假设，并在LLAMBO上实例化为FGSS-BO，在部分任务上取得优于基线的搜索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM引导的贝叶斯优化虽利用历史观测指导搜索，但其提出的搜索方向及修订过程往往是隐式的，缺乏与观测证据、可执行干预和比较性支持更新的明确关联，难以检验与追踪。

Method: FGSS由两部分构成：LLM reflector负责提出与修订假设；确定性curator负责验证假设、维护假设身份与测试记录。演化中的搜索状态用于指导候选构造与优先级排序。在LLAMBO上实例化为FGSS-BO，保留其代理模型与默认EI选择，并采用有界测试策略。

Result: 在两个使用最近邻响应oracle的回溯性协议/媒体任务上，FGSS-BO的样本均值最终最优值与搜索AUC均优于匹配的LLAMBO基线和所评估的非LLM基线；叙述式反思与仅EI的对照显示组件效应混杂。六个直接训练超参数优化任务呈现任务依赖的迁移性，在diabetes SVM上最强；另以精确网格与反应对照考察其他设置。

Conclusion: FGSS使假设、其执行过程及跨轮修订可被检视，并在特定设置下带来搜索增益，但收益具任务依赖性，组件贡献需进一步厘清。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-guided Bayesian optimization (BO) uses past observations to guide search, but proposed directions and their revision can remain implicit. We propose Feedback-Guided Search State (FGSS), a structured hypothesis-management interface connecting language-based guidance to observed evidence, executable parameter interventions, and comparative support updates. An LLM reflector proposes and revises hypotheses; a deterministic curator validates them and maintains identities and test records. The evolving state guides candidate construction and priorities. Instantiated on LLAMBO, FGSS-BO retains its surrogate and default expected improvement selection, with a bounded testing policy. On two retrospective protocol/media tasks with nearest-neighbor response oracles, it attains higher sample mean final best and search AUC than the matched LLAMBO baseline and evaluated non-LLM baselines. Prose-reflection and EI-only controls show mixed component effects. Six direct-training hyperparameter-optimization tasks show task-dependent transfer, strongest on diabetes SVM; exact-grid and reaction controls examine other settings. FGSS exposes hypotheses, their execution, and cross-round revisions for inspection while supporting search gains in selected settings.

</details>


### [191] [KBS: A Korean Benchmark Suite for Foundation Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21227)
*Yeonkyoung So,Jongmin Kim,Sungmok Jung,Gyuseong Lee,Sangho Kim,Jongyeon Park,Joonhak Lee,Seho Pyo,Gyeongje Cho,Seorin Kim,Jisoo Kim,Suyoung Park,Hyunji M. Park,Yelim Ahn,Yeongho Seo,Jaejin Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出韩语基准套件KBS（含6个任务、9,396条），采用审校、本地化、直接构建、面向韩语重设计等方式规避纯翻译式适配，并在考虑模型访问、提示格式与评分差异的协议下评估韩语原生模型、多语开放模型与闭源API模型，发现韩语基准分数受任务构建、评分规则、提示与后训练影响，主张同时报告分数与评测设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有韩语评测多依赖对英文基准的直接翻译，难以反映韩语的语言与文化特性；同时不同模型在访问方式、提示格式和评分规则上的差异使分数缺乏可比性，因此需要一个构建方式多样、评测协议规范的韩语基准套件。

Method: 构建KBS套件，包含Ko-ARC、Ko-GSM8K、Ko-EQ-Bench、Ko-WinoGrande、Ko-LAMBADA、Ko-IFEval共6项任务、9,396条样本，覆盖六种能力；数据适配综合采用审校（review）、本地化（localization）、韩语直接构建（direct Korean construction）与面向韩语的重设计（redesign）。在此之上对韩语原生模型、多语开放模型和闭源API模型进行统一评估，协议中显式控制模型访问方式、提示格式与评分规则差异。

Result: 韩语基准得分显著依赖任务构建方式、评分规则、提示格式以及模型后训练；不同设置下分数存在差异，说明分数本身不足以完整刻画模型韩语能力。

Conclusion: 韩语评测应同时报告最终分数与获取该分数所采用的实验设置，以提高评测的可比性与可复现性；KBS为韩语能力评估提供了多构建路径、覆盖多能力面的基准基础。

Abstract: We present a Korean Benchmark Suite (KBS) comprising Ko-ARC, Ko-GSM8K, Ko-EQ-Bench, Ko-WinoGrande, Ko-LAMBADA, and Ko-IFEval, covering six capabilities across 9,396 items. The suite combines review, localization, direct Korean construction, and redesign for Korean, avoiding a translation-only approach to benchmark adaptation. We evaluate Korean-developed models, multilingual open models, and closed API models under protocols that account for model access, prompt format, and scoring differences. Results show that Korean benchmark scores depend on task construction, scoring rules, prompting, and model post-training. These findings suggest that Korean evaluation should report both final scores and the settings used to obtain them.

</details>


### [192] [Beyond Retrieval Failure: Understanding Why LLMs Miss Evidence in Multi-Hop RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21184)
*Jiaqi Wei,Wenxuan Huang,Chengdong Xu,Xiang Zhang,Wanli Ouyang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出免训练的"需求登记表"ExecutionRAG，固定检索器、语料与生成模型，将多跳RAG失败分解为"检索不可达"与"搜索漂移"两类；结果显示缺失证据型错误大幅下降，但总错误未成比例减少，瓶颈由"引导搜索"转向"证据充分性验证"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准检索评估混淆了两种失败原因：所需段落本身对检索器不可达，或智能体未发出能取回该段落的查询。作者希望量化"表观检索失败"中有多少实际源于搜索漂移，即搜索不再追踪未解答的子问题。

Method: 在检索器、语料与生成模型固定的受控设置下，引入ExecutionRAG——一个免训练的需求登记表（requirement register），记录未决子问题及其支撑证据。该登记表只改变搜索引导、不改变证据可达性，因此其找回的每个金标准段落都可证明原检索器仍可访问该段落。

Result: 在1,000条MuSiQue问题上，带有未检索到必要证据的错误答案数：all-MiniLM-L6-v2由382降至234（占错误答案比例69%→44%），Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B由383降至274（66%→48%）。但证据找回并未带来总错误的成比例下降：随着缺失证据型失败减少，过早结论与弃答现象增加。

Conclusion: 核心发现是剩余瓶颈发生转移：从"将搜索引导至必要证据"转向"验证已检索证据是否充分"。这提示多跳RAG的改进重点应放在证据充分性验证机制上。

Abstract: Multi-hop retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) requires an agent to retrieve evidence for a sequence of dependent sub-questions. When a wrong answer lacks a required passage, the passage may be inaccessible to the retriever, or the agent may have failed to issue a query that would retrieve it. Standard retrieval evaluations conflate these two causes. We study how much apparent retrieval failure is instead caused by search drift: the search no longer tracks an unanswered sub-question. Holding the retriever, corpus, and generation model fixed, we introduce ExecutionRAG, a training-free requirement register that records open sub-questions and their supporting evidence. The register changes search guidance but not evidence access, so each gold passage it recovers certifies that the passage was reachable by the original retriever. On 1,000 MuSiQue questions, the number of wrong answers with unretrieved required evidence falls from 382 to 234 with all-MiniLM-L6-v2 and from 383 to 274 with Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B. These counts correspond to 69% to 44% and 66% to 48% of each system's wrong answers, respectively. Crucially, evidence recovery does not produce a proportional reduction in total error: premature resolutions and abstentions increase as missing-evidence failures decrease. The central finding is therefore a shift in the remaining bottleneck, from guiding search toward required evidence to verifying that the retrieved evidence is sufficient.

</details>


### [193] [CoSTA: Compressed Speech Token Adaptation for Efficient Spoken Dialogue Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21143)
*Yingzhuo Deng,Chong Li,Bingxin Liu,Yi Shu,Tianyu Peng,Can Xie,Siyao Song,Shuo Ren,Jiajun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoSTA是一个兼容性保持的事后框架，将语音对话模型的生成与合成词元空间解耦：在原有分词器/解码器不变的前提下，把生成空间扩展为压缩表示，再确定性还原到原始语音词元空间进行合成，从而在双语语音对话基准上获得约1.7倍生成提速、更低延迟与更高吞吐，且响应质量基本保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SDM让生成与合成共享同一语音词元空间，导致自回归序列过长、推理开销大；改用低帧率分词器虽能缩短序列，但存在压缩—质量折衷、替换成本高，且无法打破这种耦合。

Method: 提出CoSTA——兼容性保持的事后（post-hoc）框架：保留原始语音分词器与解码器，通过均值初始化的词表扩展、发音适配与对话适配，将生成词元空间扩展为压缩表示；合成前再以确定性映射扩展回原始语音词元空间。

Result: 在双语语音对话基准上，语音词元生成速度约提升1.7倍，整体响应延迟降低、吞吐提升，同时保持响应质量；分析显示在有限预算下可维持更长的交互时长，并呈现压缩、解码效率与质量之间的实用折衷；实验还验证了跨架构迁移性及其与低帧率分词的互补性。

Conclusion: 通过解耦生成与合成词元空间而非替换分词器，CoSTA在无需重训解码链路的情况下实现了语音对话模型的高效推理，为压缩、效率与质量的权衡提供了可插拔的兼容方案。

Abstract: Spoken Dialogue Models (SDMs) typically share a speech-token space for generation and synthesis, leading to long autoregressive sequences and costly inference. Lower-rate speech tokenizers shorten sequences but may introduce compression-quality trade-offs and costly replacement without breaking this coupling. To address this limitation, we propose CoSTA, a compatibility-preserving post-hoc framework that decouples generation and synthesis token spaces while retaining the original speech tokenizer and decoder. CoSTA extends the generation token space with compressed representations via mean-initialized vocabulary extension, pronunciation adaptation, and dialogue adaptation, followed by deterministic expansion back to the original speech-token space before synthesis. On bilingual speech-dialogue benchmarks, CoSTA achieves approximately $1.7\times$ expanded speech-token generation speed, lower full-response latency, and higher throughput while preserving response quality. Analyses show more sustained interaction under limited budgets and a practical trade-off among compression, decoding efficiency, and quality. Further experiments demonstrate cross-architecture transfer and complementarity to low-rate tokenization.

</details>


### [194] [DialFork: Going Beyond Factuality to Evaluate Procedural Hallucination in Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21561)
*Sergio Burdisso,Esaú Villatoro-Tello,Dairazalia Sánchez-Cortés,Yanis Labrak,Jordi Luque,Milos Cernak,Petr Motlicek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"程序性幻觉"(procedural hallucination)这一新失败模式,并构建DialFork基准(36,278段对话、24任务、12领域),用三种粒度的评测任务和流程感知指标FlowKL/FlowScore来度量对话是否偏离预期流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大量关注事实性幻觉,但忽略了另一种失败模式:智能体每句话都流畅、事实可信,却偏离了预期的对话流程。缺乏在原始对话文本上度量该问题的基准。

Method: 构建DialFork:在受控轮次位置对合规对话进行"分叉",注入四类幻觉,生成36,278段对话(24任务/12领域),由30名标注者验证。定义三个粒度评测任务——对话集合偏离度监测、单段对话偏离度检索、首次脱轨轮次定位(实时护栏);前两者提出对话流程感知指标FlowKL与FlowScore,依据对话"如何展开"而非内容进行比较。

Result: 对话级评测中,FlowScore在七种编码器上显著优于所有基线,表明语义相似度不能替代程序相似度。

Conclusion: 事实与程序是对话质量的两个维度,社区应在度量内容之外同时度量对话过程;DialFork以MIT许可开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) deployed as conversational agents are increasingly expected to follow procedural logic. While factual hallucination has received extensive attention, a different failure mode has been overlooked: procedural hallucination, in which an agent produces individually fluent and factually plausible utterances while deviating from the intended conversational process. We introduce DialFork, the first benchmark to measure this failure mode on raw dialogue text. DialFork is built by forking conformant dialogues at controlled turn positions and injecting one of four hallucination types, yielding 36,278 dialogues across 24 tasks in 12 domains; 30 human annotators validate the construction. We define three evaluation tasks that characterize procedural deviation at three granularities: how far a set of dialogues deviates from conformant ones (monitoring deployed agents), how far a single dialogue does (procedural retrieval), and at which turn a dialogue first goes off-track (real-time guardrails). For the first two, we propose dialog-flow-aware metrics, FlowKL and FlowScore, that compare dialogues by how they unfold rather than their content. At the dialogue level, FlowScore significantly outperforms all baselines across seven encoders, showing that semantic similarity is no substitute for procedural similarity. We release DialFork under the MIT license at https://github.com/idiap/dialfork, hoping the community will measure conversational process alongside content.

</details>


### [195] [OPRD: On-Policy Reminder Distillation for Long-Horizon Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21496)
*Yuechen Luo,Zhiyi Lai,Miao Ziqi,Zihang Xu,Jingxuan Xu,Songwei Yu,Wenqiang Zhu,Weihao Li,Junhao Fan,Haoyang Huang,Yanan Wu,Ken Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出On-Policy Reminder Distillation (OPRD)，通过多层级Reminder监督与基于结果的路由，在同一学生rollout上做同策略蒸馏，推理时无需Reminder，显著提升仓库级代码修复的成功率并减少错误事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库级编码任务要求智能体在长程交互中不仅生成正确代码，还要遵守约束并响应工具反馈。诊断分析表明智能体常忽略上下文中已可获取的相关信息；而仅有的最终Pass/Fail结果只能提供稀疏的过程监督。

Method: OPRD采用Multi-Scope Reminder监督与基于结果的路由，在无Reminder的学生rollout上进行同策略学习：成功rollout提供自回归自模仿目标；失败rollout则由教师在相同学生生成前缀上重新评估，施加Reminder条件化的reverse-KL监督。Global Reminder将最终测试诊断转化为轨迹级指导，Turn-local Reminder仅作用于相关环境观察后的下一次决策。师生模型共享Coding SFT与Instruction-Following SFT两阶段初始化，推理时学生不使用Reminder。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified、SWE-bench Pro与SWE-bench Multilingual上，相比开源Qwen3.5-4B基线，基准平均任务解决率提升11.3个百分点；在同一诊断协议下，聚合错误事件数由446降至222，减少50.2%。

Conclusion: 利用测试结果驱动的多层级Reminder作为稠密过程监督，并通过同策略蒸馏将监督内化到无Reminder学生中，可有效提升长程仓库级编码智能体的可靠性。

Abstract: Reliable repository-level coding requires agents not only to generate correct code but also to preserve constraints and respond to tool feedback across long interactions. However, our diagnostic analysis shows that agents often fail to act on relevant information already available in context, while final Pass/Fail outcomes provide only sparse process supervision. We introduce On-Policy Reminder Distillation (OPRD), which combines Multi-Scope Reminder supervision with outcome-based routing on reminder-free student rollouts. Successful rollouts provide autoregressive self-imitation targets, whereas failed rollouts receive Reminder-conditioned reverse-KL supervision from a teacher that re-evaluates the same student-generated prefixes. A Global Reminder converts final-test diagnostics into trajectory-level guidance, while Turn-local Reminders apply only to the next decision following the relevant environment observation. The teacher and student share a two-stage initialization from Coding SFT and Instruction-Following (IF) SFT. At inference, the student operates without Reminders. On SWE-bench Verified, SWE-bench Pro, and SWE-bench Multilingual, OPRD improves the benchmark-averaged task resolution rate by 11.3 points over the open-source Qwen3.5-4B base model. Under the same diagnostic protocol, it reduces the aggregate error-event count from 446 to 222, a 50.2% reduction.

</details>


### [196] [Can LLMs Tell Stories Out of Order? A Corpus and Benchmark for Nonlinear Narrative Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21468)
*Syed Rifat Raiyan,Md. Tariquzzaman,Md Farhan Ishmam,Tenghao Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出含83部已出版作品的基准，考察语言模型能否讲述非线性故事：模型默认产出过于线性的叙事，即便被要求重排也难达人类水准，读者更偏好默认时序版与基于故事图生成的版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 故事存在"事件发生顺序"与"叙述顺序"两种次序，二者不一致即构成非线性叙事。现有研究缺乏对语言模型能否胜任非线性叙事的系统评测，作者希望量化这一能力缺口。

Method: 构建83部已出版作品的基准，每部标注两种顺序的事件列表，以及包含因果关系、人物知识状态、叙述所提出并解答的问题的故事图；在19个模型上测试按编年顺序讲述与按指定结构讲述的表现，并用自动评判器与71名人类读者对比模型在不同条件下生成同一组事件的故事。

Result: 给定编年事件，19个模型平均比原作更线性；被要求特定结构时仅约半数落入人类区间。读者在81%的有效判断中认为模型按需生成的非线性版本更难跟随，更偏好默认的编年版本；读者以72%的有效判断偏好基于故事图生成的故事，而自动评判器无法察觉这一差异。

Conclusion: "遵循重排指令"尚不等同于"讲好一个打乱顺序的故事"；非线性叙事能力仍是语言模型的短板，且自动评判器对叙事质量差异的敏感度不足。

Abstract: Every story has two orders: the order in which its events happen and the order in which they are told. A story is nonlinear when the two differ. We introduce a benchmark of 83 published works that measures whether language models can tell nonlinear stories. For each work, we annotate one list of events in both orders, together with a story graph of causal dependencies, character knowledge, and the questions the telling raises and resolves. Given events in chronological order, all 19 models tell them, on average, more chronologically than the published works do; asked for a specific structure, they land within the human range about half the time. We then compare a model's stories of the same events under different conditions, using both an automated judge and 71 human readers. Both prefer the chronological story a model writes by default to the nonlinear one it writes on request, which readers find harder to follow in 81% of decisive judgments. Readers also prefer stories written from the story graph to stories constrained to the published telling order (72% of decisive judgments), a difference the judge does not detect. Our results suggest that following an instruction to reorder a story is not yet the same as telling it well out of order.

</details>


### [197] [Translation Circuit Discovery and Targeted Optimization for Low-Resource Multilingual Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21456)
*Atai Waris Khan,Abdullah Mazhar,Raghu Kumar,Md Shad Akhtar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TITAN机制框架，通过因果干预、稀疏特征分解与定向偏好优化定位并优化多语言LLM中稀疏、因果关键的翻译电路，仅更新不到5%参数，在印地语→乌尔都语、印地语→信德语上BLEU分别提升33.75%和45.86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言大模型翻译性能强，但低资源多语言翻译的内部机制理解不足；传统全参数微调代价高且缺乏可解释性。

Method: TITAN机制可解释框架：结合因果干预（causal intervention）识别因果重要组件、稀疏特征分解（sparse feature decomposition）刻画稀疏电路、定向偏好优化（targeted preference optimization）仅对稀疏且因果关键的电路进行优化，参数更新量低于5%。

Result: 较最强多语言基线，印地语→乌尔都语BLEU提升33.75%，印地语→信德语提升45.86%；专家人工评估在充分性与流畅性上均有显著提升；分析表明多语言翻译由作用于共享语义子空间的稀疏结构化电路实现，而非英语中心枢轴表示。

Conclusion: 机制电路发现可直接改进低资源多语言翻译，同时为多语言Transformer行为提供可解释洞见，实现参数高效、可扩展的适配。

Abstract: Recent multilingual large language models achieve strong translation performance, yet the internal mechanisms responsible for low-resource multilingual translation remain insufficiently understood. In this paper, we study translation circuits in multilingual decoder-only LLMs for Hindi-to-Urdu and Hindi-to-Sindhi (Devanagari). We propose TITAN, a mechanistic framework that combines causal intervention, sparse feature decomposition, and targeted preference optimization to identify and optimize crucial translation components in the transformer. Unlike conventional full-parameter fine-tuning, TITAN restricts optimization to sparse, causally important circuits, enabling scalable and parameter-efficient adaptation while updating fewer than 5% of model parameters. Experimental results show that TITAN achieves BLEU improvements of 33.75% on Hindi-to-Urdu and 45.86% on Hindi-to-Sindhi over the strongest multilingual baselines. Human expert evaluation further confirms substantial gains in adequacy and fluency across both language pairs. Additional analyses reveal that multilingual translation is implemented through sparse, structured circuits operating over a shared semantic subspace rather than through an English-centric pivot representation. Overall, our findings demonstrate that mechanistic circuit discovery can directly improve low-resource multilingual translation while providing interpretable insights into multilingual transformer behavior.

</details>


### [198] [SITAR: A Situational Distortion Metric for Automatic Speech Recognition Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21440)
*Mingzhe Lu,Jinhao Liu,Yunpeng Li,Hang Su,Lichun Fan,Jian Luan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SITAR：一个面向ASR的情境失真指标，通过有界递归公式聚合情境严重度与累积范围，用少量离线LLM标注训练轻量BERT回归器，单次前向即可打分，并给出外在与内在双层验证协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASR转写中词汇或语义相似的错误可能造成截然不同的情境后果，需理解话语所描述/确立/意图促成之事；而直接使用LLM作为评判器存在计算开销大与打分方差高的问题。

Method: 提出SITAR及其端到端构建框架：将有界递归公式把情境严重度与累积范围聚合为统一分数；以该公式在有限离线LLM辅助下生成的目标训练轻量BERT族回归器，对参考-假设对单次前向打分；并形式化验证协议，涵盖宏观/微观外在效度与内在评分器可靠性，后者在专门构建的受控基准上检验。

Result: 在多样数据集与模型上，SITAR能可靠反映整体能力随规模的变化趋势，并提供正交诊断价值；受控基准验证了评分工具本身的运行合理性。

Conclusion: SITAR有望成为实用、轻量的现有ASR指标补充；作者将在内部审查后开源全部配方（代码、模型权重、数据集等）。

Abstract: In automatic speech recognition (ASR), transcription errors with similar lexical or semantic discrepancies can have markedly different consequences for the situation conveyed by speech. Capturing these differences requires not merely contextual assessment, but an understanding of what the spoken discourse describes, establishes, or seeks to bring about. However, relying on LLM judges introduces computational cost and scoring variability. We introduce SITAR, a situational distortion metric for ASR, realized through a comprehensive end-to-end construction framework. Specifically, this framework aggregates situational severity and cumulative extent into a unified score via a bounded recursive formulation. Using targets derived from this formulation with limited offline LLM support, we train SITAR as a lightweight BERT-family regressor that scores reference-hypothesis pairs in a single forward pass, entirely free of any prohibitive runtime costs. Recognizing that introducing a new metric demands rigorous justification, we formalize a principled validation protocol spanning both extrinsic validity, assessed at macro and micro levels, and intrinsic scorer reliability. The former, evaluated across diverse datasets and models, demonstrates that SITAR reliably tracks broad capability scaling while providing orthogonal diagnostic utility, whereas the latter, examined on a purpose-built controlled benchmark, confirms the operational soundness of the scoring instrument itself. Through systematic validation, we demonstrate that SITAR holds strong potential as a practical, lightweight complement to existing ASR metrics. We will release the full recipes (code, model weights, dataset, etc.) after the internal review to support community research.

</details>


### [199] [Identifying Effective Strategies in Prompt Evolution for Stress and Emotion Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21407)
*SeungHyun Yi,Seungjun Yi,Sriram Sattiraju,Haeun Park,Joakim Nguyen,Terence Lim,Joseph Skrovan,Mehak Beri,Hitakshi Modi,Eloy Carrion,Andrew Well,Carlos M. Mery,Alexander Rasgon,Mia K Markey,Ying Ding*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文比较六种提示优化方法在社交媒体压力与情绪预测上的表现，发现GEPA及其合并两个提示指令的扩展（GEPA+merge）在多组设置中优于初始提示，并识别出五种与性能提升相关的提示策略及四项跨任务/评估的观察，指出提示演化方法在压力检测中的适用性边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期压力检测与风险预测对及时的心理健康评估和支持至关重要。LLM用于社交媒体压力预测虽有前景，但仅以准确率为指标会掩盖假阳性问题；同时提示改写在不同模型与任务上收益不一致，且其与策略组合、指令变更的关系尚不明确。

Method: 在社交媒体压力与情绪数据上，针对两种模型规模比较六种方法，重点评估GEPA及其扩展GEPA+merge（合并两个提示的指令）；统计与开发集收益相关的提示策略，并在多任务、多评估设置下做对照观察与额外搜索。

Result: GEPA与GEPA+merge在多个设置中相对初始提示提升预测性能。识别出五种与增益相关的策略：引导关注情绪证据、区分当前与过往痛苦、示例说明间接线索、要求情绪语境、指导标签选择。四项观察：二元压力预测早期即改善，而多标签情绪预测初期下降后部分恢复；存在错误或冲突指令；小样本对比上的增益在完整开发数据上不成立；所选提示的开发集与报告测试集排名发生变化。

Conclusion: 研究明确了有效的提示演化方法、与增益相关的策略组合，以及任务与评估层面的局限，为将提示演化方法应用于社交媒体压力检测提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Early stress detection and risk prediction are crucial for timely mental health assessment and support. While large language models have recently shown promise for social media stress prediction, relying on accuracy can obscure false positives. Also, prompt revisions yield inconsistent gains across models and tasks, with unclear links to strategy combinations and instruction changes. By comparing six methods on social media stress and emotion data across two model sizes, we found that GEPA and its extension combining instructions from two prompts (GEPA+merge) improved prediction over initial prompts in several settings. We also identified five prompting strategies associated with development gains. These strategies included directing attention to emotional evidence, distinguishing current from past distress, illustrating indirect cues, requiring emotional context, and guiding label selection. Moreover, we documented four observations across tasks and evaluation settings. In a separate set of searches, binary stress prediction improved early, whereas multilabel emotion prediction initially declined and partly recovered. The other three concerned incorrect or conflicting instructions, gains on small comparison samples that did not hold on full development data, and changes in selected prompts' development and reported test rankings. These findings identify effective prompt evolution methods, strategy combinations associated with gains, and task and evaluation limits, providing guidance for applying prompt evolution methods to social media stress detection.

</details>


### [200] [Wolof-Adapted Industry-Ready Open-Weight Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20824)
*Gwénolé Lecorvé,Delphine Charlet,Pierre Adam,Floriane Behanzin,Lionel Delphin-Poulat,Johannes Heinecke,Géraldine Damnati,Pelin Kaytmaz,Nourredine Khaous,Charbel Kindji,Meriam Maadi,Abdoulaye Mbaye,Claire Perroux,Ismaël Rousseau,Papa Séga Wadé,Gilles Xiberras,Olivier Zhang,Malek Zitouni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发布0.6B–14B的Wolof语开放权重模型，通过指令微调、偏好对齐与模型合并构建，在63项任务上表现良好，8B/14B模型可与更大规模的商业系统竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wolof语使用者约2300万（主要分布于塞内加尔），但开放权重模型对其支持薄弱；商用API模型存在成本、治理与本地部署的主权约束。

Method: 对0.6B–14B开放权重基座模型进行Wolof语适配，流程包括指令微调、偏好对齐（preference alignment）与模型合并（model merging），并配套数据处理pipeline。

Result: 在63项公开与自建任务上，模型在Wolof理解与生成方面表现良好，同时保持其他语言与跨语言能力，8B与14B模型与规模显著更大的商业系统相比颇具竞争力。

Conclusion: 模型正于内部客服场景试点评估，并已在Hugging Face公开（Orange/wolof-lm集合），为低资源语言提供可本地部署的开放权重方案。

Abstract: Wolof is spoken by 23M people, mostly in Senegal, but remains weakly supported by open-weight language models, while API models raise cost, governance, and sovereignty constraints for local deployments. In this paper, we present 0.6B-14B open-weight models adapted to Wolof through instruction tuning, preference alignment, and model merging. After presenting the pipeline and data, extensive experiments on 63~public and in-house tasks show good performance on Wolof understanding and generation while remaining effective on other languages and cross-lingual skills, with the 8B and 14B models being particularly competitive with substantially larger commercial systems. The models are currently being evaluated in internal customer-care pilots and are publicly available at https://huggingface.co/collections/Orange/wolof-lm .

</details>


### [201] [APRiL: An Open-ended Benchmark for Chinese Social Awareness in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20785)
*Tian Lan,Han Zhang,Shanshan Wang,Yahan Yu,Jiang Li,Yugo Murawaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出APRiL——中文社会意识开放式基准，含2,196个评测实例、六大类别与两类任务，并揭示模型存在"理解-回应"差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型日益参与社交互动，恰当回应需识别隐性线索、推断心理状态、识别冲突并权衡后果；但现有基准多局限于单一社交能力、受限任务格式或特定场景，覆盖不足。

Method: 构建开放式中文社会意识基准APRiL，采用实例专属评分量表（instance-specific rubrics）；包含2,196个社会意识评测实例、六大类别，设两项互补任务：社会情境分析（理解社交线索、心理状态与冲突结构）与社会意识回应（将理解转化为恰当自然的回应）。在六个模型家族的12个模型上进行实验。

Result: 实验显示模型普遍存在一致的"理解→回应"差距；高质量的情境分析可提升回应质量，而弱分析可能降低回应质量。

Conclusion: APRiL为中文语境下社会意识语言模型研究提供系统化资源，数据与代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used in social interactions, where appropriate responses require recognizing implicit cues, inferring mental states, identifying conflicts, and considering potential consequences. However, existing benchmarks often focus on specific social abilities, constrained task formats, or particular interaction settings, offering limited coverage of social awareness across diverse contexts. To address this gap, we introduce APRiL, an open-ended benchmark for Chinese social awareness with instance-specific rubrics. APRiL contains 2,196 Social Awareness Evaluation Instances across six categories and two complementary tasks: Social Scenario Analysis evaluates models' understanding of social cues, mental states, and conflict structures, while Socially Aware Response assesses whether they can translate this understanding into appropriate, natural responses. Experiments on 12 models from six families reveal a consistent understanding-to-response gap. Higher-quality scenario analyses improve responses, while weak analyses may reduce response quality. APRiL provides a systematic resource for advancing socially aware language models in Chinese contexts. Our data and code are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/APRiL-05DE/.

</details>


### [202] [Verdict: Encode Once, Decide Many](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20778)
*Avdhesh Singh Chouhan,Jack Perry*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Verdict-MQ：单次读取状态、并行回答多个类型化问题的共享编码器，用结构化注意力掩码保证问题间、状态编码与问题间的独立性，在类型化决策基准上优于专用基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织性工作中的决策是许多针对同一份状态的小型类型化问题（是否需要回复、紧急程度、归属人），需要可直接行动的可靠概率。现有开放的类型化决策模型（如标准 cross-encoder）需对每个问题单独做一次前向，效率低且问题间缺乏结构性隔离。

Method: Verdict-MQ（395M）：给定截断后相同的保留状态 token（逐例验证），通过结构化注意力掩码使状态编码不依赖问题、问题之间互不影响，并使选项顺序在构造上不可见；一次前向即可回答全部问题。与 421M Laya 专家模型、匹配的微调基线对比；并做消融——为每个问题提供独立的 question-aware 状态副本。

Result: 类型化决策基准上五个新种子上均优于 Laya（KL 0.080 vs 0.117，Brier 0.045 vs 0.061），经逐系统温度缩放后仍成立；准确率 0.801 vs 0.767 但已越过基准饱和点，解读为与标注教师更接近。RACE/BoolQ/MultiRC 上对匹配微调基线：MultiRC 胜，RACE 与 BoolQ 负（BoolQ 训练不稳定）。零样本参考中，闭源通用系统 Jev 优于所有微调编码器。消融：question-aware 独立副本在 RACE/MultiRC 提升 0.9–3.5 点，代价为每例延迟 1.4–2.5 倍，类型化决策上无可测收益，不变性保证不变。

Conclusion: 共享状态编码在多问题类型化决策上是高效的（单次读取、结构不变性、无顺序/问题耦合），且不损失该场景精度；但在 RACE、BoolQ 等单问题阅读理解/蕴含任务上不占优，需逐问题独立状态副本才能弥补，且伴随显著延迟成本。

Abstract: Automating organizational work means answering many small typed questions about one piece of state (does this email need a reply, how urgent is it, who owns it) with probabilities good enough to act on without a human. The open typed-decision model we compare against, like a standard cross-encoder, answers each question with a separate pass over the input. We present Verdict-MQ, an open encoder that reads a case once and answers all of its questions: given identical retained state tokens after truncation (verified on every reported case), a structured attention mask makes the state encoding independent of the questions, keeps questions from influencing each other, and makes option order irrelevant by construction. On the typed-decisions benchmark, a 395M Verdict-MQ matches the soft gold labels significantly better than the released 421M Laya specialist on all five fresh seeds (KL 0.080 vs 0.117, Brier 0.045 vs 0.061), an advantage that survives per-system temperature scaling; its higher accuracy (0.801 vs 0.767) lies past the benchmark's own saturation point, so we read that margin as closer agreement with the benchmark's labelling teacher. On RACE, BoolQ and MultiRC, against a matched fine-tuned baseline it wins on MultiRC and loses on RACE and on BoolQ, where its training is unstable; we measure zero-shot references ourselves, including a closed general system (Jev) that beats every fine-tuned encoder. A controlled comparison measures what the shared state costs: giving each question its own question-aware copy of the state gains 0.9-3.5 points on RACE and MultiRC at 1.4-2.5x the per-case latency, and nothing measurable on typed decisions, with the invariance guarantees unchanged.

</details>


### [203] [A Large-Scale Dataset for Multi-Class Financial News Classification in Brazilian Portuguese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20630)
*Leomax Bandeira,Gabriel Aguiar,Dalton Cézane Gomes Valadares,Luiz Pereira,Helder Fernando de Araújo Oliveira,Gustavo Xavier,Andson M. Balieiro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 构建约16万篇巴西葡萄牙语金融新闻语料，并以语义相似度的弱监督多分类方法建立基准，发现性能高度依赖标签质量与类别分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巴西葡萄牙语金融NLP缺乏领域专用数据集，制约系统开发。

Method: 从网络采集约160,000篇巴西葡萄牙语金融新闻；对子集进行人工标注与LLM标注；基于多语种语义嵌入，比较余弦相似度、KNN与FAISS近邻检索三种相似度方法，构建弱监督多分类基准。

Result: 人工标注子集上KNN最优（47.3%准确率、31.9% F1），FAISS紧随其后；LLM标注子集准确率约50%但F1降至约20%，反映标签不平衡与弱标注噪声的负面影响。

Conclusion: 该任务难度高且对标签质量敏感，凸显高质量人工标注的必要性；数据集与初步结果为巴西葡萄牙语金融NLP后续研究提供参照基准。

Abstract: The development of Financial Natural Language Processing (NLP) systems for Brazilian Portuguese is hindered by the scarcity of domain-specific datasets. To address this, we present a large-scale corpus of approximately 160,000 Brazilian Portuguese financial news articles collected from web sources. To establish initial benchmarks for a weakly supervised multi-class classification approach based on semantic similarity, we annotated subsets of this corpus both manually and using a Large Language Model (LLM). We evaluated three similarity-based methods -- cosine similarity, K-Nearest Neighbors (KNN), and FAISS-based nearest-neighbor search -- using multilingual semantic embeddings. Our experiments reveal that the task is highly sensitive to label quality and class distribution. On the manually labeled subset, KNN achieved the best performance (47.3% accuracy, 31.9% F1-score), closely followed by FAISS. Conversely, on the LLM-annotated subset, while accuracy reached approximately 50%, F1-scores dropped to near 20%, highlighting the negative impact of label imbalance and noisy weak annotations. These findings establish foundational baselines for a challenging task, underscoring the critical need for high-quality manual annotations. Ultimately, this dataset and our preliminary results provide a reference point for future research in Brazilian Portuguese Financial NLP.

</details>


### [204] [AI Consilium: a Benchmark of LLM Clinical Multidisciplinary Second Opinions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22298)
*Alexey A. Bokov,Ivan I. Panin,Nikolay B. Nechaev,Mikhail Yu. Valkov,Valeria Yu. Chernina,Natalia Gombolevskaya,Ayyaz Hussain,Vladimir M. Agafonov,Victor A. Gombolevskiy,Aleksandr V. Nesterov,Iaroslav R. Bespalov,Ivan V. Oseledets,Dmitry V. Dylov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个多维度基准，用300例NEJM–MedQA病例评估LLM生成的鉴别诊断"第二意见"在事实性、临床连贯性、可操作性与安全性上的表现，发现模型身份比回答格式更能区分报告质量，结构化报告整体更优，多学科综合环节带来增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可为复杂病例生成鉴别诊断，但有用的"第二意见"还需事实有据、临床连贯、可操作且安全；现有评估缺乏能同时区分模型能力、多学科综合贡献、回答结构效应与会诊动态的统一评价框架。

Method: 基于300例已确诊的NEJM–MedQA病例（110例用于医师参考评分与Judge校准，190例作为留出评估集），构建多维度基准。由8种单模型流水线在自由形式与结构化临床推理两种条件下生成角色专属意见及交互式会诊（consilium）报告；采用经医师校准的LLM Judge对3,040份最终报告及对应的结构化主角色输出进行评分，共享五项评价准则。

Result: 模型身份对报告质量差异的解释力强于回答格式。8条流水线中有7条的结构化最终报告总体均分更高，优势最明显在"处置"维度（2.740 vs. 2.436）。与结构化会诊前的主角色意见相比，完整结构化综合报告在全部五项共享准则上合并得分更高，提升最大的是问题优先级排序、事实依据与推理连贯性。GPT-5.6-Sol在所有评估组件中领先，并以最少的评审轮次达成共识。

Conclusion: 该基准能在统一的单病例评估框架内区分模型能力差异、多学科综合的贡献、回答结构的准则特异性效应、会诊讨论动态以及推理需求，为LLM临床第二意见的多维度评价提供了可行工具。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate differential diagnoses for complex cases, but a useful second opinion must also be factually grounded, clinically coherent, actionable, and safe. We developed a multidimensional benchmark using 300 diagnostically resolved NEJM--MedQA cases: 110 for physician reference ratings and Judge calibration and 190 for held-out evaluation. Eight single-model pipelines generated independent role-specific opinions and interactive-consilium reports under free-form and structured clinical-reasoning conditions. A physician-calibrated LLM Judge evaluated 3,040 final reports and the corresponding structured primary-role outputs. Model identity separated report quality more strongly than response format. Structured final reports had higher overall mean scores in seven of eight model pipelines, with their clearest advantage in management (2.740 vs. 2.436). Compared with the structured pre-consilium primary-role opinion, the complete structured synthesis had higher pooled scores across all five shared criteria, with the largest increases in problem prioritization, factual grounding, and reasoning coherence. GPT-5.6-Sol led every evaluated component and reached consensus in the fewest review rounds. These findings show that the benchmark can distinguish model capability, the contribution of multidisciplinary synthesis, criterion-specific effects of response structure, deliberation dynamics, and inference requirements within one common-case evaluation.

</details>


### [205] [Multi-Target Few-Shot Prompting with Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22294)
*Anton Xue,Alan Li,Rahul Saha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文提出基于扩散语言模型的"多目标少样本提示"：将示例与多个待编辑区域置于同一画布并掩码，借选择性掩码构造似然比奖励以提升对示例的遵循度，在少样本提示基准与软件重构案例上验证了示例引导对填充准确率的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令与奖励难以明确指定的场景（如按示例编辑、API 迁移需跨多处套用）适合少样本提示；但此类编辑常依赖左右双向上下文，因果自回归模型的从左到右生成方式并不自然，且少样本提示中模型可靠遵循示例本身亦具挑战。

Method: 采用扩散语言模型进行多目标少样本提示：将多个示例与多个待填充的掩码区域置于共享画布中联合掩码填充；并利用画布的选择性掩码（对比有无示例支持条件下的填充似然）导出似然比奖励，偏好受示例支持的填充结果。

Result: 在少样本提示基准与软件重构案例研究中，示例（demonstrations）提升了填充准确率；在多个设定下，示例引导（似然比奖励）带来进一步增益。

Conclusion: 扩散式多目标少样本提示结合选择性掩码奖励，可有效适配依赖双向上下文的按示例编辑任务，示例引导可作为提升少样本遵循度的通用手段。

Abstract: Few-shot prompting is useful when instructions and rewards are hard to specify. One such application is editing by example, where a demonstrated formatting change or API migration may need to be applied across multiple sites. However, such edits often depend on both left and right context, making generation with causal autoregressive models awkward. We therefore introduce multi-target few-shot prompting with diffusion language models, where examples guide the infilling of multiple masked regions within a shared canvas. To address the known challenge of reliably following demonstrations in few-shot prompting, we further leverage selective masking of the canvas to derive a likelihood-ratio reward that favors example-supported infills. Across few-shot prompting benchmarks and software refactoring case studies, we find that demonstrations improve infilling accuracy, with example guidance yielding further gains in several settings.

</details>


### [206] [Not X, but When: Diagnosing a Mandarin LLM Writing Tic through Contextual Appropriateness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22249)
*Yihang Peng,Yi Jing,Dewen Liu,Weihan Meng,Ying Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文考察语言模型对"最合适表达"的判断能力，以中文"不是X，而是Y"结构为案例，提出三分决策树（实质/可选/缺陷），发现模型用该结构密度为人类的2.5–9.2倍，缺陷占比仅1.30%但"可选"高达52.8%，说明模型在恰当性上远逊于正确性；奖励模型与LLM评判者偏好该结构，SFT与偏好优化均会推高其频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型文本中的重复表达常被视为机器作者指纹，但多数其实有其合法功能；判断其是否为"口头禅"取决于模型是否知道何时该用、何时不该用，而这种"恰当性判断"作为交际能力的核心，对语言模型基本未被研究。

Method: 以汉语纠正构式"不是X，而是Y"为研究对象，借助Gricean语用学与纠正性否定理论，构建决策树：检验语境是否使X成为"活选项"（live alternative）、否定X是否具有信息量，据此将用法分为substantive（实质）、optional（可选）、defective（缺陷）三类；对五个商用模型的307处用法进行人工标注，并与人类参照文本的密度对比，进一步测试奖励模型、LLM评判者及SFT/偏好优化对该构式频率的影响。

Result: 五个商用模型使用该构式的密度为人类参照的2.5–9.2倍；307处标注中仅1.30%为缺陷，但52.8%为"正确但非必要"的可选用法；多个奖励模型在质量匹配的变体对照下仍偏好该构式，LLM评判者在语境不许可时也偏好它，且SFT与偏好优化都会提升开源权重模型中该构式的频率。

Conclusion: 模型的失当主要不在"正确性"而在"恰当性"；训练与评估流程（奖励模型、LLM评判、SFT、偏好优化）系统性地放大这一偏差，提示需发展对自身表达恰当性保持敏感的语言模型。

Abstract: Recurring expressions in model-written text are often treated as signatures of machine authorship, yet most of them serve legitimate functions. Whether such an expression is a tic depends on whether the model knows when it is called for. This judgment of appropriateness, central to communicative competence, remains largely unexamined for language models. We study it through the Mandarin corrective construction bushi X, ershi Y ("not X, but Y"). Drawing on Gricean pragmatics and corrective negation, we introduce a decision tree that classifies uses as substantive, optional, or defective by testing whether the context makes X a live alternative and whether rejecting X is informative. Five commercial models use the construction 2.5 to 9.2 times as densely as a human reference. Of 307 annotated uses, only 1.30% are defective while 52.8% are optional, that is, correct but unneeded, so models fall short in appropriateness far more often than in correctness. Several reward models favor the construction even over quality-matched variants, an LLM judge favors it even where the context does not license it, and both supervised fine-tuning and preference optimization tend to raise its frequency in open-weight models. We hope this work encourages the development of models attentive to the appropriateness of their expressions.

</details>


### [207] [Calibration Language Shapes Block-Influence Pruning in Multilingual Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21383)
*Vedant Pandhare,Piyush Dhoka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究检验Block Influence(BI)深度剪枝中英语校准向印度语言迁移的可行性，发现英语校准系统性低估印度语言后层块重要性，等预算多语校准可缓解部分模型的困惑度损失，但缓解不具普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BI通过输入输出余弦变化为Transformer块排序以支持深度剪枝，但该排序依赖校准分布；现有工作几乎默认英语校准的普适性，缺乏对跨语言（尤其低资源印度语言、跨文字系统）迁移是否可靠的系统检验。

Method: 在Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct、Llama-3.2-1B、Gemma-2-2B上，用英语、印地语、马拉地语、泰米尔语的互斥维基百科文章分别做校准与评测；各选择器统一150 chunk预算；泰米尔语不参与多语聚合以检验跨文字迁移；采用预注册的late-minus-middle对比与95% bootstrap置信区间，并将BI在英/印地/马拉地等预算下取均值作为聚合策略，与rank-mean、minimax-rank比较。

Result: 三个模型上印度语言的后层块BI比英语高20.7–55.5%，所有预注册late-minus-middle对比的95% bootstrap区间均高于零；尽管全局秩相关很高，低BI块选择仍发生偏移导致剪枝对象改变。等预算多语BI均值在Qwen去除6块及Gemma所有测试深度（含留出泰米尔语）上缓解困惑度损伤；Llama上去除2块时改善印地/马拉地但严重损害泰米尔语，更大剪枝预算下不同校准选出相同块、困惑度全面崩溃；rank-mean与minimax-rank未优于原始均值。

Conclusion: 结果支持诊断性而非普适规则性结论：仅英语BI系统性遗漏印度语言文本的后层活动，多语校准的收益必须按架构与剪枝预算逐一验证，不能直接作为通用剪枝准则。

Abstract: Block Influence (BI) ranks Transformer blocks for depth pruning from their input-output cosine change, but the ranking depends on the calibration distribution. We study whether English calibration transfers to Indian languages in Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct, Llama-3.2-1B, and Gemma-2-2B. English, Hindi, Marathi, and Tamil use disjoint Wikipedia articles for calibration and evaluation; every selector receives the same 150-chunk budget, and Tamil is excluded from multilingual aggregation to test cross-script transfer. Across all three models, late-block BI is 20.7-55.5% higher for the three Indian languages than for English, and every predeclared late-minus-middle contrast has a 95% bootstrap interval above zero. This mismatch changes the low-BI blocks selected for removal despite high global rank correlations. Averaging BI over equal English, Hindi, and Marathi budgets mitigates the resulting perplexity damage for Qwen at six removed blocks and for Gemma at all tested depths, including held-out Tamil. The mitigation is not universal: on Llama, balancing improves Hindi and Marathi at two removed blocks but severely harms Tamil, while larger pruning budgets select the same blocks and collapse perplexity regardless of calibration. Rank-mean and minimax-rank aggregators do not dominate the raw mean. These results support a diagnostic claim rather than a universal pruning rule: English-only BI systematically misses late-layer activity for Indian-language text, but multilingual calibration must still be validated per architecture and pruning budget.

</details>


### [208] [KULTURE Bench: A Benchmark for Assessing Language Model in Korean Cultural Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.07251)
*Xiaonan Wang,Jinyoung Yeo,Joon-Ho Lim,Hansaem Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出KULTURE Bench，一个面向韩国文化的评测框架，包含文化新闻、成语与诗歌数据集，在词、句、段落三个层级评估语言模型的文化理解与推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨语言基准多基于英文版本翻译，可能带入西方文化偏见，无法准确评估其他语言与文化；模型生成内容愈发流畅连贯，评测难度上升，文化理解能力缺乏针对性评估工具。

Method: 构建KULTURE Bench评测框架，覆盖韩国文化新闻、成语、诗歌三类数据，按词级、句级、段级分层设计任务，用于考察文化理解与推理；并对比不同语料训练得到的模型表现，进行综合分析。

Result: 对不同语言语料训练的模型评测显示，模型在涉及韩国文化深层内涵的文本理解上仍存在明显不足，提升空间较大。

Conclusion: 翻译式多语言基准存在文化偏差，需构建本土文化原生基准；KULTURE Bench为韩语文化能力评估提供分层框架，并揭示当前模型在深层文化理解上的局限。

Abstract: Large language models have exhibited significant enhancements in performance across various tasks. However, the complexity of their evaluation increases as these models generate more fluent and coherent content. Current multilingual benchmarks often use translated English versions, which may incorporate Western cultural biases that do not accurately assess other languages and cultures. To address this research gap, we introduce KULTURE Bench, an evaluation framework specifically designed for Korean culture that features datasets of cultural news, idioms, and poetry. It is designed to assess language models' cultural comprehension and reasoning capabilities at the word, sentence, and paragraph levels. Using the KULTURE Bench, we assessed the capabilities of models trained with different language corpora and analyzed the results comprehensively. The results show that there is still significant room for improvement in the models' understanding of texts related to the deeper aspects of Korean culture.

</details>


### [209] [OmniVerifier-C1: Decomposed Graded Verifier for Rule-Governed Generative World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22355)
*Bowei Liu,Xinchen Zhang,Xuhuan Li,Tianlin Zhang,Kaian Jiang,Jingwei Liu,Congyang Zhao,Yifei Qian,Junhong Liu,Zhiheng Li,Yujiu Yang,Yang Cai,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"逐点分级"（pointwise graded）的生成式视觉验证器，将复杂提示/规则自动分解为细粒度子项并打分，提供连续可解释的批评信号；配合解耦强化学习训练策略联合优化子问题二元判断与全局对齐打分，在文本到图像及规则约束的世界建模任务上显著优于二元验证器，并从理论上说明在有真值图像时成对验证范式更准确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统生成式视觉验证器仅输出True/False的离散二元判断，在涉及组合性要求的复杂多模态场景中鲁棒性不足、信息量低、缺乏可解释性，且难以泛化到域外多模态批评任务。

Method: 1) 将验证器改造为自动把复杂提示或环境规则分解为细粒度组件的逐点打分机制，输出连续、结构化、可解释的批评信号；2) 提出解耦强化学习（Decoupled RL）训练策略，联合优化子问题级二元判断与全局对齐评分，用精确的局部反馈培育细粒度验证能力，为全局连续打分奠定基础；3) 在统一RL框架下先验证文本到图像场景，再扩展到显式规则下的生成式世界建模（Maze、Sudoku等）；4) 理论分析成对（pairwise）验证范式在有真值图像时相对逐点范式的准确性优势。

Result: 逐点分级验证器在传统文本到图像场景中显著优于二元验证器；结构化可解释输出提升了对域外多模态批评任务的泛化；在迷宫、数独等结构化世界建模任务的实证结果进一步验证了方法有效性；理论层面证明在可获得真值图像时成对验证能给出更准确的批评。

Conclusion: 从生成模型迈向结构化世界建模智能体的关键，在于是否具备精确的成对分级视觉验证器；细粒度分解与连续分级批评信号是提升验证器鲁棒性与泛化性的核心。

Abstract: Generative visual verifiers play a crucial role in advancing multimodal intelligence. To enhance the robustness of visual verifiers in complex multimodal scenarios involving compositional requirements, we move beyond conventional binary verifiers that produce only True/False discrete judgments and introduce a verifier that automatically decomposes complex prompts or environmental rules into fine-grained components and assigns pointwise scores to provide a continuous and more informative critique signal. Our experiments further demonstrate that these structured and interpretable outputs improve the verifier's generalization to out-of-domain multimodal critic tasks. To train this verifier, we introduce a Decoupled RL training strategy that jointly optimizes sub-question-level binary judgment and global alignment scoring, using precise local feedback to develop fine-grained verification capabilities that provide a foundation for reliable continuous scoring at the global level. Under a general reinforcement learning framework, we first empirically demonstrate that the proposed pointwise graded verifier significantly outperforms binary verifiers in traditional text-to-image scenarios. We then extend our investigation to rule-governed generative world modeling where generative models are leveraged for world simulation and world reasoning under explicit rules. From a theoretical standpoint, we further show that a pairwise verification paradigm yields more accurate critiques than the pointwise formulation when ground-truth images are available, with empirical results on diverse structured world-modeling tasks such as Maze and Sudoku further validating the effectiveness of our approach. Overall, our findings suggest a key insight: the transition from generative models to structured world-modeling agents critically hinges on the availability of accurate pairwise graded visual verifiers.

</details>


### [210] [MindEval2: Clinically Grounded Patients and Rubrics for Evaluating Language Models in Mental Health Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22314)
*John Mendonça,Daniel Tschernutter,José Pombal,Areti Vassilopoulos,Catarina Botelho,Ignacio Sarasua,Maya D'Eon,Nuno Guerreiro,Ricardo Rei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MindEval2评估框架，含可解释规则集MHQR与患者模拟器MindSim，评测18个模型发现"指导校准"是普遍主要失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心理健康AI聊天机器人基准依赖合成患者与LLM裁判，但模拟患者过于主动坦白、自我反思过度，且Likert量表判断不透明、实用性弱，难以真实反映模型能力。

Method: 框架由两部分组成：MHQR——与PhD级心理学家合作开发的质量规则集，将回复质量拆解为可操作、可解释的显式标准；MindSim——结合精心设计的患者画像与工具调用的LLM harness，内置每次交互间更新的记忆组件，以建模心理健康的纵向动态。在MindEval2上评测18个闭源与开源模型。

Result: 前沿模型在表层质量与边界问题上已有明显改善，但"指导校准"（guidance calibration）在所有模型中均为主要失败模式，构成大部分质量差距。

Conclusion: 可解释规则化评估与工具增强、带记忆的患者模拟能提升心理健康聊天机器人评测的真实性与实用性；未来改进重点应放在指导校准能力上。

Abstract: With people increasingly turning to AI chatbots for mental health support, the need to evaluate these tools is becoming more pressing. Recent benchmarks attempt to mimic a realistic setting by simulating multi-turn interactions with synthetic patients and evaluating them with an LLM-as-a-judge. However, the usefulness of these methods is limited because simulated patients can be overly forthcoming and self-reflective, and judgments typically involve opaque Likert scales with unclear practical utility. To bridge these gaps, we introduce MindEval2, an evaluation framework that consists of two components: Mental Health Quality Rubrics (MHQR), for evaluation, and MindSim, for patient simulation. For MHQR, we collaborate with PhD-level psychologists to develop a set of rubrics that break down response quality into explicit operational criteria that are more interpretable and actionable than existing evaluation taxonomies. MindSim, in turn, is an LLM harness that combines carefully-designed patient profiles with tools, enabling more varied, challenging, and realistic interactions than existing baselines. The harness also contains a memory component that is updated between each interaction, enabling MindEval2 to model the longitudinal aspect of mental health.
