We evaluate 18 proprietary and open-weight models on MindEval2, finding that while frontier models have largely improved on issues related to surface level quality and boundaries, guidance calibration remains the dominant failure mode across all models, accounting for most of the quality gap.

</details>


### [211] [Training Reasoning Models to Generate Diverse Scientific Ideas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22324)
*Manya Wadhwa,Pranav Narayanan Venkit,Hiroaki Hayashi,Yu Li,Tuhin Chakrabarty,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出集合级强化学习目标训练8B模型，使其一次生成K个有文献依据且彼此差异明显的高质量想法，在无污染基准上创意效用超过Claude Opus 4.8与GPT-6 Astra，且多样性提升不损害可执行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被广泛用于自动化研究流程中的"想法生成"步骤，但既有工作多优化单个想法的质量，忽视想法间的多样性；而文献依据下的想法生成要求模型同时满足高质量、相互区分、且扎根于输入文档三个条件。

Method: 采用集合级（set-level）强化学习目标：单次生成中模型基于源论文写出K张结构化idea卡片，对整个集合联合打分；每个想法的质量按其与先前生成想法的距离加权，从而激励后续想法主动偏离前序想法。

Result: 8B模型在无污染基准上的creative utility（质量与多样性的综合指标）高于Claude Opus 4.8和GPT-6 Astra；四位专家的人工人评估中，本方法生成的集合与Claude Opus 4.8相当，独特想法略多；编码智能体进行概念验证实验时，success@k与Claude Opus 4.8相当。

Conclusion: 以集合为单位的RL训练可有效提升文献依据想法生成的多样性并保持质量，且增加的多样性不牺牲可执行性，说明集合级目标优于逐想法优化。

Abstract: One of the first steps of a research pipeline is generating ideas. Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to automate this step, but most of this work optimizes the quality of individual ideas, while overlooking diversity. In our work, we study document-grounded ideation, focusing on increasing the diversity of LLM generated ideas and ensuring they are high quality. Given prior papers, a model must propose a set of ideas that are each high-quality and clearly distinct from one another, while also being grounded in the input documents. To optimize for this, we propose training with a set-level reinforcement learning objective. In our setup, in a single generation, the model writes K structured idea cards grounded in the source papers, and the set is scored jointly, where each idea's quality is weighted by its distance from the ideas written before it. This teaches the model to steer each new idea away from its predecessors. We show that our trained 8B model achieves higher creative utility (a measure combining quality and diversity) than Claude Opus 4.8 and GPT-6 Astra on our contamination-free benchmark. In a pilot human study with four experts, our sets are judged as promising as Claude Opus 4.8's and contain marginally more unique ideas. When a coding agent runs proof-of-concept experiments on the ideas, our model's success@k is comparable to Claude Opus 4.8's, so we see that the added diversity does not cost any executability.

</details>


### [212] [Grid-Bench: Can LLMs Predict How Discrete Worlds Change?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22312)
*Zixiang Xu,Zirui Song,Wanrong Zheng,Chiyu Zhang,Yue Huang,Yuyang Peng,Kexin Zhang,Jinkun Chen,Xiuying Chen,Xiangliang Zhang,Yan Liu,Laurent Itti,Frank Shyu,Xiaoqiang Luo,Sandeep Pandey,Xi Liu,Luke Simon,Xiyang Hu,Yue Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出可执行基准 Grid-Bench，用 2/3/4 维离散网格上的八类空间操作与事件历史，检验 LLM 能否预测多步操作后的完整空间终态；六个模型在长历史上全状态准确率急剧下降，且单事件表现相近的模型在多事件实例上差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间指令常需按序复合执行，后一步作用于前一步形成的配置，而现有评测多聚焦单步或局部判断，难以衡量模型对多步空间状态演化的整体预测能力，需要一个可逐格校验的基准来量化"完整状态恢复"。

Method: 构建 Grid-Bench：以二、三、四维离散网格表示世界，设计八类操作族（移动物体、区域变换、环境修改等），输入包括初始网格、显式规则与文本形式的事件历史，要求模型输出完整终态网格；借助参考执行器对每个预测单元逐一核验，从而在不同操作类型、历史长度与工作负载下进行可比的精确评测。

Result: 评测六个 LLM 发现：随历史长度增加，全状态准确率急剧下降；在单事件上表现相近的模型在多事件历史上出现明显分化；精确匹配率相近的模型在共同实例上的错误单元数差异很大；即使在长历史下，部分模型仍能正确恢复相当多的变更，优于直接复制初始状态的基线。

Conclusion: Grid-Bench 使多步空间状态恢复的完整正确性与返回状态的细粒度差异均可直接度量，揭示了现有 LLM 在复合空间推理上的脆弱性与评测指标单一所掩盖的模型间差异。

Abstract: Spatial instructions describe how to move objects, transform regions, or modify their surroundings. When several operations are applied in sequence, each acts on a configuration shaped by earlier changes. How accurately can large language models (LLMs) predict the resulting spatial state? We introduce Grid-Bench, an executable benchmark in which eight families of operations transform discrete worlds represented as two-, three-, and four-dimensional grids. Given an initial grid, explicit rules, and an event history expressed as text, a model predicts the complete final grid. Reference execution makes every predicted value checkable, supporting comparisons across spatial operations, history lengths, and workloads. Evaluating six LLMs shows that complete-state accuracy falls sharply as histories grow, with substantial divergence among models that perform similarly on single events. Models with similar exact-match accuracy can nevertheless differ substantially in the number of incorrect cells on shared instances. Even over long histories, some models recover many correct changes beyond what copying the initial state would yield. Grid-Bench makes both complete recovery and these differences in returned states directly measurable across a range of spatial conditions.

</details>


### [213] [Truth for Believable AI: Expressed Doubt, Provenance, and Belief Revision as an Engineerable Stance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26035)
*Sebastian Cochinescu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在固定语言模型之上构建一个"行为层"，把表达不确定性、来源感知断言和显式信念修正作为可控行为实现，并在机械评分的多会话基准上评估；合成模型通过全部五项检查，而Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct仅通过按构造保证的审计健全性与部分修正判别，预设的表达保真度、矛盾分离和来源边际均未达标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话代理通常以统一自信的语气给出答案，缺乏对不确定性的表达与对信息来源的区分。作者希望在**不改变模型本体**的前提下，检验"表达不确定性—来源感知断言—显式信念修正"能否作为一个可插拔的行为层来实现，并将验证范围明确限定为行为实现与可审计性，而非可信度或人类信任。

Method: 行为层由四部分组成：(1) 三种认知状态，包含逐断言置信度与带类型的来源(typed provenance)；(2) 一个由来源门控的表达规则(provenance-gated expression rule)；(3) 带可审计确认的持久化修正存储；(4) 对错误纠正的部分抵抗。评估采用构造的、机械打分(mechanically scored)的多会话基准，先用合成模型作为仪器检验，再在Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct上进行预设分析；另附披露的事后(post hoc)分析，比较基于平均答案token概率的门控与基于采样一致性(sampling consistency)的门控，后者在单独承诺的协议下重新评估并重抽样复现。

Result: 合成仪器通过全部五项检查。真实模型上：确认健全性(由构造保证)达100%；真实纠正的接受率高于错误纠正（持有信念上0.44 vs. 0.15；含规则接受的未持有事实纠正为0.875 vs. 0.420）；但预设的表达保真度、矛盾分离与来源边际未通过。事后分析显示，以平均答案token概率为门控端到端排序正确性低于随机(AUC=0.41，按会话聚类)，而以采样一致性为门控则具备判别力(AUC=0.66)。一致性门控配置满足会话层面的操纵检验与能力等价标准，并在重抽样的会话集上复现。

Conclusion: 结论受限：可支持的仅为按构造的审计保证、依赖存储的部分纠正判别，以及token概率门控在特定基准与特定模型上的失败。操纵效果依赖配置选择，且在60条事实层面聚类不确定性时两项标准仍未解决；在开展人类评估前需先扩大事实库规模。

Abstract: Conversational agents often express answers in a uniformly confident register. We test whether expressed uncertainty, provenance-aware assertion, and explicit belief revision can be implemented as a behavior layer over a fixed language model; we do not test believability or trust. The layer combines three epistemic states, per-claim confidence and typed provenance, a provenance-gated expression rule, and a persistent revision store with auditable acknowledgments and partial resistance to false corrections. We evaluate it on a constructed, mechanically scored multi-session benchmark using a synthetic model and Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct. The synthetic instrument passes all five checks. On the real model, acknowledgment soundness, a by-construction guarantee, holds in 100% of cases, and true corrections are accepted more often than false ones (0.44 vs. 0.15 on held beliefs; 0.875 vs. 0.420 including rule-accepted corrections of unheld facts), but the pre-specified expression-fidelity, contradiction-separation, and provenance margins fail. A disclosed post hoc analysis shows that expression gated on mean answer-token probability ranks correctness below chance end to end (AUC 0.41, conversation-clustered), whereas gating on sampling consistency discriminates (AUC 0.66). A consistency-gated configuration selected from this finding and evaluated under a separately committed protocol meets the conversation-level manipulation and capability-equivalence criteria and replicates on a redrawn conversation set. The manipulation result is selection-dependent, and both criteria remain unresolved when uncertainty is clustered over the 60 facts. The supported conclusions are limited to the by-construction audit guarantee, store-dependent partial correction discrimination, and a benchmark- and model-specific failure of token-probability gating; scaling the fact base is required before human evaluation.

</details>


### [214] [Bridging the Gap: Converting Read Text to Conversational Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18001)
*Parshav Singla,Agnik Banerjee,Aaditya Arora,Shruti Aggarwal,Anil Kumar Verma,Vikram C M,Raj Prakash Gohil,Gopal Kumar Agarwal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PACC方法，利用深度神经网络分析并调整韵律特征，结合HiFi-GAN实现朗读语音到自然对话语音的转换，在自然度、准确率与MOS评测上取得改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 朗读语音缺乏自然对话所需的细腻韵律变化，而现有转换方法还需兼顾自然度、可懂度与实时应用的计算开销，以服务虚拟助手、客服、语言学习等场景。

Method: 提出Prosodic Adjustment with Conversational Context (PACC)：用深度神经网络分析并修改语调、重音、节奏等韵律特征，并采用HiFi-GAN生成对抗网络进行语音合成。

Result: 实验显示语音转换质量显著提升，自然度增强，在语音数据集上追加训练后模型准确率更高，并在语音转换任务及MOS评测上建立了新的基准。

Conclusion: 该方法可实现朗读语音到自然对话语音的有效转换，并具备推广到其他语音转换应用的潜力。

Abstract: In recent advancements within speech processing, converting read speech to conversational speech has gained significant attention. The primary challenge in this domain is maintaining naturalness and intelligibility while minimizing computational overhead for real-time applications. Traditional read speech often lacks the nuanced prosodic variation essential for natural conversational interactions, posing challenges for applications in virtual assistants, customer service, and language learning tools. This paper introduces a novel approach, Prosodic Adjustment with Conversational Context (PACC), aimed at converting read speech into natural conversational speech used in various modern applications. PACC utilizes advanced deep neural networks to analyze and modify prosodic features such as intonation, stress, and rhythm. Unlike conventional methods, our approach uses High-Fidelity Generative Adversarial Networks (HiFi-GAN) for speech synthesis. Our experimental results demonstrate significant improvements in speech conversion, enhancing naturalness and achieving better model accuracy with additional training on speech datasets. This research establishes new benchmarks in speech conversion tasks and Mean Opinion Score (MOS) evaluation for testing model accuracy, and we show that our approach can be successfully extended to other speech conversion applications.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [215] [DocPilot: Interactive Agent Orchestration and Training for Long Visual Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21327)
*Zhuowen Liang,Xiaotian Lin,Yuyu Luo,Haixun Wang,Nan Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DocPilot：一种可表达编排的智能体文档框架，通过页面级导航与细粒度证据定位，并借助课程式智能体训练，使小模型具备多模态长文档推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态长文档问答需要在跨页面、跨模态的稀疏证据上进行检索与推理；现有VLM单次推理易忽略稀疏证据、跨模态推理不足，而现有文档智能体多依赖预定义流程或粗粒度动作，限制了推理的灵活性与粒度。

Method: 提出DocPilot框架：以页面级导航加细粒度证据定位进行证据检索，集成局部视觉检查、基于代码的数值推理与跨模态推理；并提出课程式智能体训练，从单一能力学习逐步过渡到自适应编排，监督信号来自数据接地的推理轨迹与可执行交互轨迹。

Result: 在MMLongBench-Doc、DocBench、FinRAGBench-V、MADQA上，配合前沿VLM骨干取得显著提升；DocPilot-4B可与规模远大于自身的模型竞争，体现有效性与泛化性。

Conclusion: 通过可表达的动作编排与课程式训练，可将复杂多模态文档推理能力迁移至小模型，在多样化长文档推理场景中有效且可泛化。

Abstract: Multimodal long-document question answering requires models to seek sparse evidence across pages and modalities, understand heterogeneous document content, and reason over the collected evidence. Even advanced vision-language models (VLMs) can overlook sparse evidence and struggle with cross-modal reasoning in a single pass. Existing document agents further introduce multi-step interaction, but some still rely on predefined workflows or coarse-grained actions, limiting the flexibility and granularity of document reasoning. We introduce DocPilot, an agentic document framework that supports expressive orchestration and makes complex document solving learnable by small language models (SLMs). DocPilot orchestrates evidence seeking through page-level navigation and fine-grained evidence localization, and integrates local visual inspection, code-based numerical reasoning, and cross-modal reasoning for multimodal understanding. To transfer these capabilities to small models, we propose curriculum-based agentic training that progresses from single-ability learning to adaptive orchestration through supervision from data-grounded reasoning traces and executable interaction trajectories. Across MMLongBench-Doc, DocBench, FinRAGBench-V, and MADQA, DocPilot achieves strong gains with frontier VLM backbones, while DocPilot-4B delivers competitive performance against substantially larger models, demonstrating strong effectiveness and generalization across diverse long-document reasoning settings. The code is available at https://github.com/HKUSTDial/DocPilot.

</details>


### [216] [Anticipatory Commonsense with Moving Objects: An ASP-Based Framework for Predictive Reasoning with Interacting Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20867)
*Julius Monsen,Jakob Suchan,Mehul Bhatt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个通用的声明式框架，用于复杂动态环境下交互运动的预期推理，在语义层面形成可解释的时空心智模型以启发式引导底层运动计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端随机预测缺乏可解释性，无法在语义层面显式表达交互运动的事件级假设，因此需要一种可解释的、预期式的时空心智模型来引导底层运动计算。

Method: 采用混合、模块化、易扩展（elaboration tolerant）的方法，集成三部分：1）刻画交互运动轨迹动力学的ASP（回答集编程）模型；2）语义引导的预测，用于推导“分叉式轨迹未来”；3）基于能量的优化模型，对预期结果进行评估、排序与筛选。

Result: 在Argoverse 2基准上评估，每条预期轨迹均携带显式、可检视的事件级假设，区别于端到端随机预测。

Conclusion: 该声明式框架能够在语义层面生成可解释的预期式轨迹预测，并以启发式方式引导低层运动计算，兼顾模块化与可扩展性。

Abstract: We present a general declarative framework for anticipatory reasoning about interactive motion in complex dynamic environments. Our focus is on (explainable) anticipatory space-time mental model formation at a semantic level to heuristically guide low-level motion computation. Our method is hybrid, modular and elaboration tolerant integrating: 1) An ASP model characterising the dynamics of interactive motion trajectories; 2) Semantically-guided prediction for deriving `branching trajectory futures'; and 3) An energy-based {optimisation} model to evaluate, rank and filter anticipations. We evaluate with the Argoverse 2 benchmark, where each anticipated trajectory carries an explicit, inspectable event-level hypothesis, unlike end-to-end stochastic prediction.

</details>


### [217] [From Research Goals to Scientific Closure: Evaluating Agents Through Evidence-Supported Physics Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21767)
*Yijia Mao,Song Dai,Tao Chen,Xiangyu Lu,Linqian Zeng,Chenxi Hu,Sitong Zhou,Dongyang He,Xiaodan Mao,Linxuan Zhang,Tong Zhu,Yafu Li,Fangchen Yu,Peng Ye,Feng He,Yu Cheng,Yun Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 PhysClosureBench：50 个专家核验、目标明确的研究任务，用 Atomic Score（20 项标准）衡量局部科学产出、用 Research Closure（RC0–RC4）衡量研究闭环程度；7 个前沿 LLM 中最高均分 6.29/10，但仅 32.7% 达到 RC4，揭示"局部得分高≠研究闭环"的持续缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估 AI agent 是否能自主组织科学探究并得出有充分证据支撑的结论；现有基准多考察局部任务表现，缺少对"研究闭环"（是否完整回答既定研究问题、是否满足必要证据与适用条件）的衡量。

Method: 构建 PhysClosureBench：50 个经专家验证、目标指定的研究任务（强场、阿秒及邻近物理领域），每个任务只给研究目标与关键科学约束，交由 agent 自行组织调查、生成并验证证据；采用双层评价：20 项标准的 Atomic Score 度量局部科学完成度，Research Closure（RC0–RC4）依据必要证据要求与适用范围条件的满足情况评定闭环程度；评测 7 个前沿 LLM，每任务多次运行并做案例级分析。

Result: Claude-Opus-5 取得最高平均 Atomic Score（6.29/10），但仅 32.7% 的尝试达 RC4；全基准中 36.2% 的尝试即使得分≥8/10 仍未达 RC4；7 个模型在三次运行均达 RC4 的任务数显著少于至少一次达 RC4 的任务数。案例分析显示，对生成结果的误读与未解决的物理适用性限制是闭环不完整的典型原因。

Conclusion: AI agent 的局部科学成果与研究闭环能力之间存在持续且显著的落差；需同时关注证据的完整性、对结果的正确解释以及对物理适用边界的判定，而非仅以局部正确性衡量科学自主性。

Abstract: Can AI agents organize scientific investigations that lead to well-supported conclusions? To evaluate this capability, we introduce PhysClosureBench, a benchmark of 50 expert-verified, goal-specified research tasks spanning strong-field, attosecond, and neighboring physics areas. Each task specifies the research objective and essential scientific constraints, while leaving the agent to organize the investigation and determine how to generate and validate the evidence needed to answer the question. A 20-criterion Atomic Score measures local scientific accomplishment, while Research Closure (RC0--RC4) assesses how fully the investigation resolves the specified research question based on whether its indispensable evidence requirements and applicable scope conditions are satisfied. We evaluate seven frontier LLMs, where Claude-Opus-5 achieves the highest mean Atomic Score (6.29/10) but reaches RC4 in only 32.7% of attempts. Across the benchmark, 36.2% of attempts scoring at least 8/10 still fall short of RC4, while all seven models reach RC4 on substantially fewer tasks in all three runs than in at least one. Case-level analysis further illustrates how misinterpretation of generated results and unresolved physical applicability limits can lead to incomplete closure. Together, these results reveal a persistent gap between AI agents' local scientific accomplishments and their ability to achieve complete research closure.

</details>


### [218] [Improving Environment-Agnostic Coding Agents: The Role of Scaffolding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22289)
*Sergey Rodionov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究在ARC-AGI-3、AGI Maze、Terminal-Bench上评估环境无关的Codex智能体：仅修改一条鼓励报告困难并交还控制权的指令即可提升部分环境得分，但通用智能体仍低于ARC-AGI-3专用参照。将重置决策交给脚本化控制器可追回部分差距；额外教程经验甚至会降低AGI Maze表现。结论：信息与工具"可得"不等于"有效使用"，开发者提供的程序性脚手架对当前模型仍然重要，且收益伴随更高token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 更强的语言模型本应能自行构建有用流程，从而削弱开发者提供的脚手架（scaffolding）优势。论文要回答两个问题：这一优势为何持续存在；以及在不做环境特定工程的前提下，环境无关（environment-agnostic）智能体能否被改进。

Method: 在ARC-AGI-3、AGI Maze、Terminal-Bench三个环境中，以两个近乎相同的提示变体评估同一个基于Codex的智能体，代码、提示与通用工具保持不变，仅把环境特定信息作为输入传入。变体间唯一差异是一条鼓励"报告困难并交还控制权以自动续跑"的指令。进一步做两组对照：(1) 将重置（reset）决策交给遵循智能体已获知约定的脚本化控制器；(2) 在AGI Maze中施加额外教程经验。

Result: 仅改一条指令即可提升ARC-AGI-3与AGI Maze的平均得分，Terminal-Bench变化很小且方向不一；但通用智能体（含GPT-5.6-sol）仍低于ARC-AGI-3专用参照，而该参照只是规定组织与控制方式，并不提供新规则或解法。将重置决策移交脚本化控制器可追回专用智能体的部分优势。AGI Maze实验显示额外教程经验反而降低性能。性能提升同时带来更高的token成本。

Conclusion: "拥有信息与工具"与"有效使用它们"之间存在关键区别；对所测试的模型而言，外部提供的程序仍然重要。实验未能将"更好地利用信息与流程"与"额外或不同分配的推理"区分开。作者发布EnvTraverse框架与已评测的基线智能体，用于检验后续环境无关改动能否在部分环境提升性能而不损害其他环境。

Abstract: Stronger language models may increasingly construct useful procedures for themselves, reducing the advantage of developer-supplied scaffolding. We investigate why this advantage persists and whether an environment-agnostic agent can be improved without environment-specific engineering. We evaluate a Codex-based agent in two nearly identical prompt variants on ARC-AGI-3, AGI Maze, and Terminal-Bench. Each variant is deployed with unchanged code, prompts, and general-purpose tools across environments, receiving environment-specific information as input. Changing only an instruction to encourage reporting difficulties and returning control for automatic continuation improves mean scores on both ARC-AGI-3 and AGI Maze, while Terminal-Bench shows small, mixed changes. Yet the general agent remains below an ARC-AGI-3-specific reference, including with GPT-5.6-sol. The reference prescribes organization and control rather than supplying new game rules or solutions. To investigate this gap, we transfer reset decisions to a scripted controller that follows a convention already given to the agent. This small change recovers part of the specialized agent's advantage. A complementary AGI Maze experiment shows that additional tutorial experience can reduce performance. Together, these findings highlight the distinction between having information and tools available and using them effectively. Supplied procedures still matter for the models tested here. The performance gains also come with higher token-based cost. The experiments do not separate better use of information and procedures from additional or differently allocated inference. We provide the EnvTraverse framework and an evaluated baseline agent for testing whether further environment-independent changes improve performance in some environments without degrading it in others.

</details>


### [219] [Multi-sourced AI Tutor for Non-Native English Speaker Students](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22163)
*Yiyin Shen,Xinyi Ai,Weibing Wang,Rogers Jeffrey Leo John,Meenakshi Syamkumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个通用AI助教系统，可处理学生用外语（当前为普通话）提出的问题并给出双语回答，还能生成技术术语表；其核心是基于新颖问题抽取机制的专用RAG流水线，可整合多样课程材料。基于91条Piazza帖子的评估显示，系统正确且有益地回答了83.52%的问题，为76.92%的问题提供高质量翻译，为64.47%的问题生成术语表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机专业国际学生（非英语母语者，NNES）人数上升，其在计算机课程中面临语言障碍；传统课程支持人员难以为这类学生提供充分支持，因此需要可处理外语提问并给出双语讲解的AI助教。

Method: 构建通用AI助教系统：接受外语（普通话）提问并输出双语回答，附带技术术语及解释的术语表生成功能；采用带新颖问题抽取机制的专用检索增强生成（RAG）流水线，以异构课程材料为检索来源来回答学生问题。

Result: 以91条Piazza帖子为评测集：83.52%的帖子得到正确且有益的回答；76.92%的问题获得高质量翻译；64.47%的问题成功生成技术术语表。

Conclusion: 多源RAG流水线可有效增强AI助教系统，为非英语母语学生提供传统教学辅助人员难以覆盖的支持，凸显其在计算机教育中的潜力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being adopted as teaching tools in computing education, particularly for developing AI tutors capable of answering student questions. With the rise in enrollment of international students in computing programs, researchers have begun to explore the difficulties that non-native English speaker (NNES) students face in computing courses due to language barriers. To aid such students, we developed a general-purpose AI tutor system that can process student questions in foreign languages (currently focused on Mandarin) and provide bilingual responses. Our system also incorporates the functionality of generating a glossary of technical terms and their explanations to enhance comprehension. Our approach utilizes a specialized Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipeline with a novel question extraction mechanism, enabling us to effectively utilize a diverse array of course materials for answering student queries. We evaluated our AI tutor using 91 Piazza posts, demonstrating that it could answer 83.52% of the posts correctly and in a helpful manner. The tutor also provided high-quality translations for 76.92% of the questions and generated technical term glossaries for 64.47% of them. Our findings underscore the potential of multi-source RAG pipeline in enhancing AI tutor systems, offering support for non-native speakers that are challenging to provide with traditional supporting staff of a computing course.

</details>


### [220] [FAOPs: Normal Forms and an Algebra for AI Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22061)
*Ali Mohammadi Shanghooshabad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FAOP形式化理论，将AI智能体工作流视为带类型、受限状态的函数，通过四级范式（分解、类型化、因式化、作用域化）与可靠代数重写，使完整性、隔离、类型、终止、成本与优化成为编译期关注点，并在SkillBase系统中实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受监管行业要求工作流可追溯、可审计、可复现、访问受控且时间与成本有界，但现有Agent Operating Procedures（AOPs）多以粗粒度文件形式存在：步骤捆绑多个动作、复制后产生漂移、任意步骤可访问任意工具、重构缺乏检查。

Method: 将每个工作流重构为作用于受限状态的类型化函数，对整个库施加四级渐进范式：FNF-1分解为原子步骤、FNF-2显式化并类型检查数据流、FNF-3为共享过程提供单一规范定义以传播变更、FNF-4将每步限制为最小权限工具访问；构建可证可靠的等式代数将重构与并行化转为编译器重写；正交扩展+V（终止性验证）、+O（优化）、+B（时间与成本界）；并给出0–3级架构分类器判断工作流是否需AI。实现为SkillBase管理系统及独立运行时执行器。

Result: 理论在SkillBase中实现，配套执行器在运行时强制上述保证；通过一个端到端的金融工作流案例加以说明。

Conclusion: 规范化后每个原子步骤成为具有声明式契约的独立实体，可单独进行类型检查、作用域限制、边界约束、测试、追踪与审计；由此完整性、隔离、类型、终止、成本与优化成为覆盖整个库的编译期属性而非运行时期望。

Abstract: Organizations increasingly encode operational procedures as multi-step agent skills, here called Agent Operating Procedures (AOPs), that must be traceable, auditable, reproducible, access-controlled, and bounded in time and cost, requirements that regulated sectors make mandatory. Yet AOPs are typically kept as coarse-grained files, in which steps bundle actions, copied procedures drift apart, any step may reach any tool, and refactoring goes unchecked. We present Functional Agent Operating Procedures (FAOP), a formal theory that brings database-grade discipline to AI agent workflows. FAOP recasts each workflow as a typed function over scoped state and subjects an entire library to four progressive normal forms: Decomposed (FNF-1) splits a workflow into atomic steps, Typed (FNF-2) makes its data flow explicit and type-checked, Factored (FNF-3) gives each shared procedure a single canonical definition so a change propagates instead of drifting, and Scoped (FNF-4) confines every step to least-privilege tool access. Once normalized, every atomic step is a separate entity with a declared contract and can be type-checked, scope-confined, bounded, tested, traced, and audited on its own. A FAOP algebra of provably sound equalities turns refactoring and parallelization into compiler rewrites; orthogonal extensions add verified termination (+V), optimization (+O), and time and cost bounds (+B); and an architectural-level classifier (0-3) determines whether a workflow needs AI at all. Together, these make integrity, isolation, typing, termination, cost, and optimization compile-time concerns across the whole library, not runtime hopes. We implement the theory in SkillBase, an AOP management system (what PostgreSQL is to data, SkillBase is to AOPs), together with a separate executor that enforces these guarantees at run time. A worked financial workflow illustrates the approach end to end.

</details>


### [221] [Progress Reward Model for Reinforcement Learning via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22056)
*Xiuhui Zhang,Ning Gao,Xingyu Jiang,Yihui Chen,Yuheng Pan,Mohan Zhang,Yue Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PRM4RL框架，将LLM任务规划与基于进度函数构造的稠密奖励模型结合，用于稀疏奖励长程RL任务，并在机器人控制任务上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RL在稀疏奖励长程任务上存在局限：基于LLM规划的方法依赖预定义技能库且不优化底层控制策略；基于奖励生成的方法因任务复杂而需要大量人工反馈或穷举搜索。

Method: 高层规划将复杂任务分解为一系列简单可管理的子任务，并设计面向子任务的细粒度进度函数以监测执行进度；底层奖励生成受potential-based reward shaping启发，用该进度函数构造Progress Reward Model（PRM），提供理论上的最优性与收敛保证，从而实现有效的策略优化。

Result: 在机器人控制任务上的实验表明，该方法优于基于LLM的规划方法与奖励方法，达到state-of-the-art性能；代码已开源。

Conclusion: PRM4RL通过统一高层任务规划与底层稠密奖励生成，为稀疏奖励长程RL提供了一种有理论保证且实验有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Traditional reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms face significant limitations in handling long-term tasks with sparse rewards. Recent advancements have leveraged large language models (LLMs) to enhance RL by utilizing their world knowledge for task planning and reward generation. However, planning-based approaches often depend on pre-defined skill libraries and fail to optimize low-level control policies, while reward-based methods require extensive human feedback or exhaustive searching due to the complexity of tasks. In this paper, we propose the Progress Reward Model for RL (PRM4RL), a novel framework that integrates task planning and dense reward to enhance RL. For high-level planning, a complex task is decomposed into a series of simple manageable subtasks, with a subtask-oriented, fine-grained progress function designed to monitor task execution progress. For low-level reward generation, inspired by potential-based reward shaping, we use the progress function to construct a Progress Reward Model (PRM), providing theoretically grounded optimality and convergence guarantees, thereby enabling effective policy optimization. Experimental results on robotics control tasks demonstrate that our approach outperforms both LLM-based planning and reward methods, achieving state-of-the-art performance. Code is available at https://github.com/deng-ai-lab/PRM4RL.

</details>


### [222] [CARA: Causally Guided Retrieval Agent for Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22017)
*Weicheng Liu,Xiangcheng Wu,Depeng Xu,Haopeng Zhang,Xi Niu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练、因果引导的检索智能体CARA，用"结果性"与"必要性"两个因果相关性准则评估证据，结合路径一致性引导图探索，并以倒数排名融合聚合多智能体证据，在三个STaRK数据集及增强版上一致优于现有检索器，且检查节点更少、检索操作更少，下游问答质量与忠实度更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在文本丰富的知识图谱上进行检索增强生成，关键在于识别对回答问题有贡献的证据，而非仅匹配文本内容。现有智能体检索器虽能自适应平衡全局搜索与多跳探索，但主要依赖文本相似度来引导检索，难以刻画证据对答案的真实贡献。

Method: CARA为免训练框架，显式评估检索证据的因果相关性，采用两条互补准则：结果性（加入证据是否提升可回答性）与必要性（移除证据是否削弱可回答性）；将二者与路径一致性结合以引导图探索，并用倒数排名融合（RRF）聚合多个智能体的证据。

Result: 在三个STaRK数据集上评估，并为STaRK补充真实标注答案以支持下游问答评测。实验显示CARA一致优于现有检索器，同时提升答案质量与忠实度，且检查的图节点数和所需检索操作更少。

Conclusion: 因果相关性评估能使智能体图检索更聚焦、更有效，同时提升检索性能与下游问答表现。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation over text-rich knowledge graphs requires identifying evidence that contributes to answering a question, rather than merely matching its textual content. Existing agentic retrievers adaptively balance global search and multi-hop exploration but primarily rely on textual similarity to guide retrieval. We propose CARA, a training-free, CAusally guided Retrieval Agent that explicitly evaluates the causal relevance of retrieved evidence through two complementary criteria: consequentiality, measuring whether adding evidence improves answerability, and essentiality, assessing whether removing evidence weakens answerability. CARA integrates these assessments with path consistency to guide graph exploration and employs reciprocal rank fusion to aggregate evidence from multiple agents. We evaluate CARA on three STaRK datasets and augment STaRK with ground-truth answers to enable downstream question answering evaluation. Experimental results demonstrate that CARA consistently outperforms existing retrievers. CARA also improves answer quality and faithfulness while examining fewer graph nodes and requiring fewer retrieval operations. These findings demonstrate that causal relevance assessment enables more focused and effective agentic graph retrieval, improving both retrieval performance and downstream question answering.

</details>


### [223] [CoWorld Reasoner: Agentic Multimodal Reasoning with the Evolving Video World Model-Driven Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21910)
*Dongjie Cheng,Ruifeng Yuan,Ran Wei,Ning Song,Yongqi Li,Wenjie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 CoWorld Reasoner 智能体框架，将视频世界模型（VWM）作为统一的视觉推理引擎，通过工具(Harness)与技能(Skills)让 VLM 自主决定是否想象、选择操作并解读生成证据，并辅以工具-技能-代码的协同进化（权重冻结），在七个基准上超越同骨干的直接推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 需要超越输入图像的时空推理（运动、动作、视角变化），VWM 能提供任务相关的视觉想象作为补充证据；但现有方法以固定流水线调用 VWM，局限于预设的想象形式（如新视角合成），难以适配多样推理任务。

Method: 1) 以 VWM 为统一视觉推理引擎的智能体框架；2) Video World Model-Driven Harness 通过工具暴露多种视觉操作，并用 skills 引导 VLM 判断是否需想象、选择操作、构造生成请求、解读视觉证据；3) VLM-VWM Harness 协同进化，基于执行反馈联合修订工具、技能与执行代码，模型权重保持不变。

Result: 在空间智能、时空与因果推理、感知推理七个基准上均优于同 VLM 直接推理；最强版本在全部七项上提升，MMSI 相对准确率 +11.8%，ERQA +13.0%；在五项基准上优于使用相同骨干的五个进化基线。

Conclusion: 把 VWM 从固定流水线中的单一功能模块转变为可灵活调度的统一视觉想象引擎，配合无需训练的协同进化机制，可显著提升 VLM 的复杂时空视觉推理能力。

Abstract: As Vision-Language Models (VLMs) advance, they are increasingly expected to perform spatiotemporal reasoning over motion, actions, and view changes beyond what the input images show. Video World Models (VWMs) provide a natural unified engine for visual imagination by generating task-relevant visual content as additional evidence. However, existing approaches typically invoke a VWM through fixed pipelines centered on a predefined form of visual imagination, such as novel-view synthesis. To support flexible visual imagination for diverse reasoning tasks, we introduce CoWorld Reasoner, an agentic framework that uses a VWM as a unified visual reasoning engine. Its Video World Model-Driven Harness exposes multiple visual operations through tools and uses skills to guide the VLM in deciding whether to imagine, selecting an operation, constructing a generation request, and interpreting the resulting visual evidence. We further introduce VLM-VWM Harness Co-Evolution, which jointly revises tools, skills, and execution code based on execution feedback while keeping both model weights fixed. Across seven benchmarks spanning spatial intelligence, temporal and causal reasoning, and perceptual reasoning, CoWorld Reasoner outperforms direct reasoning with the same VLM. The strongest variant improves on all seven benchmarks, including relative accuracy gains of 11.8% on MMSI and 13.0% on ERQA, and outperforms all five evolving baselines using the same backbone across five benchmarks.

</details>


### [224] [LoopAhead: Speculating Ahead for Faster Decoding in Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21594)
*Zixuan Liu,Nan Xue,Rui Bao,Yuhang Xu,Xiaofeng Cao,Jiangchao Yao,Zhiyong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LoopAhead：面向循环Transformer的推测解码框架，利用早循环状态做长前瞻草稿、深层反馈筛选分支、全深度模型最终裁决，解码加速最高7.82倍且保持目标分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer虽然有参数效率优势，但逐token生成仍需顺序计算并反复访问权重，解码延迟高。现有流水线自推测解码虽以早循环预测草稿并异步推进不同循环深度的token，但未充分利用早循环特征实现更长前瞻，且仍在验证错误推测分支上浪费算力。

Method: 提出LoopAhead框架：轻量级前瞻草稿器（LAD）利用早循环状态一次预测多个未来token；深层循环反馈决定哪些草稿分支继续、哪些暂停；全深度模型负责最终token判定，实现不同循环深度的互补分工。

Result: 相比自回归解码最高取得7.82倍解码加速，同时保持目标模型的输出分布不变（无损加速）。

Conclusion: 通过更早草稿、分支动态筛选与深度分层分工，可显著降低循环Transformer的解码延迟，且不牺牲输出质量。

Abstract: Looped Transformers improve parameter efficiency by repeatedly applying the same Transformer layers. However, generating each token still involves sequential computation and repeated access to model weights, leading to high decoding latency. Recent pipelined self-speculative decoding methods reduce this delay by drafting tokens from early-loop predictions and asynchronously advancing tokens at different recurrent depths in the same batch, but they do not fully exploit early-loop features for longer lookahead and still spend computation verifying incorrect speculative branches. We introduce \method, a speculative decoding framework for looped transformers that enables earlier drafting of future tokens and assigns complementary roles to different recurrent depths. A lightweight lookahead drafter (LAD) predicts multiple future tokens with early-loop states, and deeper-loop feedback helps decide which draft branches to continue and which to pause, while the full-depth model makes the final token decisions. LoopAhead achieves up to 7.82x decoding speedup over autoregressive decoding while preserving the target model's output distribution.

</details>


### [225] [LOADBench: A Large Open Analog Dataset and Benchmark for Machine Learning in IC Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21591)
*M. Leibl,F. Azevedo,S. Uhlich,A. Bonetti,A. Venkitaraman,C. Hsieh,P. Pham,L. Servadei,H. Graeb,R. Martins*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发布了一个面向模拟IC设计机器学习的大型开源数据集（SkyWater130下100+种运放拓扑，含结构/功能标注、尺寸约束、Ngspice测试台及NSGA-II多目标尺寸优化结果），并基于此定义了六个标准化基准任务，以解决数据封闭与评价口径不统一的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟集成电路（IC）设计的机器学习研究受限于两点：开放数据稀缺；缺乏标准化评价体系——不同论文通常基于各自定制的、往往不公开的数据集与临时性指标进行评估，导致跨方法比较不可靠。作者旨在同时填补数据与评价两方面的空白。

Method: 构建SkyWater130 PDK下的开源数据集：包含100余种运算放大器拓扑（1/2/3级），每种拓扑附带结构与功能标注、拓扑相关的尺寸约束（对称性与尺寸规则）、Ngspice测试台，以及一次多目标NSGA-II尺寸优化运行，每拓扑约产生10^3个Pareto最优解和10^6个中间尺寸—性能样本对。在数据之上定义六个基准任务并给出精确协议：逆尺寸设计、拓扑选择、子电路分类、结构化电路生成、Pareto隶属预测、多目标优化；同时开源真值标注与指标定义程序的参考实现。

Result: 提供了作者所称的、模拟IC设计自动化四十余年来最全面的机器学习数据集；六个基准任务配套精确协议与参考指标实现，使异构模拟设计自动化方法之间可进行可复现、直接可比的评测。

Conclusion: 该工作以开放许可发布数据集、真值标注与指标参考实现，为模拟IC设计的机器学习研究建立了统一的数据与评测基础设施，推动结果的可复现性与跨方法可比性。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) for analog IC design is constrained by limited open data and the absence of standardized evaluation: results reported in different papers are typically obtained on bespoke, often closed, datasets and with ad-hoc metrics, leaving comparisons across methods unreliable. We address both gaps with a large opensource dataset and a suite of standardized benchmarks. The dataset contains more than 100 operational amplifier (opamp) topologies (1-, 2-, and 3-stage) in the SkyWater130 PDK, each shipped with structural and functional annotations, topology-specific sizing constraints (symmetry and sizing rules), an Ngspice testbench, and a multi-objective NSGA-II sizing run producing around 103 Pareto-optimal and 106 intermediate sizing-performance pairs per topology. On top of this data we define six benchmark tasks with precise protocols: inverse sizing, topology selection, sub-circuit classification, structural circuit generation, Pareto-membership prediction, and multi-objective optimization. Thus, the data presented here constitutes the most comprehensive dataset for ML development in over four decades of analog IC design automation. The dataset, ground-truth annotations, and reference implementations of the metric-defining routines are released under an open license, enabling reproducible and directly comparable evaluation across heterogeneous analog design automation methods.

</details>


### [226] [ClaimScope: Label-Blind Witness Verification for Mathematical Claim Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21581)
*Mushir Akhtar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ClaimScope 基准（72 任务），用"判定—范围—反例—修复"审计数学断言；对 Qwen3-8B 对比直接生成与四种双通道条件，发现验证器反馈仅提升反例接受数与安全完成数，而不改善严格完成或判定准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学智能体多以答案正确率或证明是否被接受来评价，无法区分"断言分类"与"可执行证据"；作者希望建立一种审计式评测，检验模型能否给出带范围界定与反例的具体反驳，并厘清验证器反馈带来的增益究竟来自何处。

Method: 构建 ClaimScope：72 个任务，覆盖优化器状态搬运、有限时域仿射递推、精确 2×2 线性代数。被测模型为 Qwen3-8B，条件包括直接生成与四种匹配的两轮流程（均从同一草稿出发）。设置 witness checker，仅校验模型提交的状态，不参考参考答案、不提供替代证据；同时对照"无检查器的通用反馈"。以任务分组 bootstrap 估计置信区间，并做仅模式（schema-only）的事后修正。

Result: 验证器反馈在无效断言上获得 23/39 个被接受反例，安全完成 25/72；无检查器通用反馈对应为 15/39 与 20/72。严格完成仍为 17/72，判定准确率由 47/72 降至 46/72。按断言分组的安全完成差值为 .094，bootstrap 95% 区间 [.000,.198]。七项任务级增益均出现在 horizon/输入长度诊断下。仅对直接输出做模式修正即达到同样的 25 个安全任务，且无需再次调用模型。

Conclusion: 结果将断言分类与可执行证据分离：观测到的增益源于见证结构（witness-structure）层面的修正，而非更广泛的推理能力提升；验证器反馈的价值是可验证的证伪与结构化修复线索，而非判定能力本身。

Abstract: Mathematical agents are commonly evaluated by answer accuracy or proof acceptance. We introduce ClaimScope, a 72-task benchmark for auditing proposed claims through a verdict, scope, counterexample, and repair. Tasks cover optimizer-state transport, finite-horizon affine recurrences, and exact $2\times2$ linear algebra. We evaluate Qwen3-8B with direct generation and four matched two-pass conditions, each starting from the same draft. A witness checker evaluates only the model's submitted state, without consulting reference verdicts or supplying a replacement witness. Its feedback still conveys information about validity: a verified refutation establishes that the claim is false, and rejection reasons can suggest structural fixes. In this evaluation, verifier feedback yields $23/39$ accepted counterexamples on invalid claims and $25/72$ safe completions, compared with $15/39$ and $20/72$ for checker-free generic feedback. Strict completion remains $17/72$; verdict accuracy changes from $47/72$ to $46/72$. The claim-grouped safe-completion difference is $.094$, with a bootstrap 95\% interval of $[.000,.198]$. All seven task-level gains follow horizon/input-length diagnostics. A post-hoc schema-only correction of the direct outputs reaches the same 25 safe tasks without another model call. These results separate claim classification from executable evidence, while locating the observed gains in witness-structure correction rather than demonstrating broader reasoning improvement.

</details>


### [227] [QREF: Improving DeepSearch Agents by Aligning Search with Reasoning via Query Refinement and Evidence Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21512)
*Yujia Chen,Yuhui Wang,Junzhe Wang,Shihan Dou,Di Liang,Yujiong Shen,Yuhao Zhou,Zhiheng Xi,Ming Zhang,Senjie Jin,Jiayi Chen,Dingwei Zhu,Yajie Yang,Yuan Hua,Chenyao Lu,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出即插即用的查询精炼器与证据过滤器QREF，通过SFT+RL优化，显著提升DeepSearch智能体检索效率与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeepSearch智能体在迭代搜索与推理中存在查询意图错位、约束不足或冗余的问题，导致证据覆盖差、检索噪声累积。

Method: QREF包含两部分：Query Refiner依据当前推理意图重写查询并保证同一检索批次内的互补覆盖；Evidence Filter保留与当前检索批次及推理目标均相关的证据。先用监督微调初始化Query Refiner，再基于与真实网络搜索环境的交互用强化学习优化。

Result: 在四个挑战性基准上，无需修改架构或重训推理策略，WebSailor平均相对Pass@1提升33.55%，TongyiDeepResearch提升11.04%；相比无QREF的原始骨干，RL训练的refiner使缺乏判别性检索锚点的查询批次比例下降95%；相比SFT版本，也降低了基于过滤器的无效查询比例。

Conclusion: QREF作为即插即用模块可有效缓解DeepSearch的查询质量问题，提升证据覆盖并抑制检索噪声，具备良好的通用性与可移植性。

Abstract: DeepSearch agents achieve strong performance on complex information-seeking tasks through iterative search and reasoning. However, they often issue intent-misaligned, under-constrained, or redundant search queries, resulting in poor evidence coverage and accumulating retrieval noise. To mitigate these limitations, we propose QREF, a plug-and-play Query Refiner and Evidence Filter. Specifically, the Query Refiner rewrites search queries according to the current reasoning intent while ensuring complementary coverage across each search batch. The Evidence Filter retains evidence relevant to both the current search batch and the reasoning objective, reducing distracting retrieval results. We initialize the Query Refiner with supervised fine-tuning and optimize it through reinforcement learning based on interactions with a real-world web search environment, reducing ineffective queries. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that our approach improves the performance of DeepSearch agents across four challenging benchmarks, achieving average relative Pass@1 improvements of 33.55% on WebSailor and 11.04% on TongyiDeepResearch without modifying architectures or retraining their reasoning policies. Compared with the original DeepSearch backbone without QREF, the RL-trained refiner reduces the fraction of query batches that lack discriminative retrieval anchors by 95%. Compared with the SFT-trained refiner, it also lowers the filter-based ineffective query ratio.

</details>


### [228] [MetaExp: Learning to Self-Improve through Experience via Verifiable Cross-Task Environment Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21492)
*Renhao Li,Xinjie Shen,Yang Su,Jianhong Tu,Derek F. Wong,Dayiheng Liu,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MetaExp框架，通过具有受控跨任务结构的可验证任务族来训练"经验学习"这一可迁移模型能力，微调后的模型在下游任务上仅靠参数冻结的非参数化进化即获得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体能够积累交互反馈，但难以可靠地将其转化为可复用经验。现有方法要么优化外部经验脚手架，要么在特定任务分布上训练与经验相关的行为，因此"经验学习本身能否成为一种可迁移的模型能力"仍未被回答。而训练这种能力需要超越孤立任务的监督信号，因为孤立任务主要支持回合内问题求解，无法揭示哪些知识在跨回合间仍然有用。

Method: MetaExp是一个可扩展训练框架，构建具有受控跨任务结构的可验证任务族：每个任务族保留一个可复用因子，同时变化任务特定实现，从而在不直接复用解的前提下促进迁移。提出两种互补设计：Shared-Tool族固定黑盒工具以引出操作性经验；Shared-Workflow族在变化的实现中保留潜在流程以引出程序性经验。在这两种设计下扩展环境，采集并过滤序列交互轨迹，在来自1,000个任务族的4,956条轨迹上微调Qwen3.5-122B-A10B。

Result: 在下游进化过程中参数保持冻结，MetaExp在SpreadsheetBench上提升+8.5分，在APEX-Agents上最高提升+2.3分，在AutomationBench上提升+14.8分；且在排除相关领域的训练设置下增益依然持续。

Conclusion: 结果表明经验学习可以作为一项可迁移能力被训练出来，支持模型在部署后通过非参数化方式持续改进。

Abstract: Language-model agents can accumulate interaction feedback, but reliably turning that feedback into reusable experience remains challenging. Existing methods either optimize external experience scaffolds or train experience-related behavior on specific task distributions, leaving open whether experience learning itself can become a transferable model capability. Training such a capability calls for supervision beyond isolated tasks, which mainly support in-episode problem solving rather than revealing what knowledge remains useful across episodes. In this work, we introduce MetaExp, a scalable framework that trains this capability through verifiable task families with controlled cross-task structure. Each family preserves a reusable factor while varying task-specific realizations, enabling transfer without direct solution reuse. We propose two complementary designs: Shared-Tool families keep black-box tools fixed across tasks to elicit operational experience, while Shared-Workflow families preserve latent procedures across changing realizations to elicit procedural experience. We scale environments under both designs, collect and filter sequential interaction trajectories, and fine-tune Qwen3.5-122B-A10B on 4,956 trajectories from 1,000 task families. With parameters frozen during downstream evolution, MetaExp improves by +8.5 points on SpreadsheetBench, peaks at +2.3 on APEX-Agents, and improves by +14.8 on AutomationBench. The gains persist under domain-excluded training, supporting experience learning as a transferable capability for continued non-parametric improvement after deployment.

</details>


### [229] [Learning from Interaction Experience via Localized Context Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21427)
*Zhezheng Hao,Yongkang Yang,Xilin Xia,Jiarui Yu,Yi Liu,Fanjunduo Wei,Peijie Dong,Tianfu Wang,Qiang Lin,Can Wang,Hande Dong,Jiawei Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出局部上下文蒸馏（LoCD）：先定位学生轨迹中需要修改的关键片段，再由教师基于学生自有前缀重生成这些片段，学生仅在被重生成的片段上训练，从而在聚焦高信息量状态的同时保持较低曝光偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文蒸馏存在两难：离线（off-policy）蒸馏用教师生成的轨迹训练学生，带来曝光偏差；在线（on-policy）蒸馏用学生自身 rollout 训练，但其状态往往信息量低，无法传达学生尚不能产生的行为。

Method: LoCD 分两步：首先定位学生轨迹中需要修正的关键片段（critical spans）；随后教师仅以学生自身前缀为条件重生成这些片段，学生以重生成片段为训练目标进行蒸馏。

Result: 在数学推理、代码生成和智能体任务上的大量实验表明，LoCD 持续优于强基线，且训练更稳定，参数更新更小、更稀疏。

Conclusion: LoCD 通过片段级定位与教师重生成，在离线与在线上下文蒸馏之间取得折中，兼顾低曝光偏差与高信息量学习信号，是一种有效的部署期持续改进方法。

Abstract: Learning from interaction experience is essential for enabling large language models to continually improve during deployment. Context distillation offers a promising path: privileged information extracted from experience is provided to a teacher, whose behavior is then distilled into a student model that has no access to it. However, both existing forms of context distillation have limitations. Off-policy context distillation trains the student on teacher-generated trajectories and thus suffers from exposure bias; whereas on-policy context distillation trains on the student's own rollouts, whose states are often uninformative and fail to convey behaviors the student cannot yet produce. To resolve this dilemma, we introduce Localized Context Distillation (LoCD). LoCD first localizes the critical spans of a student trajectory that require revision. The teacher then regenerates only these spans from the student's own prefix, and the student is trained on the regenerated segments. LoCD thereby concentrates learning on the most informative states while keeping exposure bias low. Extensive experiments across math reasoning, code generation, and agent tasks demonstrate that LoCD consistently outperforms strong baselines and achieves more stable training with smaller and sparser parameter updates.

</details>


### [230] [Jev-LiteMAS: Separating Decisions from Generation in Multi-Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21411)
*Kaixiang Wang,Zhaijiacheng Zhou,Jiong Lou,Jie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Jev-LiteMAS 用轻量决策模型 Jev 承担路由与审查等有界决策，并用动态专家选择与“筛选-复核”两段式流程弥合规划与反馈接口缺口，在保持精度的同时显著降低生成式 LLM token 消耗与延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体 LLM 系统在专家执行、工作流规划和产物审查上都依赖生成式模型，但路由与审查本质上是“有界决策”，无需生成式推理。对三个代表性 MAS 框架的剖析显示，路由与审查占生成式 LLM token 的 31.7-43.5%，存在明显成本浪费。但直接用低成本决策模型替换会带来两个接口缺口：规划缺口（生成式规划器输出执行计划与交接指令）与审查缺口（审查者给出可执行的修复反馈），有界决策无法提供。

Method: 提出 Jev-LiteMAS：将轻量决策模型 Jev 与生成式执行、反馈相结合。针对规划缺口，Jev 基于任务与先前专家输出动态选择专家，用累积上下文接地后续执行，不预先生成轨迹；针对审查缺口，Jev 先按任务标准筛选中间产物，通过者直接放行，被标记者交由 LLM 二次审查，决定接受或给出纠正性反馈。

Result: 在四个基准和两个主干模型上，相比 LAS，生成式 LLM token 减少 12.7-54.6%，精度差距控制在 1.34 个百分点以内，八种设置中有七种降低延迟。将路由建立在实质性专家输出之上可避免高达 18.0 个百分点的精度损失。

Conclusion: 有界决策与生成式能力的混合编排可有效替代高成本的生成式路由与审查，关键在于用实质专家输出接地路由决策、并以“轻量筛选+LLM 复核”保留可执行反馈，从而在成本、精度与延迟间取得更优权衡。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems rely on generative models for expert execution, workflow planning, and artifact review. Yet many coordination operations require only bounded decisions, such as selecting the next expert or judging artifact quality. Profiling three representative MAS frameworks, we find that routing and review account for 31.7-43.5% of generative-LLM tokens. However, replacing these operations with low-cost decision models introduces two interface gaps: generative planners produce execution plans and handoff instructions (planning gap), while reviewers provide actionable feedback for repairing flawed artifacts (review gap), which bounded decisions alone cannot provide. We introduce Jev-LiteMAS, a multi-agent framework that bridges both gaps by combining a lightweight decision model (Jev) with generative execution and feedback. To address the planning gap, Jev selects experts dynamically from the task and prior expert outputs, using accumulated context to ground subsequent execution without a pre-generated trajectory. To address the review gap, Jev screens intermediate artifacts against task criteria: passing artifacts proceed directly, whereas flagged ones undergo LLM re-review for acceptance or corrective feedback. Across four benchmarks and two backbones, Jev-LiteMAS reduces generative-LLM tokens by 12.7-54.6% over LAS, maintaining accuracy within 1.34 percentage points and lowering latency in seven of eight settings. Grounding routing in substantive expert outputs prevents up to 18.0 points in accuracy loss.

</details>


### [231] [When Reality Outpaces Cognition: A New Scaling Principle for Reliable Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20764)
*Ahsan Adeel,Talha Bin Riaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"现实速度"与"有效连贯认知速度(S_C^eff)"的挑战框架，衡量智能系统在观测—现实差距增大时仍能可靠行动的现实变化上限；强化学习智能体基线训练后不重训、算力固定，在S_R提升至50倍条件下评测，类细胞机制的Co^4架构显著优于Transformer与门控MLP基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI范式与新兴世界模型的"理解能力"缺乏量化检验。作者希望回答一个根本问题：现实可以变多快，智能系统才会失去可靠感知、推理与行动能力？为此需要一个与理论和模型无关的挑战设定，刻画不断扩大的观测—现实差距下的可靠决策。

Method: 提出与理论/模型无关的挑战，形式化"有效连贯认知速度"S_C^eff，定义为系统从基线起持续满足预设行为可靠性准则所通过的最高"现实速度"S_R。概念验证：强化学习智能体在基线S_R下训练，随后在算力固定、不重训的条件下将S_R提升至最多50倍进行评测；对比Transformer、门控MLP基线与受细胞机制（与意识加工及清醒思维相关）启发的Co^4架构。

Result: 在所有测试场景中，Co^4达到的S_C^eff显著高于Transformer与门控MLP基线。

Conclusion: S_C^eff可作为新的基准指标，而"连贯认知的可扩展性"可作为实现可靠AI的设计原则。

Abstract: This paper challenges the understanding capabilities of existing AI paradigms and emerging world models by asking a fundamental question: How quickly can reality change before an intelligent system can no longer reliably perceive, reason, and act? We formulate this as a theory- and model-agnostic challenge in which systems must infer reliable actions for increasingly distant future states across a growing observation--reality gap. We operationalize this challenge through the effective Speed of Coherent Cognition (\(S_C^{\mathrm{eff}}\)), defined as the highest tested Speed of Reality (\(S_R\)) through which a system continuously satisfies a predefined behavioural reliability criterion from baseline. As a proof of concept, reinforcement-learning agents are trained at the baseline \(S_R\) and evaluated, without retraining, as \(S_R\) is increased by up to \(50\times\) while computational resources remain fixed. We compare Transformer and gated-MLP baselines with \(Co^4\), an architecture inspired by cellular mechanisms implicated in conscious processing and awake thought. Across the tested scenarios, \(Co^4\) achieves substantially higher \(S_C^{\mathrm{eff}}\) than the baselines, motivating \(S_C^{\mathrm{eff}}\) as a new benchmark and the scaling of coherent cognition as a principle for reliable AI.

</details>


### [232] [Constrained Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning in Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21319)
*Raz Shmueli,Shahaf S. Shperberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 CA-MAMBA：在多智能体世界模型中进行代价感知的想象式策略更新（cost-aware model-based MARL）。代价预测把大部分受限策略更新移入想象空间，从而在其他方法需 10–20 倍交互的 SMAC 中位配置上完成学习，并在等预算下取得基线 2.8–12 倍的 MAMuJoCo 回报；受控实验表明模型代价估计并非瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习中的受约束策略更新通常需要大量真实环境交互，样本效率低；若能在世界模型（imagination）中完成受限更新，可大幅降低交互成本。作者希望验证"代价建模在多智能体世界模型中是否可行且不是性能瓶颈"。

Method: 基于多智能体世界模型（学习的环境模型）进行想象式 rollout，在想象轨迹上执行代价感知/受约束的策略更新；在 SMAC 与 MAMuJoCo 上同预算对比强 model-free 基线与 cost-blind 学习器，并做"将模型代价优势按平价定价"的受控消融（单任务）以隔离代价估计误差的影响。

Result: 1) 在 SMAC 中位配置上，更强的 model-free 基线需要 CA-MAMBA 的 10–20 倍交互量；2) 等预算下 MAMuJoCo 回报为基线的 2.8–12 倍；3) 受控测试中，将模型代价优势按平价定价可使 cost-blind 学习器的代价降低超过一半，并达到学习型乘子未能满足的严格连续控制约束。

Conclusion: 将代价预测与多智能体世界模型结合可显著提升受约束策略学习的样本效率；模型给出的代价估计质量足以支撑有效约束，真正的瓶颈不在代价估计，而在于如何使用（定价）这些估计。

Abstract: Predicting cost in a multi-agent world model moves most constrained policy updates into imagination: the stronger model-free baseline needs $10$ to $20$ times CA-MAMBA's interactions on the median SMAC configuration, and CA-MAMBA earns $2.8$ to $12$ times the baselines' MAMuJoCo reward at equal budget. In controlled tests on one task per benchmark, pricing the model's cost advantages at parity more than halves a cost-blind learner's cost and meets a tight continuous-control limit the learned multiplier misses, suggesting the model's cost estimates are not the bottleneck.

</details>


### [233] [Money-Mule Detection as a Relational Learning Problem: A Study on the RBIH NFPC Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21314)
*Owais Ahmad,Anirudh Deodhar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出将银行关系型数据库模式直接视为异构图（行=节点、外键=边），以账户为目标节点、基于多跳邻域做HeteroGraphSAGE打分识别钱骡账户，并严格采用"账户被标记前"的时间截断避免信息泄露；在4亿笔交易、9.4万标注账户的真实数据上，实现90%召回、83%精确率、0.5%误报率，优于RelGNN与主流树模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钱骡网络的单笔转账看似正常，其风险信号存在于交易对手、共享地址等关系结构及账户的时间行为形态中，单笔交易分类无法捕捉；生产系统多为规则驱动，学术研究多以交易为分类单位再聚合到账户，二者均未直接对账户及其邻域建模。

Method: 以银行为账户级打分目标；将银行关系型schema读作异构图（行→节点，外键→边）；对每个账户在其多跳邻域上使用分列编码器（per-column encoders）、相对时间编码与HeteroGraphSAGE消息传递进行表示学习；并将邻域限制为该账户被标记cutoff之前的历史行，杜绝事后证据泄露；在RBIH五年零售银行数据（4亿交易、94,063个标注账户，2.6%为确认钱骡）上，与RelGNN、XGBoost、LightGBM、CatBoost在相同标签、划分与截断下对比。

Result: 在图模型下，held-out钱骡召回率约90%、精确率83%、误报率0.5%；在同等证据下比最强树模型多识别115个钱骡。调优阈值下差距主要体现在召回：XGBoost和LightGBM在队列前100个告警中领先，但只有图模型能在告警队列较深位置保持精确率。

Conclusion: 直接以关系型schema为图、对账户端到端打分，可在无需自建特征图的前提下部署于银行既有数据库，并按账户给出带日期约束的评分；关系结构与时序形态对钱骡识别具有实质增益，且在深度告警队列中精度更稳健。

Abstract: Money-mule networks move criminal funds through many small transfers, each of which looks ordinary on its own. What marks an account as a mule lies outside any single transfer: in the counterparties and addresses its network shares, and in the shape of its activity over time. Production systems remain largely rule-based and see neither, while most research classifies transactions rather than accounts, recovering account-level risk only by aggregating afterwards. We instead score the account directly, reading the bank's relational schema as a heterogeneous graph in which rows are nodes and foreign keys are edges. Each account is scored from a multi-hop neighbourhood using per-column encoders, a relative-time encoding and HeteroGraphSAGE message passing, with that neighbourhood restricted to rows predating the account's own pre-flag cutoff so that no model sees evidence recorded only after the account was flagged. On a five-year Reserve Bank Innovation Hub (RBIH) retail-banking dataset of 400 million transactions and 94{,}063 labelled accounts, 2.6\% of them confirmed mules, we compare against RelGNN, XGBoost, LightGBM and CatBoost trained on identical labels, splits and cutoffs. Our detector recovers nine in ten held-out mules at 83\% precision and a 0.5\% false-alarm rate, finding 115 more mules than the strongest tree ensemble given the same evidence. At the tuned thresholds the gap is mostly in recall: XGBoost and LightGBM lead across the first hundred alerts, but only the graph models hold their precision deep into the queue. Since the graph is the schema itself and every score is dated per account, the approach deploys on a bank's existing database without a custom feature graph build.

</details>


### [234] [Condition-Contrastive Extrapolation for On-Policy Distillation of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21195)
*Wenxuan Luo,Chenxi Li,Haoyue Liu,Yongxin Guo,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出条件对比外推的在线策略蒸馏（CCE-OPD），使教师监督对题目条件敏感，抑制教师对无效续写的偏好，从而提升蒸馏推理表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）在学生学习前缀条件下由教师提供监督，但教师会偏好违反题目前提的无效续写，导致蒸馏强化错误推理步骤。

Method: （1）固定前缀比较：用同一冻结教师同时评估完整问题与被掩蔽某一条件的对照问题；（2）有界对数概率对比：将教师分布重新加权，构造辅助蒸馏目标。

Result: 在200个诊断推理状态上，无效续写偏好率由27.0%降至16.0%；在六个数学基准上，avg@8较OPD平均提升1.60（Qwen3-8B→0.6B）与1.95（→1.7B）个百分点；四个迁移基准上取得最佳结果。

Conclusion: 条件对比可有效纠正OPD中教师的无效续写偏好，CCE-OPD在数学推理与迁移任务上均优于OPD。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) improves a student language model by training on its own generated trajectories with supervision from a stronger teacher. However, when conditioned on student-generated prefixes, the teacher can favor invalid continuations that violate the question's premises, causing OPD to encourage erroneous reasoning steps. As a remedy, we introduce Condition-Contrastive Extrapolated On-Policy Distillation (CCE-OPD), which makes teacher supervision sensitive to question conditions. CCE-OPD incorporates (1) a fixed-prefix comparison in which the same frozen teacher evaluates the complete question and a counterpart with a selected condition masked, and (2) a bounded log-probability contrast that reweights the teacher distribution into an auxiliary distillation target. On 200 diagnostic reasoning states, condition contrast reduces the rate of preferring invalid continuations from 27.0% to 16.0%. Extensive experiments across six mathematical benchmarks show that CCE-OPD improves avg@8 over OPD by an average of 1.60 and 1.95 percentage points when distilling Qwen3-8B into 0.6B and 1.7B students, respectively. It also achieves the strongest results on four transfer benchmarks.

</details>


### [235] [Mine, Sample, Render: Test-Time Diversity Alignment Of Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21185)
*Kostas Triaridis,Morris E. Sharp,Nebojsa Jojic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MSR将生成多样性外化为"挖掘-采样-渲染"三段式测试时流程，用真实语料统计指导生成模型做条件渲染，在五个任务上提升分布对齐与多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成模型单个输出质量高，但即便被明确要求，也难以实现多样化采样。作者据此提出将多样性从模型内部转移到显式的、由语料驱动的采样过程。

Method: MSR为测试时方法，含三步：(i) 从真实artifact语料中挖掘可解释的分类schema并提供采样统计；(ii) 依据语料衍生统计采样属性配置；(iii) 仅将生成模型作为采样配置的条件渲染器。

Result: 在天气预报生成、用户模拟、网页设计、文本到图像生成以及agent生成的3D资产五个任务上，评估了MSR引导生成的多样性与输出质量，结果表明MSR提升了与语料分布的对齐度及输出多样性。

Conclusion: 通过把多样性显式化的语料采样与生成模型的条件渲染解耦，MSR可在不牺牲个体质量的前提下改善生成结果的多样性与分布保真度，具有跨模态通用性。

Abstract: Modern generative models produce high-quality individual outputs but struggle to sample diversely, even when explicitly instructed to do so. We introduce MINE-SAMPLE-RENDER (MSR), a test-time approach that externalizes the diversity into an explicit, corpus-derived sampling procedure. Our pipeline (i) mines an interpretable categorical schema from a corpus of real artifacts and supplies statistics for sampling; (ii) samples attribute configurations from the corpus-derived statistics; (iii) uses a generative model purely as a conditional renderer of a sampled configuration. Across weather report generation, user simulation, webpage design, text-to-image generation, and agent-generated 3D assets, we evaluate the diversity and output quality of MSR-guided generation and show that MSR improves alignment with corpus distributions and diversity of generated outputs.

</details>


### [236] [The Density of Thought: Predicting and Steering Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21286)
*Tanush Kalhan,Anupam Chattopadhyay,Sriram Ranga,Amritendu Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究信息密度在CoT推理步骤间的分布是否能预测答案正确性并作为可控的推理期目标，发现训练范式比模型规模更关键，并提出自监督的Entropy-Gated Density Routing（EGDR），在无外部引导下将准确率提升10.0个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究CoT各步骤信息密度差异是否与答案正确性相关，以及能否将密度作为推理时可调控的目标来改进推理表现。

Method: 以冻结预训练模型通过CoT解题，用两个独立指标度量每步密度（LP方差，即平均每token负对数似然的方差；以及Caveman压缩比）；在6个模型规模、2种训练范式、60道GSM8K题上检验密度与正确性的相关性及可操控性。实验包括合成数据上的复杂度偏好测试、直接推理期密度引导、生成前"提示注入+对比KV-cache引导"的nudging，并提出EGDR——利用token级Shannon熵门控按比例调整KV-cache的自监督方法。

Result: （1）低LP方差可预测SFT模型的正确性，但该关系随规模减弱，并在RL蒸馏模型中反转，训练范式比模型规模更具影响力；（2）合成实验显示内在复杂度偏好，模型学习偏好复杂度的分布比偏好简单度的分布准确率高2.0–2.6倍；（3）直接推理期密度引导使准确率从60.0%降至8.3%；（4）生成前nudging提升13.3个百分点，且两指标一致；EGDR在无外部引导下提升10.0个百分点。

Conclusion: 信息密度既是正确性的有效预测信号，也具备可操控性；但自然密度模式对推理功能至关重要，粗暴引导反而有害，需在生成前以hint与对比引导定向干预。EGDR表明基于token级熵的自监督门控KV-cache调整是可行且无需外部监督的推理增强路径。

Abstract: We investigate whether variation in information density across chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning steps predicts answer correctness and can serve as a controllable inference-time target. We prompt frozen pretrained models to solve problems via CoT and measure the density of each reasoning step with two independent metrics: LP variance (how unevenly information is distributed across steps, computed as the variance in mean per-token negative log-likelihood) and the Caveman compression ratio. We then test whether density correlates with, and can be steered to improve, answer correctness. Experiments across six model scales, two training paradigms, and 60 GSM8K problems yield four main findings. First, lower LP variance predicts correctness in supervised fine-tuned (SFT) models, but this relationship weakens with scale and reverses in reinforcement-learning (RL) distilled models. Training paradigm therefore appears more influential than model size in determining density behaviour. Second, synthetic experiments reveal an intrinsic complexity preference: models trained on uniformly distributed data learn complexity-favouring distributions 2.0 to 2.6 times more accurately than simplicity-favouring ones. Third, direct inference-time density steering reduces accuracy from 60.0% to 8.3%, indicating that natural density patterns are functionally important to reasoning. Fourth, pre-generation nudging combining hint injection with contrastive KV-cache steering improves accuracy by 13.3 percentage points, consistent across both metrics. Finally, we introduce Entropy-Gated Density Routing (EGDR), a self-supervised method using token-level Shannon entropy to gate proportional KVcache adjustments during generation, improving accuracy by 10.0 percentage points without any external guidance.

</details>


### [237] [Sci$Π$vot: Benchmarking Principal-Investigator Judgment at Pivotal Moments of Scientific AutoResearch Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21082)
*Youran Sun,Xingyu Ren,Chugang Yi,Kejia Zhang,Di Wu,Yingkang Cao,Allen Zhou,Jianping Ye,Suying Liu,Jiaxuan Guo,Jianda Du,Weicheng Ye,Kecheng Liu,Hefan Zhang,Haixin Wang,Jie Xiao,Eric Yang,Haizhao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文构建了SciΠvot基准（248例科学判断测试），发现前沿LLM在依赖项目上下文做科研判断时系统性偏好自身原始方案、低于随机水平，最高准确率仅63.5%，不及新手研究者的73.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体已能长期自主运行实验，但可靠自主科研还依赖"科学判断力"。由于科研决策开放且高度依赖项目语境，科学判断难以评测，作者据此构建基于人类纠正记录的基准。

Method: 基于资深研究者六个月、跨六个领域、26个项目中反复纠正LLM智能体判断的真实记录，构建248例基准；每例中智能体需仅依据纠正前的项目记录，在原始LLM方案与人工纠正方案间选择；比较LLM与人类表现，并考察推理强度、项目记录可及性与重新考虑的影响。

Result: 中推理强度且有项目记录时，模型系统性偏好原始LLM方案；五个前沿模型中四个低于随机水平；各模型与设置下项目条件最高准确率63.5%，低于新手的73.4%；中推理强度下所有模型有项目记录时准确率反而更低；重新考虑未带来一致的准确率提升。

Conclusion: 即便提供人工纠正备选，前沿模型仍难以处理选定的科研决策；SciΠvot为LLM智能体的科学判断提供了系统化评测手段。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can keep scientific experiments running for months with little human supervision, but reliable autonomous research also requires scientific judgment. Evaluating scientific judgment is difficult because research decisions are open-ended and depend on project context. We construct Sci$Π$vot, a 248-case benchmark of scientific judgment based on human corrections. The benchmark draws on records from six months of research in which senior researchers ran LLM agents on 26 projects across six scientific fields and repeatedly intervened to correct the agents' research judgments. In each case, an agent chooses between the original LLM plan and the human-corrected alternative, using only the project record available before the correction as context. We compare LLM and human performance and examine the effects of reasoning effort, access to the project record, and reconsideration. At medium reasoning effort with the project record, models systematically prefer the original LLM plan over the human-corrected alternative. Four of five frontier models perform below chance on the selected cases. The highest accuracy in the project condition across the tested models and reasoning effort settings is 63.5%, compared with 73.4% for fresh researchers. At medium effort, every model achieves lower accuracy with the project record than without it, and reconsideration does not consistently improve overall accuracy. Even with a human-corrected alternative available, frontier models struggle with the selected research decisions, while Sci$Π$vot supports systematic evaluation of scientific judgment for LLM agents.

</details>


### [238] [StepSafe: Learning Timely Intervention with Context-Aware Safety Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20960)
*Siyi Gu,Kunhan Gao,Chao Peter Yang,Sukanta Ganguly,Rex Ying,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ASPO 训练框架与 StepSafe 护栏模型，实现 agent 系统安全中的步骤级及时干预，在五个基准上取得开源护栏最优的安全-效用 Pareto 表现（Youden's J 53.0 vs 46.2，4B）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: agent 系统的安全性本质上是序列性的：某一步动作是否安全取决于此前动作、工具输出、信息泄露及多智能体交互。现有工作虽已从轨迹级事后评估转向步骤级动作诊断与主动风险预测，但训练目标对“步骤证据如何随时间累积、何时已足够支持干预”缺乏监督信号，导致干预时机难以校准。

Method: 1) Pre-fix level stage alignment：在同一前缀下采样的多个响应间做比较，奖励与当前阶段匹配的动作——风险不可见时继续、澄清仍可避免伤害时询问、伤害不可逆前停止；2) 自适应反馈自蒸馏：模型自身作为教师，在干预步使用步骤标注、在安全步使用由自身误报挖掘的规则，为决策背后的推理提供信用分配；3) 训练数据每步带有违规分类体系与干预窗口标注，并通过自演化多智能体红队测试扩充；4) 据此训练步骤级推理护栏 StepSafe。

Result: 在五个 agent 安全基准上，StepSafe 在安全-效用 Pareto 曲线上取得开源护栏中的 SOTA，Youden's J 达 53.0（4B 模型），对比基线为 46.2。

Conclusion: 将干预时机本身作为监督对象、并以同前缀对比做阶段对齐、辅以自蒸馏推理信用分配与自演化红队数据，是提升 agent 护栏步骤级及时性与安全-效用权衡的有效路径。

Abstract: Safety in agentic systems is inherently sequential: whether an action is safe depends on prior actions, tool outputs, information disclosures, and interactions among agents. Recent work has shifted agent safety guardrails from post-hoc trajectory-level assessment toward step-level action diagnosis and proactive risk prediction, yet existing training objectives provide limited supervision for how step evidence accumulates over time and when it becomes sufficient to justify intervention. We propose \methodlong~(ASPO): a training framework for step-level timely intervention. Pre-fix level stage alignment allows crediting each decision at its own step: it compares each response with the other responses sampled at the same prefix and rewards the action that fits the stage, continuing before the risk is visible, asking while clarification can still avert harm, and stopping before harm is irreversible. Adaptive feedback self-distillation further credits the reasoning behind each decision, with the model itself as a teacher that sees the step annotations on intervention steps and rules mined from its own false alarms on safe steps. Its training data carry a violation taxonomy and an intervention window at every step and are extended through self-evolving multi-agent red-teaming. Finally we present \textsc{StepSafe}, a step-level reasoning guardrail with timely intervention, that achieves the state-of-the-art peformance among open-source guardrails across five agent-safety benchmarks on safety-utility pareto curve with Youden's $J$ of 53.0 (4B) against 46.2.

</details>


### [239] [LEASH: Uncertainty-Aware Weak-to-Strong Escalation and Oversight for Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22280)
*Qingni Wang,Changdae Oh,Yuzhe Yang,Sharon Li,Jiachen Yang,Ang Li,Xin Eric Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架LEASH，利用弱智能体的隐状态不确定性信号决定何时升级到强智能体，并在交接后继续监控强黑盒模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程计算机使用任务中，每步都调用前沿模型成本高昂，而小开源模型单独运行常失败；弱到强升级需在每个步骤用足够廉价的信号判断何时升级，且交接后强智能体（黑盒API）仍可能迷失而无内部信号可监控。

Method: LEASH为三层免训练框架：初始任务不确定性用于早期路由；步骤级不确定性用于运行时升级；交接后设置影子观察器对强黑盒智能体持续监督。三层均以隐不确定性为信号，通过将弱模型隐状态与其历史成功/失败轨迹比较，越接近失败轨迹则不确定性越高；估计器复用动作生成时已产生的激活，无需额外前向传播或专门训练监控器。

Result: 在OSWorld上四种主流模型骨干上，LEASH稳定优于单独弱智能体，并在相同升级率下比随机升级高6.55–11.94个百分点；使用Qwen3.5-35B-A3B时以每任务0.4117美元达到73.66%成功率，匹配始终开启Claude Opus 5（72.90%）且推理成本降低19.2%。

Conclusion: 隐状态不确定性可作为廉价通用的升级与监督信号，使弱-强级联在保持强模型性能的同时显著降低成本，且无需训练与内部访问。

Abstract: Computer-use agents increasingly tackle long-horizon tasks, where running a frontier model at every step is expensive and a small open-weight model alone often fails. Weak-to-strong escalation offers a middle ground where a weak agent acts by default and hands control to a strong one only when needed. The core challenge is deciding when to escalate, which requires an uncertainty signal cheap enough to check at every step. However, even after handoff, the strong agent can still get lost, but a black-box API that the strong agent is usually served exposes no internals to monitor. To this end, we propose LEASH, a training-free framework that unleashes the strong agent only when the weak agent's latent uncertainty signals it is needed, and keeps it on a leash afterward. LEASH has three layers: (1) initial task uncertainty for early routing, (2) step-level uncertainty for runtime escalation, and, after handoff, (3) a shadow observer for continued oversight of the strong black-box agent. Here, we use latent uncertainty as a fuel for all three layers. Specifically, we compare the weak model's hidden states with those from its past successful and failed trajectories, and a state that looks more like the failures signals higher uncertainty. During weak-agent execution, our uncertainty estimator reuses activations already produced during action generation, so it needs no extra forward pass or dedicated monitor training. Experiments on OSWorld across four contemporary model backbones show that LEASH consistently improves over standalone weak agents and outperforms random escalation by 6.55--11.94 points at matched escalation rates. With Qwen3.5-35B-A3B, LEASH achieves 73.66% task success at $0.4117 per task, matching always-on Claude Opus 5 (72.90%) while reducing inference cost by 19.2%.

</details>


### [240] [GNN-Guided Local Refinement for Efficient Layout Decomposition with DSA-MP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22214)
*Yu Feng,Liyi Zhou,Haoyu Wang,Yiming Xu,Hao Geng,Shaozhi Deng,Ningsheng Xu,Shisheng Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对DSA-MP（导向自组装与多重曝光光刻集成）的版图分解问题，提出新的冲突图模型以刻画此前被忽略的DSA模板边到边间距约束，并设计由图神经网络驱动的局部精化策略，实现高效可扩展的分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPL在先进节点面临成本限制，DSA可作为低成本补充并将光刻分辨率延伸至10nm以下，二者结合的DSA-MP能进一步提升接触孔/通孔图形分辨率。但随着设计复杂度上升，现有分解方法在解质量与运行时间之间难以平衡，且忽略了DSA模板的边到边间距约束，损害可制造性。

Method: 构建新的冲突图（conflict graph）模型，显式纳入此前被忽略的DSA模板边到边间距约束；在此基础上提出由图神经网络（GNN）驱动的局部精化（local refinement）策略，用于高效的版图分解。

Result: 实验表明，与现有最先进方法相比，所提方法获得更高的解质量且收敛更快，并在版图规模、设计风格、工艺节点及不同掩模数量下保持稳健性。

Conclusion: 通过引入考虑DSA模板间距约束的冲突图建模与GNN驱动的局部精化，可有效兼顾DSA-MP版图分解的解质量、运行效率与可制造性，并具备良好的跨规模与跨节点泛化能力。

Abstract: Multiple patterning lithography (MPL) has been widely used to improve lithography resolution in advanced nodes, but it faces cost limits as feature sizes shrink. Directed self-assembly (DSA) offers a cost-effective complement, extending the resolution of optical lithography to sub-10 nm and beyond. Integrating DSA into MPL (DSA-MP) further enhances the resolution of contact/via patterning. Nevertheless, growing design complexity exposes the limits of existing decomposition methods, which struggle to balance solution quality and runtime, and neglect edge-to-edge spacing constraints of DSA templates, compromising manufacturability. To address these issues, we introduce a new conflict graph model capturing the previously overlooked constraints and develop a local refinement strategy powered by the graph neural network, enabling efficient and scalable layout decomposition. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves higher solution quality with faster convergence than state-of-the-art methods, while maintaining robustness across layout scales, design styles, technology nodes, and varying mask counts.

</details>


### [241] [Terminal-RSI: Recursive Self-Improving Terminal Agents via Recursive Self-Rewrite and Policy-Refine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22192)
*Junyao Yang,Zongxia Li,Yucheng Shi,Zhongzhi Li,Ruhan Wang,Ruiwen Zhou,Haitao Mi,Shuicheng Yan,Leowei Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Terminal-RSI框架，通过递归自重写（RSR）与继承评论家的递归策略精炼（RPR）交替迭代，使终端智能体将验证信号转化为有效策略更新，并获得Terminal-RSI-27B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终端智能体面临两大关键问题：如何将验证器结果转化为有用的策略更新，以及如何把部分进展转化为完整任务解决。

Method: 交替执行两条流程：对已验证轨迹进行递归自重写（RSR），以及与继承评论家的递归策略精炼（RPR）。具体技术包括：Turn-Boundary Skip-Observation GAE（TB-GAE），在跨交互轮次衰减的同时保留token级基线；Family Dense Reward（FDR），跨任务族校准语义进展；Success-Prioritized Reward（SPR），将严格成功与有界部分得分分离；并辅以预热评论家模型、递归评论家交接与分阶段学习率来协调两阶段精炼。整体为五阶段流程：Qwen3.8-27B基座 → RSR-1 → RPR-1 → RSR-2 → RPR-2。

Result: Terminal-RSI-27B在Terminal-Bench 4.0上达19.84%，Terminal-Bench 2.1上86.52%，Terminal-Bench 3.0上16.95%，Terminal-Bench Hard上79.00%。在LHTB上，过程奖励由0.21升至0.37，严格完成由0/46提升至5/46；SWR100成功率由3.00%翻倍至6.00%。

Conclusion: 通过递归耦合已验证轨迹重写与继承评论家的策略精炼，每个保留阶段都巩固已发现的行为，使智能体从局部修复逐步推进到严格终端验证。

Abstract: Converting verifier outcomes into useful policy updates and partial progress into complete task resolution is crucial for terminal agents. We present Terminal-RSI, which alternates Recursive Self-Rewrite (RSR) of verified trajectories with critic-inheriting Recursive Policy-Refine (RPR). We formulate Turn-Boundary Skip-Observation GAE (TB-GAE) to retain token-specific baselines while applying decay across interaction turns. Family Dense Reward (FDR) calibrates semantic progress across task families; Success-Prioritized Reward (SPR) separates strict success from bounded partial credit. A Warm-up Critic Model, recursive critic handoff, and stage-specific learning rates coordinate the two refinement phases. The five-stage sequence from Qwen3.8-27B base model through RSR-1, RPR-1, RSR-2, and RPR-2 yields Terminal-RSI-27B: 19.84% on Terminal-Bench 4.0, 86.52% on Terminal-Bench 2.1, 16.95% on Terminal-Bench 3.0, and 79.00% on Terminal-Bench Hard. On LHTB, process reward rises from 0.21 to 0.37 and strict completion from 0/46 to 5/46; SWR100 success doubles from 3.00% to 6.00%. By recursively coupling verified trajectory rewriting with critic-inheriting policy refinement, each retained stage consolidates discovered behaviors and progressively advances the agent from partial local repairs to strict terminal verification.

</details>


### [242] [Realtime-Bench: Can Frontier Models Solve Real-time Interactive Problems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22155)
*Jingyu Tang,Xu Yang,Zhehao Zhang,Zheng Wang,Qi Cheng,Haozhe Zhao,Manling Li,Wenxuan Li,Xide Xia,Jiaxin Pei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Realtime-Bench 评测套件，在动态任务中检验流式模型的实时行动能力；最佳成功率仅 44%、Pass^3 仅 36%，并通过 Wall/Paused/Token 三种时钟分离服务延迟与时间感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实时流式模型评测侧重对话质量与流式感知，未检验模型在严格时间约束下的有效行动；而实时交互要求智能体感知动态环境并在时限内行动。

Method: 构建 Realtime-Bench：一组精心筛选的动态任务，延迟行动会直接影响成功率。设置三种时间推进机制：Wall Clock（按真实推理延迟推进环境）、Paused Clock（移除推理延迟的理想参照）、Token Clock（按生成 token 数推进模拟时间，独立于物理服务速度检验时间感知）。

Result: Wall Clock 下最佳成功率仅 44%，最佳 Pass^3 仅 36%；对比三种时钟发现时间开销限制动态环境中的成功，且更高推理强度在时间压力下效果不一，体现决策质量与及时执行之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 实时交互仍是显著挑战；改进方向包括具备时间感知的自适应推理，以及连续感知与行动的一体化。

Abstract: Real-time interaction requires agents to perceive evolving environments and act under temporal constraints, yet existing evaluations of real-time streaming models focus on conversational quality and streaming perception, leaving effective action under strict temporal constraints untested. We introduce Realtime-Bench, a suite of carefully curated dynamic tasks where delayed actions directly affect success. Under Wall Clock, where environments advance according to actual inference latency, the best success rate reaches only 44% and the best Pass^3 is only 36%, demonstrating that real-time interaction remains challenging. To separate serving latency from models' real-time streaming capability, we compare Wall Clock with Paused Clock, which removes inference delays as an idealized reference, and introduce Token Clock, which advances simulated time according to generated token counts, testing temporal awareness independently of physical serving speed. We find that temporal costs limit success in evolving environments and that higher reasoning effort has mixed effects under time pressure, reflecting a trade-off between decision quality and timely execution. These findings suggest directions for improving real-time multimodal agents, including adaptive reasoning with temporal awareness and continuous perception and action.

</details>


### [243] [Prism-1: Position- and Rival-Independent Scoring for System One Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22066)
*Yao Long Teng,Jianguo Huang,Yanchen Deng,Jiayi Cai,Bo An*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对System One决策模型读取式打分，提出Prism-1：让每个选项在独立block、共享位置、单向注意力下各自打分，使softmax读出天然满足顺序无关性与Luce选择公理，且单次前向即可完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web系统中的System One决策模型多由语言模型直接读出选项分布。由于选项列表与选项集由其他组件决定、可被更改甚至操纵，决策应满足两条理性公理：选项排列顺序不影响结果，新增选项不改变其余选项的相对概率（Luce选择公理）。而现有读出方式（SCQ提示、打乱训练、顺序平均、排序选项、不变集架构）均至少违反其中一条。

Method: 依据Luce定理，仅当读数为各选项独立得分的softmax时两公理才成立；在Transformer中即要求为某选项打分的token不接触其他选项token。据此实现Prism-1（Position- and Rival-Independent Scoring）：每个选项置于独立block、使用共享位置编码、保留模型常规的左到右注意力，在一次前向中完成全部选项打分。

Result: 基线问题显著：一次打乱即可改变12–20%的SCQ答案，排序只是固化任意约定，精心挑选的增选项可左右4–34%的决策。在5个基准集、Qwen3-8B与Gemma 4 12B上，同数据训练的SCQ仍有一次打乱改变7%答案、加入无关选项改变12%决策；Prism-1准确率与基线相差不超过1个百分点，在仅统计重排后仍稳定的答案时高出2–4个百分点，且打乱与增选项均不改变其决策。

Conclusion: 通过隔离选项间信息通路，可在架构层面构造满足顺序无关性与Luce公理的System One读出，几乎不损失准确率并在一致性过滤后更优，为Web系统中可被外部组件改动选项集的决策提供了鲁棒方案。

Abstract: Given a state and a set of options, such as intents, tools or interface elements, System One decision models return a distribution over the options in one forward pass, without generated reasoning. Web systems increasingly build them by reading decisions from language models. Two axioms of rational choice are natural requirements for such decisions, because in web systems the listing and the option set are determined by other components and can change or be manipulated: the order in which the options are listed should not matter, and adding an option should not change the relative odds of the others (Luce's choice axiom). We test the standard reads, namely single-choice question (SCQ) prompting, with shuffled-order training, order averaging or sorted options, and invariant set architectures, and find that each violates at least one axiom: one shuffle changes 12-20% of SCQ answers, sorting only freezes an arbitrary convention, and a well-chosen added option steers 4-34% of decisions. By Luce's theorem, a read satisfies both axioms only if it is a softmax over scores that each depend on one option alone, so in a transformer they hold by construction only if no token that scores an option sees another. We implement this as Prism-1 (Position- and Rival-Independent Scoring for System One Models): every option is read in its own block at shared positions, with the model's usual left-to-right attention, in one forward pass. On five benchmark sets with Qwen3-8B and Gemma 4 12B, SCQ prompting trained on the same data still changes 7% of its answers under one shuffle, and an added irrelevant option steers 12% of its decisions. Prism-1 stays within one point of that baseline's accuracy and is 2-4 points more accurate once only answers that survive a reordering are counted, while no shuffle changes any of its answers and no added irrelevant option moves its choice among the others.

</details>


### [244] [Reinforcement Learning-driven Diffusion Models: Language, Vision, and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21539)
*Zhiyuan Ma,Jiabao Wei,Tianyu Wang,Hanqing Wang,Jiadong Pan,Haowen Xu,Liangliang Zhao,Ruixun Liu,Yuhan Wang,Yuwei Zhang,Liang Feng,Junhao Xiao,Kaiyan Zhang,Xuekai Zhu,Daizong Liu,Biqing Qi,Yihao Liu,Ganqu Cui,Ning Ding,Linfeng Zhang,Jianjun Li,Kun He,Xiang Bai,Bowen Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是首篇关于强化学习（RL）驱动扩散模型（DMs）的综述兼实证分析，提出技术全景图，覆盖扩散轨迹优化与奖励工程，并在多任务基准上系统评测代表性方法，揭示三大部署瓶颈并给出路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扩散模型虽在"预训练-微调"范式下实现高保真可控生成，但在复杂推理、生成路径自主探索以及人类对齐的复杂目标适配上存在关键局限；受RLHF在LLM中成功的启发，RL作为后训练机制被引入以通过奖励驱动的轨迹优化迭代精炼DMs，但该领域尚缺乏系统性梳理与实证对比。

Method: 1）提出新的技术全景图，阐明RL在基于扩散的语言、多模态、机器人与科学模型中的多重作用，重点聚焦扩散轨迹优化与奖励工程；2）在多个任务特定基准上对代表性RL后训练方法进行系统实验评测与直观对比；3）剖析阻碍实际部署的三大瓶颈：奖励设计脆弱性、高维生成空间中的探索-利用权衡、稀疏奖励下的训练不稳定性。

Result: 通过大量基准实验系统评估代表性方法，直观呈现不同RL后训练策略的优势；识别并解析三类关键瓶颈，并据此提出将RL融入DMs的原则性路线图与未来研究方向（面向鲁棒、可解释的RL-扩散系统），同时构建公开仓库持续追踪进展。

Conclusion: RL已成为扩散模型后训练的关键机制，可将扩散过程重塑为经验驱动、目标导向的生成范式。综述与实证共同表明该方向潜力显著但仍受奖励设计、探索效率与训练稳定性制约，需在原理层面系统整合RL与DMs，并推动鲁棒的、可解释的RL-扩散系统发展。

Abstract: Diffusion models (DMs) have steadily demonstrated outstanding performance in high-fidelity, controllable generation across diverse scenarios, following the "pretraining-finetuning" paradigm established in LLMs. Despite these advances, conventional DMs still face critical limitations in sophisticated reasoning, autonomous exploration of generation pathways, and adaptability to complex, human-aligned objectives. Inspired by RLHF's success in LLMs, RL has recently emerged as a pivotal post-training mechanism to iteratively refine DMs through reward-driven trajectory optimization.
