This paper presents the first comprehensive review of RL-driven DMs and an empirical analysis of how RL reshapes the diffusion process towards experience-centric, goal-directed generation. We propose a novel technical panorama elucidating RL's multifaceted roles in diffusion-based language, multimodal, robotic, and scientific models, with a granular focus on diffusion trajectory optimization and reward engineering. Through extensive experiments on multiple task-specific benchmarks, we systematically evaluate representative methods to intuitively highlight the advantages of different RL post-training strategies. Furthermore, we reveal and dissect three crucial bottlenecks that currently hinder the real-world deployment of RL-driven DMs: reward design fragility, exploration-exploitation trade-off in high-dimensional generation spaces, and training instability under sparse rewards. Based on these perspectives, we provide a principled roadmap for the integration of RL into DMs and outline promising future research directions for robust, interpretable RL-diffusion systems. We also construct a public repository to continuously track the latest developments in this rapidly evolving field: https://github.com/MAIR-Lab-HUST/Awesome-RL-for-Diffusion-Models.

</details>


### [245] [CriticaLLM: Using LLMs as Autonomous Game Master for Tabletop Role-Playing Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21525)
*Gaëtan Berlaimont,Justin Vanwichelen,Benoît Ronval,Hélène Verhaeghe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 CriticaLLM 框架，构建可全自动运行完整 TTRPG 游戏的 LLM 游戏主持人，并评估其剧情与反派生成的多样性与可玩性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 具备处理长文本、遵循指令与创意生成能力，使其可用于游戏及桌面角色扮演游戏（TTRPG，如《龙与地下城》）。但 TTRPG 组件复杂：既需构建有趣故事，又需遵循规则，同时还要保留玩家行动的自由度，现有系统难以统一满足这些要求。

Method: 首先梳理 LLM 在 TTRPG 场景中应具备的关键能力。在此基础上提出 CriticaLLM 框架：一个完全自动化的游戏主持人（Game Master），能够运行完整 TTRPG 游戏，涵盖从生成多样化、有趣的故事情节到管理探索阶段与战斗阶段。评估方面，邀请真实 TTRPG 玩家参与评测，并在四种不同规模 LLM 上考察所生成情节与反派的多样性。

Result: 真实玩家参与的评测与多模型多样性分析表明，该框架在运行完整 TTRPG 游戏方面表现良好，是颇具前景的方案；不同规模 LLM 在情节与反派生成的多样性上存在差异。

Conclusion: CriticaLLM 为 LLM 驱动全自动 TTRPG 游戏主持人提供了可行路径，兼具叙事生成与规则/阶段管理能力，具有应用潜力；摘要未给出量化指标，后续可进一步细化评估维度与基线对比。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown impressive capabilities across many tasks, including processing large amounts of text, following instructions, and generating ideas. This allows the use of such models in various contexts, including games and Tabletop Role-Playing Games (TTRPGs), such as Dungeons & Dragons. However, these games include many complex components, from crafting interesting stories to adhering to rules, while allowing some freedom in players' actions. In this work, we first highlight key components that LLMs should exhibit in the context of TTRPGs. Then, we introduce our framework, CriticaLLM, a fully automated Game Master that can run entire TTRPG games, from creating diverse and interesting stories to managing exploration and combat phases. We evaluate our framework with actual TTRPG players and examine the diversity of generated plots and antagonists across four LLMs of varying sizes. Overall, our framework appears to be a promising option for running entire TTRPG games.

</details>


### [246] [Agent Status Protocol (ASP): A Lightweight Specification for State Reporting in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21538)
*Diego Gosmar,Deborah A. Dahl,Emmett Coin,Csaba Bolyós,Andreas Zettl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ASP（智能体状态协议）v0.1：智能体通过 HTTP POST 推送 JSON 状态事件，服务器以 SSE 向观测方广播聚合状态，并以心跳缺失推断存活、检测"存活但无进展"，同时接纳监督者/护栏/操作员/同伴的外部观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构 AI 智能体缺少统一、轻量的状态上报机制；且崩溃、卡死或被攻陷的智能体无法被信任自报故障，单纯自报不足以反映真实运行状态。

Method: 定义协议规范草案：智能体侧 HTTP POST 小体积 JSON 事件，服务器侧聚合后通过 SSE 推送；服务器基于错失心跳推断存活、检测无进展的存活智能体，并融合监督者、护栏、操作员与同伴的观测（部分覆盖自报、部分并列展示）；状态沿生命周期、健康、任务、floor、治理五个正交维度建模；复用 A2A 任务状态、OFP floor 状态及 OpenTelemetry GenAI 语义约定属性。

Result: 给出 ASP v0.1 的协议设计，支持如"在线—降级—执行任务—等待 floor"并存的组合状态，实现多源观测的可信融合与低开销状态分发。

Conclusion: ASP 0.1 为提案而非已批准标准，属工作草案，旨在开启讨论；作者邀请社区提供意见、替代方案与独立实现。

Abstract: The Agent Status Protocol (ASP) defines a lightweight way for heterogeneous AI agents to report their operational state to a status server, and for observers such as dashboards to receive that state as soon as it changes. Agents push small JSON events over HTTP POST; the server exposes the aggregated state to observers over Server-Sent Events (SSE). Because an agent that has crashed, hung or been compromised cannot be trusted to report its own failure, ASP does not rely on self-reports alone: the server infers liveness from missed heartbeats, can detect agents that are alive but no longer making progress, and accepts observations from supervisors, guardrails, operators and peers, some of which take precedence over what the agent says about itself while others are shown alongside it. ASP models state along independent dimensions (lifecycle, health, task, floor, governance), so that an agent can be, for example, online, degraded, working on a task and waiting for the floor at the same time. It reuses existing vocabularies: task states from the Agent2Agent (A2A) protocol, floor states from the Open Floor Protocol (OFP), and attribute conventions from the OpenTelemetry GenAI semantic conventions. This paper presents version 0.1 of ASP as a proposal, not as an approved standard. It is a working draft intended to open a discussion, and the authors invite comments, alternative proposals and independent implementations from the community.

</details>


### [247] [Shortcut Sensitivity in Agent-Safety Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22243)
*Hasan Demirkiran,Jens Ernstberger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出三级“捷径审计”框架，用于检验AI智能体工具调用安全护栏（guard）评测数据中的捷径线索；审计发现标签可预测性、观察性关联与配对决策敏感性三类捷径在多个基准中均存在，且表示层改写会实质改变护栏决策与评测成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体通过工具行动，部分工具调用具有敏感性，因此监督其行为的guard必须被可靠评测。但评测数据中可能包含无需测量目标安全构念即可预测标签的“捷径线索”，这些线索也会影响guard决策本身，导致评测失真。

Method: 提出三级捷径审计框架：①标签可预测性；②观察性guard关联；③配对决策敏感性。审计对象为已完成的LinuxArena轨迹及两个由TS-Bench衍生的step级数据集（AgentDojo-Traj、AgentHarm-Traj）。在冻结的64步AgentDojo-Traj队列上，以重复、交错的Haiku调用进行七配置筛选，对最强安全行标记关联做格式一致性检验；并在Luna与确定性Q8 TS-Guard上做复现检验。

Result: LinuxArena中较短的平均shell命令可强预测轨迹标签；AgentDojo-Traj中精确工具查找F_1由混合域折的.901降至域留出下的.495；AgentHarm-Traj中TS-Guard的F_1由行加权的.902降至等交互加权的.719，而平衡准确率上升。改写&lt;INFORMATION&gt;标记使拦截率下降8.33个百分点，不透明工具别名使其上升11.46个百分点；不安全调用始终被拦截，变化集中于对继承标签的假阳性。扣除格式安慰剂效应后两效应仍非零，探索性95%区间不含零。

Conclusion: 基准的表示形式可实质改变guard决策及所测评测性能，构成直接证据。效应规模与模型相关：Haiku量级的效应在Luna与确定性Q8 TS-Guard上未复现。建议采用域分组留出、以评测单元而非行为单位的度量，以及安慰剂对照的配对干预。

Abstract: AI agents take actions through tools. Some actions are sensitive, so guards that supervise them must themselves be evaluated reliably. Yet the data used to evaluate a guard can include cues that make labels predictable without measuring the intended safety construct, and those cues can also influence guard decisions. We introduce a three-level shortcut-auditing framework and audit completed LinuxArena trajectories and two TS-Bench-derived step datasets. We distinguish label predictability, observational guard association, and paired decision sensitivity. In LinuxArena, shorter average shell commands strongly predict trajectory labels. In AgentDojo-Traj, exact-tool lookup falls from F_1=.901 with mixed-domain folds to .495 under domain holdout. In AgentHarm-Traj, released TS-Guard F_1 falls from .902 under row weighting to .719 with equal interaction weighting, while balanced accuracy rises. We then test the strongest safe-row marker association in a seven-configuration screen on a frozen 64-step AgentDojo-Traj cohort using repeated, interleaved Haiku calls. Rewriting the <INFORMATION> marker lowers blocking by 8.33 percentage points, while opaque tool aliases raise it by 11.46 points. All unsafe calls remain blocked; the changes concern false positives against inherited labels. Both effects remain nonzero after subtracting the formatting-placebo effect, with exploratory 95% ranges excluding zero. The same edits also change measured accuracy against inherited labels. This provides direct evidence that benchmark representation can materially change a guard's decisions and measured evaluation performance. The Haiku-sized effects do not reproduce in Luna or deterministic Q8 TS-Guard. We recommend domain group holdouts, evaluation-unit metrics, and placebo-controlled paired interventions.

</details>


### [248] [L2G-SR: Learning to Guide LLM-based Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20685)
*Letian Huang,Xiangyu Wang,Haowei Lin,Di He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 L2G-SR 框架，用学习到的结构验证器为基于 LLM 的符号回归提供结构监督信号（基于树编辑距离 TED），在 LLM-SRBench 上取得数值拟合、方程级准确率与 TED 的最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号回归的目标是找到能解释观测数据的方程，但通常仅用数值误差指导搜索，这会偏好高精度的替代式（surrogate），而忽略结构正确的候选方程。因此需要引入结构层面的引导信号。

Method: 以树编辑距离（TED）构造结构监督信号，并考虑交换律算子与等价表达形式；先由验证器对齐数值观测与公式，再学习为候选公式与目标公式的结构相似度打分；搜索时将目标公式隐藏，验证器与数值误差共同参与父代选择；随后由分析智能体依据验证器反馈与诊断工具提出修改，由生成器转换为精炼表达式。

Result: 在 LLM-SRBench 上，跨所评估的多个 LLM 骨干与各领域，L2G-SR 在数值拟合、方程级准确率和 TED 三项指标上均达到 state-of-the-art。

Conclusion: L2G-SR 证明了学习到的结构引导对基于 LLM 的符号回归具有重要价值。

Abstract: Symbolic regression searches for an equation that explains observed data. Numerical error is commonly used to guide the search, but it alone can favor an accurate surrogate over a candidate with the right structure. We propose L2G-SR, a framework for LLM-based symbolic regression that guides search with a learned structural verifier. We construct structural supervision signal using tree edit distance (TED), accounting for commutative operators and alternative expression forms. A verifier first aligns numerical observations with the formulas, then learns to score a candidate's structural similarity to the target formula. At search time, the target formula is hidden and the verifier complements numerical error in parent selection. An analyst then uses verifier feedback and diagnostic tools to propose revisions, which a generator converts into refined expressions. On LLM-SRBench, L2G-SR achieves state-of-the-art numerical fit, equation level accuracy and TED across the evaluated LLM backbones and domains. L2G-SR demonstrates the value of learned structural guidance for LLM-based symbolic regression.

</details>


### [249] [IneqAgent: Agentic Inequality Proving in Lean with Symbolic Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20681)
*Gaolei He,Jinhui Li,Ruobing Zuo,Xia Zeng,Zhengfeng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IneqAgent是一个将LLM推理与符号计算结合的智能体框架，在Lean4中做形式化不等式证明。它用引理级MCTS协调引理构造、目标重写与符号后端选择，配合SoS/Bernstein/逐差代换三种后端生成精确证书，最终经Lean反馈拼装并校验完整证明。在四个基准上以DeepSeek-V4-Flash解出186题中的175题（94.09%），并给出Dittert猜想四、五维的Lean验证强化及Holens–Djokovic (4,4)不等式的认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化不等式证明需要数学推理与精确计算相互衔接，通常依赖中间引理与目标重写；单一LLM直接生成Lean证明难以保证正确性，而纯符号计算又受限于可处理的多项式/约束形式。作者希望将二者协同，降低LLM推理成本并提升证明成功率。

Method: 提出IneqAgent框架：以引理级蒙特卡洛树搜索统筹三类动作——引理构造、目标重写、符号后端选择；符号侧提供平方和(SoS)、Bernstein、逐差代换三类后端，为局部子目标产生精确证书并生成对应的Lean证明片段；再由LLM生成其余推理步骤，二者组合后利用Lean编译/校验反馈迭代修正，最终对原始定理陈述给出完整证明。

Result: 在四个基准（共186题）上，IneqAgent搭配DeepSeek-V4-Flash解出175题，成功率94.09%，为所评估方法中最高；在四个基座模型上，于较低LLM推理成本阈值下成功率高于同基座的Direct Agent，且在各方法成功运行的推理成本均值上更低。此外得到Dittert猜想在四维与五维的Lean验证强化版本，并认证了Holens–Djokovic (4,4)不等式，从而推出四维的Merris猜想。

Conclusion: 将引理级搜索调度与精确符号证书结合LLM构造性推理，是形式化不等式证明的有效范式：既提高了成功率与成本效率，也能产出经Lean机检的数学新结果（猜想强化与相关不等式认证）。

Abstract: Formal inequality proving requires mathematical reasoning and exact computation, often connected through intermediate lemmas and goal reformulations. We introduce IneqAgent, an agentic framework combining LLM reasoning with symbolic computation for inequality proving in Lean4. Lemma-level Monte Carlo Tree Search coordinates lemma construction, goal reformulation, and symbolic backend selection. Complementary sum-of-squares, Bernstein, and successive difference-substitution backends produce exact certificates for local Lean proof generation. The agent integrates these proofs with LLM-generated steps and uses Lean feedback to construct complete proofs checked against the original theorem statements. On four benchmarks, IneqAgent with DeepSeek-V4-Flash solves 175 of 186 problems (94.09%), achieving the highest overall success rate among the evaluated methods. Across four backbones, it achieves higher success rates at low LLM inference cost thresholds than Direct Agent using the same backbone, with lower mean inference costs over each method's successful runs. We also obtain Lean-verified strengthenings of Dittert's conjecture in dimensions four and five, and certify the Holens--Djokovic $(4,4)$ inequality, implying Merris's conjecture in dimension four.

</details>


### [250] [When Correctness Feedback Erases Evidence: Phase-Separated Completeness Auditing in Verus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20676)
*Adarsh Agrawal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对 Verus/Rust 规范补全审计任务，无反馈的独立发现比"正确性反馈修正循环"更能产出经认证的反例见证（59.0% vs 27.1%），且保留这些见证可显著提升规范修复质量（81.3% vs 62.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规范补全审计的目标是发现"满足书面契约但违反预期行为"的程序，而正确性反馈（把程序改"对"）恰恰会抑制这类证据，二者目标相互对立。作者希望实证这一目标反转现象，并回答：审计阶段是否应避免正确性反馈。

Method: 在 12 个 Verus/Rust 任务、四个模型族、匹配调用预算下，对比无反馈独立发现与 IntentShield 式纠正反馈循环；设置格式匹配的迭代控制以区分"反馈内容"与"独立重启"的贡献；进行解析器修复验证与相位分离执行；下游以两类见证源驱动规范修订（Phase-Revised、IS-Revised、Direct-Revised），并在 Dafny 与 Lean 上做外部效度检验。

Result: 认证见证产出率：无反馈 59.0%，IS 反馈循环 27.1%；差距主要来自反馈内容而非独立重启；优势在全部模型族成立并通过解析器修复检验；相位分离执行在不复用响应的前提下可复现总体产出；多数认证见证在留出用例上失败；规范修复效果：Phase-Revised 实现成功率 81.3%，IS-Revised 62.5%，Direct-Revised 紧随其后，均优于弱化契约；该效应在 Dafny 与 Lean 上未复现。

Conclusion: 完整度审计与正确性修复的即时目标相互冲突：应先保留并认证"被承认的失败"，再进行修复。"无反馈发现—认证—随后修订"的相位分离流程优于交织式的正确性反馈循环；该结论的经验边界限于所研究的 Verus/Rust 工作流。

Abstract: Correctness feedback suppresses the very evidence that specification-completeness auditing is designed to uncover: programs that satisfy a written contract but violate intended behavior. We demonstrate this objective reversal on 12 Verus/Rust tasks across four model families under matched call budgets. Independent no-feedback discovery more than doubles certified-witness yield relative to IntentShield's corrective-feedback loop (59.0% versus 27.1%). A format-matched iterative control places the larger share of this gap in feedback content, not independent restarts. The advantage holds in every model family and survives parser repair. A separate phase-separated execution matches the aggregate yield without reusing responses; most certified witnesses also fail held-out cases. Preserving these witnesses delivers stronger specification repair: Phase-Revised contracts achieve 81.3% implementation success, versus 62.5% for IS-Revised, with Direct-Revised close behind. Both no-feedback witness sources substantially outperform weakened contracts. The effect does not reproduce in the available Dafny and Lean evaluations, establishing an empirical boundary around the studied Verus/Rust workflow. Completeness auditing and correctness repair have opposing immediate objectives. Preserve and certify the admitted failure before repairing it.

</details>


### [251] [Quantum Versus Classical Machine Learning for Neuropsychiatric Disorders: A Feature-Matched Multi-Cohort Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21344)
*Utsav Poudel,Anuj Nepal,Vyas Viswanath,Subramaniyaswamy Vairavasundaram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在4个公开队列(4,308名被试)上使用特征匹配的基准比较,多数量子模型与经典模型性能相当,但无一能持续超越经典模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习模型已被用于静息态功能连接数据,但缺乏在留站点验证(leave-site-out)下以及与匹配的经典模型对照的系统评估。

Method: 在ABIDE I、ADHD-200、REST-meta-MDD三个队列上采用留站点验证,UCLA-CNP采用分层10折交叉验证;在完全相同的相关性衍生特征下比较六个量子模型族与对应经典模型,共构建284组匹配比较;并通过有效维度与特征谱分析解释量子-经典性能持平的成因。

Result: 量子核与匹配经典核在四个队列上统计不可区分(95%置信下平均准确率差异在1.4个百分点内);ABIDE I上混合量子卷积网络准确率与经典版本相当(0.645 vs 0.641),可训练量子核AUC高出0.064;量子卷积网络在该队列AUC更高且四队列训练-测试间隔更小,但无准确率提升。

Conclusion: 在特征匹配的严格基准下,量子模型与经典模型总体持平、未呈现一致优势;该工作为ABIDE I留站点验证下的首批量子评估之一,并提供了284组匹配比较的基准及基于保真度的图构建分析。

Abstract: Resting-state functional connectivity is widely used in machine learning-based classification of neuropsychiatric disorders. Quantum machine learning models have been proposed for functional connectivity data, but few studies have evaluated them under leave-site-out validation or against matched classical models. We compared six quantum model families with classical ones using identical correlation-derived features across four public cohorts (4,308 subjects). Leave-site-out validation was used for the Autism Brain Imaging Data Exchange (ABIDE I), ADHD-200 (attention-deficit/hyperactivity disorder), and REST-meta-MDD (major depressive disorder) cohorts, while the UCLA Consortium for Neuropsychiatric Phenomics (UCLA-CNP) was evaluated using stratified 10-fold cross-validation. The quantum kernel was statistically indistinguishable from a matched classical kernel across all four cohorts, with the average accuracy difference within 1.4 percentage points at 95% confidence. On ABIDE I, a hybrid quantum convolutional network achieved similar accuracy to its classical counterpart (0.645 vs. 0.641), while a trainable quantum kernel exceeded its classical counterpart by 0.064 in area under the curve (AUC). Quantum convolutional networks also achieved higher AUC than their matched classical networks on this cohort and showed smaller train-test gaps on all four cohorts, without an accuracy gain. Overall, most quantum models matched their classical counterparts, but none consistently outperformed them. This study, among the first quantum evaluations of ABIDE I under leave-site-out validation, provides a feature-matched benchmark of quantum and classical models comprising 284 matched comparisons. We further analyse fidelity-based graph construction and account for the observed quantum-classical parity through effective-dimension and eigenspectrum analyses.

</details>


### [252] [Rubric Judgments Lack Uncertainty: Conformal Weighting for LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21214)
*Licheng Pan,Haocheng Yang,Haoxuan Li,Yuzhou Chen,Yichen Sun,Yuan Lu,Xiaoxi Li,Hao Wang,Zhixuan Chu,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练的URW框架，用保形校准将评分细则（rubric）各准则的判断不确定性转换为权重，改进偏好准确率与下游RL对齐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于rubric的LLM输出评估将多准则二值判断统一聚合为总分，隐含假设各准则判断同等确定，忽视了准则间判断确定性的显著差异，使不确定判断无差别地进入奖励估计，可能给下游强化学习带来误导性训练信号。

Method: URW为无需训练的框架：从固定池的改写判断模板中提取软性不确定性信号，再通过拆分保形（split-conformal）p值将其转化为每个准则的权重，并具备可证明的覆盖保证。

Result: 大量实验表明URW稳定提升成对偏好准确率，并在下游RL对齐上取得更强表现。

Conclusion: 准则层面的判断不确定性可通过保形校准被有效量化并用于加权聚合，从而在无需额外训练的情况下改善rubric式评估质量与RL训练信号可靠性。

Abstract: Rubric-based evaluation is widely used to assess large language model (LLM) outputs by decomposing quality into multiple criteria and assigning each a binary judgment from an LLM verifier. However, judgment certainty varies substantially across criteria. Existing methods uniformly aggregate these judgments into a final score, implicitly treating every criterion as equally certain and overlooking this heterogeneity. As a result, uncertain judgments remain incorporated into reward estimation without distinction, potentially yielding misleading training signals for downstream reinforcement learning (RL). To address this limitation, we propose Uncertainty-aware Rubric Weighting (URW), a training-free framework that quantifies criterion-level judgment uncertainty via conformal calibration. Specifically, URW elicits a soft uncertainty signal from a fixed pool of paraphrased judgment templates and converts it into a per-criterion weight using split-conformal $p$-values with provable coverage guarantees. Extensive experiments demonstrate that URW consistently improves pairwise preference accuracy and delivers stronger downstream RL alignment.

</details>


### [253] [Does Latent Chain-of-Thought Actually Reason? A Single, Unifying Taxonomy for Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21182)
*Jingjie Qiu,Sheldon Yu,Xunyi Jiang,Jiajin Liu,Zihan Huang,Zhendong Chu,Tong Yu,Julian McAuley,Junda Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文系统检验潜在思维链（latent CoT）是否真正被模型用于推理。结合链删除、内容替换、探针与轨迹分析，跨21种模型-任务配置发现：逻辑任务中隐藏状态在链开始前即可读出答案、删除链几乎不影响预测，表明其为"占位符"而非推理；算术任务中所有配方均依赖链内容。作者提出区分"链表征什么"与"模型如何使用链"的分类体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在思维链用连续隐藏状态替代显式推理步骤，但尚不清楚这些步骤实际执行了什么计算。高任务准确率与可解码的中间答案，并不能证明模型确实利用潜在链得出答案，因此需要因果层面的证据来界定其性质。

Method: 在三种训练配方、三类推理任务、两种骨干模型共21种配置上，将链删除（chain deletion）与内容替换（content replacement）两类因果干预，与探针（probes）和状态轨迹分析相结合，检验三方面问题：链是否影响答案、链编码了什么、链状态如何演化。

Result: 逻辑任务上，COCONUT的答案在链开始前即已可读，状态过早收敛，删除链后预测几乎不变；逐步监督可产生有序的步骤布局并让模型依赖链，但有序链仍可能是占位符——删除损害性能而内容替换不改变答案。算术任务上，所有测试配方均表现出对链内容的依赖。

Conclusion: 可读的潜在状态本身不足以作为推理的证据。作者提出将"链表征什么"与"模型如何使用链"分离的分类体系：占位符式链、坍缩叠加式链与步骤依赖的因果角色，分别对应不同任务、训练配方与测量方式，从而化解此前相互冲突的报告，并使关于潜在推理的主张可在同一框架下比较。

Abstract: Latent chain-of-thought replaces written reasoning steps with continuous hidden states, giving models additional steps to compute before answering. It remains unclear what computation these steps actually perform. High task accuracy and decodable intermediate answers do not establish that a model uses its latent chain to reach an answer. We study this question across twenty-one model-task configurations covering three training recipes, three reasoning tasks, and two backbones. We combine chain deletion and content replacement with probes and trajectory analysis to test whether the chain affects the answer, what it encodes, and how its states change. On logical tasks, COCONUT often carries an answer that is already readable before the chain begins. Its states settle early, and deleting the chain leaves predictions almost unchanged. Per-step supervision produces an ordered layout of reasoning steps and can make the model depend on the chain. Yet even an ordered chain can act as a placeholder, with deletion hurting performance while content replacement leaves answers unchanged. On arithmetic tasks, all tested recipes show dependence on chain content. These results show why readable latent states alone are insufficient evidence of reasoning. We introduce a taxonomy that separates what a chain represents from how the model uses it. Seen this way, earlier reports no longer compete: placeholder tokens, collapsed superposition and step-dependent causal roles are each consistent with a different kind of chain, each obtained on a different task, training recipe or measurement. Once a study says which property it tested, whether the model uses the chain, what the chain holds, or how its state moves, claims about latent reasoning can be compared.

</details>


### [254] [AgentEval: Evidence-Bounded Assessment of Stateful Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21177)
*Ruiqi Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AgentEval系统，以"合约绑定的评估记录"贯穿工具执行、状态与事件捕获、报告合格化与案例维护，将"智能体做了什么""证据确立了什么""结论在当前是否仍被支持"三者分离，从而使命中范围、证据支撑与报告复用可被审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭任务是否完成无法判定有状态智能体是否履行了其义务；而已记录的判断也不能保证其支撑证据仍然有效。因此需要一种机制，使评估结论与其证据、适用范围和时效性一并可追溯、可检验。

Method: AgentEval系统：把合约绑定的评估记录贯穿工具执行、状态/事件捕获、报告合格化与案例维护；分离"行为—证据—结论有效性"三个层次。实验设置包括：(1) 32次工具世界管理（30个完整回合由独立检查交叉验证，2个中断案例被保留）；(2) 对48个证据视图做受控干预，考察仅哈希校验的漏检；(3) Scenario Studio将审阅过的案例连接为12份两轮报告、含24个独立核验答案；(4) 在192个留出执行上开展独立的公开基准研究。

Result: 30个完整回合上独立检查完全一致，可区分"已满足/已违反/未决"三类义务；48个证据视图的受控干预暴露出36个仅靠哈希检查会漏掉的绑定型故障，且不改变智能体行为；Scenario Studio产出12份两轮报告与24个独立核验答案；192个留出执行中识别出10个"整体任务失败但回合子目标已完成"的情形，并隔离出证据缺失的效应。

Conclusion: 可执行的评估记录使评估工作流中的范围（scope）、证据支撑（evidential support）与报告复用（report reuse）变得可检查，弥补了任务完成度与哈希校验的不足。

Abstract: Task completion alone does not determine whether a stateful agent satisfied its obligations, and a recorded judgment does not guarantee that its supporting evidence remains valid. We present AgentEval, a system that carries a contract-bound assessment record through tool execution, state and event capture, report qualification, and case maintenance. It separates what the agent did, what the evidence establishes, and whether the conclusion is supported for present use. On 32 tool-world administrations, independent checks agree on all 30 complete episodes, distinguishing satisfied, violated, and unresolved obligations; two interrupted administrations are retained. Controlled interventions on 48 evidence views expose 36 binding faults missed by hash-only checks without changing agent behavior. Scenario Studio connects reviewed cases to 12 two-turn reports with 24 independently checked answers. A separate public-benchmark study on 192 held-out executions reveals ten completed return subgoals within broader task failures and isolates the effect of missing evidence. These studies show how an executable assessment record makes scope, evidential support, and report reuse inspectable across an agent evaluation workflow.

</details>


### [255] [A Multi-Tiered AI Accessibility Model (MAIAM): Proportionate Access and Capability Limits for Comprehensive Frontier AI Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20921)
*Chai K. Toh,Vasu Raj Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多层次AI可及性模型（MAIAM），将“是否发布权重”的二元之争拆分为“能力上限”与“谁能以何种条件获得能力”两个决策，主张按能力、安全与风险比例分配访问层级，并与SAFE AI操作点组合成“能力边界+可及性边界”的双边界框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于能力型AI发布的辩论把问题简化为“API托管还是开放权重”的二元选择，混淆了两个独立决策：系统可达到多高的能力，以及谁可以、以何种条件获得该能力。作者旨在聚焦第二个被忽视的决策，并论证前沿权重一旦公开即丧失发布后的所有安全杠杆。

Method: 提出MAIAM三层访问模型：Tier 1“AI for Learning”广泛可及但能力受限；Tier 2“AI for Exploration”面向研发，需注册、身份与使用控制，且控制强度随能力上升；Tier 3“Full-Scale Frontier AI”仅经结构化、可记录、可问责的通道开放。给出按能力、自主性、领域后果、使用/安全四轴定级的标准，并将MAIAM与SAFE AI Operating Point结合为两部分框架：固定的“能力边界”与界定谁可获得上限内能力的“可及性边界”。

Result: 论证开放前沿级权重会放弃开发者发布后的一切安全杠杆；并援引证据表明，安全微调可被以数百美元成本从开放权重中剥离，因此对基座模型的评估几乎无法预示其衍生物的安全属性。结论是仅设能力上限不足以保障AI安全，还需由监管机构实施的分层访问制度。

Conclusion: AI治理应把“能力边界”（不可逾越的安全上限）与“可及性边界”（上限内能力的获取资格）分开设定；MAIAM提供了按能力/风险比例分层的访问路径，并须由监管主体执行，不能仅依赖能力限制。

Abstract: Most debate about releasing capable AI systems treats the choice as binary: keep the model behind an API, or publish the weights. That framing runs together two separate decisions. One is how capable a system may become. The other is who may obtain a given capability, and on what terms. This paper is about the second decision. We propose a Multi-Tiered AI Accessibility Model (MAIAM) that sorts access by purpose, safety, and capability instead of by release artifact. Tier 1, AI for Learning, is broadly accessible and deliberately capability-bounded. Tier 2, AI for Exploration, serves research and development under registration, identity, and usage controls that tighten as capability rises. Tier 3, Full-Scale Frontier AI, is available only through structured, recorded, and accountable access. The principle behind the tiers is that accessibility should be proportional to capability, safety, and risk. We show why publishing frontier-class weights forfeits every safety lever a developer has after release, and, using evidence that safety fine-tuning can be stripped from open weights for a few hundred dollars, why an evaluation of the base model says little about its derivatives. We give criteria for assigning a system to a tier along capability, autonomy, domain-consequence, and usage/safety axes, and combine MAIAM with the SAFE AI Operating Point into a two-part framework: a Capability Boundary that fixes the safety limits not to be crossed, and an Accessibility Boundary that fixes who may obtain a capability below that limit. Capability limits alone do not make AI safe; a tiered access regime administered by regulating bodies is also needed.

</details>


### [256] [Learning When to Withdraw: Curriculum Coverage and Rule-Specific Failures in Fine-Tuned Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21045)
*Mohamed Abouzahra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出在NAQD-Env中研究语言智能体的“选择性撤回”能力，并用规则级诊断揭示聚合准确率所掩盖的规则特定回归；微调可提升决策一致与撤回召回，但不足以单独保证智能体安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用型语言智能体必须在证据变化或权限撤销时修订已准备的动作：暂停受影响动作、保留仍受支持的工作、并在充分修复后再恢复。现有评估多以聚合准确率衡量，难以暴露规则层面的失效，因此需要系统研究监督微调能否习得“选择性撤回”并检验其局限。

Method: 构建NAQD-Env合成环境，含显式依赖关系与确定性参考策略；以Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct的四个LoRA检查点，对比原始课程与增强课程（后者配三种系统提示框架，视图与目标相同，每次训练仅一次）。在全新验证样本、留出依赖族与留出结构组合上，用三种推理提示评测四个检查点与三个指令微调基线，并进行规则级诊断。

Result: naqd提示下，SFT检查点的决策一致率为0.77–0.92、撤回召回为0.46–0.86，基线仅为0.45–0.63与≤0.06。原始课程检查点在34个仍有替代支持的动作中误撤回26个，增强课程为0–1个。但所有SFT检查点对禁止组合的收窄频率低于7B与8B基线（该情形两种课程均未覆盖）；委派场景撤回更多而对独立授权动作的保留更差；四个检查点均遗漏有效事件报告。提示框架效应随指标与划分而异，未确立可复现优势或等价性。单次训练、课程属性联合变化、先前评测暴露及基线—SFT量化差异限制因果归因。

Conclusion: 本文贡献在于一种可检测聚合准确率所掩盖的规则特定回归的方法，而非微调本身即可确保智能体安全的证据；选择性撤回需配合依赖感知的规则级评测与更严格的实验设计。

Abstract: Tool-using language agents must revise prepared actions when evidence changes or authority is revoked. Selective withdrawal means suspending affected actions, preserving supported work, and resuming only after sufficient repair. We study supervised fine-tuning in NAQD-Env, a synthetic environment with explicit dependencies and a deterministic reference policy. Four LoRA checkpoints of Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct compare an original curriculum with an enriched one, the latter under three system-prompt framings with identical views and targets. Three instruction-tuned baselines and all four checkpoints are evaluated under three inference prompts on fresh validation cases, held-out dependency families, and held-out structural combinations. Under the naqd prompt, SFT checkpoints reach decision agreement of 0.77-0.92 and withdrawal recall of 0.46-0.86, versus 0.45-0.63 and at most 0.06 for the baselines. Rule-level diagnostics qualify these gains. The original-curriculum checkpoint wrongly withdraws 26 of 34 actions with surviving alternative support; enriched checkpoints withdraw 0-1. Conversely, all SFT checkpoints narrow prohibited combinations less often than the 7B and 8B baselines, a case absent from both curricula. Delegation results show more withdrawal but poorer preservation of independently authorized actions, and all four checkpoints omit valid event reports. Framing contrasts vary across metrics and splits and do not establish a reproducible advantage or equivalence. One training run per arm, jointly changed curriculum properties, prior evaluation exposure, and baseline-SFT quantization differences limit causal attribution. The contribution is a method for detecting rule-specific regressions that aggregate accuracy can conceal, not evidence that fine-tuning alone ensures agent safety.

</details>


### [257] [Failure Is the Mother of Success: Recovering Supervision with Grounding Hindsight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21018)
*Sheldon Yu,Tong Yu,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Zhendong Chu,Zhenwei Tang,Sungchul Kim,Lina Yao,Julian McAuley,Junda Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Replay-Selected Hindsight Distillation (RSHD)，将 GRPO 训练中失败的尝试转化为经过验证的额外监督信号，在多个模型族、规模与训练域上稳定优于 GRPO，并具备域外泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO 依赖组内相对优势：当某题所有 rollout 均失败时，既无成功样例也无非零组内相对优势，导致模型恰在最需要学习的问题上得不到任何学习信号。作者观察到失败推理本身可能提示下一步该尝试什么，因此希望从失败中回收监督。

Method: 策略在不见参考答案/解答的情况下，对每次失败尝试撰写“纠正性笔记”，然后在上下文中带上笔记重解该题。通过结果验证器对重解（replay）做筛选：先剔除直接泄露答案的笔记，仅当重解成功率高于原始组的成功率时笔记才被接纳；被接纳的重解轨迹再以无笔记的普通提示形式蒸馏进策略。仅训练策略本身，唯一评判者为结果验证器，部署时无需笔记或额外推理。

Result: 在多个模型族与模型规模、两个训练域上，RSHD 在域内基准上一致优于 GRPO，且增益可迁移至域外评测集；该机制同样适用于混合组中的失败样本。

Conclusion: 以“新鲜成功”而非失败 rollout 本身作为接纳判据，使方法能够从全失败组中恢复监督信号，同时保持实现简洁（仅训练策略、无额外推理开销），是一种可插拔的 GRPO 增强方案。

Abstract: When every rollout for a problem fails, GRPO receives neither a successful exemplar nor a nonzero group-relative advantage, so it learns nothing from exactly the problems it most needs to learn. The failed reasoning can nevertheless suggest what to try next. We present Replay-Selected Hindsight Distillation (RSHD), which turns failed attempts into an additional, tested source of supervision during GRPO training. The policy writes a corrective note about each failed attempt without seeing a reference answer or solution, then re-solves the problem with the note in context. A note is admitted only if its replays succeed more often than the original group did, after screening for notes that state the answer, and an admitted replay is distilled into the plain-prompt policy. Because admission is decided by fresh successes rather than by the failed rollout itself, the procedure can recover supervision from groups in which every attempt failed, and it applies equally to failures in mixed groups. Only the policy is trained, the only judge is the outcome verifier, and deployment requires no notes or extra inference. Across model families and sizes and two training domains, RSHD consistently improves over GRPO on in-domain benchmarks, and the gains carry over to out-of-domain evaluation sets.

</details>


### [258] [MAI-Gym: An Open All-in-One Platform for GUI Agent Evaluation and RL Training across Mobile and Computer Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20939)
*Panrong Tong,Quyu Kong,Pengxiang Li,Tianyu Xia,Hanzhang Zhou,Lei Wang,Chenglin Cai,Long Li,Long Chen,Jianan Zhang,Zhengwen Zeng,Xu Zhang,Gongjie Zhang,Yue Wang,Steven Hoi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAI-Gym是一个统一的GUI智能体平台，整合评测、数据采集与在线强化学习训练，支持移动端、计算机和网页环境，并基于此训练出10B参数以下性能领先的MAI-Gym-9B智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线可验证奖励强化学习在数学、编程和工具使用上进展迅速，但迁移到GUI智能体面临两大障碍：交互式环境成本高昂，以及真实训练任务稀缺。

Method: 提出MAI-Gym：通过共享rollout流水线保证评测与训练中智能体行为一致，支持十个基准、可扩展到2万并行环境实例；并支持自动化合成多样化GUI应用与挑战性任务，包括可重置的mock应用、连贯的跨应用世界，以及经执行验证的任务验证器。

Result: 训练出的MAI-Gym-9B在10B以下开源GUI智能体中达到SOTA：MobileWorld 78.4%，OSWorld-Verified 76.2%；在mock订票应用中的RL可迁移到真实应用，物理手机上任务成功率从28.8%提升至48.1%。

Conclusion: MAI-Gym验证了可扩展GUI智能体在线RL训练的可行性，并开源框架、合成技能、mock应用、2000个经验证任务及模型权重以支持可复现研究。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning (RL) with verifiable rewards has driven rapid progress in mathematics, coding, and tool use, but scaling this success to GUI agents remains challenging due to the high cost of interactive environments and the scarcity of realistic training tasks. We introduce MAI-Gym, an open all-in-one platform that unifies GUI agent evaluation, data collection, and online RL training across mobile, computer, and web environments. Through a shared rollout pipeline, MAI-Gym ensures consistent agent behavior across evaluation and training, supports ten established benchmarks, and scales to 20,000 parallel environment instances. Beyond scalable infrastructure, MAI-Gym enables automated synthesis of diverse GUI applications and challenging tasks, including resettable mocked apps and coherent cross-app worlds with execution-validated task verifiers. Using MAI-Gym, we train MAI-Gym-9B, a unified 9B agent for mobile and computer use, achieving state-of-the-art performance among open GUI agents under 10B parameters: 78.4% on MobileWorld and 76.2% on OSWorld-Verified. Importantly, RL in a mocked train-ticketing app transfers to its real-world counterpart, improving task success on a phys- ical phone from 28.8% to 48.1%. We release the framework, synthesis skills, mocked apps, 2,000 verified tasks, and the model checkpoint to support repro- ducible research and further advances in scalable GUI agent training.

</details>


### [259] [RAC: A Relational Agent Contract for Constraint-Governed Engineering Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20935)
*Kaiyuan Zheng,Fengqing Jiang,Yichen Feng,Zubin Aaron Assadian,Yuetai Li,Bhaskar Ramasubramanian,Basel Alomair,Luyao Niu,Radha Poovendran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RAC——一种与框架无关的工程智能体"动作与反馈层"契约，将语义决策与可机械推导的后果分离，显著提升受约束工程任务的通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方案要求智能体既做语义决策，又自行推导精确几何、时序等后果，导致约束型工程任务失败率高。作者希望把"决定满足哪些关系"与"由工具推导具体后果"解耦。

Method: RAC契约规定五个要素：权威观测、语义动作、基于工具的落地实现、独立验证、修复证据。智能体只负责选择制品应满足的关系，专用工具负责推导具体数值后果；该契约不依赖特定harness。评测涵盖PCB布局/布线/修复、作业车间调度、四个微控制器上的141个嵌入式代码任务。

Result: PCB任务中，RAC在全部9个模型上取得最高干净通过率，平均90.9%，最强基线（仅需求委派）为74.0%；作业车间调度中，直接关系型动作质量通过率96.3%，而经由代码中介、后端相同的交付仅59.6%。

Conclusion: 动作契约本身是智能体能力的来源：保留语义控制、把可机械推导的输出委托给工具，可提升受约束制品的可靠性。

Abstract: Engineering agents must produce artifacts that satisfy exact, coupled constraints. Existing solutions often require agents to make semantic decisions and also derive consequences such as precise geometry or timing from those decisions, leading to failures in constraint-governed engineering tasks. In this paper, we introduce RAC, a harness-agnostic contract for the action and feedback layer of engineering agents. It specifies authoritative observations, semantic actions, tool-based realization, independent verification, and repair evidence. The agent decides which relations an artifact should satisfy; specialized tools derive their concrete consequences. On PCB placement, routing, and repair tasks, RAC achieves the highest clean-pass rate for all nine evaluated models, averaging 90.9%, compared with 74.0% for the strongest baseline, requirements-only delegation. In job-shop scheduling, direct relational actions achieve 96.3% quality pass versus 59.6% for code-mediated delivery with access to the same scheduling backend. Our evaluations on 141 embedded-code tasks across four microcontroller identify the action contract as a source of agent capability: preserving semantic control while delegating mechanically derivable outputs improves the reliability of constrained artifacts.

</details>


### [260] [Defining and Generating Compositional Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20922)
*Eric Elmoznino,Tejas Kasetty,Blaise Agüera y Arcas,Yoshua Bengio,Guillaume Lajoie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文用算法信息论形式化"组合结构"：当部分被联合建模时所揭示的、单独建模时无法捕获的共有结构，即为组合性；据此可定位结构涌现的尺度，并能反向生成具有层级复用与部分-整体组织的对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合性（部分的复用与重组）是自然与人工系统中复杂性的根本来源，但我们对"组合结构"的直觉含义缺乏严格的数学刻画，现有理论难以说明结构在何处、以何种方式涌现。

Method: 以算法信息论（信息与压缩的数学）为工具，将组合结构定义为：各部分合起来考虑时呈现、而分别建模时不存在的共享结构；该定义无需预设结构形式，可跨尺度应用以定位组合结构的涌现位置；随后用可处理的近似反演该框架，在设计者只指定组合结构形式、不指定具体内容的前提下，直接在字符串与图像上做优化以生成组合对象。

Result: 定位分析可揭示对象内部组合结构在哪个尺度、以何种方式出现；反向生成得到的字符串与图像呈现出可识别的层级复用与部分-整体组织，表明该定义刻画的是组合结构本身，而不仅仅是复杂度、规律性或共享信息。

Conclusion: 该形式化给出了对组合结构的可操作刻画，并启示机器学习：为理解神经架构中的"深度"提供新视角，以及构建能支撑开放式学习的组合性数据课程原则。

Abstract: Compositional structure is pervasive in nature and across scales: atoms combine into molecules, molecules into cells, and cells into organisms, while further compositional hierarchies appear within each level. More broadly, compositionality, the reuse and recombination of parts into novel structures, is a fundamental source of interesting complexity in both natural and artificial systems. Yet despite its importance, and despite our apparent ease in recognizing it, mathematical accounts capturing what we intuitively mean by compositional structure remain limited. Here, we fill this gap by formalizing a simple idea: compositional structure emerges when parts, considered together, reveal shared structure that is not captured when those parts are modeled separately. We express this idea using algorithmic information theory, the mathematics of information and compression, which provides a natural way to identify structure in arbitrary data without specifying in advance what that structure must look like. Applied across scales, our definition informs where and how compositional structure emerges within an object. Using tractable approximations, we then invert the framework to generate compositional objects: we specify the desired form of their compositional structure without prescribing their particular content, and optimize directly over strings and images. The resulting objects exhibit readily recognizable hierarchical reuse and part-whole organization, suggesting that the definition captures compositional structure rather than merely complexity, regularity, or shared information. Finally, we discuss implications for machine learning, including a new perspective on depth in neural architectures and principles for constructing compositional data curricula capable of supporting open-ended learning.

</details>


### [261] [EvolvingBrain: An Adaptive Skill System for Multi-modal Agents Self-Evolution in Open Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20786)
*Runxuan Liu,Jingqi Shi,Baoxin Wang,Honghong Zhao,Haoyuan Xu,Yueqing Sun,Jiafeng Liang,Tao He,Dayong Wu,Qi Gu,Shijin Wang,Ming Liu,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 EvolvingBrain，以自适应技能系统作为智能体自进化"大脑"，通过分层技能自进化与状态感知自适应执行两大模块，提升多模态智能体在开放世界任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态智能体在开放世界中交互能力提升明显，但自进化能力有限：监督微调只能模仿、无法主动组织经验；基于结果的强化学习虽促进探索，却未充分利用中间轨迹信息。

Method: EvolvingBrain 含两部分：(1) 分层技能自进化——从模型特有轨迹中提取过程性模式与问题性模式，通过生成技能总结经验，并与开放世界多次交互迭代进化，形成由领域技能、规划技能、动作/陷阱技能构成的分层技能架构，提供多粒度知识库；(2) 状态感知自适应执行——将长时程交互压缩为结构化抽象记忆，并用于指导状态感知技能融合策略，使智能体按当前状态调用知识。

Result: 在 VAB-Minecraft 与 DiscoveryWorld 上的实验表明，EvolvingBrain 提升了开放世界任务成功率，并展现出泛化能力。

Conclusion: 以分层技能系统替代单纯模仿或结果奖励驱动，可有效实现智能体自进化，并兼具多粒度知识复用与状态自适应执行的优势。

Abstract: Multi-modal agents have made significant progress in interacting with open worlds. However, their ability to self-evolve remains limited. Supervised fine-tuning enables imitation but not active experience organization, while outcome-based reinforcement learning promotes exploration yet underuses intermediate trajectory information. Therefore, we propose EvolvingBrain, which uses an adaptive skill system as the brain for agent self-evolution, consisting of two following components. Stratified Skill Self-Evolution: extracts Procedural Patterns and Problematic Patterns from model-specific trajectories, summarizes experience by generating skills, and evolves through multiple interactions with the open world, ultimately forming a Stratified Skill Architecture consisting of Domain, Plan, and Action/Trap Skills, which provides the agent with a multi-granularity knowledge repository and a novel evolution approach. State-Aware Adaptive Execution: summarizes long-horizon interactions into Structured Abstractive Memory and uses it to guide the State-Aware Skill Fusion Strategy, enabling the agent to utilize knowledge according to its current state. Experiments on VAB-Minecraft and DiscoveryWorld demonstrate that EvolvingBrain improves success rates on open-world tasks and exhibits generalization capabilities. Our code is provided in the supplementary materials and will be publicly released in the camera-ready version.

</details>


### [262] [A team of Gemini 4 Argon agents beats Minecraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22319)
*Maxence Faldor,Satinder Singh,Roberta Raileanu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 四个Gemini 4 Argon智能体在沙盒版Minecraft生存世界中，仅通过Mineflayer库自主编写2783个程序，用27小时14分钟击败末影龙，实现首个端到端自主通关Minecraft的多智能体系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证AI智能体能否在数千个相互依赖的步骤上维持连贯计划超过一天，从而支撑复杂真实世界的软件工程与企业级长周期任务。

Method: 四个Gemini 4 Argon智能体各通过Mineflayer控制一个玩家，借助游戏内聊天与共享内存协作；无人分配角色或编写游戏逻辑，智能体自行规划、编写并调试全部程序。研究人员仅通过实时看板监控、可随时暂停但不介入。起点为随机生成的Easy难度世界，无预先侦察的种子或人工编写的专用工具。

Result: 团队共编写2783个程序，死亡605次，最终耗时27小时14分钟击败末影龙，达成首个仅用标准Mineflayer库的AI智能体端到端自主通关Minecraft。

Conclusion: 成功通关依赖智能体在超过一天、数千依赖步骤中保持连贯计划，其分工、经验共享、物品互相回收等策略可为长周期编码与多智能体协调类任务提供参考；论文亦讨论了该结果的局限性。

Abstract: We placed four Gemini 4 Argon agents in a sandboxed Minecraft survival world and gave them one goal: defeat the Ender Dragon. Each agent controlled a player via an open-source library called Mineflayer and coordinated with its teammates through in-game chat and shared memory. No one assigned roles or wrote game logic. The agents planned, wrote, and debugged every program themselves. Researchers could watch the run on a live dashboard and pause or stop it at any time, but they had no cause to intervene. Starting from a randomly generated world on Easy difficulty, the team wrote 2,783 programs and defeated the dragon in 27 hours and 14 minutes, dying 605 times along the way. To our knowledge, this is the first end-to-end autonomous completion of Minecraft by AI agents, achieved using only the standard Mineflayer library without pre-scouted seeds or other game-specific tools written by humans. Winning required the agents to keep a coherent plan across thousands of dependent steps for more than a day. Complex real-world software engineering and enterprise tasks demand the same long-horizon coding and multi-agent coordination. We describe the setup, how the run unfolded, the team's strategies (dividing the work, sharing what they learned, recovering each other's items), and the limits of the result.

</details>


### [263] [CIM-Embed: Learning Instruction-Conditioned Multimodal Embeddings for Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21805)
*Yinghua Xu,Pengyang Xie,Yanxiang Zhou,Chuanzhi Tong,Xiao Xiao,Chaoqun Feng,Xiaoqing Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CIM-Embed 学习"指令条件化"的多模态嵌入，使同一批对象在不同指令下形成不同分区，在推理时配合 HDBSCAN 直接得到聚类结果，无需指定簇数；并构建 ProductMIC 基准，每任务平均 V-measure 达 95.01，远超通用嵌入基线 WeMM 9B（53.06）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态对象在不同指令下需要不同分区（如电商商品按颜色、材质或二者分组）。检索式嵌入难以恢复这些分区，生成式聚类则依赖中间描述或迭代决策，流程繁琐。

Method: 提出 CIM-Embed 框架：用监督对比学习，以同组样本为正、其他组样本为负（可选加入其他任务），并设计异构结构化批处理以适配不同组数的任务；推理时模型将图像、文本、指令直接映射为嵌入，再由 HDBSCAN 在未知簇数下形成分区。同时基于 Amazon Reviews 2023 商品元数据构建 ProductMIC 数据集与评测基准。

Result: ProductMIC 基准含 5,373 个商品、449 个单属性与组合式分组任务，覆盖 50 个未参与训练的训练叶类目。使用全维嵌入与 HDBSCAN，任务内直接 SupCon 训练的平均逐任务 V-measure 为 95.01，而最强通用嵌入基线 WeMM 9B 仅为 53.06；冻结的产物训练编码器也能改善域外条件分组，受控迁移实验可区分"分区迁移"与"指令响应"。

Conclusion: 指令条件化嵌入结合密度聚类可实现高质量、免簇数的多模态聚类，优于通用嵌入基线，并具备一定域外泛化能力；代码与数据集将在录用后公开。

Abstract: Multimodal objects can require different partitions under different instructions, as when products in an e-commerce catalog are grouped by color, material, or both. Retrieval-oriented embeddings may not recover these partitions, while generative clustering often relies on intermediate descriptions or iterative decisions. This paper presents CIM-Embed, a framework for learning instruction-conditioned multimodal embeddings for clustering. Supervised contrastive learning uses same-group positives and negatives from other groups, optionally including other tasks; heterogeneous structured batches accommodate tasks with different group counts. At inference, the model maps each object's image, text, and instruction directly to an embedding; HDBSCAN then forms a partition without the ground-truth cluster count. We construct ProductMIC from product metadata in Amazon Reviews 2023, comprising training data and an evaluation benchmark for instruction-conditioned clustering. The benchmark contains 5,373 products and 449 single-attribute and compositional grouping tasks spanning 50 leaf categories excluded from our training. With full-dimensional embeddings and HDBSCAN, direct within-task SupCon training achieves a mean per-task V-measure of 95.01, compared with 53.06 for WeMM 9B, the strongest general-purpose embedding baseline evaluated. Frozen product-trained encoders also improve out-of-domain conditional grouping, while controlled transfer experiments distinguish partition transfer from instruction response. We will release the code and dataset upon acceptance.

</details>


### [264] [In-Context Preference-Conditioned Pareto Set Learning from Limited Offline Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21736)
*Yiming Yao,Liang Zhao,Qingfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ICPSL 提出一种"上下文内偏好条件化帕累托集学习"框架：在大量合成先验数据上预训练基础式超网络，给定未见问题的离线观测后仅需一次前向传播即可生成问题专属的偏好条件化帕累托解模型，避免逐偏好搜索与逐问题重训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标优化中偏好各异且目标相互冲突。偏好条件化帕累托集学习希望将偏好直接映射为帕累托最优解，但真实场景常为离线设定：仅有覆盖稀疏的静态数据集，无法继续评估目标。现有方法要么对每个偏好单独优化、要么为每个问题训练专属模型，导致跨偏好重复搜索、跨问题重复训练，效率低下。

Method: 1) 构建大规模、多样化的合成先验数据，预训练一个"基础式"超网络；2) 面对未见问题的离线观测时，超网络以这些观测为上下文，通过单次前向传播生成该问题专属的、以偏好为条件的帕累托解模型；3) 生成的模型可复用，对任意偏好直接预测解，无需问题特定训练或逐偏好搜索，从而同时摊薄偏好层面的解搜索与问题层面的模型构建成本。

Result: 在合成问题与真实世界问题上的实验表明，ICPSL 能产生高质量的帕累托解，同时在问题专属解模型的构建速度上取得显著加速。

Conclusion: ICPSL 通过"预训练超网络 + 上下文内单次前向生成"的方式，统一摊销了偏好维度与问题维度的搜索成本，为离线、少数据的多目标优化提供了一种可复用、可快速部署的帕累托集学习范式。

Abstract: Multi-objective optimization involves diverse preferences over conflicting objectives. Rather than returning a finite set of trade-off solutions, preference-conditioned Pareto set learning seeks to map a specified preference directly to a corresponding Pareto optimal solution. Yet in many real-world scenarios, optimization must proceed offline without further objective evaluations, leaving only a limited static dataset that sparsely covers the decision and objective spaces. Existing methods either optimize separately for each preference or train a problem-specific Pareto solution model, requiring repeated search across preferences or repeated training across problems. We introduce in-context preference-conditioned Pareto set learning (ICPSL), a framework that amortizes both preference-wise solution search and problem-specific model construction. ICPSL pretrains a foundation-style hypernetwork on a large and diverse set of synthetic prior data. Given the offline observations of an unseen problem, the pretrained hypernetwork learns to generate a problem-specific, preference-conditioned Pareto solution model in a single forward pass. The generated model can then be reused to predict solutions for arbitrary preferences without problem-specific training or preference-wise search. Experiments on synthetic and real-world problems show that ICPSL produces high-quality Pareto solutions while providing substantial speedups in problem-specific solution-model construction.

</details>


### [265] [Learning Intrinsic Dynamics in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21612)
*Jiamin Shi,Yuanhong Zhang,Zhiyang Liu,Jiawei Sun,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出鼓励动作可预测性（从连续隐状态重建动作）可作为归纳偏置，抑制编码器丢弃控制相关转移信息，从而更好地保留内在动力学；提出的 Concat IDM 用拼接隐状态预测动作更新共享编码器，在 Push-T 多外观设置下将规划成功率从 9.1% 提升到 77.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型需要学习内在动力学（动作与其后果的关系），但与控制过程无关的视觉变化会污染学到的表征，掩盖该关系。作者希望探究动作可预测性与内在动力学保留之间的联系，并以此作为表征学习的引导信号。

Method: 理论方面：推导出当编码器把同一状态、同一外观下不同动作的可区分结果合并时，平方逆预测误差存在正下界，同时说明前向预测在这些转移上仍可精确。实证方面：采用受控的外观多样性对比、监督干预和隐状态探针检验动作预测对控制相关转移信息保留的作用。方法实例化为 Concat IDM——以拼接的连续隐状态预测动作，利用离线轨迹中已有的动作标签更新共享编码器，且不改变测试时规划器。

Result: 在 Push-T、24 种外观设置下，三次独立训练中 Concat IDM 将规划成功率从 9.1% 提升至 77.2%；其配对提升比单一外观情形大 52.9 个百分点。理论与实证结果一致支持动作可预测性作为指向内在动力学的归纳偏置。

Conclusion: 动作可预测性能够作为保留内在动力学的归纳偏置，Concat IDM 在视觉多样性下的隐空间规划中具有实际收益，且无需修改测试时规划器、仅利用离线动作标签即可实现。

Abstract: Learning intrinsic dynamics---the relationship between actions and their consequences---is essential for world models. However, visual variations unrelated to the underlying control process can obscure this relationship in learned representations. We investigate the connection between action predictability, defined as the ability to reconstruct an action from consecutive latent states, and the preservation of intrinsic dynamics. Our central hypothesis is that encouraging action predictability discourages representations from discarding the distinctions between different actions and their consequences. We provide two complementary forms of evidence for this connection. Theoretically, we derive a positive lower bound on squared inverse prediction error when the encoder merges distinguishable outcomes of different actions from the same state and appearance, and show that forward prediction can still be exact on those transitions. Empirically, controlled appearance-diversity comparisons, supervision interventions, and latent probes support the role of action prediction in preserving control-relevant transition information. We instantiate action predictability with Concat IDM, which predicts actions from concatenated latent states and updates the shared encoder using action labels already available in offline trajectories, without changing the test-time planner. Across three independent training runs on Push-T with 24 appearances, Concat IDM increases planning success from 9.1% to 77.2%; its paired improvement is 52.9 percentage points larger than that observed with a single appearance. Together, these findings support action predictability as an inductive bias toward intrinsic dynamics and demonstrate the practical benefits of Concat IDM for latent planning under visual diversity.

</details>


### [266] [Functional Subnetwork Localization for Efficient Continual Learning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21537)
*Shuoran Jiang,Wentao Zhu,Yang Xiang,Dongfeng Li,Yunan Zhang,Mengchen Zhao,Yuhui Guo,Kai Chen,Youcheng Pan,Xiangping Wu,Qingcai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CiMEO框架，通过梯度归因定位LLM中互重叠极低的功能子网络，将训练样本路由至对应子网络并仅更新其参数与优化器状态，在140万条序列指令上实现抗遗忘的持续学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物智能可借助局部可塑性在保留旧技能的同时持续适应新信息，而LLM持续学习仍受灾难性遗忘困扰；已有工作提示LLM内存在功能特化子网络，可用于定向且资源高效的适配，但缺乏对子网络结构化定位与训练路由的系统方案。

Method: 采用梯度归因剖析（gradient-attribution profiling）定位与认知功能相关的功能子网络，发现其相互重叠度极低；据此设计CiMEO（Function-isolated Memory-Efficient Optimization）持续学习框架，将每个训练样本路由到对应功能子网络，把参数更新与优化器状态限制在选定区域，实现功能隔离的稀疏化训练。

Result: 在覆盖编程、考试类任务与数学推理的五个生成式基准上，历经140万条序列指令的持续训练，CiMEO取得对比方法中最高的平均性能并缓解灾难性遗忘；子空间重叠与主角度分析表明局部化更新有助于保持预训练表征。

Conclusion: 功能子网络定位是一种实现LLM高效、稳定持续学习的可行路径，功能隔离式参数更新可兼顾新知识吸收与预训练表征保持。

Abstract: Biological intelligence continually adapts to new information while retaining prior skills through localized plasticity. In contrast, continual learning in large language models (LLMs) remains vulnerable to catastrophic forgetting. A promising direction is to identify functionally specialized subnetworks within LLMs that enable targeted and resource-efficient adaptation. Using gradient-attribution profiling, we localize cognition-associated functional subnetworks exhibiting extremely low mutual overlap. Building on this finding, we propose Function-isolated Memory-Efficient Optimization (CiMEO), a continual learning framework that routes each training example to its corresponding functional subnetwork, restricting parameter updates and optimizer states to selected regions. Across 1.4 million sequential instructions, CiMEO achieves the highest average performance among the compared methods on five generative benchmarks spanning programming, examination-style tasks, and mathematical reasoning, while mitigating catastrophic forgetting. Subspace-overlap and principal-angle analyses further indicate that localized updates help preserve pre-trained representations, highlighting functional subnetwork localization as a practical approach to efficient and stable continual learning in LLMs.

</details>


### [267] [FlashAttention with Statistics: Low-Overhead Attention Features for Compact Hallucination Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21483)
*Andrei Volodichev,Maksim Savichev,Nikita Sviridov,Artem Yuliy,Dmitry Simakov,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出无需具体化注意力矩阵即可计算注意力摘要统计量的方法（FlashAttention 累加/稀疏查表/分块二次遍历），在 H100 上 2K 上下文仅增 16% 延迟；冻结 Qwen3 代理模型（0.6B–8B）的注意力统计探针构成强幻觉/支撑性检测器，4B 代理在两个基准上胜过九个 4–9B 零样本 LLM 评判者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 会产生上下文中无依据的流畅回答，需要检测。基于注意力的检测器依赖代理模型注意力权重的摘要统计量，但标准流程需具体化注意力矩阵，内存随序列长度二次增长，长上下文下不可行。

Method: 在不具体化注意力矩阵的前提下计算统计量，按代价分为近乎零开销与较高开销两类：在 FlashAttention 前向中累加、前向结束后稀疏查表、或对分数做第二次分块遍历。用冻结的 Qwen3 代理（0.6B/4B/8B）提取的注意力特征训练线性探针，与零样本 LLM 评判、专用 grounding checker、以及直接 prompt 代理作评判进行对比，覆盖三个基准、12 个基准-代理组合。

Result: H100 上五个已发表检测器的统计量使 Qwen3-8B 前向延迟在 2K token 时增加 16%，35K 时降至 1.4%，每响应 token 存储 22.5 KiB。4B 代理在两个基准上的 F1 超过九个零样本 4–9B LLM 评判者中最强者；代理匹配或超过最高 7B 的专用 grounding checker；探针优于将代理作为评判者提示；在 12 个组合中有一半注意力特征的加入显著提升 F1，从未显著下降。

Conclusion: 注意力摘要统计量可高效（近零额外开销）且非具体化地获得，冻结小型代理的探针是实用、可扩展的上下文支撑性检测器，优于同规模提示式评判与专用检查器，长上下文下开销可忽略。

Abstract: Large language models can produce fluent answers that the supplied context does not support. A compact frozen proxy model can detect such answers by reading the context and another model's response, even from a proprietary generator. Attention-based detectors use summary statistics of the proxy's attention weights, but standard pipelines materialize the attention matrix, whose memory grows quadratically with sequence length. We compute these statistics without materializing attention, by accumulation in the FlashAttention pass, sparse lookups after it, or a second tiled pass over the scores, and classify them by this path into nearly free and costlier ones. On an H100, the statistics of five published detectors add 16% to the latency of a Qwen3-8B forward at 2K tokens, falling to 1.4% at 35K, and store 22.5 KiB per response token. On three benchmarks, probes trained on frozen Qwen3 proxies of 0.6B to 8B parameters prove strong detectors: a 4B proxy outperforms the strongest of nine zero-shot 4-9B LLM judges in F1 on two of the three, the proxies match or outperform specialized grounding checkers of up to 7B parameters, probing each proxy beats prompting it as a judge, and adding attention features significantly improves F1 in half of the 12 benchmark-proxy pairs, never significantly lowering it.

</details>


### [268] [Tool-Augmented Large Language Models for Reliable Medical Calculation from Patient Notes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21477)
*Yang Zhong,Ling Luo,Xuanlyu Zhu,Yanhua Wang,Yuanyuan Sun,Jian Wang,Hongfei Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出工具增强的LLM框架，从自由文本病历中可靠完成医学计算器运算，在MedCalc-Bench上达86.73%最终答案准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接用LLM提示完成医学计算器任务时，规则回忆、变量抽取、算术推理和输出格式纠缠于单次生成步骤中，导致不可靠；需要将临床语言理解与规则执行、算术分离。

Method: 三步框架：(1) 检索并结构化计算器规范；(2) 基于规范生成可复用的可执行工具；(3) 用受约束（schema约束）的抽取器将病历映射为计算器可用输入，再由工具执行确定性计算。

Result: MedCalc-Bench上最终答案准确率86.73%，分别超过单次提示和监督微调基线24.0与17.27个百分点；消融与误差分析表明增益主要来自规则驱动的工具合成与schema约束抽取，残余错误集中在临床语义抽取和基准特定输出约定。

Conclusion: 将计算器逻辑落地为可执行工具可缓解算术幻觉，提升临床输出计算可靠性；未来改进方向为临床语义抽取与输出契约适配。

Abstract: Medical calculators translate raw patient observations into clinically actionable values and scores. However, applying these calculators directly to free-form patient notes requires precise variable extraction combined with the exact execution of complex formulas, scoring rules, and output conventions. Relying solely on direct large language model (LLM) prompting is unreliable in this context, as rule recall, parameter extraction, arithmetic reasoning, and formatting become entangled within a single generative step. To address these limitations, we propose a tool-augmented LLM framework for reliable medical calculation from patient notes. The framework retrieves and structures calculator specifications, generates reusable executable tools, and uses a constrained extractor to map each patient note to calculator-ready inputs. The tools then perform deterministic calculation, separating clinical language understanding from rule execution and arithmetic. Evaluated on the MedCalc-Bench dataset, our method achieves 86.73% final-answer accuracy, outperforming the one-shot prompting and supervised fine tuning-only baselines by 24.0 and 17.27 percentage points. Ablation and error analyses show that these performance gains are primarily driven by rule-grounded tool synthesis and schema-constrained extraction, whereas remaining failures are concentrated in clinical-semantic extraction and benchmark-specific output contracts. By grounding calculator logic in executable tools, the proposed approach alleviates arithmetic hallucinations and improves the computational reliability of the final clinical outputs.

</details>


### [269] [JevAgentHarness: Benchmarking, Deploying and Training Decision Models That Share Control with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21412)
*Kunlun Zhu,Tao Feng,Xu Han,Heng Wang,Ling Yang,Xiangru Tang,Jiaxuan You,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出JevAgentHarness与AgentDecision-4B，将智能体框架中每步的LLM调用委派给决策模型，在几乎不损失成功率的前提下显著降低成本与延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体框架在每一步都调用大型LLM，而多数调用最终只是多选决策（如下一步调用哪个工具），导致长任务慢且昂贵；决策模型（如Jev）能以远低的时延和价格直接回答这类问题，因此需研究如何让框架以极小成功率损失把决策委派出去。

Method: 构建AgentDecisionBench（从孤立问题到控制运行中智能体，含公开基准与自建DecisionGym环境）以评估决策模型；据此设计JevAgentHarness，在智能体自身轨迹上为每个选择拟合置信阈值，低于阈值时交由LLM决策；并训练开源4B模型AgentDecision-4B，用包含智能体自身失误后状态的数据、以Jev答案为标签。

Result: 孤立题上最准的决策模型仅解决69.5%的智能体任务；无拟合置信阈值时委派工具选择使成功率下降19.0个点。在与训练数据不同的任务上，AgentDecision-4B与Jev分别达成91.5%和82.0%成功率；在相似任务上使智能体成本降低81.1–89.0%、速度提升最高2.5倍，且任务成功率损失不足3个点。

Conclusion: 仅在孤立题目上准确不足以支撑可靠的决策委派，需按选择拟合置信阈值并让决策模型学习智能体自身失误后的状态；所提框架与4B模型可在低成功率损失下大幅降本提速，模型、数据与框架将开源。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents now fix software, operate computers and serve customers, but their harnesses, the programs that run them, call a large LLM at every step, which makes long tasks slow and costly. Most of these calls end in a multiple-choice decision, such as which tool to call next. Decision models such as Jev answer these questions directly, at a fraction of an LLM call's time and price. We ask how a harness can delegate these decisions to a decision model with little loss of success. To evaluate decision models in agentic tasks, we build AgentDecisionBench, which tests them from isolated questions up to control of a running agent, on public benchmarks and our DecisionGym environment. The decision model most accurate on isolated questions solved only 69.5% of agent tasks, and without a fitted confidence threshold, delegating tool choices lowered success by 19.0 points. Building on these findings, we then design JevAgentHarness, which fits this threshold for each choice on the agent's own trajectories and lets the LLM decide below it. Because existing decision models never see the states an agent reaches after its own mistakes, we train AgentDecision-4B, an open 4B model, on such states with Jev's answers as labels. On tasks unlike its training data, the agent solved 91.5% of them with AgentDecision-4B and 82.0% with Jev. On tasks like their training data, 4B models trained this way made agents 81.1-89.0% cheaper and up to 2.5 times faster, without losing 3 or more points of task success. The models, data, and harness will be released at https://github.com/JevAgentHarness/JevAgentHarness.

</details>


### [270] [MemCut: Every Secret Leaves a Trail in the Long-Term Memory of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21405)
*Cheng Zhang,Xuanning Cao,Zhicheng Zheng,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出MemCut——首个面向LLM智能体长期记忆中"用户请求遗忘"能力的系统基准，包含456条人工设计的秘密及完整证据链标注，并提出原生恢复与残余恢复两项指标，揭示现有删除基线在候选覆盖上的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体长期记忆研究主要聚焦于相关信息的准确高效检索，而一个可靠的记忆系统还必须能在用户请求时遗忘敏感数据。目前缺乏对该能力的系统评估手段，因此需要一个兼顾"直接披露"与"间接证据"的遗忘评测基准。

Method: 构建MemCut基准：在长程对话中设置456条人工设计的秘密，并为每条秘密标注完整证据链（含未直接陈述秘密的消息）。提出两项恢复指标——原生恢复（经由系统检索评估reader成功率）与残余恢复（直接向同一reader提供幸存的关联对象）。设立三种参考删除基线，在五种记忆设计与四种LLM上测试检索器与写入模型对遗忘的支持程度；并进行子集扫描（144条事实、三套系统、两个模型）分析候选池与预算的影响。

Result: 删除披露消息后，原生恢复的下降幅度远大于残余恢复；由LLM引导从20个检索候选中额外删除10个轮次，仍残留39–76%的残余恢复。子集扫描显示，在固定预算下扩大候选池比删除全部原始候选更能降低残余恢复，表明候选覆盖是参考基线的瓶颈。

Conclusion: MemCut系统性地暴露了现有记忆系统在用户请求遗忘方面的不足：删除表层披露内容不足以阻断经由间接证据的恢复，候选覆盖成为关键瓶颈。该基准为可信LLM智能体记忆的遗忘机制研究提供了可复现的评测框架与诊断指标。

Abstract: Long-term memory has become a crucial component of large language model (LLM) agents, with most research focusing on retrieving relevant information accurately and efficiently. However, a dependable memory system must also forget sensitive user data upon request. To evaluate this capability, we introduce MemCut, a systematic benchmark for user-requested forgetting. Its 456 hand-designed secrets in long-horizon conversations have fully annotated evidence trails to test recovery after deletion, including from messages that never state the secrets directly. We propose two recovery metrics. Native recovery measures reader success through the system's retrieval, whereas residual recovery measures the same reader's success when directly given surviving objects traced to annotated disclosures or evidence. We establish three reference deletion baselines to test how far existing retrievers and write models support forgetting across five memory designs and four LLMs. Disclosure removal reduces native recovery far more than residual recovery. LLM-guided deletion of ten additional turns from twenty retrieval candidates leaves 39-76% residual recovery. Subset sweeps (144 facts, three systems, two models) show that widening the pool at a fixed budget reduces residual recovery more than deleting all original candidates. These sweeps identify candidate coverage as a bottleneck in the reference baselines.

</details>


### [271] [MemScope: Evolving Agent Memory Architectures through Localized Code Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21170)
*Miao Su,Yucan Guo,Zixuan Li,Long Bai,Zhongni Hou,Xiaolong Jin,Jiafeng Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 MemScope 框架，利用原始执行轨迹、运行时记忆状态与架构代码作为细粒度证据，定位失败并对记忆架构做有界、原位、局部化演进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆架构部署后固定不变，难以适应不同任务类型；而基于执行反馈的自进化方法通常只用评估分数或压缩摘要等粗粒度反馈，无法在包含多组件交互的长程任务中精确定位失败点，导致缺少代码级修改目标、更新范围约束弱，易对原本可用的架构进行大规模重写。

Method: MemScope 采用工具调用的 Patch Agent，联合检查原始执行轨迹、运行时记忆状态与当前架构代码，识别修复目标，并在提供者身份与记忆兼容性约束下进行有界、原位（in-place）编辑。

Result: GAIA 上，轨迹+记忆检查变体准确率 86.67%，高于仅轨迹 70.00% 与仅摘要 63.33%；xBench 上进化后架构正确数从 49 提升至 55（DeepSeek V3.2），从 50 提升至 53（Kimi K2.7 Code）；ALFWorld 未见任务成功率由 50.75% 升至 71.64%；架构快照迁移中代码变更比例中位数为 4.4%。

Conclusion: 细粒度执行证据可作为记忆架构局部化、原位演进的有效依据，在显著提升性能的同时将修改范围限制在较小规模。

Abstract: Agent memory architectures define how LLM agents store and reuse experience across tasks, but they are typically fixed after deployment, limiting their adaptability across task types. Recent self-evolving approaches instead adapt these memory architectures using execution feedback, typically represented as evaluation scores or compressed summaries. However, such coarse-grained feedback is insufficient for precise failure localization in current agent systems, where long-horizon task execution involves multiple interacting components. Without a precise failure location, existing approaches lack a clear code-level target for modification, leaving the update scope weakly constrained and often leading to large-scale rewrites of an otherwise capable memory architecture. We propose MemScope, a framework for evidence-grounded, localized evolution of an existing memory architecture. Its tool-using Patch Agent jointly inspects raw execution traces, runtime memory state, and current architecture code to identify repair targets and apply bounded, in-place edits under provider-identity and memory-compatibility constraints. On GAIA, the trace-and-memory inspection variant achieves 86.67% accuracy, compared with 70.00% for trace-only and 63.33% for summary-only feedback. Under xBench tasks, the evolved memory architecture improves over its original version from 49 to 55 correct answers with DeepSeek V3.2 and from 50 to 53 with Kimi K2.7 Code. On disjoint ALFWorld unseen tasks, success rises from 50.75% to 71.64%. Across archived architecture-snapshot transitions, the median code-change ratio is 4.4%. These results support fine-grained execution evidence as a basis for localized, in-place memory-architecture evolution.

</details>


### [272] [All-in-One Memory: Process-Rewarded Reinforcement Learning for Memory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21147)
*Bingbing Wang,Jingjie Lin,Weiming Qiao,Jing Li,Ruifeng Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 All-in-One Memory (AiO-Mem)，将检索、写入与停止决策统一为共享证据状态上的转移，并在统一奖励空间中评估各动作语义质量，通过轨迹条件过程奖励模型与 PA-DAPO 优化记忆策略，在长上下文记忆基准上持续提升答案准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有外部记忆方法将检索与记忆构建分别优化，缺乏用于评估搜索、写入和停止决策的共享奖励空间，导致各操作之间目标不一致、难以协同。

Method: 1) 将异构历史编译为可溯源的多视图事件，作为检索与记忆写入的共享基础；2) 用统一的记忆策略 actor 在共享证据状态上展开完整交互轨迹；3) 训练轨迹条件的过程奖励模型，经证据锚定的监督微调与轨迹级 DPO 获得，为轨迹分配过程分数；4) 将分数聚合为动作均衡的轨迹奖励，由 Process-Aligned DAPO（PA-DAPO）优化记忆策略。

Result: 在多个长上下文记忆基准上，AiO-Mem 相比静态记忆系统与经强化学习训练的记忆智能体，均一致提升答案准确率。

Conclusion: 以共享证据状态和统一奖励空间联合建模检索、写入与停止决策，配合过程级奖励与 PA-DAPO 优化，可有效提升长时程历史下的记忆问答性能。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly rely on external memory to answer queries grounded in long-horizon histories such as multi-session dialogues and lengthy documents. However, existing memory methods often optimize retrieval and memory construction separately, without a shared reward space for evaluating search, writing, and stopping decisions. To address this, we propose All-in-One Memory (AiO-Mem), which organizes these operations as transitions over a shared evidence state and directly evaluates the semantic quality of each action in a unified reward space. AiO-Mem compiles heterogeneous histories into source-traceable multi-view events, providing a shared substrate for retrieval and memory writing. A unified memory policy actor then rolls out complete interaction trajectories over this shared evidence state. A separate trajectory-conditioned process reward model is trained through evidence-grounded supervised fine-tuning and trajectory-level DPO to assign process scores to the collected trajectories. These scores are aggregated into an action-balanced trajectory reward and used by Process-Aligned DAPO (PA-DAPO) to optimize the memory policy. Across long-context memory benchmarks, AiO-Mem consistently improves answer accuracy over both static memory systems and RL-trained memory agents.

</details>


### [273] [CleanRM: Reward Modeling from Noisy Feedback with a Few Clean Feedback for RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21138)
*Licheng Pan,Haocheng Yang,Yuzhou Chen,Yichen Sun,Yuan Lu,Yunfei Teng,Jiajun Fan,Guangyi Liu,Zhixuan Chu,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CleanRM利用少量干净反馈，通过最优传输校准逐样本噪声比，构建无偏的奖励建模目标，在去噪与下游RLHF对齐上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好数据中存在来自人类主观判断、标注错误和不完美LLM评判的反馈噪声，标准奖励建模会得到有偏的干净风险估计。经典去噪方法假设噪声比在样本间均匀，而反馈数据的噪声本质上是逐样本（sample-wise）的，该假设不成立。

Method: 提出CleanRM：借助少量干净反馈，通过最优传输（optimal transport）校准逐样本噪声比；具体将对噪声反馈反演得到的偏好联合分布与干净反馈的联合分布对齐，再用噪声校正后的替代损失（noise-corrected surrogate loss）训练奖励模型。

Result: 理论上证明所得目标相对干净风险是无偏的；大量实验表明CleanRM优于最先进的去噪基线，并在下游RLHF对齐中更有效。

Conclusion: 针对偏好数据中逐样本噪声问题，CleanRM以少量干净数据经最优传输估计样本级噪声比，实现无偏奖励建模，提升了去噪性能与RLHF对齐效果。

Abstract: Feedback noise is pervasive in preference data, arising from subjective human judgments, annotation mistakes, and imperfect LLM judges. Standard reward modeling trained on such feedback yields a biased estimate of the clean risk. Although classical denoising methods are directly applicable to noisy feedback learning, they generally assume a uniform noise ratio across samples. However, this assumption fails for feedback data, where noise is intrinsically sample-wise. To address this challenge, we propose CleanRM, which uses a few clean feedback to calibrate sample-wise noise ratios via optimal transport. Specifically, CleanRM aligns the joint distribution of preferences reverted from noisy feedback with that of clean feedback and then trains the reward model using a noise-corrected surrogate loss. Theoretically, we prove that the resulting objective is unbiased with respect to the clean risk. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CleanRM outperforms state-of-the-art denoising baselines and yields more effective downstream RLHF alignment.

</details>


### [274] [X-RIC: An Agentic RAN-Intelligent Controller Benchmark for Enhancing Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22269)
*Adhwaa Al-Chaab,Ayman Younis,Dario Pompili*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出X-RIC框架，将智能体式AI（agentic AI）引入O-RAN，结合反应式强化学习与基于电信大模型的推理模块，并配套BARX评测框架，在网络切片场景实现89%运营成本降低、SLA合规、收敛速度提升75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O-RAN通过解耦与AI驱动控制带来架构灵活性，RIC支持集中式优化，但现有AI方法缺乏推理能力与可解释性，难以满足部署所需的可信与合规要求。

Method: X-RIC包含三部分：(i) 利用领域启发式进行自动化行为克隆（BC）以实现样本高效训练；(ii) 电信语言模型（TLM），基于Transformer的推理模块，提供上下文抽象与可解释性；(iii) 在Near-RT RIC控制环内的闭环协同自适应，实现策略持续精炼。同时提出BARX评测框架，从时延、合规性、确定性维度评估智能体控制。

Result: 在仿真与O-RAN合规测试床的SLA感知网络切片场景中验证：运营成本降低89%并保持SLA合规，收敛速度快75%，训练步数减少50%，方差降低46%。

Conclusion: 将agentic reasoning与反应式RL结合可提升O-RAN控制的可解释性与样本效率，BARX为智能体式RAN控制提供了面向部署的评估基准。

Abstract: The O-RAN ALLIANCE introduces architectural flexibility through disaggregation and AI-driven control. While the RAN Intelligent Controller (RIC) enables centralized optimization, current AI approaches lack reasoning capabilities and explainability. We propose eXplainable RIC (X-RIC), a novel framework integrating agentic AI into O-RAN architectures. X-RIC combines reactive Reinforcement Learning (RL) with strategic agentic reasoning through: (i) automated Behavioral Cloning (BC) using domain heuristics for sample-efficient training, (ii) a Telecom Language Model (TLM), a transformer-based reasoning module providing contextual abstraction and interpretability, and (iii) closed-loop co-adaptation within the Near-RT RIC control loop enabling continuous policy refinement. To ensure deployment readiness, we introduce BARX (Benchmarking Agentic Reasoning for RAN), a domain-specific evaluation framework assessing agentic control across latency, compliance, and determinism metrics. We validate X-RIC on SLA-aware network slicing in simulation and O-RAN-compliant testbed deployments. X-RIC achieves 89% operational cost reduction while maintaining SLA compliance, converging 75% faster with 50% fewer training steps and 46% lower variance.

</details>


### [275] [Disagreement-Guided Weak-to-Strong Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22245)
*Sima Noorani,Shayan Kiyani,George Pappas,Hamed Hassani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究无需真值监督的弱到强模型路由：先刻画允许弱模型多次本地推理时的最优路由策略，进而基于模型间比较推导出一种可直接从模型生成响应中测得的"模型分歧"信号来近似最优路由，并在智能体与单轮任务上实现成本-准确率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小模型被广泛部署于边缘设备和机器人系统以控制成本与延迟，但能力受限。用强模型在需要时提供帮助是可行方案，然而"何时将弱模型响应路由给强模型"通常依赖响应正确性信号，而开放式和智能体任务中获取真值监督代价高昂且难以规模化。

Method: 重新审视弱到强路由的理论基础：首先刻画在允许弱模型通过本地推理生成多个响应情形下的最优路由策略；基于该刻画，利用模型间比较（model-to-model comparison）推导最优路由的原则性近似，提出可直接从模型生成响应中测量、无需真值监督的"模型分歧"（model disagreement）概念；并证明当强模型越接近真值分布的代理时，该近似越精确。

Result: 在智能体任务与单轮任务上，该方法在无真值监督条件下有效平衡了路由成本与响应准确率；此外，即使在训练时可获得真值正确性信号，基于分歧的路由仍可优于直接预测正确性的方法。

Conclusion: 路由决策可不必依赖真值监督信号，模型间分歧是可行且有效的路由依据；其理论近似随强模型质量提升而收敛，实验中兼顾成本与准确率并优于正确性预测基线。

Abstract: Small language models are increasingly deployed on edge devices and robotic systems to meet cost and latency constraints, yet their limited capabilities can make reliable deployment challenging. Stronger models can provide assistance when needed, but deciding when to route a weak-model response to a stronger model often relies on signals of response correctness. In open-ended and agentic tasks, however, obtaining such signals can require costly ground-truth supervision that is difficult to obtain at scale. We revisit the foundations of weak-to-strong routing and ask whether routing decisions can instead be guided by signals that do not require ground-truth supervision. We first characterize the optimal weak-to-strong routing policy, while allowing for multiple weak-model responses generated through local inference. Building on this characterization, we derive a principled approximation to the optimal routing based on model-to-model comparison. In particular, we introduce a notion of model disagreement that can be measured directly from model-generated responses, without ground-truth supervision. Importantly, the approximation becomes increasingly accurate as the strong model becomes a better proxy for the ground-truth distribution. Across agentic and single-turn tasks, our approach effectively navigates the trade-off between routing cost and response accuracy without ground-truth supervision. Moreover, even when ground-truth correctness signals are available for training, disagreement-based routing can outperform methods trained directly to predict correctness

</details>


### [276] [PROBE: Learning to Audit Policy Compliance in Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22124)
*Kshitij Mishra,Abhijith Sharma,Nils Lukas,Salem Lahlou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 PROBE，通过多轮工具介导对话训练基于 LLM 的审计员，以探测部署中智能体对预设策略（定价、隐私、确认、安全等）的违规行为，填补"能力评测"与"内容安全红队"之间的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测存在关键缺口：能力基准衡量智能体"能做什么"，内容安全红队衡量"有害生成"，但二者都不检验智能体在合法工具使用中是否遵守预定义策略。此类违规难以审计，因为其影响可能延迟数轮才显现、横跨多次工具调用，或体现在下游系统中。

Method: 将智能体审计形式化为二人马尔可夫博弈；设计复合奖励，平衡违规发现、行为合理性、对话推进与策略多样性；在多轮、以工具为媒介的对话中训练审计员充当真实用户进行探测；在受控环境下比较 GRPO、DPO 与基于 GFlowNet 的优化目标。

Result: PROBE 训练的审计员发现的策略违规范围更广、命中率更高，显著优于同架构的零样本对抗者，即使审计员规模远小于目标智能体亦然。

Conclusion: 多轮行为审计是智能体评测中独立且必要的维度，可补充能力基准与安全基准；PROBE 为真实智能体系统提供可扩展的自动化策略审计路径，并暗示一种"审计员暴露失效、智能体据反馈改进"的协同演化闭环。

Abstract: Modern agentic systems extend language models with external tools, enabling them to complete complex tasks through multi-turn interactions. As these agents are deployed in consequential settings, they are often constrained by policies governing pricing, privacy, confirmation, safety, and other requirements. However, existing evaluation methods leave a critical gap: capability benchmarks measure what agents can accomplish, while content-safety red-teaming measures harmful generations, but neither tests whether agents comply with predefined policies during otherwise valid tool use. Such policy violations are difficult to audit because their effects may emerge only after several turns, span multiple tool calls, or surface in downstream systems. We propose PROBE (Policy Rule Observation via Behavioral Exploration), an algorithm for training LLM-based auditors that interact with deployed agents as realistic users and probe them for policy violations through multi-turn, tool-mediated dialogue. We formalize agent auditing as a two-player Markov game and propose a composite reward that balances violation discovery, behavioral plausibility, conversation progress, and policy diversity. To study training objectives and reward design, we compare GRPO, DPO, and GFlowNet-based optimization in a controlled setting. Across extensive experiments, auditors trained with PROBE uncover substantially broader and higher-yield policy violations than same-architecture zero-shot adversaries, even when the auditor is significantly smaller than the target agent. Our results show that multi-turn behavioral auditing is a distinct and necessary axis of agent evaluation, complementing capability and safety benchmarks. PROBE offers a scalable path toward automated policy auditing for real-world agentic systems and suggests a co-evolutionary loop in which auditors expose failures and agents improve from auditor feedback.

</details>


### [277] [AutoOnboarder: Autonomously Onboarding LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21218)
*Tanay Arora,Cong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 AutoOnboarder：用"入职培训"范式让 Onboarder 编码智能体迭代改造被测智能体的基座模型、提示词与 harness 源码，在基因组测序预授权表单提交任务上以至多 29 倍更低成本达到或超过 Gemini 3.5 Flash 基线，但结果方差大且仍需更强的 Onboarder 运行环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有观察表明，智能体大模型在置入 harness（中介其观测与动作的软件层）后推理能力会下降。为弥补这一损失，需要一套能自动让智能体适配具体任务与工装的机制，而非依赖人工调优。

Method: AutoOnboarder 将智能体置于一个智能"Onboarder"编码智能体的监督下，仿照企业给新员工做入职培训：仅提供数十条示例，由 Onboarder 迭代地定制被测智能体的基座模型、提示词与 harness 源代码，目标是同时提升性能与降低开销；评测数据隔离在独立虚拟机上以防泄漏。以基因组测序预授权表单提交作为验证任务。

Result: 经自主 onboarding 的智能体在性能上可持平或超过 Gemini 3.5 Flash 基线，成本最多低 29 倍。但智能体仍不完美，且 Onboarding 过程的最终性能波动很大。

Conclusion: 结论是未来 Onboarding 系统需要为 Onboarder 提供稳健环境，包括结构化思维接口、（受控的）互联网访问以及更宽的模型搜索空间。

Abstract: Agentic large language models have been documented to lose reasoning capability when placed inside a harness, the software that mediates their observations and actions. To reconcile these losses, we introduce AutoOnboarder, an autonomous system that adapts an agent to a task the way a trainer onboards a new employee. It places the agent under the supervision of an intelligent ``Onboarder'' coding agent, which iteratively tailors its foundation model, prompt, and harness source code to simultaneously improve performance and decrease cost. Like a real onboarding process, the Onboarder is only given tens of examples, and all evaluation data is isolated on a separate virtual machine to prevent leakage. We report the lessons learned from building and testing AutoOnboarder on genomic-sequencing prior authorization form submission. Autonomously onboarded agents are able to match or exceed the performance of a Gemini 3.5 Flash baseline at up to 29$\times$ lower costs. However, these agents remain imperfect, and the Onboarding process varies greatly in terms of final performance. We conclude that future Onboarding systems require a robust environment for the Onboarder, including a structured thought interface, (controlled) internet access, and a wide model search space.

</details>


### [278] [Co2In: a Bi-level Memory Incremental Learning Framework with Knowledge Encoding, Consolidation, and Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21804)
*Wenju Sun,Qingyong Li,Boyang Li,Wen Wang,Siyu Zhang,Yi Wei,Yangliao Geng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出双层记忆架构Co2In，通过工作记忆与长期记忆协同及编码-巩固-整合流程，在无样本类增量与任务增量学习上兼顾可塑性与稳定性并达SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增量学习需在新知识获取（可塑性）与旧知识保持（稳定性）之间取得平衡，这一权衡仍是智能系统的重大挑战；人脑凭借多种记忆单元协同编码与存储实现优异平衡，故本文受人类记忆机制启发设计IL框架。
