Method: Co2In为双层记忆架构：工作记忆负责自适应知识获取，长期记忆负责信息持久保留，二者协同工作。流程分三步：(1) 工作记忆先在新数据上学习，将知识编码进参数；(2) 执行巩固过程，识别已学参数中的潜在模式并重表示为紧凑的知识表征；(3) 将知识表征与识别出的模式转化为独立网络层并整合进长期记忆。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100与Tiny-ImageNet上，于无样本Class-IL与Task-IL设定下评测，Co2In取得state-of-the-art性能，且内存消耗高效。

Conclusion: 借鉴人类多记忆单元协同机制的双层记忆设计可有效缓解可塑性-稳定性权衡，使增量学习在高可塑性、高稳定性下积累知识，同时保持较低内存开销。

Abstract: Incremental learning (IL) aims to continually acquire new knowledge (plasticity) while retaining previously learned information (stability). However, striking a balance between plasticity and stability remains a significant challenge for intelligent systems. The human brain achieves exceptional balance, owing to various memory units that collaboratively encode and store information. Inspired by human memory mechanisms, this paper introduces an IL framework with knowledge enCoding, Consolidation, and Integration (Co2In). Co2In is designed as a bi-level memory architecture: a working memory for adaptive knowledge acquisition and a long-term memory dedicated to the persistent retention of information. The two memory modules work cooperatively during IL. The working memory first learns from new data to encode knowledge into parameters. Subsequently, Co2In performs a consolidation process to identify underlying patterns in the learned parameters and re-express them into a compact knowledge representation. Next, the knowledge representation and the identified patterns are transformed into separate network layers and integrated into the long-term memory. These designs empower Co2In to accumulate knowledge with high plasticity and stability. We evaluate Co2In on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Tiny-ImageNet under exemplar-free Class-IL and Task-IL settings. Experimental results show that Co2In achieves state-of-the-art performance with efficient memory consumption.

</details>


### [279] [RSIFlow: Adaptive Self-Improvement Workflows with an Evolving Meta-Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21791)
*Huan Zhang,Mingju Chen,Dongxu Zhou,Can Lv,Sen Cui,Faguo Wu,Shiji Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RSIFlow，将自适应工作流编排与元智能体能力演化结合，实现递归自我改进，在六基准两骨干模型上取得SOTA平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我改进方法（模型优化、harness演化、可执行产物精炼）及递归自我改进（RSI）未能同时解决两个问题：如何让自我改进工作流随性能瓶颈动态适配，以及如何利用执行反馈改进工作流编排本身。

Method: RSIFlow从任务-智能体轨迹与环境反馈中诊断性能瓶颈，动态选择针对具体组件（component-specific）的改进工作流；随后元智能体将工作流结果蒸馏为组件特定技能与跨组件原则，并演化自身harness以改进后续工作流编排。

Result: 在六个基准与两个骨干模型上，RSIFlow在全部基准取得SOTA平均性能；消融实验验证了自适应工作流编排与元harness演化两项设计的贡献。

Conclusion: 将瓶颈诊断驱动的自适应编排与元智能体harness自我演化闭环结合，可有效实现递归自我改进；代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have demonstrated strong capabilities in complex tasks, including code generation, tool use, and multi-hop question answering. Existing self-improvement methods enhance agent performance through model optimization, harness evolution, and executable artifact refinement, while recursive self-improvement (RSI) further extends optimization to the improvement mechanisms themselves. However, adapting self-improvement workflows to evolving performance bottlenecks while improving workflow orchestration through execution feedback remains a key challenge. We propose \textbf{RSIFlow}, a \textbf{R}ecursive \textbf{S}elf-\textbf{I}mprovement framework that integrates adaptive work\textbf{flow} orchestration with meta-agent capability evolution. RSIFlow diagnoses performance bottlenecks from task-agent trajectories and environmental feedback to dynamically select component-specific improvement workflows. The meta-agent then distills workflow outcomes into component-specific skills and cross-component principles, evolving its own harness to improve subsequent workflow orchestration. Experiments across six benchmarks and two backbone models show that RSIFlow achieves the SOTA average performance on all benchmarks. Ablation studies further demonstrate the contributions of adaptive workflow orchestration and meta-harness evolution. Code is available at https://github.com/1HUAN1/RSI-Flow.

</details>


### [280] [Ontology Refinement with Embeddings and LLMs: A Case Study on CASTON](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21466)
*Hui Yang,Ziyu Li,Xuan Liu,Anders Jarfors,He Tan,Jiaoyan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CASTON本体精化工作流，结合微调本体嵌入模型与LLM进行本体公理修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本体（如生物医学、医疗及铸造领域）中的类层次结构无论人工或自动构建，都可能隐含微妙的包含（subsumption）错误，需仔细精化，而可靠本体的构建仍具挑战性。

Method: 以铸造领域本体CASTON为研究对象，构建轻量且可扩展的精化流程：先用CASTON微调的本体嵌入模型（OnT）抽取少量候选公理及可能的替换候选，再由LLM（Qwen3-8B）给出修复建议。

Result: 在小规模专家评估中，默认配置（CASTON微调OnT嵌入模型 + Qwen3-8B）产生可靠的高置信度修复：全部六个高置信度替换均被两位领域专家接受。

Conclusion: 嵌入模型负责候选筛选、LLM负责修复建议的组合流程可有效支持本体精化，代码已开源（GitHub: HuiYang1997/OntRefine）。

Abstract: Ontologies are widely used to represent and organize complex knowledge in domains such as biomedicine and healthcare. However, constructing reliable ontologies remains challenging, as class hierarchies, whether manually or automatically constructed, may contain subtle subsumption errors that require careful refinement. We study this problem in CASTON, a casting-domain ontology, and present a lightweight but scalable refinement workflow that combines a fine-tuned ontology embedding model with Large Language Models (LLMs). The embedding model is used to extract a limited number of candidate axioms for refinement together with possible replacement candidates, and an LLM then returns repair suggestions. In a small expert evaluation, the default configuration using a CASTON-fine-tuned OnT embedding model and Qwen3-8B produced reliable high-confidence repairs: all six high-confidence replacements were accepted by both domain experts. The implementation is available at https://github.com/HuiYang1997/OntRefine.

</details>


### [281] [Watts If? A Benchmark for Counterfactual Reasoning About the AI Data Center Buildout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21397)
*Pankaj Chouhan,Rajarshi Das,Aditya Dubey,Manminder Tomar,Sreekar Reddy,Samta Sharma,Satyapragnya Kar,PR Panigrahi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 DCBench-440，用于检验大模型能否把握 AI 数据中心早期资本承诺的“可行性边界”。基准分两臂：200 道基于 STRIPS 世界的生成式问题（搜索验证金标，任意题型无超过 12% 的捷径）与 240 道基于引用场址记录、排放方程和给定情景的多选题。开放权重模型准确率分别为 12.5–71.5% 与 58.3–92.5%；但“列出决策关口全部可行动作”这一技能全面失败，无模型精确匹配超过 4.0%，最强模型仅列出 72–85% 会改变项目状态的动作而漏掉多数无状态变化的允许动作，豁免这些后最佳精确匹配仍仅 24%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 数据中心在耗电前数年即锁定若干离散承诺（并网或自备燃气、蒸发或干冷、选址县），决定其资本、取水与碳路径；LLM 已被用于辅助筛选，却缺少检验其是否理解“每项承诺排除了什么”的测试。

Method: 构建 DCBench-440，双臂分别报告：1）200 道生成式问题，建立在五种美国交易原型的 STRIPS 世界上，采用搜索验证的金标准答案，且各题型无可利用捷径（最高 12%）；2）240 道多选题，以引用的场址记录、排放方程与明述情景为依据。在多个开放权重部署上进行评测。

Result: 生成式臂准确率 12.5–71.5%，多选题臂 58.3–92.5%。关键失败项为“列出决策关口所有可行动作”：无模型精确匹配超过 4.0%；最强模型覆盖 72–85% 的会改变状态的动作，但遗漏多数不改变状态的允许动作；即便豁免后者，最佳精确匹配仍为 24%。

Conclusion: 模型在情景化选择题上表现尚可，但在需要完整枚举动作空间、把握承诺所排除选项的生成式推理上显著不足；DCBench-440 提供了可验证的评测手段，揭示 LLM 尚不足以可靠用于数据中心早期承诺筛选。

Abstract: An AI data center is committed years before it draws a watt: a few discrete choices (grid or on-site gas, evaporative or dry cooling, which county to file in) fix its capital, water draw, and carbon path, and LLMs are now asked to help screen them with no test of whether they track what each commitment rules out. We introduce DCBench-440, with two arms reported separately: 200 generative questions over STRIPS worlds for five US deal archetypes, with search-verified gold answers and no tested shortcut above 12% on any question type, and 240 multiple-choice questions grounded in quoted site records, emission equations, and stated scenarios. Across open-weight deployments, accuracy spans 12.5-71.5% (generative) and 58.3-92.5% (multiple choice). One skill fails everywhere: asked to list every action available at a decision gate, no model exceeds 4.0% exact match. The strongest list 72-85% of the actions that would change the project's state but omit most permitted actions that change nothing; with those excused, the best exact match is still 24%. Code, data, and other materials are available upon request.

</details>


### [282] [OmicsBench: a long-horizon multi-turn benchmark of AI agents on research-study-scale bioinformatics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21074)
*Gideon Shaked,Siddharth Nagisetty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OmicsBench——面向AI智能体的长程、多轮生物信息学基准，含97个任务（662子任务，DAG依赖），并配套无监督失败模式发现流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体生信能力评测多为单一短任务、单轮提示，无法反映真实科研中长程、多轮、子任务相互依赖的分析场景，也无法刻画失败方式。

Method: 每个任务将依赖子任务组织为有向无环图，每子任务对应一轮用户输入并按答案键确定性评分，答错时可给出上下文反馈；在12个模型×3个运行框架上评测；另构建无监督失败模式发现流程：描述、嵌入并聚类失败尝试以归纳涌现性失败模式。

Result: 最慢智能体平均每任务耗时3.6小时；无组合接近饱和，无反馈均分0.43–0.57，有反馈0.54–0.67；依赖前置子任务的子任务得分更低，上下文反馈可部分挽回；聚类揭示如擅自替换指定统计过滤、凭记忆整段作答等质性缺陷。

Conclusion: 长程多轮评测与确定性评分互补的无监督失败分析，能更真实地刻画智能体生信能力并定位其失败模式。

Abstract: Computational biologists increasingly delegate substantial analyses to AI agents in long-horizon, multi-turn sessions, yet most benchmarks of agents' bioinformatics capabilities pose a single short task with a single-shot prompt. We present OmicsBench, a long-horizon, multi-turn bioinformatics benchmark for AI agents. Each of its 97 tasks links dependent subtasks into a directed acyclic graph, for 662 underlying subtasks in all. Each subtask is posed as one user turn and graded deterministically against an answer key, with optional in-context feedback on incorrect answers. Across 12 models on three harnesses, the slowest agent averages 3.6 hours per task, and no combination comes near saturating the benchmark, with mean scores of 0.43 to 0.57 out of a possible 1 without in-context feedback and 0.54 to 0.67 with it. Multi-turn evaluation also uncovers behavior that single-shot prompts cannot, as agents score lower on subtasks that depend on earlier subtasks, and in-context feedback helps agents recover some of that loss. Because deterministic scoring gives no qualitative account of how an agent failed, we also introduce an unsupervised failure mode discovery pipeline that describes, embeds, and clusters agents' failed attempts to surface emergent failure modes. Applied to OmicsBench, this pipeline exposes qualitative shortcomings in how agents carry out bioinformatics analyses, ranging from agents applying their own statistical filters in place of the specified ones to agents writing whole answers from memory.

</details>


### [283] [Recursive Reward Improvement via Adversarial Meta-Prompt Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21040)
*Wei Dai,Qianchu Liu,Guanghui Qin,Xing Han,Tristan Naumann,Paul Pu Liang,Sheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出递归奖励改进（RRI），通过引入“奖励黑客对抗者”监督模型自生成评分标准（rubric）来修复开放域专家任务中自博弈的学习腐化问题，在HealthBench Professional上显著提升表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进（RSI）在数学、编程等答案可自动校验的任务上已成功，但临床报告生成等开放式专家任务没有标准答案，简单问答生成不可行。此类任务需由proposer模型编写rubric，而模型生成rubric的核心挑战在于全面性：朴素生成的rubric易被solver利用，即只罗列期望主题而无实质内容，从而污染学习过程。

Method: 提出RRI（Recursive Reward Improvement），用奖励黑客对抗者监督奖励。在solver于生成任务上训练前，由对抗者写一个“钻空子”答案（利用rubric但尽量无用）；同一模型在不看rubric的情况下写诚实答案；经最多两步修复迭代修改rubric，使诚实答案胜出。发现的漏洞还会反过来改进未来任务与rubric的生成方式。

Result: 在HealthBench Professional上，RRI的adjusted score较Generation Only自改进基线在9B上提升10.9分、27B上提升12.4分；无任何外部模型时提升10.3分。增益在困难任务与写作任务上最大。仅在HealthBench Professional描述上训练的27B模型，在未见过的PRBench Hard上提升6.6分、ProfBench上提升8.0分。

Conclusion: RRI有效缓解了模型生成rubric的可利用性问题，在开放式专家任务上实现显著且可迁移的自我改进，并分析了模型生成问答-rubric对的可利用点，为未来开放式自博弈研究提供参考。

Abstract: Recursive Self-Improvement (RSI) has gained wide attention for its ability to work with limited data. In a proposer-solver loop, a proposer model writes training tasks and a solver model learns to solve them through reinforcement learning. Such loops have succeeded in mathematics and coding, where every answer can be checked automatically. Open-ended expert tasks, like clinical report generations, have no answer key, which makes simple question-answer generation not viable. Here the proposer must also write a rubric that lists the content an answer should include and the errors it should avoid. However, the unique challenge behind model-written rubric is the comprehensiveness of it. For naively generated rubric, solvers tend to exploit it, naming the expected topics without the substance behind them, which corrupts the learning process. To solve this problem, we introduce RRI, Recursive Reward Improvement, which uses a reward hacking adversary to supervise the reward. Before the solver trains on a generated task, an adversary writes an answer that games its rubric while being as useless as possible, the same model writes an honest answer without seeing the rubric, and up to two repair steps revise the rubric to make the honest answer win. The exploits found also rewrite how future tasks and rubrics are generated. On HealthBench Professional, RRI improves the adjusted score over the Generation Only self-improvement baseline by 10.9 points at 9B and 12.4 at 27B, and by 10.3 points without any external model. In particular, the gains are largest on difficult and writing tasks. We show that the 27B model trained on the HealthBench Professional description improves by 6.6 points on PRBench Hard and 8.0 on ProfBench without seeing a task from either. We further analyze the exploitable points of model-generated question-rubric pairs to facilitate future research on open-ended self-play.

</details>


### [284] [Deep Reinforcement Learning for HPC Job Scheduling: A Statistical Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21008)
*Justin M. Cheney,Clement N. Nyirenda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在两种真实Slurm负载上，以掩码随机策略为对照，只有MaskablePPO在异构负载上相对对照显著提升三项调度指标，其余掩码DRL未优于对照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DRL用于HPC作业选择时，仅与启发式基线比较不足以证明其超出"动作掩码"本身的增益，需引入种子配对的掩码随机对照来分离学习带来的真实收益。

Method: 比较PPO、A2C、DQN三种算法各含/不含动作掩码共六种DRL处理；以三种启发式和种子配对的掩码均匀随机策略为基线；在两个真实Slurm负载（一个同构、一个异构）上各跑十个种子，采用统一非参数统计框架（含Nemenyi检验），评估平均等待时间、平均与最大有界慢速比。

Result: 所有掩码处理均完成评估，非掩码处理被截断。同构负载上，无掩码DRL处理显著优于对照；异构负载上MaskablePPO三项指标均优于对照，平均等待时间在10%误差内与三种启发式相当，最大有界慢速比LCFS低22%。MaskablePPO在两负载上平均等待时间排名领先，但与MaskableA2C无显著差异。掩码变体中MaskablePPO训练时间最短，MaskableDQN决策延迟最低。

Conclusion: 研究区分了"能否完成调度"与"相对基线的具体增益"两类结论，建议在评估学习式作业选择价值时纳入掩码随机对照基线。

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) offers learned job selection for High-Performance Computing (HPC), but performance against heuristics alone does not establish value beyond action masking. Six DRL treatments are compared: Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), Advantage Actor-Critic (A2C), and Deep Q-Network (DQN), each with and without masking. Three heuristics and a seed-paired masked uniform random control provide baselines across ten seeds on two real Slurm (Simple Linux Utility for Resource Management) workload traces, one homogeneous and one heterogeneous, under a common non-parametric framework. All masked treatments complete evaluation, whereas unmasked treatments are truncated. On the homogeneous trace, no masked DRL treatment significantly improves average waiting time or average and maximum bounded slowdown over the control. On the heterogeneous trace, MaskablePPO improves all three metrics relative to the control. Its average waiting time is equivalent to that of the three heuristics within a 10% margin, and its maximum bounded slowdown is 22% lower than that of Last-Come, First-Served (LCFS). MaskablePPO has the leading average waiting-time rank among complete treatments on both traces, but Nemenyi comparisons detect no significant difference from MaskableA2C. Among masked variants, MaskablePPO has the lowest recorded mean training time, while MaskableDQN has the lowest mean decision latency. These findings distinguish completion from baseline-specific scheduling gains and support including a masked random control when assessing the value of learned selection.

</details>


### [285] [Open-world AI: a Tale of Three Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20782)
*Jianyu Zhang,Léon Bottou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文主张开放世界要求模型具备推理时学习能力，并理论推导出三个必要条件：推理时学习、丰富表征、预测性解耦；在8B以内语言模型上验证，满足条件的模型能随相关任务数据积累而提升，其余模型退化，且规模与层间交错均无法修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 封闭世界只要求AI具备专门能力，并可制造iid数据直至任务被覆盖；开放世界则要求通用性——从少量样本、缺乏人类先验地接手未预设任务，且数据是成组而非打乱的。由于任务未知，机器必须进行推理时学习，因此需要厘清开放世界下模型应具备哪些性质。

Method: 将任务建模为共享潜在因子并分解训练目标，理论推导出三个必要性质：推理时学习、保留超出iid泛化所需的丰富表征、将输入输出对齐为匹配低维组的预测性解耦；分析还预测一致的内层学习器的必要性及跨相关任务长上下文的收益。在至多8B参数的语言模型上以定性方式检验模型是否具备某性质，而非比较性能增益大小。

Result: 满足所需性质的模型在积累相关任务数据时表现提升；其余模型则退化，且模型规模扩大与层级交错（layer-type interleaving）均无法修复这一问题。

Conclusion: 开放世界通用性由结构性必要条件决定，而非由规模驱动；推理时学习、丰富表征与预测性解耦是模型能否从相关任务数据中受益的关键判据。

Abstract: The closed world asks AI for specialized competence and lets it manufacture the iid data its theory assumes: a fixed criterion licenses collecting examples until the task is covered. The open world asks for versatility --- taking up tasks a machine was not built for, from few examples and with little prior from a human designer --- and offers data that is grouped, not shuffled. Its tasks are not known in advance, so a machine must do inference-time learning (1st requirement). Modeling tasks as sharing latent factors and decomposing the training objective, we derive two more: a *rich representation*, retaining more than iid generalization requires, and a predictive disentanglement that aligns inputs and outputs into matched low-dimensional groups. The analysis further predicts the need for a consistent inner learner, and the benefit of long contexts spanning related tasks. We test these claims on language models up to 8B, asking whether a model has a property (qualitative) rather than how much it wins (quantitative). Models that meet the requirements improve as data from related tasks accumulates; the rest degrade, and neither scale nor layer-type interleaving repairs this.

</details>


### [286] [The World Won't Wait: Evaluating and Teaching Agents Under a Live Clock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20665)
*Ningxin Pan,Hanyu Li,Yeqing Shen,Ning Ding,Yehui Tang,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出实时交互基准 RealtimeBench（6类游戏、180个任务）与双时间尺度离线递归自我改进框架 TORSI，让慢速教师离线学习并产出可复用指令、快速学生在线无教师调用地行动，使 Jev 平均测试分数从 13.51 提升至 24.19（0–100），并零调优迁移到其他三个模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体的推理与工具调用耗时较长，在持续变化的环境中，"正确但迟到"的动作会失效；现有评测多在暂停/回合制设定下进行，掩盖了推理时延带来的性能衰减。因此需要能够反映实时性约束的基准，以及兼顾深思质量与响应速度的智能体方法。

Method: 1）构建 RealtimeBench：180 个任务、6 类游戏，游戏在模型推理期间持续运行，通过"暂停/不暂停"配对评测量化时延影响；2）提出 TORSI（Two-Timescale Offline Recursive Self-Improvement）：慢速教师在两轮尝试之间于暂停环境中分析失败、验证纠正方案，并将经验写成可复用指令；快速学生在实际对局中不调用教师，直接依指令行动；以验证集分数决定是否采纳更新，形成离线的递归自我改进闭环。

Result: 配对评测显示 GPT-6.1-sol 在暂停设定下优于更快的 Jev，但在游戏持续运行时反被超越；经两轮 TORSI 训练，Jev 测试平均分由 13.51 升至 24.19（0–100 进度标度），六类游戏全部提升；所得指令在无需重新调优的情况下提升了另外三个模型的平均分数。

Conclusion: 研究指向"实时智能"：通过在时间尺度上分离深思、学习与行动（教师离线慢学、学生在线快行），使能力强的 LLM 智能体在持续变化的世界中保持有效性；该范式具备跨模型可迁移性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can solve complex interactive tasks, yet reasoning and tool calls take time. In continuously changing environments, an otherwise correct action may arrive too late. We introduce RealtimeBench, with 180 tasks across six game types, to evaluate agents while games continue running during inference. Paired evaluations show that GPT-6.1-sol outperforms the faster Jev when games are paused, but falls behind when they keep running. We propose Two-Timescale Offline Recursive Self-Improvement (TORSI): a slow teacher studies failures and tests corrections in paused games between attempts, then writes reusable instructions for a fast student. The student acts without teacher calls during gameplay, and validation scores determine which updates are adopted. Two rounds raise Jev's average test score from 13.51 to 24.19 on a 0--100 progress scale, improving all six game types. The instructions also improve average scores for three other models without retuning. These findings point toward real-time intelligence: enabling powerful LLM agents to remain effective in a continuously changing world by organizing deliberation, learning, and action across timescales.

</details>


### [287] [Learning from Unverified Peers via Shared Residual Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22080)
*Feiyang Chen,Runze Ma,Zichen Wang,Jiong Lou,Zihan Wang,Shuangjia Zheng,Jie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Shared Residual Distillation (SRD)，一种 on-policy 自蒸馏方法，无需判断模型自身生成的解是否正确，即可从"未验证的同侪轨迹"中学习；在 Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B 上显著提升 AIME24–26 avg@12，且通用能力无退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有利用模型多条生成轨迹做后训练的方法（监督微调、可验证反馈、外部教师）都依赖参考答案或外部监督信号；当缺乏监督时，单条轨迹的正确性无法可靠评估。但错误解也可能包含有用的中间推理，因此一条解的价值不应仅由其最终答案决定。核心问题是：模型能否在不先判定哪些解正确的前提下，从自己未验证的同侪解中获益？

Method: SRD 为 on-policy 自蒸馏：以模型自身生成的同侪轨迹为条件，计算相对于对照组（control）的预测分布变化，即"残差"；只保留方向一致、幅度保守（conservative in magnitude）的调整作为学习信号。整个流程不把任何完整解标记为正确，且训练后模型推理时不需要同侪（无额外上下文开销）。

Result: 从对应基座模型出发，AIME24–26 的 avg@12 平均分：Qwen3-1.7B 由 11.02 提升至 16.20；Qwen3-4B 由 19.07 提升至 45.46；Qwen3-8B 由 21.39 提升至 54.44。受控消融实验支持"共享残差"构造的有效性；在两个通用能力基准上相对基座模型无性能退化。

Conclusion: 无需正确性判定或外部监督，仅凭模型自身未验证的同侪轨迹即可构成有效学习信号；SRD 通过共享残差式的方向/幅度约束实现稳健自蒸馏，在数学推理上带来大幅提升且不损害通用能力，推理阶段零额外成本。

Abstract: A language model can generate several solutions to the same problem, some leading to correct answers and others to incorrect ones. Many post-training methods turn these trajectories into learning signals using supervision from reference answers, verifiable feedback, or external teachers. Without such supervision, learning from the model's own generations becomes less straightforward, as their correctness cannot be reliably assessed. An incorrect solution may nevertheless contain useful intermediate reasoning, so its value as supervision need not be determined solely by its final answer. Can a model improve by learning from its unverified peer solutions without first determining which are correct?
  We propose Shared Residual Distillation (SRD), an on-policy self-distillation method that learns from the model's own unverified peer trajectories. SRD compares how peers change the prediction distribution relative to a control, retaining adjustments that agree in direction and are conservative in magnitude. This yields supervision without treating any complete solution as correct, and the trained model requires no peers at inference time. Starting from the corresponding base models, SRD raises mean AIME24--26 avg@12 from 11.02 to 16.20 on Qwen3-1.7B, from 19.07 to 45.46 on Qwen3-4B, and from 21.39 to 54.44 on Qwen3-8B. Controlled ablations support the value of the shared-residual construction, and we observe no degradation relative to the base models on two general-capability benchmarks.

</details>


### [288] [When Reasoning Is Not Enough: Proactive Inquiry for Cross-Modal Safety in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22068)
*Wei Cai,Jian Zhao,Yuchen Yuan,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 SAFE-PRO，利用风险条件不确定性（RCU）信号判断何时提出安全澄清问题、何处进行路径优化，以缓解多模态大模型在图文联合解读时的意图级风险，并构建 SSUI-Dialog 数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单独良性的图像与文本联合解读时，MLLM 可能表现不安全。除隐式推理风险（推理漂移向有害）外，作者识别出意图级风险：跨模态歧义会悄无声息地将模型锁定在一个危险前提上，仅靠路径级优化无法纠正。

Method: 提出 SAFE-PRO：使用风险条件不确定性（RCU）信号，将预测熵与学习得到的跨模态风险分数结合，据此决定何时提出基于安全的澄清问题、何处施加路径优化；采用优先安全并惩罚过度拒答的奖励；构建具有双意图监督的多模态多轮数据集 SSUI-Dialog；并从理论上分析 RCU 下的梯度方差与不安全完成风险。

Result: 在七个基准和两个基础 MLLM 上，SAFE-PRO 相比 SRPO 持续提升安全性，过度拒答约减半，平均单轮安全性超过 GPT-4o 与 Claude-3.5-Sonnet。

Conclusion: 将不确定性与跨模态风险评分结合用于触发澄清提问与定向优化，可有效应对意图级风险并兼顾拒答率；SAFE-PRO 在安全性与过度拒答之间取得更好平衡，优于现有路径优化方法与主流闭源模型。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can act unsafely when individually benign images and texts are interpreted jointly. Beyond implicit reasoning risk, where reasoning drifts toward harm, we identify intent-level risk, where cross-modal ambiguity silently commits the model to a hazardous premise that path-level optimization alone cannot correct. We propose SAFE-PRO, which uses a Risk-Conditioned Uncertainty (RCU) signal, combining predictive entropy with a learned cross-modal risk score, to decide when to ask safety-grounded clarifying questions and where to apply path optimization, under a reward that prioritizes safety and penalizes over-refusal. We also build SSUI-Dialog, a multimodal multi-turn dataset with dual-intent supervision, and theoretically analyze gradient variance and unsafe-completion risk under RCU. Across seven benchmarks and two base MLLMs, SAFE-PRO consistently improves safety over SRPO, roughly halves its over-refusal, and surpasses GPT-4o and Claude-3.5-Sonnet in average single-turn safety.

</details>


### [289] [A Self-Improving Agent that Grows its Own DSL: Oracle-Free Operator Induction for Exact Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20809)
*Yun-Shiuan Chuang,Yirou Ge,Yixi Li,Yiming Bian,Zijie Pan,Jenna L. Ballard,Uma Kona,Linsey Pang,Prakhar Mehrotra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种无需奖励、无需评审模型、无答案键的自改进代理机制：单冻结模型在小型精确DSL上运行内外嵌套循环，内循环靠双独立表达式逐记录一致作答，外循环自发现缺失能力并编写算子，仅当独立第二实现一致且满足守恒律时准入；该准入规则在138个含bug算子变体中全部拒绝，远超基线信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自改进代理以高奖励、说服评审模型或压缩库作为保留技能的标准，即使技能本身错误也会被保留；但在需要精确正确的场景（如交易表求和，只能对或错）下失效。核心问题：代理能否在无奖励、无评审、无答案键的条件下扩展可计算能力且保持精确正确。

Method: 单冻结模型在小型精确领域特定语言（DSL）上运行两个嵌套循环。内循环仅在两个独立编写的表达式对每条记录都一致时才回答新问题（冗余互证）。外循环自行发现缺失能力并为其编写算子，准入须满足：独立第二实现结果一致，且结果满足守恒律，全程不使用ground truth。另设无归纳对照组。

Result: 内循环使可回答问题集约增至三倍；外循环在不相交起始集上自我扩展，发明出超出起始闭包的算子，无归纳对照组则对新问题零作答。准入规则经验上可靠：在独立参考下拒绝138/138个buggy算子变体（含不同模型家族所写者），而业界常用的"能跑即准入"、自一致性、压缩信号则大多放行。机制未改动即迁移至业务账本与临床接诊问答两个schema无关域，可作答集分别扩大逾十倍与近二十倍；跨模型家族亦可迁移，准入门槛保持可靠，仅归纳产出随各提议者的DSL熟练度变化。

Conclusion: 在严格精确性要求下，可通过双重独立实现一致性与守恒律作为无监督准入判据，实现无需奖励、评审与答案键的自扩展计算能力，且该判据对buggy变体的拒绝能力显著优于现有信号，并在多领域、多模型家族间具备可迁移性。

Abstract: A self-improving agent has to decide which new skills to keep, and in practice it keeps whatever earns a high reward, convinces a judge model, or compresses its library, even when the skill is wrong. That is fine until the answers have to be exactly right: over a table of transactions, a total is either right or wrong. We ask a harder question: can an agent grow what it can compute with no reward, no judge, and no answer key, and still be exactly right? A single frozen model runs two nested loops over a small, exact domain-specific language (DSL). The inner loop answers new questions only when two independently written expressions agree on every record. The outer loop discovers its own missing capability and authors an operator for it, admitted only when an independent second implementation agrees and the result obeys a conservation law, all without ground truth. The inner loop roughly triples the answerable-question set, and the outer loop self-extends on disjoint starting sets, inventing operators outside the starting closure; a no-induction control answers none of the new questions. The admission rule is empirically sound: it rejects 138 of 138 buggy operator variants under an independent reference, even one written by a different model family, where the admit-if-it-runs, self-consistency, and compression signals the field relies on admit most of them. Unmodified, the same mechanism self-extends on two further domains with unrelated schemas, business-ledger and clinical-encounter question answering, growing their answerable sets more than tenfold and nearly twentyfold. The loop transfers across model families too: run with further proposers, the gate stays sound and independent families self-extend comparably, with only the induction yield tracking each proposer's DSL fluency.

</details>


### [290] [Value-Informed Schedule Trimming for Accelerated Sequential Monte Carlo Guidance in Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20669)
*Jaehyeon Kim,Ziseok Lee,Siyoung Choi,Kyungsu Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VISTA是一种免训练方法，在固定预算下自动为离散扩散模型的奖励引导选择稀疏引导时间步，通过最小化理想全引导路径的ℓ1偏差上界并用动态规划精确求解该经验代理目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励引导的离散扩散能在测试时对齐下游目标，但每步都做SMC引导计算开销大（额外奖励评估，梯度提案还需值梯度计算）。稀疏引导只引导部分时间步可降本，但常用调度是固定启发式，跨任务效果差异大。

Method: 将稀疏引导建模为对理想全引导概率路径的近似，推导可估计的ℓ1路径偏差上界；用少量全引导warmup生成收集值统计量，得到经验代理目标，并用动态规划精确优化；warmup生成可保留为模型输出，选定调度后每次生成的引导复杂度与其他同预算稀疏调度相同。

Result: 在文本、生物序列、图像生成任务上，VISTA发现任务相关调度，在多数设置下优于手工稀疏调度，并在若干指标上接近或超过全引导。毒性文本生成中unique-toxic比达0.99（全引导为1.00），选定调度后单次生成延迟由204.23 s降至38.16 s，加速5.35×。

Conclusion: 以路径偏差上界为原则的自动稀疏引导调度选择可替代固定启发式，在几乎不损失对齐质量的前提下显著降低推理成本，且免训练、开销与同预算稀疏方法相当。

Abstract: Reward-guided discrete diffusion enables test-time alignment with downstream objectives, but applying Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) guidance at every denoising step is computationally expensive. Active guidance steps require additional reward evaluations and, for gradient-based proposals, value-gradient computations. Sparse guidance reduces this cost by guiding only a subset of timesteps, but commonly used schedules are fixed heuristics whose effectiveness can vary substantially across tasks. We propose VISTA, a training-free method that automatically selects guided timesteps under a fixed budget. VISTA formulates sparse guidance as approximation of the ideal full-guidance probability path and derives an estimable upper bound on \(\ell_1\) path deviation. Using value statistics collected from a small number of full-guidance warmup generations, VISTA optimizes the resulting empirical surrogate exactly via dynamic programming. The warmup generations can be retained as model outputs, and after schedule selection VISTA has the same per-generation guidance complexity as other sparse schedules with the same budget. Across text, biological sequence, and image generation tasks, VISTA discovers task-dependent schedules and outperforms hand-designed sparse schedules in most tested settings, while approaching or exceeding full-guidance performance on several task metrics. In toxic-text generation, for example, VISTA attains a 0.99 unique-toxic ratio compared with 1.00 for full guidance while reducing post-selection per-generation latency from 204.23 s to 38.16 s, a 5.35$\times$ speedup.

</details>


### [291] [Probe-and-Resume: When and How to Evaluate Agent Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21088)
*Jiakai Xu,Tiancheng Ge,Tujie Guan,Yusheng Li,Rohan Timmaraju,Eugene Wu,Kostis Kaffes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在终端式computer-use环境中，MCTS配置并未显著提升任务成功率；作者将critic失效归因于观测缺失环境状态和中间状态判别力弱，进而提出Probe-and-Resume：仅在agent自认完成时调用具备读写执行权限、可对状态分叉进行探测的critic，在Terminal-Bench 2.1上较传统MCTS critic提升8.4个百分点，并以不到一半成本接近Codex CLI的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时搜索通过探索替代动作并用critic选择有希望的分支来提升agent表现，但在仅暴露大型底层状态部分视图的终端式computer-use环境中，评估得到的MCTS配置并未显著改善任务成功。作者希望定位critic性能不佳的根源：观测未包含评估所需的环境状态，且中间状态对轨迹是否成功的证据有限。

Method: 实证分析critic在中间状态的排序能力（仅略优于随机）以及给予环境访问的增益有限；据此设计策略：(1) 仅在agent自认为终态的节点调用critic，而非部分进展阶段；(2) 为critic提供计算机当前状态与初始状态分叉的读、写、执行权限，使其可通过探测甚至破坏性操作暴露轨迹遗漏的隐藏状态。

Result: 中间状态上critic对续接的排序仅略优于随机，提供环境访问改善有限；在Terminal-Bench 2.1上，Probe-and-Resume相较使用传统critic的MCTS成功率提升8.4个百分点，并以不到一半成本接近同底模Codex CLI的成功率。

Conclusion: 在部分可观测的终端环境中，测试时搜索的收益瓶颈主要在critic的调用时机与环境访问方式，而非搜索本身；将critic集中于终态判断并赋予对状态分叉的完全操作权限，是更有效的设计范式，同时具备成本优势。

Abstract: Test-time search can improve agent performance by exploring alternative actions and using a critic to select promising branches. We study this approach in terminal-based computer-use environments, which expose only a partial view of a large underlying state, and find that the Monte Carlo tree search (MCTS) configuration we evaluate does not significantly improve task success. We identify two sources of poor critic performance: the agent's observations omit environment state needed for evaluation, and intermediate states offer limited evidence of whether a trajectory will succeed. At intermediate states, critics rank continuations only modestly better than chance, and environment access improves this little. We study when to invoke a critic and what access to give it to evaluate a node. Our findings motivate a policy that invokes the critic only at nodes the agent believes are final, rather than during partial progress. Further, the policy gives the critic read, write, and execute access to a fork of the computer's current and initial state, allowing it to probe and perform potentially destructive actions to surface any hidden state the trajectory omits. We call the resulting design Probe-and-Resume: when the agent claims to be done, the critic probes a fork of its state, and a rejected agent resumes where it stopped. On Terminal-Bench 2.1, this raises success by 8.4 percentage points over MCTS with conventional critics, and approaches the success rate of the Codex CLI agent with the same underlying model at less than half its cost.

</details>


### [292] [AlphaDiana: A Measurement System for Agents in Verifiable Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20675)
*Zhanke Zhou,Zongze Li,Weikai Huang,Xuan Li,Chentao Cao,Xiao Feng,Xiangyu Lu,Jinbo Hu,Yi Xie,Nico Pelleriti,Shiyang Liu,Menghan Lu,Max Zimmer,Brando Miranda,Jiangchao Yao,Bo Liu,Sanmi Koyejo,Sebastian Pokutta,Bo Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AlphaDiana测量系统，将执行条件与结果、行为、资源使用关联，揭示harness及工具/技能/记忆等组件干预并不能稳定提升推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent由推理模型与harness（组织模型调用、执行工具、加载技能指令、维护记忆历史）组合而成，需将执行条件与结果、行为、资源使用建立联系才能理解其表现。现有评测缺乏在共享任务与资源策略下对完整agent做宏观分析、对组件做匹配条件下微观干预的手段。

Method: 构建AlphaDiana测量系统，提供数据集、agent、环境、评分器的模块化接口；保留harness原生控制循环，同时分别控制文件复用与记忆保留；支持对完整agent的宏观分析，以及在与匹配条件下对工具、技能、记忆等组件的微观干预。实验覆盖3个开源模型×3个开源harness、5个答案型基准，另2个模型测试2个交互式基准。

Result: harness并不稳定提升推理准确率：模型单独无法解决的问题上的增益，可能被其无工具时本可正确回答的问题上的损失抵消；该不一致性延伸至组件干预，技能引导与历史保留均不能可靠提升准确率；行为与输出token需与正确率联合评估——低token熵可能伴随错误答案，准确率下降时单次执行更少输出反而意味着每个正确答案消耗更多token。

Conclusion: agent评测须将答案正确性与执行状态同轨迹和资源记录关联，在共享任务与资源策略下比较agent并诊断失败；harness与组件并非普遍有效，需基于正确率与成本的联合指标审慎评估。

Abstract: Agents pair reasoning models with harnesses that organize model calls, execute tools, load skill instructions, and retain history in memory. Understanding agent performance requires linking execution conditions to outcomes, behavior, and resource use. We introduce AlphaDiana, a measurement system with modular interfaces for datasets, agents, environments, and scorers. It preserves harness-native control loops while controlling file reuse and memory retention separately. AlphaDiana supports macro analyses of complete agents and micro interventions on agent components such as tools, skills, and memory under matched conditions. We study three open-source models and three open-source harnesses on five answer-based benchmarks, with two models also tested on two interactive benchmarks. We find that harnesses do not consistently improve the accuracy of reasoning: gains on questions unsolved by the model alone can be outweighed by losses on questions it answers correctly without tools. This inconsistency extends to component interventions: neither skill guidance nor retained history reliably improves accuracy. Behavior and output-token use must be assessed with correctness: low token entropy can accompany wrong answers, and less output per execution can mean more output tokens per correct answer when accuracy falls. AlphaDiana links answer correctness and execution status to traces and resource records for comparing agents and diagnosing failures under shared task and resource policies.

</details>


### [293] [Counterfactual Neighborhood Risk Fields: Evidence-Grounded Selective Safety for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20679)
*Tianshi Wang,Jingsong Wang,Fengling Li,Jingjing Li,Xin Li,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出反事实邻域风险场（CNRF），将"能力相关风险方向"与"邻域支持"分离估计，实现VLM的选择性风险干预而非一刀切拒答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于表征的VLM安全防御只学习"有害/良性"的分离边界，但分离本身无法说明风险分数的含义及可信条件，容易把"不熟悉"误判为"有害"。

Method: 构造上下文匹配的良性-有害反事实配对，以配对表征差异作为能力相关风险方向；将邻域支持度与风险分开估计；设计受害者自适应接口以适配不同VLM架构的安全观测，并将被选中请求路由至同一受害模型的响应复核，输出可接受候选或受限替代响应。

Result: 在3个VLM、5类多模态越狱族上取得86.06–89.30%宏平均防御成功率，同时保持较高良性任务效用；受控实验表明查询匹配参照与保留上下文方向对应性显著提升低假阳性区间的风险排序。

Conclusion: CNRF把基于表征的VLM安全从"学习有害性边界"重构为"构造可用于选择性风险决策的证据"。

Abstract: Representation-based safety defenses for vision-language models (VLMs) often learn to separate harmful from benign inputs, but separation alone does not establish what a risk score means or when it should be trusted. We introduce Counterfactual Neighborhood Risk Fields (CNRF), an evidence-grounded framework for selective VLM safety. CNRF constructs context-matched benign-harmful counterfactual pairs, uses their representation differences as capability-related risk directions, and estimates neighborhood support separately from risk. This separation prevents unfamiliarity from being conflated with harmfulness and enables selective intervention rather than blanket rejection. A victim-adaptive interface accommodates different safety observations across VLM architectures and routes selected requests for same-victim response review, which either releases acceptable candidates or produces constrained alternatives with reduced harmful capability. Across three VLMs and five multimodal jailbreak families, CNRF achieves 86.06-89.30% macro defense success rate while retaining substantial benign-task utility. Controlled experiments further show that query-matched references and preserved context-direction correspondence materially improve risk ranking in the low-false-positive regime. CNRF thus reframes representation-based VLM safety from learning harmfulness boundaries to constructing applicable evidence for selective risk decisions.

</details>


### [294] [Evaluating Model Capabilities After Benchmark Saturation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20667)
*Morio Hamada,Jehyeok Yeon,Matteo Migliarini,Justin Olive,Magda Dubois*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出利用已饱和基准中"正确解答的完整转录文本"（脚本）提取能力信息，发现这些脚本保留超出基准准确率的能力信号；在评估的协议中成对判断（pairwise judging）效果最好，可将预测误差相对仅用饱和分数的基线降低5.3倍，但其预测力在分布偏移下并非总能泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI能力快速提升导致基准趋于饱和：当多个模型接近满分时，基准失去区分力，也无法再为AI安全案例与前沿政策提供依据。然而基准条目本身产生远多于分数的信息——即模型如何解���任务的完整转录。作者希望探究：在基准分数饱和之后，这些转录能否继续用于评估模型能力，从而延长现有基准的寿命。

Method: 回顾性地模拟饱和：利用历史基准与模型版本构造"已饱和"设定，仅访问正确解答的转录；测试基于转录的协议能否预测在留出的、未饱和条目上测得的能力。评估三类代表性方法：成对判断（pairwise judging）、单转录绝对打分、以及基于确定性转录特征或嵌入的预测。

Result: 饱和转录中保留超出基准准确率的能力信息；成对判断表现最佳，相比仅用饱和基准分数的基线将预测误差降低5.3倍；能力相关信息分布在转录各段落，但集中于推理与工具调用等特定信息通道；协议的预测力在分布偏移下不总泛化，例如会低估2025年初首批推理模型的能力。

Conclusion: 饱和基准的任务转录中仍存有可预测能力的信号，因此要延长现有基准的使用寿命，需要设计能在分布偏移下稳健提取这些信号的方法，而非仅依赖基准分数。

Abstract: Rapid advances in AI capabilities are causing benchmarks to saturate. Once several models approach perfect scores, a benchmark loses its power to differentiate between them, and with it its utility for informing AI safety cases and frontier policies. Yet benchmark items produce far more information than the scores we typically extract from them: a full transcript of how each model solved the task. We investigate whether these transcripts can be used to assess model capabilities even after benchmark scores have saturated. We simulate saturation retrospectively using historical benchmark and model releases, and test whether protocols that access only correctly solved transcripts from saturated benchmarks can predict capabilities measured on held-out, unsaturated items. We evaluate representative contemporary approaches for extracting information from model transcripts: pairwise judging, single-transcript absolute scoring, and predictions from deterministic transcript features or embeddings. We find that saturated transcripts retain capability information beyond benchmark accuracy. Amongst our evaluated protocols, pairwise judging performs best, reducing prediction error by 5.3 times compared to a baseline using only saturated benchmark scores. This capability-relevant information is distributed across transcript sections, but concentrated in specific channels of information such as reasoning and tool-calls. However, the predictive power of protocols does not always generalise across distribution shifts -- for example by underestimating the capabilities of the first reasoning models introduced in early 2025. Saturated benchmarks thus retain capability-predicting signal in their task transcripts, but extending the lifetime of existing benchmarks requires methods designed to extract these signals robustly.

</details>


### [295] [Hierarchical Graph Convolutional Network Approach for Detecting Low-Quality Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21350)
*Jaeyoung Lee,Joonwon Jang,Misuk Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出层次图卷积网络（HGCN）用于自动检测文档内部不一致及标题-正文不匹配的低质量文档，并构建了Inconsistency数据集，取得91.20%准确率，在公开数据集上达92.00%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各类媒体中大量低质量文档缺乏内部一致性或标题与内容无关，令读者疲劳并损害媒体公信力，因此亟需基于自然语言处理的自动检测方法。

Method: 提出层次图卷积网络（HGCN），同时建模文档内部不一致与标题-正文不一致；利用已发布新闻数据及其元数据构建Inconsistency数据集用于训练。

Result: 在自建Inconsistency数据集上准确率达91.20%，优于对比模型；在公开的不一致相关数据集上性能达92.00%，验证泛化性；消融实验证实元数据的显著增益作用。

Conclusion: HGCN可有效检测文档内部及标题-正文不一致，元数据利用对性能提升贡献显著，模型有望广泛应用于真实场景中低质量文档的检测与过滤。

Abstract: Consistency within a document is a crucial feature indicative of its quality. Recently, within the vast amount of information produced across various media, there exists a significant number of low-quality documents that either lack internal consistency or contain content utterly unrelated to their headlines. Such low-quality documents induce fatigue in readers and undermine the credibility of the media source that provided them. Consequently, research to automatically detect these low-quality documents based on natural language processing is imperative. In this study, we introduce a hierarchical graph convolutional network (HGCN) that can detect internal inconsistencies within a document and incongruences between the title and body. Moreover, we constructed the Inconsistency Dataset, leveraging published news data and its meta-data, to train our model to detect document inconsistencies. Experimental results demonstrated that the HGCN achieved superior performance with an accuracy of 91.20% on our constructedInconsistency Dataset, outperforming other comparative models. Additionally, on the publicly available incongruent-related dataset, the proposed methodology demonstrated a performance of 92.00%, validating its general applicability. Finally, an ablation study further confirmed the significant impact of meta-data utilization on performance enhancement. We anticipate that our model can be universally applied to detect and filter low-quality documents in the real world.

</details>


### [296] [FedSkillGit: Federated Skill Evolution via Version Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21348)
*Boxun Lan,Jiamei Li,Weijian Li,Chengyu Yang,Zhuang Qi,Chengcheng Wan,Xiaohong Chen,Ming Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FedSkillGit 用“提交补丁（commit）+ 两阶段隐私过滤 + 服务器去重与经验文档”替代整篇技能文档上传聚合，在三个数据集与三种模型族上比 SOTA 基线最高提升 22%，并在不共享原始客户端数据的前提下接近集中式技能演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent 可从执行经验中演化可复用的自然语言技能，但真实部署中经验分散在各数据持有方且原始数据不可汇聚，因此需要联邦式技能演化。最直接的方案是让每个客户端演化完整技能文档再上传聚合，但这存在三个错配：演化后的文档仍可能夹带从私有轨迹蒸馏出的客户端专属内容；反复传输几乎不变的整篇文档会浪费通信在群体已掌握的知识上；单个客户端的贡献被埋没在整篇文档中，服务器无法有效归因。

Method: 以 commit 替代全文档更新：每个客户端提交对具名规则的有界补丁集合，并附上概括本轮所学内容的本地经验，二者在离开客户端前通过两阶段隐私过滤器；服务器对所有参与者提交的补丁与本地经验去重，更新仅优化器可读的全局经验文档，并将存活补丁合并为候选技能；候选技能下发各客户端在本地验证集上打分并回报，仅当分数优于当前版本时才发布。

Result: 在三个数据集和三种语言模型族上的实验显示，FedSkillGit 相较最先进基线最高提升 22%，并且在不共享原始客户端数据的情况下接近集中式演化的效果。

Conclusion: 细粒度、可归因的补丁式联邦技能演化在隐私、通信与贡献归因之间取得更好折中，是可行且高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Large language model agents can improve their capabilities by evolving reusable natural-language skills from execution experience. In real-world deployments, this experience is distributed across data owners who cannot pool their raw data, which motivates federated skill evolution. The natural first design lets each client evolve the whole skill document and upload the result for server-side aggregation. That design is poorly matched to skill evolution for three reasons. An evolved document can still carry client-specific content distilled from private traces, repeated transmission of a nearly unchanged document spends communication on knowledge that the population already holds and the contribution of a single client is buried in a document that the server cannot meaningfully attribute. We propose FedSkillGit, a federated skill evolution framework that replaces full-document updates with commits. Each participating client submits a bounded set of patches over named rules together with its local experience, which summarizes what the round taught it, and the pair passes a two-stage privacy filter before it leaves the client. The server deduplicates the submitted patches and the local experience of all participants, updates a global experience document that only the optimizers read, and merges the surviving patches into a candidate skill. The candidate is distributed to the clients, which score it on their own validation sets and report the results, and it is published only when the reported scores improve on the current version. Experiments on three datasets and three language model families show that FedSkillGit outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by up to 22% and approaches centralized evolution without sharing raw client data.

</details>


### [297] [ARGUS: An Agenda-Driven Framework for Reliable User Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21346)
*Hang Zheng,Nan Jiang,Zichen Zhu,Zixu Yang,Zhiyang Tang,Shuai Fan,Lu Chen,Xiaobao Wu,Kai Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出神经符号框架ARGUS，用符号化动态议程栈跟踪用户目标状态并与语言生成解耦，并通过蒸馏管线压缩为小模型，配合新指标CBS在τ³-Bench与MultiWOZ上验证优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的用户模拟器存在目标遗漏与策略不合规等可靠性问题，影响交互环境中智能体的评估与训练质量。

Method: ARGUS以符号操作维护动态议程栈来表征用户目标状态，将状态跟踪与自然语言生成严格解耦，保证目标推进与约束对齐；随后通过统一管线蒸馏为紧凑模型：先评估器引导的SFT，再进行带稠密步级奖励的在线强化学习。

Result: 在τ³-Bench和MultiWOZ上，ARGUS架构显著优于基线，且其蒸馏训练管线有效；同时提出基于评分量规的行为保真度指标CBS。

Conclusion: 神经符号解耦设计可提升LLM用户模拟器的可靠性与行为保真度，且可通过蒸馏实现低成本部署。

Abstract: High-fidelity user simulators are essential for evaluating and training agents in interactive environments. However, current LLM-based simulators struggle with reliability issues, such as goal omission and policy non-compliance. We propose ARGUS (Agenda-dRiven Goal-oriented User Simulator), a neuro-symbolic framework that maintains the user's goal state using a dynamic agenda stack manipulated via symbolic operations. By rigorously decoupling state tracking from natural language generation, ARGUS facilitates strict goal progression and constraint-aligned interactions. For cost-efficient deployment, we distill ARGUS into compact models using a unified pipeline: evaluator-guided SFT followed by online RL with dense step-level rewards. To rigorously evaluate behavioral fidelity, we introduce a rubric-based metric Composite Behavioral Score (CBS). Evaluations on $τ^3$-Bench and MultiWOZ demonstrate the significant superiority of the ARGUS architecture and the efficacy of our training pipeline. Code and data will be released soon.

</details>


### [298] [Diviner: Agentic RCA with Human-Governed Adaptation for Large-Scale Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21281)
*Pradipta Ghosh,Gaurav Mitra,Shripad Nadgowda,Akshil Shah,Junyu Liu,Canturk Isci,Syamla Bandla,Xinyao Hu,Onkar Deshpande,Anik Dasgupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Diviner——Meta部署的agentic根因分析系统，以"编码-执行-评估-适配"四阶段可靠性生命周期组织，通过领域知识编码与可验证证据链提升分布式系统RCA的准确性与可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模分布式系统的根因分析跨服务、数据、配置、算力与组织边界，单一症状难以定位；响应者需拼凑不完整证据并协调多方专家。通用LLM agent仅靠工具调用无法解决：可能使用过时或错时上下文、将查询失败误判为症状缺失、报告无依据的系统状态，并给面向人工节奏设计的可观测服务带来过载。

Method: 四阶段生命周期：Encode将大量已评审事件与服务负责人经验整合，产出有界、带版本的技能、工作流、类型化工具与事件知识图谱；Execute采用自适应Lead与上下文隔离的worker，将相关知识应用于时点证据并维护证据链接的假设；Evaluate将保存的调查轨迹与响应者评审结果比对，度量精确根因、有用方向与Groundedness；Adapt依据反复出现的缺口与值班反馈生成针对性运维知识更新，仅在回放、质量检查与负责人批准后采纳。

Result: 系统在Meta实际部署运行；评估以精确根因、有用方向与Groundedness三项指标衡量，并用于驱动知识迭代。摘要未给出具体量化数值。

Conclusion: RCA agent的可靠性来自知识编码、上下文隔离执行、可验证证据链与受控知识更新的闭环，而非单纯扩大模型工具权限。

Abstract: Root-cause analysis (RCA) in large-scale distributed systems is challenging because a single symptom can cross service, data, configuration, compute, and organizational boundaries. Responders must assemble incomplete evidence and coordinate experts who hold different parts of the causal path. Generic language-model agents do not solve this problem through tool access alone: they can use stale or mistimed context, interpret failed queries as absent symptoms, report unsupported system state, and overload observability services designed for human-paced use.
  We present Diviner (Domain-Informed Verifiable Incident Navigation through Evidence and Review), an agentic RCA system operating at Meta and organized around a four-stage reliability lifecycle. Encode combines a large corpus of reviewed incidents with service-owner expertise to produce scoped, versioned skills, workflows, typed tools, and an incident knowledge graph. Execute uses an adaptive Lead and context-isolated workers to apply relevant knowledge to point-in-time evidence and maintain evidence-linked hypotheses. Evaluate compares saved investigation trajectories with responder-reviewed outcomes to measure exact cause, useful direction, and Groundedness. Adapt uses recurring gaps and on-call feedback to derive focused operational- knowledge updates, admitting them only after replay, quality checks, and owner approval.

</details>


### [299] [GRADS: Graph-Regulated Agentic Data Synthesis with Controllable Difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21167)
*Gaocheng Yang,Ruijie Tao,Kai Ye,Senhua Zhang,Zhengqin Zhu,Ziyang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GRADS：以证据-推理依赖图为引导、显式控制难度的闭环 agentic 数据合成框架，生成可验证且难度分布可分离的多跳长程任务，SFT/RL 后训练在工具调用效率与领域基准上显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究型 agent 需从大规模语料中检索精确信息，但多跳、长程任务仍困难。现有合成数据流水线主要靠"扩展种子任务"拉高表观深度与广度，导致新引入证据与答案耦合弱，意图难度与实际难度不匹配。

Method: GRADS 构建并维护一张图，编码推导答案所需的证据与推理依赖；以子图为条件执行"种子选择—证据扩展—问答构建—验证"的闭环。每一步仅以相关子图指导生成，保留不可省略的推理链，避免无关上下文累积，从而实现显式难度控制。

Result: 在安全、软件工程、科学文献三类领域实验中，持续产出经独立求解器验证、且难度分布经验上可分离的任务。相同训练预算下：SFT 将解终止率提升至 95% 以上，工具调用次数与轮数最多降低 75%；RL 进一步降低工具使用最多 19.5%；所得 4B 模型在自建领域基准上软件工程与 Sonnet 4 持平，科学、安全领域超越之。

Conclusion: 显式建模证据-推理依赖并以图为条件的合成方式，比单纯扩展种子更能保证推理必要性与难度可控性；该数据在多领域带来训练效率与实际性能双重收益，小模型可对标强闭源模型。

Abstract: Deep research agents are increasingly deployed to retrieve precise information from large corpora, yet multi-hop, long-horizon tasks remain challenging. Synthetic data generation has emerged as a scalable solution, with existing pipelines typically expanding seed tasks to increase their apparent depth and breadth. However, this expansion does not reliably translate into necessary reasoning: newly introduced evidence may be only weakly coupled to the answer, creating a mismatch between intended and empirical difficulty. To address this problem, we introduce GRADS, a graph-guided agentic data synthesis framework with explicit difficulty control. GRADS runs a closed loop of seed selection, evidence expansion, question--answer construction, and verification while maintaining a graph of the evidence and inference dependencies required to derive each answer. At each step, it conditions generation on a relevant subgraph, preserving indispensable inference without accumulating irrelevant context. Experiments across security, software-engineering, and scientific-document domains show that GRADS consistently generates verified tasks with empirically separated difficulty distributions, as measured by independent solvers. Moreover, under matched training budgets, post-training on this data delivers consistent gains at every level: SFT raises the solution-termination rate to over 95% while cutting tool calls and turns by up to 75%, RL trims tool use by up to a further 19.5%, and on our held-out, domain-specific benchmarks, the resulting 4B model matches Sonnet~4 on software engineering and surpasses it on the scientific and security domains.

</details>


### [300] [Evaluating Inquiry Learning in LLM-Based Agents: Hypothesizing, Reasoning, and Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21604)
*Marta Knežević,Seyed Parsa Neshaei,Jibril Frej,Vinitra Swamy,Tanja Käser*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一、交互式的评测框架，将 Countdown 任务扩展为数学环境，让 LLM 智能体通过操纵变量、收集观测并推断变量间关系，从而整体评估其科学探究能力（探索策略、因果发现、假设精炼）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往工作多在孤立条件下评估 LLM 的归纳推理、假设生成、数据分析等单项能力，无法反映模型在完整探究循环中如何整合这些能力。

Method: 构建交互式数学环境（Countdown 扩展版），智能体通过操纵变量探测系统、收集观测并推断底层表达式；在 7 个 LLM 与多种提示策略下，分析探索策略、因果发现与假设精炼表现。

Result: LLM 固有探究能力有限，发现底层表达式的成功率较低；但受教学脚手架启发的结构化引导能显著提升性能。

Conclusion: 当前 LLM 在策略性探索与迭代假设精炼方面存在明显局限；为人类探究式学习设计的简单引导策略可有效支持 LLM 的探究行为。

Abstract: Scientific inquiry involves iterative cycles of exploration, hypothesis generation, and reasoning over observations to uncover relationships in complex systems. Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in several components of this process, including inductive reasoning, hypothesis generation, and data analysis. However, prior work typically evaluates these abilities in isolation, leaving open how models integrate them across a full inquiry cycle. In this work, we introduce a framework for evaluating LLMs' inquiry capabilities in a unified, interactive setting. We extend the Countdown task to a mathematical environment where an agent probes the system by manipulating variables, collecting observations, and inferring relationships between them. This setup enables analysis of exploration strategies, causal discovery, and hypothesis refinement during inquiry. Across seven LLMs and multiple prompting strategies, our experiments reveal limited inherent inquiry capabilities and low success rates in discovering the underlying expressions. However, structured guidance inspired by educational scaffolding significantly improves performance. Our findings highlight current limitations of LLMs in strategic exploration and iterative hypothesis refinement, and show how simple guidance strategies developed for human learners in inquiry learning can support more effective inquiry behavior.

</details>


### [301] [ISW-Agent: Integrating LLM-Driven Dialogue with n8n Workflow for Internal Solitary Wave Remote Sensing Image Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21502)
*Hao Zhang,Hengrui Liu,Lina Sun,Junmin Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ISW-Agent，将大语言模型解析与 n8n 工作流、独立科学模型结合，实现从自然语言请求到内孤立波波锋预测的可追溯空间处理链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内孤立波（ISW）遥感需在影像获取、检测、条纹提取与传播预测各阶段间传递任务参数与空间信息，跨阶段衔接繁琐、易出错且缺乏可追溯性。

Method: 以 LLM 负责指令理解，通过结构化任务约束、地理空间坐标变换与分阶段输出校验，将 n8n 工作流编排与独立科学模型（检测、条纹提取、传播预测）串联；每后端 120 条中文指令测试、50 个计时场景、3,408 次故障注入实验评估。

Result: GPT-5.6 Sol 与 DeepSeek 严格任务匹配率均为 94.2%，意图与执行决策全部与参考一致；50 个定时场景中 39 个完成检测—提取—预测全流程，中位周转时间 48.04 s，11 个在空提取后终止；重试恢复 234 次瞬态故障，输出校验拦截 234 个结构性缺陷，另有 61 个溯源/完整性错误暴露内容一致性检查缺口。

Conclusion: ISW-Agent 可将自然语言请求对接为可追溯的空间处理链，支撑多模型协同的 ISW 分析；后续需补强内容一致性校验以覆盖溯源与完整性缺陷。

Abstract: Internal solitary wave (ISW) remote sensing requires task parameters and spatial information to be transferred across image acquisition, detection, stripe extraction, and propagation prediction. Here, we present ISW-Agent, which integrates large language model (LLM) interpretation with n8n workflows and independent scientific models through structured task constraints, geospatial transformations, and stage-specific output validation. In tests of 120 Chinese instructions per backend, GPT-5.6 Sol and DeepSeek both achieved a strict task-match rate of 94.2%, with all intents and execution decisions matching the reference. Sentinel-1 cases demonstrated continuous processing from dialogue requests to wavefront prediction for the specified scene. Of 50 timed scenes, 39 completed detection, extraction, and prediction with a median turnaround time of 48.04 s; 11 stopped after empty extraction. Across 3,408 controlled fault-injection runs, retries recovered 234 transient-failure conditions and output validation rejected 234 structural defects, while 61 provenance or completeness errors indicated the need for content-consistency checks. Overall, ISW-Agent thus connects natural-language requests to traceable spatial processing chains for multimodel ISW analysis.

</details>


### [302] [Incentivizing Tool-Use Reinforcement Learning for Post-Arrival Emergency Care Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21449)
*Pengfei Hu,Chenwei Wu,Qiao Jin,Jun Deng,Zhiyong Lu,Liyue Shen,Yue Ning*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TIR-EC：将EHR特征组包装为可调用工具，用梯度提升树教师提供奖励信号（参考工具集+分级风险目标），并通过逆向课程在RL中逐步撤除提示中的临床技能以蒸馏进权重，4B模型在三项临床预测任务上取得最高macro F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可同时处理结构化测量与自由文本病历，适合急诊预测；但现有LLM在EHR预测任务上不及树模型等传统ML。作者将差距归因于两点弱点：1）LLM不能选出有信息量的特征；2）即使给定正确特征，LLM也误判数值对风险的含义。

Method: 提出TIR-EC（Tool-Integrated RL for Emergency Care）：(1) 将EHR特征组暴露为LLM可调用的工具，实现显式特征选择；(2) 用梯度提升树（GBT）教师提供奖励，包括参考性信息工具集与分级风险目标，对工具选择与数值-风险组合进行监督式奖励塑形；(3) 通过逆向课程（reverse curriculum）在RL过程中逐步从提示中移除任务相关临床技能，使技能蒸馏进模型权重。属于受控分析+强化学习框架。

Result: 在三个临床预测任务上，4B规模agent的macro F1为所有对比模型中最高，优于传统ML基线，并与大得多的前沿LLM相当；进一步分析显示其以更少的工具调用选出更有用证据，并能更有效地组合测量值。

Conclusion: LLM在EHR预测上的落后源于特征选择与数值风险解读两类可定位缺陷，而非模型规模本身；以工具调用+树教师奖励+逆向技能蒸馏的组合可显著缩小并超越传统ML差距，小模型亦可达前沿LLM水平。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can reason over both structured measurements and free-text records, which makes them attractive for emergency department (ED) prediction. However, most existing LLMs still fall short of conventional ML methods like trees on EHR-based prediction tasks. Our controlled analysis traces this gap to two weaknesses: LLMs do not select informative features, and they misjudge what numerical values imply for risk even when given the right features. We propose TIR-EC, a tool-integrated reinforcement learning framework that addresses both. TIR-EC exposes EHR feature groups as tools the LLM can call. It trains the LLM with rewards from a gradient-boosted tree teacher, which provides a reference set of informative tools and a graded risk target. TIR-EC also distills task-specific clinical skills into the model weights through a reverse curriculum that gradually removes the skills from the prompt during RL process. Evaluation on three clinical prediction tasks shows that our 4B agent achieves the highest macro F1 of all compared models, outperforms conventional ML baselines, and is competitive with much larger frontier LLMs. Further analysis shows that TIR-EC selects more useful evidence with fewer tool calls and combines measurements more effectively.

</details>


### [303] [Self-Guided LLM Reasoning for Error Diagnosis in Vehicle Routing Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21393)
*Ni Zhang,Jianan Zhou,Yue-Jiao Gong,Zhiguang Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估LLM检测（与纠正）VRP实例中错误的能力，并提出自引导推理框架，让LLM自主生成代码提供结构化洞见以辅助错误诊断，在多种问题设置与LLM上显著提升检测性能，接近完美准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的VRP求解方法多假设输入实例无错误，忽视真实数据中的常见故障；同时LLM在复杂实例或显著增加推理负担的错误上存在明显局限。

Method: 系统性地考察LLM对VRP实例中错误的检测与纠正能力，识别关键局限；进而提出自引导推理框架，由LLM自主生成代码，产出结构化信息以辅助错误诊断。

Result: 在多样化问题设置与多个LLM上的实验表明，该框架显著提升错误检测表现，达到接近完美的准确率。

Conclusion: 将LLM的自由推理与自生成代码的结构化分析相结合，可有效缓解复杂VRP实例中的错误诊断难题，为鲁棒的LLM驱动组合优化求解提供可行路径。

Abstract: Vehicle Routing Problems (VRPs) are fundamental to many real-world applications. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have spurred progress in solving these NP-hard problems. However, most existing approaches assume that input instances are error-free, neglecting the presence of faults commonly encountered in real-world data. In this work, we systematically investigate the capability of LLMs to detect (and correct) errors in VRP instances, and identify key limitations, particularly in handling complex instances or errors that substantially increase the LLM's reasoning burden. To overcome these challenges, we propose a self-guided reasoning framework in which LLMs autonomously generate code that provides structured insights to aid in error diagnosis. Experiments across diverse problem settings and LLMs demonstrate that our framework significantly improves error detection performance, achieving near-perfect accuracy.

</details>


### [304] [Learning to Reason with Selective Internal Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21392)
*Xun Lu,Shumeng Yang,Xinrui Wang,Songjun Tu,Linjing Li,Daniel Dajun Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SIF是一种基于内部反馈的强化学习方法，通过比较式奖励构造与选择性策略更新，缓解自置信度与正确性背离的问题，在12个数学与科学推理基准上取得更优准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于内部反馈（如自置信度）的强化学习无需真值答案或外部验证器即可提升语言模型，但训练中置信度与正确性可能发生背离，持续优化平均自置信度会提高置信度却损害推理准确率，因此需要新的奖励与更新机制。

Method: 提出SIF方法，将比较式奖励构造与选择性策略更新相结合：在多个窗口与同伴之间比较排序后的token级自置信度剖面，仅当两个层级的投票均提供充分支持时赋予正奖励；并通过样本级与组级过滤决定哪些回答参与策略优化。

Result: 在3个骨干模型、12个数学与科学推理基准上的实验表明，SIF最终检查点的平均准确率高于Intuitor与Vigor的最佳验证检查点，在Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B上较Intuitor提升4.71个百分点；进一步分析显示持续优化过程中准确率保持更好，测试时训练实验表明每个提示仅需4次rollout即可获得持续改进。

Conclusion: SIF通过比较式奖励与过滤机制有效缓解自置信度与正确性的背离，在无需真值标签的条件下实现更稳健的推理能力提升，并具备较低采样成本下的持续改进能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from internal feedback can improve language models without ground-truth answers or external verifiers. However, confidence and correctness can diverge during training, and continued optimization of average self-certainty can increase confidence while degrading reasoning accuracy. We introduce SIF, a reinforcement learning method that combines comparative reward construction with selective policy updates. SIF compares ranked token-level self-certainty profiles across multiple windows and peers, assigning positive rewards only when both levels of voting provide sufficient support. Sample and group level filtering determines which responses contribute to policy optimization. Experiments on three backbones across 12 mathematical and scientific reasoning benchmarks show that SIF's final checkpoints achieve higher average accuracy than the best-validation checkpoints of Intuitor and Vigor, with a gain of 4.71 percentage points over Intuitor on Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B. Further analyses show better retention of accuracy during continued optimization, while test-time training experiments demonstrate sustained improvements with as few as four rollouts per prompt.

</details>


### [305] [KnowGPO: Knowledge-Grouped Policy Optimization for Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22331)
*Siqi Chen,Zhengxi Lu,Yuchen Yan,Xingyu Wu,Qipeng Chen,Xin Zhang,Aiting Liu,Chao Deng,Jie Liu,Jin Ma,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KnowGPO提出以任务相关知识状态而非表面观测来对齐轨迹的强化学习框架，通过原子知识条件分解与知识增益过程奖励实现细粒度信用分配，在多个深度搜索基准上显著超越GRPO、GiGPO、IGPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度搜索智能体需通过长程多轮交互完成知识密集型任务，个体搜索决策的信用分配极为困难。结果型RL仅提供共享的单一终端奖励；现有过程监督方法将信用锚定于表层轨迹状态或模型内部信号，而深度搜索场景中观测异构、检索上下文长且噪声大，导致这些信号不可靠。

Method: 提出KnowGPO框架：(1) 针对每个查询，将推导答案所需的信息分解为原子知识条件（atomic knowledge conditions）；(2) 用每步已满足的条件子集表示轨迹的知识状态；(3) 将知识状态相同的轨迹分组进行组内比较；(4) 以相邻步骤间的增量知识增益作为环境锚定的过程奖励。

Result: 在Xbench、BrowseComp-ZH与GAIA上，KnowGPO相较纯结果监督（GRPO）与过程监督基线（GiGPO、IGPO）稳定提升3.8–4.2分，8B模型可媲美或超越多数32B开源系统。

Conclusion: 以知识状态对齐轨迹可有效缓解深度搜索中的信用分配难题，提供比表层状态或内部信号更鲁棒的过程监督，验证了环境锚定的知识增益奖励的有效性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Deep search agents solve knowledge-intensive tasks through long-horizon, multi-turn interactions, where assigning credit to individual search decisions is fundamentally difficult. Outcome-based reinforcement learning provides only a single terminal reward shared across an entire trajectory. Recent process-supervision methods aim to assign credit at finer granularity, but they typically anchor credit to either surface-level trajectory states or model-internal signals, both of which become unreliable in deep search where observations are heterogeneous and retrieved contexts are long and noisy. We propose KnowGPO, a reinforcement learning framework that aligns trajectories by the task-relevant knowledge they have acquired rather than by their surface observations. For each query, KnowGPO decomposes the information required to derive the answer into atomic knowledge conditions, and represents the state of a trajectory at each step by the subset of conditions it has satisfied. Trajectories sharing the same knowledge state are grouped for within-group comparison, and the incremental knowledge gain between consecutive steps serves as an environment-grounded process reward. Experiments on Xbench, BrowseComp-ZH, and GAIA show KnowGPO consistently outperforms outcome-only (GRPO) and process-supervision baselines (GiGPO, IGPO) by 3.8 to 4.2 points, with our 8B model rivaling or exceeding most 32B open-source systems.

</details>


### [306] [Designing Transformers for Continuous Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22330)
*Weijian Luo,Yujian Chen,Hao Jiang,Guang Cheng,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究连续扩散语言模型（CDLM）的Transformer架构，提出LangDiT骨干，在匹配对比下显著降低生成困惑度并刷新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CDLM多直接套用因果LLM或图像DiT的Transformer架构，未针对连续扩散语言生成进行优化，架构本身可能是被忽视的性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出Language Diffusion Transformer（LangDiT），融合特征与query-key归一化、更宽的噪声条件化、似然捷径梯度路由与校准、以及置信度门控的训练自条件化；作为即插即用骨干接入LangFlow与Plaid式CDLM。

Result: 匹配对比中显著降低生成困惑度。LangFlow+LangDiT在OpenWebText 1024 token无条件生成上达GenPPL 21.72（1024 NFEs）与44.72（128 NFEs）；Plaid式表述下达11.33（1024步）与27.51（128步），均为新SOTA。

Conclusion: 在保持各模型表示、训练目标与采样器不变的前提下仅替换架构即获提升，表明架构设计是CDLM改进的重要来源。

Abstract: Continuous diffusion language models (CDLMs) offer an alternative approach to parallel language generation; however, most existing studies directly use transformer architectures from either causal large language models (LLMs) or Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) for image generation, which were not optimized for CDLMs. In this paper, we conduct a systematic study on Transformer architectures for CDLMs and introduce Language Diffusion Transformer (LangDiT), combining feature and query-key normalization, wider noise conditioning, likelihood-shortcut gradient routing and calibration, and confidence-gated training self-conditioning. LangDiT significantly reduces generation perplexity in matched LangFlow comparisons, establishing a new state of the art in continuous language generation across different model formulations. Using LangFlow as a representative CDLM, LangDiT as a plug-and-play backbone leads to a new state-of-the-art model with a generation perplexity (GenPPL) of 21.72 using 1024 NFEs and 44.72 GenPPL using 128 NFEs on the unconditional generation of 1024-token sequences on the OpenWebText dataset. When adapted to Plaid-style CDLM formulations, LangDiT results in a new leading GenPPL of 11.33 using 1024 denoising NFEs, and 27.51 GenPPL using 128 denoising NFEs. These gains are achieved while retaining each model's underlying representation, training objective, and inference sampler, isolating transformer architecture as a fundamental source of improvement. Our study identifies architectural design as an important source of improvement in continuous diffusion language models.

</details>


### [307] [Private Retrieval Augmented Generation via Random Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22297)
*Dixi Yao,Tahseen Rabbani,Tian Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于随机投影的简单方法，在RAG系统中保护检索数据存储隐私：对数据存储嵌入与查询嵌入施加同一投影，在保持查询—检索项语义相似性的同时显著缓解数据抽取攻击，性能优于依赖形式化差分隐私保证的先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG通过查询外部结构化知识增强LLM能力，但会从检索数据存储中泄露敏感信息，带来隐私风险；现有形式化DP方法虽有理论保证，但往往牺牲检索与生成质量，亟需更轻量且实用的隐私保护方案。

Method: 采用随机投影（random projection）：对数据存储嵌入和查询嵌入施加相同的投影矩阵，从而在共享低维子空间中计算相似度。该方法可证明保持查询与检索项之间的语义相似性，同时降低嵌入可逆性以缓解数据抽取攻击；并在多种RAG架构与数据集上，与基于形式化差分隐私的方法进行对比评估（检索性能、生成质量及强攻击模型下的经验隐私）。

Result: 在多种RAG架构与数据集上，该轻量方法在检索与生成性能上优于依赖形式化DP保证的先前方法，同时在强攻击模型下表现出可与之相当的经验隐私水平。

Conclusion: 首次表明随机投影可作为隐私保护RAG系统的一个有竞争力且实用的基线方案，兼具轻量性、语义相似性保持与经验隐私保护效果。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances the capabilities of large language models (LLMs) by querying external structured knowledge. However, it can also introduce privacy risks by leaking sensitive information from the retrieval datastore. We propose a simple method to preserve datastore privacy in RAG systems via random projection. By applying the same projection to both datastore embeddings and query embeddings, our method provably preserves semantic similarity between queries and retrieved items while substantially mitigating data extraction attacks. Across multiple RAG architectures and datasets, we show that this lightweight approach achieves superior retrieval and generation performance compared to prior methods which leverage formal differential privacy (DP) guarantees, while exhibiting comparable empirical privacy under strong attack models. Our results for the first time suggest that random projection can serve as a competitive and practical baseline for privacy-preserving RAG systems.

</details>


### [308] [EliteReplay: Preserving Diverse Solutions in RL Post-Training via Quality-Diversity Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22290)
*Hanane Nour Moussa,Doeun Lee,Sachin Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EliteReplay 将 GRPO 与基于质量-多样性搜索构建的"精英"存档结合，并通过回放损失在训练中保留基座模型的多样性，从而在 text-to-SQL 任务上同时提升准确率与正确解的多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习作为 LLM 可验证任务的后训练手段虽能提升准确率，却会收窄模型可产生的解集合（行为锐化）。现有缓解方法多优化不反映"正确解多样性"的指标；而理想目标是产生多个优质解，而非仅一个可靠解。

Method: 提出 EliteReplay 后训练配方：训练前用冻结基座模型收集覆盖不同解题路径的解，经质量-多样性搜索构建精英存档；训练时在 GRPO 基础上叠加针对该存档的回放损失（replay loss）。

Result: 在多个 text-to-SQL 任务上，EliteReplay 的 pass@128 高于所有基线，产生的不同正确策略最多比最强基线多 77%；保留基座模型大部分正确策略，策略保留率优于现有方法。在歧义问题数据集 AmbiQT 上，同时覆盖两种解释的频率约为最强基线的两倍。

Conclusion: 以质量-多样性存档回放约束 RL 后训练，可在提升准确率的同时缓解行为锐化，保持解题策略多样性，并在歧义问题上带来实际收益。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become a common post-training technique to improve the accuracy of LLMs on verifiable tasks. However, such training can narrow the set of solutions the model can produce. Several approaches have attempted to address this behavior-sharpening effect, but they often optimize metrics that do not reflect the diversity of correct solutions. Ideally, we want a model that can produce multiple good solutions, not only a single reliable one. We propose EliteReplay, a post-training recipe that pairs GRPO with an "elite" archive built by quality-diversity search. Before training, we use the frozen base model to collect solutions that span distinct approaches; during training, GRPO is combined with a replay loss on this archive. The result is a post-trained model that improves accuracy while preserving more of the base model's solution diversity. On multiple text-to-SQL tasks, EliteReplay achieves a higher pass@128 than all baselines, producing up to 77% more distinct correct strategies than the strongest baselines. It preserves most of the base model's correct strategies, outperforming existing methods in strategy retention. This diversity translates into practical benefits on ambiguous questions, where EliteReplay covers both interpretations nearly twice as often as the strongest baseline on AmbiQT.

</details>


### [309] [RAG-RST: Retrieved Exemplars as In-Context Discourse Schema for RST Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22150)
*Mohammadreza Sediqin,Shlomo Engelson Argamon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RAG-RST，用检索增强的上下文示例向LLM提供RST话语图式，无需训练即在核性/关系预测与整树解析指标上超越现有解析器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RST树边的核性标签与关系标签并非表层属性：同样的两个子句可能因修辞意图不同而分别为Elaboration或Background，其边界由标注约定而非词汇线索决定，需要形式化的话语图式。仅靠富含话语结构的预训练文本并不能提供该图式——给定目标EDU对时，强LLM的核性仅0.794 F1、关系仅0.738 F1。

Method: RAG-RST以检索增强方式将该图式作为上下文示例注入，而非通过参数更新获取，无需训练。比较两种配置：(1) 预计算策略，在解析前为所有EDU对打标签；(2) 动态策略，在每个reduce步骤检索，使到达生成器的示例依赖于解析器已确定的归约。

Result: 核性提升至0.846 F1，关系提升至0.788 F1，且无需训练。两种配置在span、核性、关系及整树指标上均优于现有RST解析器，动态变体始终最强。检索图式呈两点性质：效用随示例数量有界，超过约五十个示例后饱和并下降；且其效用依赖结构位置。

Conclusion: 形式化语言学图式可以作为示例提供给基础模型，而不必训练进其参数；动态、依赖解析状态的检索优于一次性预标注。

Abstract: In Rhetorical Structure Theory (RST), each edge of a discourse tree carries a nuclearity label, marking which span holds the central content, and a relation drawn from a closed inventory of functional categories. Neither label is a surface property: the same two clauses can be Elaboration or Background depending on rhetorical intent, and the boundary between them is fixed by annotation convention rather than by lexical cues. Predicting these labels therefore requires a formal discourse schema, and exposure to discourse-rich pretraining text does not supply it: given only a target EDU pair, a strong LLM recovers nuclearity at 0.794 F1 and relation at 0.738 F1. We propose RAG-RST, a retrieval-augmented framework that supplies this schema as in-context demonstration rather than through parameter updates, raising nuclearity to 0.846 F1 and relation to 0.788 F1 with no training. Two configurations are compared: a precomputed strategy that labels all EDU pairs before parsing, and a dynamic strategy that retrieves at each reduce step, so that the exemplars reaching the generator depend on the reductions the parser has already committed to. Both outperform existing RST parsers across span, nuclearity, relation, and full tree metrics, with the dynamic variant consistently strongest. Two properties of the retrieved schema emerge: its utility is bounded in number, saturating and then declining beyond fifty exemplars, and it depends on structural position. Both indicate that a formal linguistic schema can be supplied to a foundation model as a demonstration rather than trained into its parameters.

</details>


### [310] [DropKV: Decoupling Residual-Output Perturbation for Near-Optimal KV-Cache Eviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22130)
*Aozhong Zhang,Selcuk Gurses,Yanxia Deng,Naigang Wang,Chi-Chun Liu,Davis Wertheimer,Derrick Liu,Xin Li,Zi Yang,Felix X. -F. Ye,Penghang Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DropKV，一种基于解耦残差-输出扰动的KV缓存逐出框架，将组合优化解耦为逐token打分，在锥条件下具常数因子近似保证，并配Triton融合核实现高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文下KV缓存线性增长成为推理瓶颈；现有逐出方法在最小化输出扰动上是NP难组合问题，导致启发式次优或难以kernelize，需兼顾原理性与工程可行性。

Method: 将联合逐出决策解耦为独立的逐token打分（Decoupled Residual-Output Perturbation），规避组合不可解性，并在锥条件下给出常数因子近似保证（于3个长上下文LLM上实证验证该条件）；用融合Triton核实现，避免物化完整注意力矩阵。

Result: 打分核提速19.8x，匹配批次下端到端prefill最高提速9.9%；在RULER与LongBench同缓存预算下持续优于SOTA基线，逐出方法中延迟最低，且快于动态缓存基线。

Conclusion: DropKV在理论近似保证与工程效率间取得平衡，兼顾精度与延迟，为长上下文KV缓存管理提供原理化且实用的方案。

Abstract: Key-value (KV) cache management has become a critical bottleneck for scaling Large Language Models (LLMs) to long-context scenarios due to linear memory growth. While recent eviction methods have moved beyond simple attention-score heuristics to incorporate value representations, they typically face a fundamental trade-off: the underlying optimization for minimizing output perturbation is an NP-hard combinatorial problem, leading to mathematically suboptimal heuristics or implementations that are difficult to kernelize. In this work, we propose DropKV, a simple principled eviction framework based on Decoupled Residual-Output Perturbation. By decoupling the joint eviction decision into independent per-token scoring, DropKV circumvents combinatorial intractability and admits a provable constant-factor approximation guarantee under a cone condition that we empirically verify on three long-context LLMs. To ensure practical viability, we provide a high-performance implementation using fused Triton kernels that avoid materializing the full attention matrix, delivering a 19.8x scoring-kernel speedup that translates to up to a 9.9% matched-batch end-to-end prefill speedup. Extensive evaluations on the RULER and LongBench benchmarks demonstrate that DropKV consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines at equivalent cache budgets, achieving the lowest inference latency among eviction methods while also remaining faster than the dynamic cache baseline.

</details>


### [311] [GraphACM: Graph-based Action Consistency Measurement for Uncertainty Quantification in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22031)
*Yaxin Ma,Wenbo Zhang,Satya Krishna Pothapragada,Alina Zare*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于图的框架，将同一任务多次 rollout 的等价状态合并为有向、动作标记图，在共享决策状态下进行细粒度动作一致性度量，并以全局一致性分数作为不确定性代理预测任务成败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅用单一任务级分数概括一致性，既无法定位行为差异发生的位置，也未保证动作比较处于等价决策上下文；因此需要状态条件下的细粒度一致性度量与不确定性量化。

Method: 给定同一任务的多条执行轨迹，将等价状态合并为有向、动作标记的图；在共享决策状态上比较动作，提出节点级与全局级动作一致性度量；以全局一致性分数作为不确定性代理，用于预测任务成功或失败。

Result: 在 ALFWorld、AppWorld、WebShop 三个环境、三种 LLM 上的实验表明，该度量与任务成功正相关；任务成功预测平均 AUROC 达 0.811，优于最强行为一致性基线（0.729）与言语化置信度（0.769）。

Conclusion: 状态条件下的行为一致性度量有效，既能提升任务成败预测，又能定位智能体动作一致的决策位置。

Abstract: Behavioral consistency across repeated executions provides an informative signal for assessing the reliability of large language model (LLM) agents. However, existing approaches typically summarize consistency with a single task-level score, without identifying where behavioral differences occur or whether actions are compared under equivalent decision contexts. We propose a graph-based framework for fine-grained behavioral consistency measurement and uncertainty quantification. Given multiple rollouts of the same task, we merge equivalent states into a directed, action-labeled graph, enabling action comparisons at shared decision states. We introduce node-level and global measures of action consistency, using the global consistency score as a proxy for uncertainty to predict task success or failure. Experiments across three LLMs on ALFWorld, AppWorld, and WebShop demonstrate that our measure is positively associated with task success and achieves an average AUROC of 0.811 for task-success prediction, outperforming the strongest behavioral-consistency baseline (0.729) and verbalized confidence (0.769). These results highlight the effectiveness of state-conditioned behavioral consistency and pinpoint where agent actions agree.

</details>


### [312] [OntoAct: Hypothesis-Based Neuro-Symbolic Agents through Ontological Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21983)
*Donghyeok Lee,Bong Gyun Kang,Junyong Ahn,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OntoAct：基于本体抽象与假设的神经符号框架，将经验转为类型化公理以复用知识，并由符号验证器校验假设条件；同时提出Tacit-FLE基准，在三个环境中取得领先性能且交互与推理高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从经验中学习需回收可复用知识并判断其适用条件；现有方法难以将个体经验抽象为可跨情境迁移的知识，也缺乏对假设成立条件的显式校验与基于缺失证据的主动补证。

Method: 构建OntoAct神经符号框架：以本体抽象把单次经验转化为关于实体类型、关系与条件的公理，实现跨情境复用；LLM基于该世界知识提出假设与动作，符号验证器对照当前观测评估其支撑条件；验证识别缺失证据，新观测与干预结果用于更新假设评估并指导后续动作。另提出Tacit-FLE基准（基于Factorio Learning Environment），用继承的运行记录诊断并修复工厂隐藏故障，合成维护语料将证据分散于记录中，且执行修复可能未解决故障，迫使智能体通过交互重新评估诊断。

Result: 在Tacit-FLE、Mars与ALFWorld三个环境中，OntoAct取得领先的任务表现，并保持高效的交互次数与推理开销。

Conclusion: 本体抽象+符号条件验证的神经符号闭环能有效实现经验的知识化复用与假设的动态修正；Tacit-FLE为需跨记录整合证据与交互式再诊断的维护类任务提供了诊断与修复评测手段。

Abstract: Learning from experience requires recovering reusable knowledge and determining when it applies to a new situation. We introduce OntoAct, a hypothesis-based neuro-symbolic framework built on ontological abstraction. OntoAct turns individual experiences into axioms over entity types, relations, and conditions, making their underlying patterns reusable across situations. A large language model (LLM) uses this world knowledge to propose hypotheses and actions, while a symbolic verifier assesses their supporting conditions against current observations. Verification identifies missing evidence, and new observations and intervention outcomes update hypothesis assessments and guide subsequent actions. We also introduce Tacit-FLE, a benchmark built on the Factorio Learning Environment (FLE) for diagnosing and repairing hidden faults in existing factories using inherited operational records. Its synthetic maintenance corpus distributes evidence across records, and an executed repair may leave the fault unresolved, requiring agents to reassess their diagnoses through interaction. Across Tacit-FLE, Mars, and ALFWorld, OntoAct achieves leading task performance with efficient interaction and inference.

</details>


### [313] [Data World: Scaling Data Environments from Scratch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21656)
*Zhanli Li,Zeyuan Xu,Chaoxu Pang,Zeyuan Liu,Anyang Ji,Te Sun,Kongcheng Zhang,Wenbo Xu,Xubin Yue,Shipeng Luo,Junjie Zhao,Yingfan Hua,Jingqing Ruan,Zhongda Su,Feng Hong,Zhizhao Zeng,Ke Zeng,Xunliang Cai,Hanjia Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Data World 多智能体引擎：先生成文件背后的「世界机制」再据此合成文件、任务与评分标准（rubric），构建 DataWorld-Bench 基准与 2,135 条训练任务，并用 RL-zero 训练得到 DataWorld-35B-A3B，在四个基准上平均显著优于基座模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据智能体（data agents）通过代码处理异构文件并得出分析洞见，但规模化训练面临三大瓶颈：环境构建成本高昂、隐私与知识产权限制、以及开放式结论缺乏可验证的生成真值（generative truth），导致难以判定答案正确性。

Method: 核心思路是「先规定文件背后的世界，再生成文件」。Data World 引擎基于显式机制（explicit mechanisms）由多智能体协同合成环境、任务与评分 rubric；引入独立审计检查目标结论能否从观测文件中被复原（target recoverability）；rubric 同时用于评测与 RL 奖励。随后基于人类偏好做问题真实性筛选，在 50 个留出世界、16 个领域中选出 100 个任务构成 DataWorld-Bench；引擎另从 1,037 个互不相交的世界产出 2,135 条训练任务。
